First Volume

When Tanya opened her eyes for the first time after reincarnating, she noticed that she was being fed by a strange woman with outdated nun's clothes, she also noticed that there wasn't any electrical devices nearby, which is strange considering that they are on a civilized society in the twenty century, not only this, but there were multiple extremely old items in that place. With these ideas in mind, Tanya came to the conclusion that she was probably in a Mennonite or Amish community.

(Abductive reasoning, critical thinking, Observation, Connecting The Dots)

Tanya then thinks about her past life, she recalls the moment before her death when she was firing an employee, “I always knew that I wasn't the best, incapable of competing against the geniuses and becoming equal to the ones with talent by merely using dedication and effort, my personality became quite distorted. I've become a mess of jumbled complexes. Truly benevolent people are inspiring. As far as hypocrisy goes, I have what society as a whole considers to be healthy. Despite my awareness of how unpleasant I am, I still maintain the arrogant belief that I am superior to this incompetent idiot crying in front of me. At least in terms of cost-benefit, I have maintained superior results. So, even though restructuring departments designed to consolidate through layoffs are a pain, I take it seriously”. When he was about to die, time started to be in slow motion, and he noticed an old man facing him, who was similar to those kind of old man's who commonly appear in Isekai novels. By noticing this, he(Tanya was a man in her past life, so this might be a little confusing) formulated three possible explanations for this.

First possibility: Miraculously, I survived and a doctor is examining me, but I can't quite figure it out. In other words, it's possible that my eyes or brain have suffered severe trauma.

Second possibility: I'm dying and this is an illusion caused by the hallucination. Maybe my life is flashing before my eyes.

Third possibility: I woke up in the real world after mistaking a dream for reality. Maybe I'm still half asleep.

Here Tanya demonstrates impressive inductive reasoning. After hearing the old man say: “many of you have such a distorted personality… what is all this bull crap on your head?”. Tanya came to the conclusion that the old man was capable of reading minds.

Tanya thinks with herself about the identity of the entity in front of her. “Only God or the Devil would be able to defy universal laws. If God really existed, He would not ignore all the injustice in the world. Therefore, this world does not have a god. Therefore, this “Being X” in front of me is the Devil. Case closed”. Hearing the  “Being X” saying “Do you non-believers want to kill your creator of overwork?”. Tanya notices the fact that the Being X said “non-believers” in the plural, which he concludes that there are others like him who don't believe in the existence of God, demonstrating Tanya's verbal reasoning skills.

(FRI=[abstract thinking], VCI, Fluid intelligence, Self-Awareness, Social Cognition, Social Awareness, ER, abductive reasoning, deductive reasoning, abstract reasoning, critical thinking, divergent thinking, non-linear thinking, Holistic thinking, abstract thinking, Observation, connecting the dots)

When the Entente alliance gave orders for the empire to leave their territory in 24 hours, Tanya thought that it was merely an internal propaganda, after all, there was no way that the neighboring nations would let two nations that are close to them start a war, they would try to stop the alliance by using intimidation or mediation, she predicted that the idea of the Entente alliance doing anything was merely a bluff. But her predictions were unexpectedly wrong. While Tanya was being chased by enemy soldiers, she was in panic since the communications are bad, but when it came back to normal, Tanya intuitively felt 100 percent of the chance of trouble, and in fact, she was correct. Tanya received orders to fight against a big group of mages all by herself without having permission to retreat.

Even when being extremely furious with those who gave the orders to her, and scared of the possibility of dying, Tanya still managed her emotions and pretended to be completely calm while talking to those who gave such orders for her, concealing her anger due to practically being put in a certain death situation by her own allies. Tanya was in a disadvantageous situation, she knew that she would end up dead, but she engaged her emotions and went to face the enemies all by herself, after all, if she win, she will receive a big boost of influence on the military, before, she was afraid of death, exactly like any human being, but now, Tanya is completely excited, even when she is in a situation of certain death.

(Acting skills, concealment, Prediction, Critical thinking, Intuition, Intra EM, Intra EE)

Tanya anticipated that if she decides to run away from the battle against the platoon of enemies chasing her, the enemy wouldn't bother chasing her due to the possibility of reinforcements of the empire coming to save Tanya. After some time, it's revealed that Tanya acted as a suicidal bomb to eliminate a big portion of the enemy's platoon and somehow survived, she did this without demonstrating any signs of fear, in fact, she was extremely happy about the possibility of getting recognized by the empire's army by eliminating a whole platoon all by herself. With this, Tanya's influence in the army became so big that she received the silver wings assault badge, which is literally one of the greatest signs of recognition that the Empire could possibly offer to her.

This badge is rarely given to a person who survives in the battlefield, the vast majority of those who received it were already dead corpses, but a rare case happened with Tanya, she received it while still being alive and while being merely a child, this is extremely impressive if we take in consideration the fact that before these events, she was merely a normal mage soldier who was just highly talented and extremely young. After all this, it's revealed that Tanya was the top 2 of her class in the imperial army military academy, in fact, she received excellent scores in every single area in the military academy. This demonstrates Tanya's high academic talent and knowledge.

After this, it's said that during Tanya's job as a mentor of newbie soldiers, she was contradicted by a second-class student, who underestimated her young age and appearance, after hearing this, Tanya tried to execute him in the spot for insubordination and Lergen, who was a man with a considerably high authority on the army and a witness of Tanya's failed attempt to kill one of her students for such a minor thing, was completely terrified by her methods, he was left with an extremely bad first impression of Tanya's personality, in his mind, out of all the imperial magic army officers, Tanya Degurechaff was a dangerous one who was worth remembering, this is an impressive level of Inter EM.

(Anticipation, academics, Problem Solving, decision making, knowledge, methods, Intra EM, psychological warfare, Inter EM, Intra EE, Cunning, influence building)

After being assigned to the technology inspection personnel, which is a domestic service that don't require combat that could put Tanya's life in danger, she abducted that the army held her in high regard due to the fact that not only a domestic position was in all intents and purposes, rear service, but the instructor unit and tester positions were still associated with a strong career path.

Get back! Please get—!”, after hearing this, Tanya quickly comprehended that a struggle for the radio set was occurring and that someone was throwing a fit to forcefully take it back, she came to this conclusion by merely hearing the words stated above which once again demonstrates good verbal reasoning skills.

After the fight, the scientist who created the prototype ended up taking control of the radio set and right now he is insulting Tanya while ignoring all the flaws of the computation Orb prototype that he himself created. Tanya notices this, but she manages her emotions and resists the urge to curse him for putting all the blame on her while ignoring the actual problem, which is, the prototype made by him that has extremely dangerous flaws. After this, Tanya demonstrates her knowledge of the PDCA Cycle, which essentially speaking is: designing a plan (plan), try it out (do), evaluate the results (check), and implement improvements where needed (act). Not only this, but in the same page, she also demonstrates her knowledge of Murphy's law and associates it with her current situation. Murphy's law implies the idea that anything that can go wrong, will go wrong. This is also an impressive abstract thinking feat.

Tanya's Thoughts:
|
“Humans have learned through experience that, as indicated by Murphy’s Law. Anything that can go wrong will. If an office employee has the ability to cause trouble, trouble there shall be. For example, it’s common knowledge in personnel management not to keep a bankrupt employee in a department dealing with finances. In the same vein, flying with an orb that can explode at any time is like sitting around waiting for the blast to happen”.

This item quite literally gives divine power to its user, something that should be completely beyond the comprehension of a Human Being, a person that received divine power wouldn't even be capable of understanding how to use it at first, let alone understanding the power of the type 95 Computation Orb.

But on her first time interacting with this item, Tanya adapted herself cognitively to its power and managed to use it to its full potential in the later volumes of the story. Demonstrating a cognitive adaptability and cognitive discipline feat that allows her to adapt to divine power, something far beyond human understanding, something that demonstrates that her cognitive adaptability and cognitive discipline is completely unrivaled. This would also be a good demonstration of Learning Ability since Tanya managed to fully control this power after using it once while being 8 years old and not being so experienced with computation orbs yet.

Another things that is quite surprising here is that while having only 8 years old, Tanya is adapting to the best considerable level of magic and in her first time processing all the overloading information in her brain at this specific moment of the story, she was processing the speed of magical weapons and this even happens within her training where she was sent the height above clouds, what's even more insane is the fact that she doesn't pass out while processing all the magic information in the Type 95 Computation Orb. She was literally adapting to being thrown to a height above clouds and adapting to the Type 95.

During Tanya's flight to analyze the battlefield, she predicts that the François republic would decide to mobilize a big troop of soldiers to fight against the empire, the reasoning she used to come to this conclusion was exactly as follows:

P1: The relationship between the empire and the François republic is such that they both have a fairly good idea of the cards in each other's hand.

P2: Naturally, the François republic can predict that the empire will confront the encirclement via interior lines.

C1: As a result, its defense strategy would focus tightly on how to defeat the interior lines strategy of its potential enemy.

C2: The solution for this would be quite simple. Before the empire completes its large-scale mobilization, the Republic's core standing army would storm away and subdue the empire's western region, the source of its great industry and military power. That would dramatically cut down the empire's war potential. The Republic's strategy also includes taking action if the empire invades a third nation.

P3: Strictly speaking, the Republic's current position gives it no other choice but to frame all its actions as responses to the empire.

P4: If the Republic's leaders let the situation be, they would eventually have to contend with an empire free of northeastern pressure.

C3: In other words, they have to act now, while they still can gain the upper hand in the war against the empire.

P5: It's possible to notice that the northern front will be decided in one blow, with this, it's also possible to know that the war will come to an end soon.

C4: The Entente alliance's resistance wouldn't amount to anything, and it would be forced to surrender to the empire.

P5: In the perspective of a specialist, it isn't so simple. Some few months is a bit fast for a country to fall into ruin, it's also far too long to have your main forces tied up.

P6: In a few weeks, the mobilization will be completed and the Republic's troops would be capable of marching in great numbers.

C5: Under these circumstances, an offensive would become a very seductive option for the leaders of the François republic.

P7: It's akin to the empire’s conviction that it could use the Northern theater to break the fetters that had kept it bound to their defensive policy for years.

P8: The François republic is equally confident that, with this one move, it can eliminate a significant threat that has been plaguing its fatherland’s national defense for so long(AKA the Empire).

FINAL CONCLUSION: The François Republic will probably start an offensive against the empire in a few weeks when they gather enough troops for a big attack. With this, Tanya predicts the plans of the François Republic's leaders about making an offensive against the empire. And her predictions were correct.

In the battle to save the mage soldier, Tanya receives the information that a group of mages was coming to the battlefield, she then analyzes her current situation.

C1: Judging from the way the enemy is moving, they are probably two companies equipped for long-range movement.

P1: the enemy also must be elite soldiers, who came a long way on high alert.

C2: They must be extremely tired from flying for a long distance.

P2: the enemies have to break through the empire's defensive line and be capable of saving energy for the equally long trek back to base.

C3: In other words, they can't expend too much energy in this battle.

P3: Meanwhile, the imperial mages can put up a fierce defense and then wait for friendly forces to pick them up afterward.

P4: If the artillery makes it to their positions in time, a cleanup bombardment can be arranged.

P5: Of course, even if the enemy is exhausted, Tanya can't count on them to be careless, still, the body often betrays the will.

C4: Based on the premises available, Tanya's platoon still has good chances of winning the battle.

C5: In fact, the enemies are still scattered due to the sweeping operation, their units are too spread out and can only coordinate in groups up to platoon size.

P6: The mages from the imperial army are on defense against the enemy.

C6: In other words, they can go full throttle, even if the current battle they are fighting follows directly the previous one.

P7: Meanwhile, the Republican side has to operate on enemy territory with limited support and supplies.

P8: Assuming even numbers, the scale will still tip heavily towards the empire's side.

FINAL CONCLUSION: Even if the enemies are two whole companies, Tanya's platoon still has a good chance to win the battle.

During her conversation with General Zettour, Tanya predicts that the war will result in an even greater scale war against the World powers in the world, A World War. Her reasoning for this was as follows:

P1: Even if the empire is merely an emerging state, compared to the other world powers, the empire boasts quite an advantage.

P2: Obviously, if the empire were to fight against each one of these nations one-on-one, the empire would surely come out victorious.

P3: But actually, it's difficult to imagine the commonwealth and the Russy Federation simply standing by, I'm also not sure about the Kingdom of Ildoa.

P4: Even if these countries don't have any interest in the war, they would be forced to confront the question of whether they will allow the birth of a dominant state or put a stop to it.

P5: If the Empire, situated in the center of the European continent, eliminates the Republic. They will not have a relative advantage but absolute superiority. Which would motivate the other countries into trying to attack the empire initiating a world war.

C1: If the Empire doesn't eliminate the Republic quickly in a way that doesn't give other nations enough time to interfere, the fighting will trigger a domino effect of other countries getting involved.

P6: Obviously, there's also an alternative where the Republic ends up winning the war and becoming the dominant state.

C2: With this, it's possible to Conclude that the other World Powers would try to interfere in the war in a way that both the empire and the Republic fall.

C3: These interferences will probably start with financial assistance to the Republic, which is currently losing the war, other conceivable methods could include providing them with weapons and dispatching volunteer troops(this prediction made by her was shown to be correct in the future of the LN).

C4: The Empire and the Republic would have their fun little war, while the Republic would exhaust the empire's resources with the support given by the other countries.

P7: But it's also worth noticing the fact that the Republic is currently losing the war and has lost a lot of resources.

C5: This would result in both countries being completely exhausted, which would be the perfect chance for the other world powers to intervene and eliminate both countries. They could even pretend to be good Samaritans about it by spreading newspapers favoring a false narrative created by them.

C6: In case the Empire overwhelms the Republic, it's very likely that the national security policies of the world powers would say to team up with other countries and intervene directly. Even if they can't do that, they may not hesitate to intervene on their own.

FINAL CONCLUSION: The World Powers of the world will try to indirectly interfere in the war by sending resources to the Republic to exhaust both countries involved in the war and finally eliminate both countries.

“In World War I, Germany slaughtered Russia and hit France and England hard, they were overwhelming the other countries on the tactical level. They realized that even if their lines hadn’t broken, they could no longer continue fighting and had no choice but to accept their defeat. So they lost. There’s an important lesson to be learned from this memory. That is, how defeat looks in total war. Battalion's Training Strategy/Plan

After finishing grouping enough people for her special unit, Tanya now has to train them and turn them into elite soldiers in a single month, but the truth is that she wants to make these soldiers give up on the training, so she makes a long-term plan to do this. In the training, Tanya gave some barracks with beds for the soldiers and allowed for them to go to sleep so they can rest, after some time, she purposely caused an explosion close to the beds that blew the entire barracks away, she did this to test the soldiers quick thinking, after all, in situations like this, they are instructed to grab their weapons and Computation orbs, and after doing this, they are instructed to create defensive magic shells around them to protect their bodies and Tanya's soldiers did exactly this. Tanya purposely caused an explosion close to that place to test if they at least knew the basic things to do in this kind of situation, if someone failed to do the basic things she would take them out of the training immediately. She obviously doesn't want to end up hurting her men so she caused that explosion in a place where no one would be hurt, but still did it in a way that made them see her as a dangerous person that doesn't care about them at all, the reason for this is because she wants to make them question themselves this exact thing: “do I really want to be commanded by such a brute and ruthless person?” Which would result in them giving up on the training in order to not be on the same battalion as someone like Tanya, she purposely antagonizes herself inside the soldiers’ minds in order to indirectly induce such a thing.

To be sure that the defensive magic shells made by the soldiers were fine, she was ready to attack and take a soldier out of the training in case she saw any single opening in the soldiers' defense. After being sure that the defensive magic shells were perfect, Tanya announced that all the soldiers will be conducting mobility exercises in the training ground B-113. To guarantee the smooth progress of her plan, she gave maps to the soldiers with three different points marked on it. In the moment the exercise begins, the soldiers would be instructed to move as quickly as possible to the first point in a time limit of 48 hours without the use of magic to fly. They would have to go to the first point by walking without using any magic while still having to worry about the 48 hours time limit, which would obviously cause intense fatigue on them and indirectly manipulate them even more into abandoning this training to become part of Tanya's battalion of elite aerial mage soldiers.

But there's a special detail on this test, the soldiers must go to the first point within 48 hours while having to conceal the magic signal that any of them produced. If they failed to do so, an observer-assisted artillery shell and magic guided fire would fall on them in case their mana signals are detected and they would have to protect themselves with defensive shells, this kind of training is extremely brutal no matter how you think about it and Tanya wanted for it to be like this, she made this extremely dangerous training on purpose in order to make someone fail or make the soldiers notice how dangerous the training is and voluntarily decide to quit. Not only this, but due to the fact that Tanya blew the Soldier's barracks to smithereens, they had a strong lack of resources with the only thing with them being some few bottles of water and some other things they had with them which weren't really useful for the training. Which would indirectly persuade them into abandoning the training even more.

After some time, the soldiers finally reach the second waypoint and receive orders to prepare for optical interception(in other words, they were ordered to stop the training temporarily), due to the severity of the training, when all the soldiers saw Tanya smile and say that she was happy that no one abandoned the training yet, they felt a mysterious chill run down their spines, they knew that smile meant that something even worse was coming for them, this demonstrates Tanya's impressive Aura and shows that exactly like Tanya wanted, the soldiers saw her as being a cruel being that didn't cared about their safety at all, but sadly for her, they still wanted to continue the training. Now that she noticed that the soldiers aren't weaklings with low determination and perseverance, she decided to step up the training regimen according to their abilities, which means that the overall difficulty of the training will get even higher. After this, Tanya shoots an infrared beam using a magic formula while aiming at the sky, this was a signal that Tanya sent to the artillery to use all the remaining ammunition on the soldiers to test their defensive capabilities. After this, a training round flew down the line of fire which was a Barrage attack from the artillery against the soldiers’ assembly point. She purposely gave orders for the soldiers to prepare for optical interception to make them stand still at that exact same place without moving, with this, the artillery would know exactly where to shoot without even needing to find Magic signals from the mages, Tanya simply had to give the coordinates of the soldiers location to them and use the infrared beam in order to give a signal for the artillery to shoot.

After all this, Tanya announces for the soldiers to prepare themselves against anti-artillery training, what they need to do is simple, they have to defend the foothold where they are located as practice, she purposely initiated this training in order to guarantee that the soldiers wouldn't try to run away from the artillery shoots, what she gave to them was an order to stay on point and if someone tried to escape, they would be taken out of the training like Tanya wishes to occur. They were given fifteen minutes to prepare their positions and be capable of enduring multiple anti-artillery training rounds for something like 36 hours. Tanya also says that she will cause a magic bombardment in case anyone leaves the training route, doing this to guarantee that no one would decide to do such a thing to escape from the training. One of the soldiers was so scared that he thought to himself that he wanted to die since he knows that Tanya doesn't bluff about things like this and she was true to her word. Doing this training out of nowhere would also influence the soldiers into leaving the training since they would know for sure after this that Tanya doesn't care about their safety due to how insanely dangerous the training was, which would indirectly persuade them into leaving the training.

After this, the artillery initiates fire against the soldiers that were already fatigued due to walking for entire hours in order to reach the second waypoint marked in the map. To protect herself from the artillery explosions while still being capable of looking to her soldiers to take them out of the training if they commit the slightest mistake or if someone tries to escape from the training, which would also result on them being taken out of the training, Tanya uses a defensive shell magic to protect herself from the artillery shells while being close to her soldiers so she can take them out of the training due to the slightest mistake committed by them in this training. In this training, the soldiers must be capable of enduring the artillery through the use of defensive shells while observing carefully the soldiers giving information to where the artillery should aim at on trajectory so they could shoot down to knock them out of the sky. It's something really easy to say but extremely draining and tiring to do. Which is exactly what Tanya wants, she wants to cause as much fatigue on her soldiers which would increase the chances of them committing the slightest mistakes and in order to make them see her as being a cruel commander, which would indirectly manipulate them into abandoning the training voluntarily in order to not be under the command of someone so ruthless. While this was happening, there would be 72 different trainees saying for the artillery where they should aim at, which means that the soldiers in the training should knock down 72 different soldiers in midair without being capable of using magic to fly and while protecting themselves from the artillery shells. In other words, they are in an extremely limited situation with limited resources and fatigue, which would increase the chances of mistakes being committed.

But there's another way to avoid receiving severe damage in this training due to the artillery shells, the soldiers must simply pick out the live rounds mixed in with the training ones, this training continues for 36 entire hours until it finally comes to an end. But since the artillery shooting at them still had some ammunition remaining with them, Tanya warned her soldiers about it in order to give time for them to prepare themselves for the artillery shots and after this, she gave permission for the trainees controlling the artillery to shoot all the remaining ammunition on the soldiers, her soldiers were hit with a strong barrage of artillery shells that would definitely kill them if they were taken by surprise, such a thing that once again would make clear to the soldiers that Tanya doesn't care about their safety at all and would inflict fatigue on them since they would have to use a lot of mana in order to protect themselves from the artillery shells. But the soldiers still managed to keep going with the training. After this, the soldiers received instructions coming from Tanya to continue marching to the third location shown on the map she gave to them in the very beginning before this training even started.

They would have to continue walking for hours once again while still facing fatigue caused by all the previous events of the training. This time there were no conditions despite the usual time limit of 48 hours and the fact they can't use magic to fly, they didn't have any relevant information about what was going to occur in the training at all, which obviously made them feel tense since they are aware of how ruthless Tanya is, the fact that they didn’t knew what was going to occur after all this was like hell to them, something that probably was made by Tanya on purpose to apply Psychological pressure on them, but sadly the LN doesn't give any information as to why she doesn't have any relevant information to them, so take this as a simple hypothesis that has really high chance of being true taking the context of what's her actual aim with this plan. Before they started marching to the third waypoint, Tanya gave advice to them, telling her soldiers to move carefully, such a thing made her soldiers feel paranoid about what they were going to face this time, they didn't had any relevant information about what was going to occur and now Tanya gave advice for them to act carefully without any apparent reason, such a thing made the soldiers feel paranoid and probably had the aim of making them quit the training due to fear that what was going to occur would be worse than all the things that already occurred previously in this training.

After this, the soldiers started to march in order to reach the third waypoint and continue the training. While they were marching, the soldiers found multiple Military dobermans and an armed bomber company flying a search overhead, but nothing that was unavoidable for the soldiers participating in this training. But despite seeing that the things they were facing were easy to evade, Tanya's warning for the soldiers to move carefully, alongside all the previous actions of her plan made them paranoid, they were unconsciously thinking that there must be something dangerous ahead, despite this not being true, the idea of something dangerous being ahead was a bluff that Tanya used to apply Psychological pressure on them and made the soldiers quit the training due to their fear. But surprisingly for these soldiers there was nothing. Tanya technically didn't bluff when she told her soldiers to walk carefully, she warned them about this in order to avoid the possibility of anyone dying to the military dobermans or the armed bomber Company, at least that's what the soldiers participating in this training would wrongly think, she purposely placed military dobermans and made an armed bomber Company fly above the soldiers in the middle of the training purposely in order to misdirect them into associating her advice for them to walk carefully with these things, such a thing would conceal the fact that her true intentions with this plan was to purposely apply Psychological pressure on the soldiers to indirectly manipulate them to quit the training.

(Inter EM, Leadership, Cunning, Aura, Psychological warfare, Long-term planning, Strategy, Methods, Tactics, concealment, Non-Verbal deception, misdirection, mass deception, Bluffing, indirect manipulation, direct manipulation, emotional manipulation, Logical manipulation, Fear inducement, Mass manipulation, Gaslighting)

After all the past events of Tanya's plan, the soldiers reach the third waypoint and meet Tanya there, after reaching the third waypoint, they are trained on resistance to interrogation and have success in doing so. After this, the soldiers were thrown to the Alpen mountains without having any proper time to rest in order to get rid of fatigue, something that Tanya does on purpose to indirectly increase the chances of them committing a mistake, which would immediately result in them failing on the training. During this part of the training, exactly like before, Tanya would be following the soldiers to guarantee that nothing goes wrong and to take any soldier that commits the slightest mistake out of the training. After this, it's revealed that some time before, one of the soldiers dropped like he was dead and with a simple kick, Tanya managed to put him back on his feet. Seeing this, the other soldiers thought that she was an ally more horrible than the enemy and not a human being. Now you might be asking me, why didn't Tanya take that soldier that dropped like he was dead out of the training and instead made him return back on his own feet? The reason for this being because Tanya wanted to make everyone see how ruthless she was with them, she guaranteed that her soldiers would see her kicking the soldier that dropped on the floor in order to indirectly make them see her as being a ruthless person that doesn't care about them at all, which would indirectly manipulate them into abandoning the training in order to not be on the same battalion as someone as ruthless as Tanya who doesn't care about their safety. If she simply took that soldier out of the training, the other soldiers wouldn't even care about it since a lot of soldiers were already taken out of the training before, another one being taken out wouldn't mean nothing to them, that's why Tanya decided to kick him back on his feet in front of everyone in order to make a brilliant Psychological warfare tactic on her soldiers.

During the training of the soldiers an avalanche occurred in the Alpen Mountains and a soldier with a broken leg was about to be seriously harmed by it, noticing this Tanya saved him with a kick to throw him away, but she decided to act like a rigid and rude captain, calling him an amateur, the reason for this probably being because she doesn't want for her soldiers to see her as a kind-hearted captain, she wants for them to see her as being a cold-hearted captain that doesn't really worry about their safety in order to guarantee that her plan will go on smoothly without any unforeseen event. She also purposely saved that soldier by flying in his direction and kicking him with all her strength so the kinetic force would throw him far away from the avalanche. Such a thing was seen by the other soldiers in the training and they continued to see Tanya as being a ruthless person that didn't care about them at all due to the fact that she saved the soldier that was going to be caught in the avalanche with an extremely violent kick with all her strength. After having his life saved, the soldier started seeing Tanya with other eyes, he noticed that she was in fact a good captain, but still wasn't sure if she was human at all.

After this, it's shown Tanya's aim with this plan that was already mentioned before by me, but this time, her motivation to initiate this plan is shown to us. In fact, Tanya is aware that turning a bunch of losers into elites in a single month isn't possible, but she still stated to be capable of doing this in front of multiple high-ranking officers a few pages ago. Noticing this stressful situation, she decided to create the most torturing and insane training possible. She understands that if she trains them as mercilessly as she can and pushes them to their absolute limits, they will eventually give up. In this scenario where the soldiers give up, things will go well with the soldiers that gave up being called gutless quitters, while Tanya will come through this situation completely unscathed. With this plan to make her soldiers give up on the training, Tanya decided to use the most insane training methods from every special unit known to military history. The first phase of her training would be american-style stuff like water acclimation training, but her soldiers will do an even harder version of it, they would do an extremely hard altitude acclimation training. The second phase will be the infamous hell week, in this phase, the soldiers will be forced to sleep a total of four hours in four days. With this, she would push them to their breaking point while causing extremely high levels of fatigue on them which would increase the chances of a soldier committing the slightest mistake in the next phases of the training so Tanya can take this soldier out of the training. In case someone manages to somehow resist the hell week, they would have a long SERE training, which will be a packed schedule of resistance to enemy interrogation and intense survival training. She even had plans prepared for War-Crazy soldiers that would have enough determination to pass through all this.

After the SERE and the hell week, Tanya would throw out her soldiers to an extremely long distance and they would have to march without the use of magic through the Alpen Mountains for an entire week. Tanya wants to make her soldiers give up on the training so she can avoid punishment coming from the Higher-ups due to failing to turn the soldiers into elites in a month and that's why she frequently tries to pass as much fear as possible on her soldiers to make them give up already, she does this by making them pass through extremely intense and life threatening training methods in order to make them see her as a ruthless commander that doesn't care about their safety, which would make them abandon the training to not be on the same battalion as someone like Tanya, while also acting in an extremely ruthless way in order to make clear to the most stupid soldiers, that couldn't have deduced such a thing merely based on the trainings, that she doesn't care about their safety.

Obviously during this entire training, Tanya still has to give her best to avoid any soldier’s death, after all, if someone died during her training due to an event that she could have avoided due to being a literal Named soldier, then the high-ups of the empire would start to doubt her abilities and she would be in trouble. After all, in a situation where a soldier is about to be hit by an avalanche, Tanya, who possesses the silver wings badge and is literally a Named soldier, should be capable of saving this soldier without any problem, if she's not even capable of doing such an easy thing then she isn't really competent enough to be worthy of becoming a battalion's commander at all. During this training, the soldiers would have only the absolute minimum sleep and rest. The training methods used are based on the worst battle records, like half water rations, the soldiers not being allowed to carry food and the use of computation orbs lead to instant failure, they'll only be allowed to carry a knife with them one for two people, in other words, only half of the soldiers would have a knife with them while the other half wouldn't have any knife with them. She does everything that she can in order to make the soldiers fall into insanity and give up on the training. In this training, even the slightest error made by a soldier results in them failing on the training.

And in case the plan goes wrong, Tanya has foolproof insurance to guarantee that things will go well for her. This foolproof insurance being an early mass-production prototype made by Scientist Schugel, the same Chief engineer that made the type 95 computation Orb that Tanya uses during the entire story of the LN, the same guy who is famous for making prototypes of computation orbs that end up exploding or malfunctioning. What Tanya plans on doing with these early prototypes is making her soldiers use it and wait for it to malfunction, after all, these are early versions of computation orbs that Schugel is working on, they are not even completed yet and he's literally known for making computation Orbs that malfunction and explode. When a single one of these Prototypes of Computation Orbs made by Schugel malfunction and explode, the other soldiers seeing such a thing will notice how dangerous the situation is and will finally decide to quit, what Tanya want to do here is appeal to humanity's natural instinct to preserve its own life on her soldiers in order to indirectly manipulate them into abandoning the idea of becoming a member of her battalion. When such a thing occurs, Tanya doesn't have to worry about receiving the blame for such a thing since it would be an accident in the eyes of everyone, including the soldiers and the Higher-ups of the empire, in fact, it was even more logical to think that the blame would fall on Schugel instead of Tanya. But even Tanya herself Hopes to avoid having to use these computation orbs against her soldiers; she wants them to give up on the battalion's training already before she has to resort to this foolproof insurance. This plan and the great amount of contingencies it has demonstrates Tanya's impressive problem solving skills.

Tanya also concealed her true intentions with this plan by manipulating everyone's perception to make them think that she was really trying to train them, she even used her acting skills to pass this idea to all the soldiers involved and not a single one of them suspected that she had second intentions with this training. The fact that she was using training methods that actually buffed the soldiers' overall skills would also conceal her true intentions with this plan. Half water rations, only half of the soldiers carrying a knife to protect each other and not being capable of using the computation Orbs forces the soldiers in the training into adapting to unexpectedly bad situations, SERE reinforces their abilities to resist extensive enemy interrogation and their abilities to survive in unexpected scenarios, and the hell week reinforces their abilities to keep themselves focused on difficult situations where sleeping or get distracted results on instant death, all the training methods in this plan were things that actually buffed the soldiers' overall abilities to surpass any kind of adversity, which would conceal her true intentions with this plan.

But surprisingly, her plan failed. But it isn't explained to us how this happened since Tanya lost parts of her memory due to the curse of the Type 95 Computation Orb. This means that Tanya used the type 95 computation Orb in some moment during the training, but she still maintained her sanity despite having to suffer an insane amount of pain and agony that was already mentioned previously. With this, she had to suffer the pain from the curse of the computation orb type 95 two times up to this point, but still managed to keep her sanity. Despite this not being the aim of Tanya's plan, she actually managed to transform a bunch of losers into elite mages in a month, soldiers capable of using two formula type magic continually without any problem. And for some reason, the prototypes made by Schugel actually worked and didn't malfunctioned, despite such a thing not being directly said in the LN, it's highly probable that Being X interfered and made the prototypes work on Tanya's soldiers in order to ruin her plans since throughout the entire story, he always tries to make Tanya's life harder by forcing her into facing multiple adversities and ruining her plans. This means that the only reason as to why Tanya's plan failed was due to the intervention of a literal transcendental and Higher-dimensional Being that can stop time itself.

One thing that I want to say about this plan is that it's both an strategy and a Planning feat, the execution of the plan IS a strategy, while all the elements of preparation process made by Tanya and the actual progress of the plan are planning feats. In other words, Tanya actually leading her subordinates here and executing the plan is the strategy part of this plan, while the preparation process, the plan as a whole and the progress of the plan is the Planning part. Also, all Tanya's actions during this plan count as being an insane demonstration of Trap setting including the big amount of contingencies that this plan had, this plan is also a good demonstration of anticipation due to Tanya foreseeing many Short-term ways the plan could go wrong and creating contingencies for them. This entire plan is also an insane demonstration of a Psychological warfare feat, probably one of the best in Semi-realistic SCD.

[Overall Amount Of Contingencies In This Plan]

Here's a short document explaining this feat in more detail.

In the second Chapter of the Light Novel, It's stated by Tanya herself that she has a mental grade book inside her mind containing the grades of her 48 soldiers, these grades are based on things like discipline, how useful they are, Social and communication skills, etc. It's also implied that she also constantly changes the grades of her soldiers based on observations she made on their actions. After some time, Tanya thinks about the current situation of the war with the Entente alliance, after all, the north is the place where the battle against the Entente Alliance is taking place and it's also the place where Tanya's battalion was asked to go to.

P1: The great army has already pulled out from the north side of the war against the Entente Alliance, but it's strange that the fighting in the north against the Entente Alliance hasn't settled down yet.

P2: According to military standards, the Entente alliance isn't considered a major world power like the empire.

C1: The fact that they are capable of qualitatively competing with the empire, on any level, implies that they're receiving aid from somewhere.

P3: Needless to say, its ally in the war, the François Republic had already given it a formidable helping hand.

C2: So there's no doubt that the majority of support going to the Entente Alliance is coming from there as well.

P4: The real crux of the problem is whether those that claim neutrality are intervening.

P5: Of course, these bystander countries deny any participation on a national scale, but they are silent whenever the possible existence of voluntary armies comes up.

C3: The participation of world powers like the commonwealth(the UK from Tanya's world) and the Russy Federation(it's totally obvious which country this is) which are located in the European continent where the war is taking place is certain due to the fact that the empire is a possible threat to them.

P6: To begin with, the Entente Alliance is dramatically less capable than the Empire when it comes to the national strength that can be devoted toward total war.

P7: Entente Alliance mages alone shouldn't even be able to put up such stiff resistance. Its troops ability to fend off the great army while holding steady against the pressure of the regional imperial army group speaks volumes to the amount of aid they must be receiving.

C4: Tanya understands that this is the reason why even her battalion is stuck going on a “picnic” to the north despite the Dacia situation not being quite settled yet.

FINAL CONCLUSION: The Higher-ups purposely sent Tanya's battalion to the Norden in order to make them fight against the Entente Alliance's mages who received aid from a neutral country. With this, Tanya managed to see through the orders given to her by the Higher-ups and figure out their true intentions with this.

While the fourth company does this, Tanya would be targeting the two bombers since at war, being capable of destroying bombers while being a simple mage is an insane feat considered to be impossible even for experienced mages. She purposely decides to do this in order to once again increase her influence and popularity in the army. She also uses her acting skills and gives a motive for this decision made by her… which is simply a little funny joke, the reason as to why she made a joke in this situation was because Tanya is aware that it's not bad to show a human side once in a while in front of one's own subordinates in order to develop trust and loyalty.

(FRI=[Problem Solving], Fluid intelligence, Leadership, Social skills, Cunning, influence building, Strategy, Tactics, Methods, Problem Solving, indirect manipulation, direct manipulation, mass manipulation, emotional manipulation, Positive Manipulation)

Without anyone saying it to her, Tanya already figured out that the supply lines of the empire are dangerously overextended, this is the reasoning she used to come to this conclusion:

P1: Our now-dismissed predecessors, dreaming of smashing the encirclement, mobilized the great army with gear appropriate for fighting in the north.

P2: But suddenly it was hastily deployed to the Rhine Front without any proper explanation.

P3: It's not just Major General Von Rudersdorf, no one could ask whose fault the messy supply lines were, because the blame lay with their predecessor’s blunders.

P4: On the other hand, if it was simply an organizational failure, the problem shouldn't require Central to handle it so delicately.

C1: The fact that the Central is handling it like this implies that there's something more about that.

P5: The issue at hand is that the empire is in a state of panic and the enemy is taking advantage of that.

P6: Winter is already on its way, and partially, because the imperial army's Northern Army group is lacking supplies it needs to weather Norden’s cold.

C2: The empire's movements are becoming extremely and severely limited.

P7: The Entente Alliance, of course, is on its home turf, so its commandos are dominating and constantly conducting Guerrilla attacks on the empire's supply bases.

C3: Which results in the empire's situation getting even worse than it already was before.

P8: Security at small depots is already in shambles and growing difficult to maintain. Not only this, but the soldiers still need bread if they are going to march on the enemy logistics base.

C4: Which turns everything even worse for the empire's management of supplies.

P9: If it were a tactical disadvantage they needed to fix, commanders could still have room to work. Or if simply fighting hard could solve things. But the burned-down depot can't be recovered.

C5: With these premises, the conclusion Tanya can reach is simple, it isn't clear if the imperial army has enough supplies to survive the winter. The supplies exist, but they need to be carefully managed.

C6: In this situation, the best thing to do is avoiding losses and keeping casualties at a minimum, while also moving the supply lines back in order to lessen the inevitable burden of distance on logistics, this would also be useful for simplifying plans for a spring offensive. And that time could also be efficiently used to reorganize the army supply lines.

P10: Before this entire meeting had occurred, Major General Rudersdorf proposed for Tanya to make preparations for an airborne assault on the enemy without any apparent reason.

C7: That's probably why Major General Rudersdorf suggested for Tanya to make preparations for an airborne assault on the enemy, buying time with airborne operations is an extremely effective option and that's why Rudersdorf suggested this action.

FINAL CONCLUSION: The Empire's winter supply lines are in a dangerously overextended situation.

(Deductive reasoning, critical thinking, convergent thinking, Associative thinking, Holistic thinking, Linear thinking, abstract thinking, Observation, Connecting The Dots, Information Control)

During the events of the second volume when Tanya was going to initiate the Empire's amphibious operation against the Entente Alliance, she demonstrates impressive Logical reasoning skills by thinking about that entire operation as a whole:

P1: An imperial Navy staff member appears and reveals to Tanya and her battalion that they would be performing a support mission of the Northern Army's group operation.

P2: But this statement made by him is strange since the idea of making a large-scale amphibious attack operation in the rear followed by an encirclement to the enemy's supply chain was Tanya's idea, which she mentioned to Rudersdorf only, no one other than him should know about this except him, who is an officer from the General staff.

P3: Before all these events, there was a discussion between the General staff and the Northern army group regarding the army mobilization issues that were being faced in the North front, the result of this discussion was that the relationship between the General staff and the Northern army group was tense.

C1: Tanya abducts that the Central army is nominally giving credit for the operation to the Northern Army Group, she comes to the conclusion that it's probably a show of kindness from the General staff as a reconciliation for the awkwardness between them over the Great Army mobilization issues.

P4: The Northern Army group is going on the offensive, and as a bonus they’ll get the honor of leading the amphibious operation.

P5: The General staff themselves were the ones who decided to implement this operation and they plan to make a reconciliation with the Northern Army Group.

P6: But there's also the possibility that the amphibious operation proposed by Tanya can go wrong and the empire might suffer with several disadvantageous losses while fighting against the enemy.

P7: If such a thing occurred and the amphibious operation was unsuccessful, the blame would fall on the Northern army group since they are supposedly the ones who proposed this operation to the General staff, such a thing occurring would ruin the General staff plans to have a reconciliation with the Northern army group.

C2: Tanya then comes to the conclusion that this entire operation on the Norden against the Entente Alliance is a lousy plan, the reason for this being that even if the Northern Army Group fail, no one in the Northern Army Group will be harmed if the General staff take responsibility for implementing the action plan, and if it goes well, the war situation will improve for the empire, so it's worth it for the General staff.

P8: The Imperial Navy staff then reveals to the soldiers that their target is the Osfjord, the empire plans to make the soldiers go on a direct strike on the enemy's rear communication lines.

C3: Fjords are bad news for naval ships. The detestable narrow bodies of water with terrible cliffs on either side enable the enemy to shoot at them from any direction, even without counting the threat of mines, if there are guns on both sides, any ship that makes a run for it can only lament its fate to be pummeled to death.

P9: The Gunners will surely shower the marks with shells as they struggle to maneuver in the narrow waters.

P10: And although the enemy's navy may only have a few destroyers left, if that, the cramped space forces the soldiers to worry about torpedoes.

P11: For the Entente Alliance, the region of Os has an extremely Geographic importance for their defense in the war against the empire. It's an extremely important region that they wouldn't want to lose.

C4: Given the Geographical importance of Os for the Entente Alliance, the Osfjord will definitely be heavily guarded compared to the average fjord.

P12: The Osfjord will be heavily guarded by the enemy with things like coastal guns, maritime mines and patron ships surrounding the sea in order to eliminate anyone.

P13: These heavy defenses won't allow for the Fleet to perform the landing operation and take control of the Osfjord.

P14: Major General Rudersdorf gave secret orders for Tanya to prepare for an airborne operation some days ago before the execution of this operation.

C5: With this, Tanya already expected that she would need to neutralize coastal guns in an aerial mage sneak attack in order to allow for the ships to perform the landing operation without being destroyed, and her expectations were correct.

P15: Neutralizing the coastal guns in an aerial mage sneak attack is practically the same thing as supporting the fleet as they make a run down the fjord, in other words, it's a step of the Amphibious operation that needs to be concluded in order for the operation as a whole to occur.

P16: If the aerial mage sneak attack is not a success and they fail to destroy the enemy coastal guns, the operation as a whole will collapse and all investment will be meaningless, so the empire needs for this sneak attack to be successful no matter what.

C6: She understands that's the reason as to why the army put their aerial mage elites against a rear echelon coastal stronghold in order to guarantee that the sneak attack will be successful.

P17: The only thing located in the backside of the place where the operation will occur is communication lines to the rear echelon.

C7: It's highly probable that the backside won't be protected since the only thing located there is the communication lines to the rear echelon.

C8: This means that at the moment, attacking the backside of the Osfjord is the best tactic Tanya can make to ensure that the sneak attack will be successful.

FINAL CONCLUSION: In this operation, Tanya’s battalion must neutralize the coastal guns in the Osfjord in order to allow for the operation as a whole to progress, and it feels like Tanya's battalion has a chance of succeeding even if the enemy is on guard against a sneak attack by attacking the backside of the Osfjord.

After thinking about all this, Tanya questions the imperial Navy staff about what she should do in case things don't go as planned, she says that her unit is simply an augmented battalion. Setting aside the issue of firepower, she considers to not have enough men to take all the guns and cover the enemy reinforcements who are bound to attack. Tanya purposely mentions her case while acting so confidently to indirectly manipulate the staff into thinking that her case was logical, it would sound more persuasive for him. In case people think that one's giving a constructive suggestion with the aim of accomplishing one's mission, even an excuse(which is what Tanya is trying to do here by indirectly manipulating the officer into allowing retreat in case things don't go well) transforms into the genuine truth. And even if she fails, what she said isn't rebellious enough to cause trouble to her.

She also ask if she has permission to abort the mission in a worst-case scenario, she says that if her unit fails, the fleet would be exposed to needless danger, she also says that if they don't take preserving the fleet seriously enough, they may let the republican or commonwealth fleet slip in, which would render the blockade meaningless and create a very dangerous situations. Here Tanya purposely uses Logical arguments to persuade the officer into accepting the idea of aborting the mission if a bad scenario appears. After this, she states that due to a quirk of the command structure, she can neither command nor be commanded by marine mages, so she asks permission to advise aborting the mission if the marine regiment commanders agree. The reason as to why she said this is because she understands that the imperial Navy staff officer must know how things work on the military, the Navy's original job is only providing escorts to the mouth of the bay and maybe firing their cannons, so he definitely doesn't want to exacerbate mage command structure issue.

With this, Tanya managed to persuade the officer into accepting aborting the mission if the marine regiment commanders agreed while also being capable of concealing her true intentions with this. This also demonstrates an extremely impressive level of VCI. Here, Tanya demonstrates to be extremely good with words and be extremely persuasive. Tanya is aware that if her battalion doesn't manage to descend without being detected by the enemy, it would be the end for her, she would be killed with 100% certainty. Despite knowing about this cruel fact, Tanya still manages to hide her anxiety with a carefree tone of someone announcing picnic plans in order to not end up accidentally inflicting anxiety on her subordinates too.
