Tanya's Amphibious Operation Plan

During the events of the second volume, while talking with General Von Rudersdorf, Tanya thinks about a plan to take the Entente Alliance out of the war once and for all. Tanya's plan involves making an amphibious operation to cut the enemy supply lines by sneak attacking them from behind. During the events of this plan, the Imperial army tied up the Entente Alliance army in the front lines while cutting the enemy supply lines in a sneak attack while the enemy forces barely had any soldiers to protect their supply lines. Tanya's aerial mage battalion will drop ahead of the landing party tasked with the mission to encircle the enemy supply lines and cut it off, the reason for this being to eliminate any resistance from the enemy forces and guarantee that the route they will take on the sea is unprotected.

In this amphibious operation, the imperial fleet of warships will come to take control of the Entente Alliance’s Osfjord and cut off their supply lines, which will, in the long-term, guarantee the Entente Alliance's loss in the war against the empire. But doing such a thing obviously wouldn't be easy, the enemy Fjord has coastal guns and batteries guarding the Fjord, that's why Tanya's aerial mage battalion will come to attack the enemy batteries from behind first and destroy all the coastal guns since they all will be facing the sea and won't be prepared for a sneak attack from behind, with this, the imperial warships would be capable of invading the Osfjord without any difficulty. This would also give a chance for them to encircle and cut off the enemy supply lines as planned.

But there's a problem with all this, there's a possibility that the Northern forces, that were advised to keep the enemy forces away from the rear where the amphibious operation will occur, could possibly attempt to march north, which would make things complicated for the amphibious operation. In an unexpected situation where they do this and end up failing on marching north, the empire's General staff will take advantage of the fact that the world will be watching the foray by attempting a major operation of their own in Norden. If such a thing works, the General staff will have taken the initiative to lead the war compared to other military groups composing the imperial army.

In this amphibious operation, the imperial army will advance to the Osfjord with a large number of transport ships with imperial soldiers. During these events, Tanya's aerial mage battalion will be responsible for drop in and capture the coastal guns to clean the path for the Empire's transport ships. The General staff also made the decision to give the honor of leading the operation to the Northern army group as a way to reconcile after the awkwardness between the General staff and the Northern army group. Even if they fail, no one in the Northern army group will be harmed if the General staff take responsibility for implementing the action plan. But if it goes well, the war situation for the empire will definitely improve.

During the events of the amphibious operation, the imperial Northern Sea Fleet and the High Seas Fleets would be deployed to a Northern support mission, they would be on a mission to prevent the Entente Alliance's warships from escaping to Republic waters while simultaneously supporting the army maneuvers on land. At least that's the idea they want to pass, but in truth, the Northern Sea Fleet and the High Seas Fleet received orders to ignore the Entente Alliance Warships to perform the amphibious operation in a sneak attack that the enemy would have literally zero way of countering, they will be merely pretending to be trying to prevent the Entente Alliance's warships to conceal their true intentions. This is also a highly impressive trap setting feat from Tanya.

The Northern Sea Fleet is going beyond the parameters of its “original mission” of intercepting the Entente Alliance's ships and sortieing for the purpose of receiving a huge number of reinforcements in the High Seas Fleet to perform the Amphibious operation. The Entente Alliance ships that will come to intercept the Northern Sea Fleet will be kept at bay by the imperial High Seas Fleet. If the Northern Sea Fleet manages to complete their operation during the time while the High Seas Fleet is intercepting the Entente Alliance's ships, the war situation will see a literal reversal and the empire will be in a highly advantageous situation where it will only have to worry about fighting the François Republic. Not only this, but Tanya is in a good position where her battalion will play a major role in this operation, which means that it's a really good opportunity to receive a promotion. What's even better is that a sneak attack would allow Tanya to get promoted without not even having to pass through an extremely dangerous situation since the empire's Victory will be guaranteed if they succeed.

But of course, if Tanya is placed in a situation where performing the amphibious operation is impossible, then she will be in real trouble since she won't be capable of retreating considering how important this operation is. Due to knowing about this, Tanya persuaded the imperial Navy officer explaining the operation to her to allow the imperial army to retreat in a specific situation. And even if Tanya failed to persuade him into accepting a retreat in a worst-case scenario, the record of Tanya asking for the right to call a withdrawal will make it possible for Tanya to argue that she made every effort to avoid risks in case the amphibious operation fails. Not only this, but by guaranteeing the right to call a withdrawal, Tanya would be capable of avoiding a high amount of imperial losses in a worst-case scenario where something goes wrong in the execution of the amphibious operation.

(Short-term Planning, Methods, Tactics, Trap Setting, Mass deception, Non-Verbal Deception, Misdirection, Concealment)

A few days later, the amphibious operation is to be carried out as scheduled, Tanya and the members of her aerial mage battalion are in a transport plane being taken to the drop zone as the vanguard where they will destroy the enemy's coastal guns from behind in a sneak attack. The plan is to jump at the crack of dawn and capitalize on the enemy's confusion in order to seize the coastal guns and clear the path for the Imperial Fleet. Due to the fact that coastal fortifications are vulnerable to sneak attacks and due to the fact that Tanya's aerial mage battalion has an extremely high mobility, they are perfect for this job since they will easily be capable of destroying all the coastal guns before the enemy even has a chance to interfere. Tanya's aerial mage battalion will drop in at the enemy's backside since there's only communication lines there for the rear, which aren't really important so the Entente Alliance won't bother to keep that area protected. With this, Tanya predicted that even if the enemy was prepared for a sneak attack, their units were likely to be only lightly outfitted. And her prediction was confirmed to be true in the Light Novel.

After the airplanes taking Tanya's aerial mage battalion reach the desired location, they would cut the engines and start gliding to avoid enemy detection. After this, Tanya's aerial mage battalion jumps from the plane, their drop will be performed with airborne gear and not magic to once again avoid enemy detection and conceal their location. In The amphibious operation, the targets of the 203rd aerial mage battalion are the guns and mines guarding the Fjord. Capturing them is optimal, but if such a thing proves to be difficult, then her subordinates will prioritize disabling or damaging them to inhibit effective functioning. During the events of this operation, the imperial marine mages will deal with enemy reinforcements on the ground. But in a situation where Tanya's aerial mage battalion fails to destroy the enemy coastal guns thirty minutes after they drop from the airplane, the imperial marine mages will arrive ahead of the imperial ships to back them up and help them in disabling the coastal guns and mines.

And if things get bad, Tanya can make a joint suggestion with the commanders of the troops coming later to abort the entire operation. Which means that her life will barely be at risk with this plan. Tanya even planned beforehand the enemy batteries in the Osfjord that her subordinates should capture, Tanya for example, will capture the Narva battery while her second-in-command, Weiss will capture the Albert battery. If Tanya's subordinates fail to seize the position, they should contact her immediately so that she can give support. Otherwise, the plan is to maintain radio silence until the reinforcements come, with this, they would conceal their presence there and keep the enemy on low-guard since they wouldn't know that there are imperial troops in their territory. If enemy reinforcements appear and Weiss can't deal with them only with his company, he should come to Tanya so that she can give support to him against the enemy forces.

Not only this, but in case both Tanya and Weiss go dark and don't answer to the radio anymore, she should order the retreat of the rest of the battalion. Tanya and Weiss will act as canaries that will warn the rest of the battalion about the danger. Of course, Tanya doesn't have any intention to nobly sacrifice herself for such a thing, that's why she will simply make a scene screaming about the danger to everyone as she makes an airborne escape to preserve her own life. During the events of the amphibious operation plan, the imperial Fleet managed to eliminate all the enemy patrol ships, leaving their path to the Osfjord almost completely unprotected, the only thing stopping them from invading was the coastal guns and mines, but even such a thing was already being taken care of by Tanya and her aerial mage battalion.

While all these things were occurring in the Entente Alliance, the François Republic was waiting for a naval battle against the imperial Fleet, but unexpectedly for them, the empire was merely moving with transport ships, the empire had no intention of engaging in a naval battle against the François Republic. The François Republic Fleet was completely confused about what was happening and even their intelligence wasn't capable of predicting the empire's actions. A few moments ago, after the empire's transport ships left the port and were coming to Norden, the imperial Navy formed a restraining line with submarines and enemy mages.

Now what does this mean for the François Republic? The primary mission of the Republican channel Fleet was to confront and annihilate the imperial High Seas Fleet. But in this situation where the empire is performing their landing operation, while the Republic had to split their main forces between the north and south, the empire could concentrate their naval power in the north. With the Northern Sea Fleet and the High Seas Fleet combining forces, the Republican Channel Fleet would be forced to a disadvantageous situation. That's the trap that the empire prepared to force the François Republic to let the empire take down the Entente Alliance without doing anything to stop such a thing.

And even in a situation where the Republic decides to do something, the landing operation would be made so quickly that the empire would be capable of cutting the Entente Alliance's supply lines before the François Republic even has a chance to do anything. In the end of the amphibious operation, the empire managed to perform a landing operation in the Entente Alliance territory, cut off their supply lines and a few days after these events, the empire managed to take down the Entente Alliance. All of these things were only possible thanks to the amphibious operation plan proposed by Tanya. With this plan, she solved the problem of the Entente Alliance's Resistance against the imperial forces that had prolonged the war for so long. What's even more impressive is that Tanya formulated this plan in a few minutes. Even the Commonwealth intelligence wasn't capable of predicting the empire's actions here.

(FRI=[Problem Solving], Fluid intelligence, Trap Setting, Prediction, Concealment, Short-term planning, Methods, Tactics, Problem Solving)

Tanya's Norden Sea Strategy

During a mission to search for enemy warships, enemy mages trying to protect the warships suddenly appeared and started to attack the empire's submarines that were also helping in this mission. After finding the enemy's location in the sky, Tanya gave orders for her battalion to make a full-speed charge at the enemy's ships that were being protected by those enemy mages. The situation faced by them is complicated since the members of the battalion can't find themselves due to the night sky, they have been flying and patrolling for more than 48 hours, which obviously caused fatigue on then, they also didn't expected this situation, so they didn't had any time to prepare themselves and they don't have any experience fighting ships. Not only this, but they are literally at war where the slightest mistake can result in death which makes the entire situation even more difficult. Facing such a troublesome situation, Tanya makes the split-second decision of scattering her unit in order to attack the enemy as fast as possible and avoid for their allies in the empire's submarines to be severely hurt since Tanya's battalion would have to fight against the enemy all by themselves if such a thing occurred, while doing this she dodges anti-air fire all the while, Tanya's strategy here is to make every member of her unit swoop in at a specific location at the same time in order for them to be finally capable of finding themselves. Tanya understands that the ability of air-craft to attack ships was only just starting to be discussed, so no one was focused on mages, who have less firepower. As a result, they had only just touched on anti-mage combat in training exercises, that's why Tanya doesn't hesitate on giving orders for her battalion to attack the enemy warships since they wouldn't have a way to cause effective and great harm to them, but of course the same applies to the mages, that's why in a sense, the battle was an extremely crude firefight for both sides.

Taking advantage of the fact that the enemy's ship shots are easy to dodge due to ship’s anti-air fire not being made to eliminate mages, Tanya calls the Command Post, she reveals that she's under enemy fire and that the enemy is composed by two hundred battle cruisers, and also reveals her current location in order to receive reinforcements from the Command Post. She also figures out that she was specifically being attacked by battle cruisers by merely looking at the flash made by the warship’s guns. While making the report, she immediately make her battalion break formation due to the fact that Naval guns are a much greater threat than small arms or even most field artillery, the shells that the enemies are aiming at her won't allow for anyone with a human body to just walk away from a direct hit, if her battalion stay at tight formation, the enemy anti-air guns will have their way with them, not only this, but gathering the entire 203rd Aerial mage battalion only had the purpose to make them find themselves, now that they already had success in doing such a thing, continuing to be close to each other wasn't needed anymore. After finishing making the report, Tanya gives orders for her battalion to attack the enemy mages and ships, she also tells them to not get too distracted by one or the other. Tanya looks around and perceives that everywhere around her is completely dark, but she still intuitively figured out that she was exposed to the enemy.

After thinking for some time, Tanya decides to give orders for the members of her battalion to engage in battle at their will while following their commanders lead, she simply decided to allow for each one of the four companies that composes her battalion to operate individually instead of trying to control an entire battalion in the dark without not even having a good idea about where everyone is located at due to poor visibility. She also gives orders for her battalion to not lose their depth perception since visibility is poor, she says that the air is dense and that they shouldn't forget they're over the sea, so they should take the humidity from the water into account and maintain altitude since the enemies are used to fighting on the sea. After some time, Tanya decides to push all the dangerous stuff to the fourth company, the reason for this being due to the fact that the third and second companies are the weakest in battle, the first company, which is the one that she usually commands, has Tanya as their commander and she definitely doesn't want to put herself in danger, so the best thing to do in this situation is to make the fourth company deal with all the hard stuff. She then gives orders for her battalion to draw the mages away from the enemy ships in order to make it easier to destroy the enemy warships, she gives specific orders for the second and third company to keep the mages busy since they are on the vanguard. She understands that marine mages are a threat to aerial mages, so exposing herself to anti-air fire and enemy mages at the same time isn't a good idea, that's why she decides to attract the enemy mages away from the ships to avoid working on dangerous áreas.

After this, she gives specific orders for the fourth company to guard the rear and help the second and third companies while avoiding a shootout with the enemy ships. The reason as to why she gathered the second, third and fourth company at the same place was to indirectly manipulate the enemy ships into focusing on them, after all, it's natural for the enemy to try to shoot where the majority of imperial mages are located while ignoring the rest since they have higher probability of hitting someone by doing this. An action made by the enemy that was not only already anticipated by Tanya but was also caused by her. She also did this while concealing her true intentions, using the false idea that the orders she gave for the fourth company was only to help the third and second company and she didn't have any hidden intentions behind this order. She wants for the third, second and fourth company to act as decoys to attract the enemy ships’ attention while Tanya and the first company attack the ships. With this, she had her underlings do the dangerous counter-mage warfare while she destroyed the enemy ships that wouldn't even be aiming at her. Despite this situation not being expected by Tanya, she managed to instantly adapt to the situation and give effective split-second orders for her battalion.

(Leadership, Cunning, Social Awareness, social skills, psychological warfare, Observation, Perception, Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Trap Setting, Anticipation, Decision Making, Intuition, Adaptability, indirect manipulation, Mass manipulation, Gaslighting, concealment, mass deception, Fast Thinking)

During the battle in the Norden Sea, Tanya understands the utility of the idea that commanders should lead the way in that specific situation. Most of the bullets aimed at the leader of a pack will end up hitting the people behind them, in a little more detail, with deflection shooting. If they open fire assuming Tanya's coming in at two hundred fifty kilometers per hour, all she has to do is approach at three hundred. The difference will keep her safe in the lead, but the enemies will correct their deflection to her speed and it's the ones following her who will rush straight into that, that's the reason as to why she prefers to lead the way for the first company in order to not expose herself to enemy fire. Not only this, but with this tactic she would have a shield behind her after moving away from an enemy attack. After this, Tanya decides to attack a destroyer alongside the first company, the reason for this being due to the fact that Destroyers aren't big ships known for anti-air fire, they aren't really exceptional in this regard when compared to other enemy ships, so attacking a destroyer would be the safest option which is exactly what Tanya wants. Demonstrating impressive decision making. And as expected by her, the destroyer demonstrates to have difficulty to hit them midair.

And even if one of her soldiers end up dying or being severely hurt, they are still complete newbies, they have really low experience in battle and Tanya can easily substitute them for other aerial mages, losing them wouldn't be so bad for her since they are still complete newbies with low experience. While being on the battlefield, Tanya thinks that the ship she was about to attack was a destroyer by merely seeing a turret firing at random, by merely seeing the ship's turret, she was capable of making out the shape of the entire warship which looked like a destroyer despite suffering with poor visibility. She also abducts that it's an isolated destroyer due to the fact that there were no ships in consort. Noticing this, she decides to attack it since she doesn't have to worry about backup from other ships due to it being an isolated destroyer. Based on the interpretation that the ship is an isolated destroyer, the first company gets into assault formation, in order to dive all at once from forty-five hundred feet, they maintain the spindle shape and make minute adjustments to the angle of their assault. But unexpectedly, one of her men was hit by the destroyer, Tanya then decides to allow for him to drop out of the mission and return to Base, she purposely lets him go away all by himself while hoping that he acts as a good bait for the enemy shots, after all, it's completely normal for the enemy to try to focus on eliminating an enemy soldier that is already injured, the chances of killing a injured soldier would be higher than the chances of killing a completely healthy soldier without any injuries. After this, Tanya gives orders for her soldiers to prepare explosion type formula magic, since given a typical destroyer’s armor, they should be able to take it out if they aim for exposed depth charges or the torpedo cubes.

While charging on the enemy, Tanya recognizes one of the enemy soldiers acting as direct support from those destroyers, differently from the other mages fighting against the second, third and fourth company, he was still helping on defending the ships, so he could be a problem for Tanya's plans since fighting against anti-air fire from ships and marine mages at the same time is like working on dangerous waters which is exactly what Tanya wants to avoid, so she decides to charge towards him by using the kinetic force she gathered while charging towards the destroyer. With this speed, all she has to do is assault him with the sharp end of her rifle. And she decides to do that in order to eliminate one of the mages giving support to the destroyer. The enemy even tries to use a defensive shell to protect himself but Tanya's bayonet thrust against her enemy over four hundred knots completely destroys the defensive shell. After this, she steals her enemy's gun for herself and kicks his corpse away from her weapon's bayonet since, unexpectedly for her, it got stuck. With this, Tanya got rid of all the enemy mages acting as obstacles for her goals completely, almost all the enemy mages were fighting against the second, third and fourth company, while the remaining enemy soldier was killed by her just now. The only thing remaining for her to do is evade the ship’s anti-air fire, strike her blow and disappear under the veil of the night since the reinforcements she requested by calling the Command Post would soon come to take control of the situation.

After thinking for some seconds, she finds a way to eliminate the enemy fleet. She is aware that the enemy fleet is being pestered by the allied submarines helping Tanya in this mission, so they have to take counter-sub measures. If she can use an explosion type formula with short activation time and get either the ship's depth charges or torpedoes to blow in a secondary explosion inside the enemy ships, she will be capable of sinking it easily. If she concentrates her attack on the stern, even with low expectations, there's the possibility of speed reduction and rudder damage, and if the destroyer dumps the torpedoes to avoid the danger of secondary explosions, the destroyer's ability to counter the imperial submarines will definitely take a dive, which will allow for the imperial submarines to take control of the enemy ships without any problem. In other words, she wouldn't have so much risk with this action which is perfect for her since she doesn't want to die. Now that the enemy mages aren't there to save the destroyer, she can freely dive towards it without worrying about being attacked from above.

Tanya is aware that holding the enemy until the reinforcements she called come isn't going to be easy, that's why she gives her best in this mission while also making her subordinates do a significantly great job by hastening the enemy's attrition which will make their influence get even bigger. Since the situation was unexpected for her, the only thing she has to say in her report is that they did all they could in a split-second decision. The fact that she managed to locate the enemy would also make things easy for her since her battalion's original mission here was to locate and destroy the enemy, even if they didn't manage to destroy them, they found the enemy location so this would be good enough for the army. She then decides to focus on giving a good hit to the enemy while attacking the ships would be a secondary objective since doing such a thing is the Northern Sea Fleet's job, her entire battalion is already suffering with fatigue due to tiredness and she doesn't plan to make them get even more tired unnecessarily. After this, Tanya alongside the first company accelerates again to dive while she is still leading the way to avoid receiving enemy shots, of course, her subordinates are in danger with this action, but as long as she stays in a safe position she doesn't really care.

After this, the first company shot their explosion type formula magic on the destroyer under Tanya's orders. She is aware that having the first company staying at the exact same position for an extended amount of time in order to observe the results of their attack wasn't a good idea since the enemy could make a sneak attack while they're on low-guard, that's why she makes the fourth company seeing everything from a distance give reports regarding the results of the first company's attack while the first company keeps some distance. After deciding that her battalion already did enough against the enemy, Tanya orders an immediate retreat since any further combat would wear them down without gaining anything, she then pulls everyone into return formation as fast as possible and retreats back to Base. Due to the destruction caused by the first company on the ship, Tanya found it hard to believe that it would be capable of escaping from the North Seas fleet, she purposely assured that the destroyer would be affected as much as possible with their attacks in order to guarantee that her battalion’s efforts in this battle wouldn't be completely meaningless. The crazy adversity she was facing and the fact that she didn't expect this situation at all makes this strategy even more impressive. Throughout this entire strategy, Tanya demonstrated impressive resource management, Social skills and Leadership. Obviously, this entire strategy is also a problem solving feat since this situation was completely unexpected.

Tanya's Arene Plan

After some time has passed, the Republic's soldiers invade Arene, one of the empire's cities, and Tanya has to solve this problem. The situation here is that due to the fact that the Republic soldiers are located in an Urban city full of civilians, the Empire couldn't go all out, after all, if they ended up hitting a civilian, in other words, a Non combatant, the world powers would have a fair motive to intervene in the war which is something that the empire wants to avoid due to the fact that their “neutrality” isn't certain. Not only this, but the Military police and troops located there are having problems to solve this situation, if Tanya leave them alone, the empire's rear will get completely burned, but if they put out the fire, the empire's front lines will get tampered, one wrong move and it'll be trench warfare without ammunition or food since the rear and the supply lines will be ruined. Another adversity here is that they are literally in a war where they're enemies won't hesitate for even a second to kill them. Not only this, but Tanya's battalion would also have to face the enemy while being fatally outnumbered.

Facing this situation, Tanya proposes a plan to solve this problem, they would basically turn Urban Warfare Legal, Tanya is aware that due to the international law about countries not being supposed to attack non combatants, the Republic will also be highly restricted since exactly like the empire they could end up hurting a civilian and be punished by international law. Knowing about this, Tanya proposes for the empire to demand for the Republican soldiers to protect the civilians in the shelters according to international law. In other words, if they don't take the evacuees and withdraw, the empire could interpret that as meaning there are no civilians and that the Republic captured the city.

According to International law, both countries have the duty to protect noncombatants, so it's expected that the empire will do everything in their power to fulfill it and Tanya plans to use this fact. In fact, if they manage to make them tell to the empire that there are no noncombatants, the restriction of the international law will be instantly lifted, in other words, if their enemy say that every person down to the last civilian will resist them, they could interpret that as meaning that every last civilian is militia. With this, they wouldn't have to recognize any prisoner's rights and would be capable of attacking full-on without holding back. This is what Tanya plans to do here, she plans to make all the civilians in Arene look like enemy combatants in order to make the empire have permission to attack with everything they have without having to worry about International Law. While this plan would be put in action, Tanya and her battalion would eliminate the enemy mage unit that penetrated the rear city of Arene and join up with the reinforcements to suppress the city.

Artillery and air units had been ordered to prepare to attack in case the people of Arene decided to not surrender as expected by Tanya, not only this but evacuation orders had been issued to the people in Arene, if things went according to Tanya’s plan and if the people of Arene(who had been making partisan riots, are literally rebelling against the Empire and were previously Republicans before Arene was annexed by the Empire in the war) didn't obediently surrender due to their animosity against the Empire, the Empire would have a valid reason to make a Full-Scale attack on the city and eliminate all “enemies”. With this plan, the empire would be capable of solving the problem regarding the Republican soldiers in an Urban Area and get rid of the rebelling people of Arene. Before the execution of this plan, Tanya asked the one who gave the orders for her to go to Arene if the enemy was the “Republican Army”, the reason as to why she did this was because she wanted to indirectly manipulate him into confirming the definition of “enemy” to her. This definition being “people who don't follow the Land War Convention”.

In this plan, the recruits of the Rhine Front will also help, in other words, if Tanya desires to not get her hands dirty in order to avoid the possibility of people accusing her of serious things later, she can simply say that her battalion had casualties and they would be capable of withdrawing, after all, she has a bunch of burdensome new recruits with her, with the new recruits slowing her down, the other units will probably arrive by the time Tanya's battalion finish eliminating the enemy mages. During the actual execution of the plan, it's revealed that commander Weiss, who is Tanya's vice commander, was shot and was bleeding, in other words, Tanya not only has to win this battle with the amount of disadvantages mentioned earlier, but now she has to win while having lost one of the commander's of one of the Companies that compose her battalion. Noticing this situation, Tanya gives orders for Weiss to fall back since she's apprehensive about losing her capable assistant; she comprehends that he's one of the ones on her battalion who have common sense, so losing him in this battle would possibly be bad for her in the future. She also gave orders for him to round up the other soldiers who've been hit and retreat back to base.

(Cunning, Social skills, Leadership, resource management, Short-term Planning, Tactics, Methods, Problem Solving, Indirect manipulation, individual manipulation)

With Weiss out of the battle, Tanya had no other choice but to give the control of Weiss's company to Lieutenant Konig(a random guy who probably won't be mentioned again in the LN), Tanya quickly reorganized the command at her own discretion while being in the middle of a battle. After this, Tanya gives orders for her battalion to prepare to swoop in for a close-quarters fight and she also tells them to watch out for ambushes. But the enemy decided to shut themselves up in the city which is a place where the enemy can easily make ambushes against Tanya and her battalion, something that was already expected by Tanya. Noticing this, she aborts the charge and gives orders for her battalion to keep pressure on. The reason as to why she decided to do this is because her battalion's job is to take on the mage ambushes on the periphery of the city and nothing more. In other words, if they remove any mages from the attackable areas around the edges of the city, their mission will be mostly accomplished. In other words, Tanya's plan here is to drive the enemies to positions where they couldn't reach the bombers or artillery, and right after doing this, she would issue the surrender warning and get out of there immediately after doing this in order for Tanya to keep the dirt in her hands at a minimum. By doing this, their part of this operation will be over and they would be capable of letting their allies finish everything up.

After this, Tanya gives orders for one of her soldiers to contact the artillery and the bomber unit in order to tell them that they are issuing the surrender warning, in other words, she wanted to tell them beforehand that they were going to issue the warning in order to allow for them to prepare themselves in case the enemy won't surrender. If the enemy surrenders, great, if not, the bombardments will put an end to this entire situation. By issuing the surrender warning before the enemy surrenders by themselves, it's extremely easy to appeal to them emotionally and make them surrender quickly. Not only this, but if there's a trial to judge Tanya's actions of killing the Arene people, the surrender warning can be used as evidence in her defense by putting the blame on the Republican soldiers who decided to not surrender.

When Tanya made the surrender warning, the militia(people of Arene who were rebelling) ended up shooting someone dead and shouting something obscene like “go to hell, empire bastards”, something that was already predicted by Tanya days before the execution of this strategy and was part of her plans. Tanya even had one of her soldiers paying attention to what happens in order to make him record a video of the militia shooting someone. After this, Tanya sends the video to the Headquarters in order to achieve her goal of making the civilians in Arene look like combatants, after all, they had guns and shot someone from the Empire dead. With this, Tanya, her battalion and the empire soldiers would have a valid motive to eliminate everyone in the city, including civilians, the Republican mages that were attacking Tanya's battalion, and any other witnesses of what occurred. After all this, all Tanya's actions below this little text will now contain some aspects of strategy. Now that they have already finished their job, Tanya asks for permission to begin immediate rescue of the civilians in order to have a valid motive to not join the people that are going to execute the mass extermination in Arene. By doing this, she would have a valid motive to not participate in the mass extermination and avoid any harm for her political position, after all, she already passed through some political problems involving the Commonwealth's submarine, and she definitely doesn't want to be involved in any other type of things that could result in harm being caused to her political image. With this action, she also thinks about the future, after all, the fact that she didn't participate directly during the battle but was engaged in rescuing fellow countrymen should work as a get-out-of-jail safe card for her.

Now that the Empire had a justification to cause a mass extermination in Arene, the bombers and Artillery started to destroy the entire city completely. After all this, Tanya regroups her entire battalion and requests a target, surprisingly for everyone, she apparently planned on participating in what was happening in Arene firsthand. Of course she's not going to kill the citizens of Arene, only a few Republican soldiers. Tanya then receives orders to eliminate a group of Republican soldiers acting like rear guards as they retreat. The Republican Soldiers are using defensive formulas in order to protect themselves and the people of Arene from the empire's artillery, so eliminating them was definitely necessary for her plan. After this, Tanya eliminated the Republican soldiers, the people of Arene were almost completely eliminated, she managed to save the lives of some people of Arene, and her Arene plan was a success. With this, she also managed to solve the problem of countering the Republic's strategy of fighting in an area of rebels in an urban area.

Third Volume

While talking with Colonel Von Lergen, Tanya demonstrates to be suspicious that the operation of the empire to do a sneak attack and confuse the Republic's headquarters had another purpose:

P2: Rails had been laid on the runway to launch the supplemental acceleration devices. And resting on top of these tracks are the constructs themselves, hooked up to a mind-numbing number of boosters while workers fill the fuel tanks with an unbelievable amount of highly volatile liquid propellant.

P3: Considering how much impact all this activity has on secrecy, Tanya can't avoid sensing a firm intention to go through with the operation by the time the rails were laid out and the rockers began fueling.

P4: In fact, this is a totally disproportionate amount of effort for hitting the enemy headquarters, principally for the Empire, which was low on resources when the Entente Alliance was still fighting in the war.

FINAL CONCLUSION: There's probably a concealed second intention behind this operation made by the General Staff which Tanya isn't aware of.

After hearing Tanya's suspicions regarding the operation, Colonel Von Lergen says: “I don't believe it's a mistake to assume that striking at the enemy headquarters will require a great deal of advance preparation”, and after this, it's revealed that Tanya already anticipated this kind of response coming from Lergen. P1: The Empire's Shock and Awe operation has the purpose to cause confusion in the enemy headquarters and give an opportunity for the empire to attack with all their power.

P2: Rails had been laid on the runway to launch the supplemental acceleration devices. And resting on top of these tracks are the constructs themselves, hooked up to a mind-numbing number of boosters while workers fill the fuel tanks with an unbelievable amount of highly volatile liquid propellant.

P3: Considering how much impact all this activity has on secrecy, Tanya can't avoid sensing a firm intention to go through with the operation by the time the rails were laid out and the rockers began fueling.

P4: In fact, this is a totally disproportionate amount of effort for hitting the enemy headquarters, principally for the Empire, which was low on resources when the Entente Alliance was still fighting in the war.

C1: There's probably a concealed second intention behind this operation made by the General Staff which Tanya isn't aware of.

P5: After this, She thinks that the cost-effectiveness of the operation is suspiciously bad, questioning if the General staff would really invest so much to achieve such a single objective.

P6: She is aware that this isn't the sort of operation General Von Zettour would propose with his cool-headed thoughts on attrition warfare.

P7: She also considers Rudersdorf’s participation in this operation to be strange, after all, it's an unorthodox operation that relies on cunning tactics, so the bigwig maneuver warfare specialist being involved too is strange.

Initial-conclusion: Maybe the secret intention with this operation was to cause chaos in the enemy headquarters and lead to an even larger battle, Rudersdorf involvement supports this idea since he is a specialist in causing chaos on the enemy.

Counter-conclusion: they would be definitely put out of commission for such a large-scale battle. So causing chaos in the headquarters with the purpose of leading to an even larger battle must be wrong, the aim of the empire with destroying the enemy headquarters must be another thing that I didn't notice yet.

P8: Destroying the enemy headquarters would throw them into chaos, in war, the headquarters work as the head that controls everything, destroy it and the enemy will be completely aimless.

P9: At that point, even a modern army devolves into little more than a mob, which will allow for the empire to advance and initiate maneuver warfare with the Republic.

C4: That's the General staff's true objective, If General Von Rudersdorf capitalizes in the confusion to make a move, he'll be able to break from the current trench Warfare back into maneuver warfare.

P10: A Modern Army, even when nestled in trenches, only exists thanks to its brain, the headquarters. If you look at how weakened the Red Army was after Stalin's purge, it's possible to see that there's no room for debate about what happens to an army that has lost its command structure.

P11: Not only this, but in a normal nation under regular circumstances, probably the only country that could continue fighting after losing its regular army on the front is the American empire.

P12: For quite some time, the Republic has been advancing in the empire's territory, but if we think that such a thing was made on purpose to lure the enemy in, then everything starts to make sense.

P13: Dare to allow the enemy to take a strategic location, it's impossible for the enemy to ignore something so tempting right before their eyes.

C6: In other words, it's possible to deduce that the Republican Army will take the bait as planned by the Higher-ups of the empire and advance inside an strategic position of the empire territory.

P14: Before all these events, the Higher-ups in the empire decided to make an entire reorganization of the defensive lines.

C7: Possibly even such a thing was done by the Higher-ups with the intention of baiting the enemy into the empire's territory.

C8: The aim of this operation is to lure the enemy inside the empire's territory, encircle and eliminate them, and advance while they are confused due to not having contact with the headquarters.

C9: If that's the case, then this will be a mobile battle, but not just a mere breakthrough, it's going to be like a revolving door where the enemy enters inside the empire's territory and is pushed all the way back.

Final Conclusion: The Empire's actual goal with this entire Operation is to lure the Republican Army into their territory, destroy their headquarters to confuse the enemy soldiers to make them easy preys for the empire, encircle the confused Republican Army and then force a battle to push the enemy all the way back to their territory. Tanya simply abducted the empire's entire operation before it even came to an end and while having little information about it. This feat is highly impressive due to the fact that the empire's goal here was thoroughly concealed by the army in a way that not even the Francois Republic noticed what they were planning to do before it was too late, but Tanya was capable of abducting everything in seconds(at max a minute if you low-ball this feat massively) with basic information about the empire's plan.

After hearing Tanya's hypothesis, Colonel Von Lergen shouts and asks where she heard about the true goal of the empire with this operation, and she easily manages to perceive how surprised he was. With this, Tanya abducted that her hypothesis about the empire's true goal wasn't far off.

[Tanya's Thoughts]
|
“Sheesh, if the Republic would just surrender already, they could get out of this without squandering the nation's human resources. What point is there in slowly depleting your workforce? It would be a real waste if they wiped themselves out without even considering economic rationality. Should I assume that our opponent can calculate their economic gains and losses and counsel them to surrender? Resisting an enemy you can’t possibly beat—to the point of annihilation—Is above and beyond a soldier’s duty. The state is basically telling these cornered troops to die. Shouldn’t there be some limit to the suppression of human rights? I’m sure states have their logic, but there’s no reason individuals should have to sacrifice themselves to it. At this point, the state is expecting way more of individuals with rights than it should be able to. A soldier’s duty is to fight. I have no objections to serving for national defense. But it shouldn’t be anyone’s duty to get obliterated.”

(Intra EE, Seeing through people, Observation, Prediction, connecting the dots, information control, abductive reasoning, critical thinking, Holistic thinking, Linear thinking, convergent thinking)

After trying her best to convince the commander to allow for her to attack the enemy at the Brest Naval Base, Tanya analysis her current situation in order to judge what could be the results of her actions:

(Tanya's Thoughts)
|
“If I don’t do anything, there won’t be any trouble, but that’s completely missing the point. Think of Rudel—I shouldn’t be reproached for attacking an enemy country. After stating their objective about attacking the Brest Naval Base to her battalion in her usual manner, Tanya manages to perceive the tense expression on the face of her officers and realizes that what she said sounded strange for them. Something that was out of her expectations, Tanya thought that she understands her subordinates feelings as someone who previously worked in human resources, so it's a bit of a shock for her to discover that she doesn't understand their feelings at all, she understands that she's supposed to be managing her troops, and that if she doesn't understand their Hopes and dreams, them it can only mean that she's inept.

Due to being aware of the Republic's real plans, Tanya decided to attack the Brest Naval Base with her troops without any support, she was so determined and motivated to avoid such a catastrophic thing to occur that she was literally going against suggestions given by the Group Commander. But it was all in vain, the General staff already initiated the cease-fire and Tanya had no other choice but to allow for the Empire to fall on the Republic's trap.

(EP, ER, Self Awareness, Analytical thinking, convergent thinking, Linear thinking, Decision Making)

After the General staff Accepted tge cease-fire without any logical motive for Tanya, she went there to talk with Zettour, but when she appeared in the General staff office and asked to meet Zettour, one of the soldiers said that he wasn't in the office in the moment, she then asked if she could meet Rudersdorf, who's Zettour's friend, and by merely perceiving the troubled face of the soldier, she comprehend that such a thing was also impossible. And the reason for this being because all the people of the General staff were out, she thought that the motive for this was something highly important due to her expectations of the empire's military management, but it was informed to Tanya that they left the General staff office to go to the beer hall and celebrate the cease-fire, despite having heard such a ridiculous motive that completely betrayed all her expectations, Tanya managed her emotions and kept her classic blank face.

After some time, Tanya returns to that place once again and speaks with Zettour, during their conversation, he says that no one would get upset at her for going against the orders of the Group Commander now that the end of the war was near. Upon hearing the words “end of the war”, Tanya managed to perceive that Zettour also believed that the cease-fire represented the end of the war, demonstrating impressive verbal reasoning skills.

(VCI, EP, Intra EM, Observation, Cold reading)

After nearly being shot by the enemy in a mission, Tanya didn't even had to analyze the damage caused to her in order to abduct that the gun used to hit her was a 40 mm anti-matériel rifle due to the fact that it is popularly known for being the natural enemy of any mage. Due to knowing that the enemy can hide themselves in the shadow of sand dunes due to them fighting on a desert, Tanya gives orders for her support soldiers to blow up the entire area in order to get rid of the snipers.

After Weiss managed to take control of the situation, Tanya decided to get cracking in her own prioritized list of tasks due to the fact that she recognizes that she can't just wait around for orders and Intel to come in, she recognizes that if she doesn't get an understanding of the situation and decide what to do about it soon, her soldiers will end up suffering losses. Here she demonstrates impressive decision making. Despite being in such a stressful situation, Tanya conceals her nervousness completely and initiates contact with the radio operator while wearing a smile on her face which is responded with another smile, which demonstrates impressive charisma due to the radio operator stating that he almost met the same fate as Tanya's boss, which was death. It's also stated later on that Tanya was flying in the lead of a combat mission while commanding combat at the same time which demonstrates impressive CPI and multitasking due to the fact that she also has to worry about not being shot down by the enemy.

Turus Naval Base Strategy

Tanya's situation here is that her battalion has to fight without water since they are in a desert. Not only this, but if they retreat, the enemy is going to follow them forever. Even if they confront the enemy, Tanya's battalion will run out of water and die of dehydration. If Tanya retreats before the enemy and allows her allies to be eliminated, she's going to be executed due to laws that prohibit soldiers from fleeing before the enemy. If she doesn't do anything in this situation, her friendly troops will end up being eliminated by the enemy, even if she tries to strike down the enemy directly, her battalion is merely a single aerial mage battalion, they are hopelessly outnumbered and they would definitely lose if they attacked the enemy directly. Even if they were going to support the troops withdrawal, it would be practically impossible by orthodox methods. And even the idea of a long-range penetration in the enemy airspace to strike the water transport lines is unreasonable. If she actually manages to cause some confusion on the enemy forces, the army would give orders for Tanya to hold their ground to keep causing confusion on the enemy for a longer period, which would mean that she would have to put her life at great risk which is something that she wants to avoid. The enemy also had mages on the flanks whose job was to get rid of an average battalion's worth of soldiers in an unexpected situation. Not only this, but as I already said some few times before, they are at war where the enemy will kill them the moment they see a chance to do so. In all shapes and forms, Tanya's situation here appears to be impossible to surpass.

Facing this situation, Tanya decides to advance on the enemy and break through their defenses, she uses the fact that the enemy thought that they had surrounded all Imperial soldiers in her favor to make a strong sneak attack on them while they were on low-guard. She make a sneak attack on the enemy's right flank, the reason for this being because the mages on both flanks had the special purpose of getting rid of battalion-sized group of soldiers, the fact that the enemy troops were trained for counter-land attacks due to the original plan being to capture every soldier ended up working in Tanya's favor. After some fighting, Tanya's battalion has success in eliminating two entire enemy companies, Tanya's main aim here is to save her allies in order to allow for them to help in the battle too, the reason for this obviously being because she understands that fighting alone wasn't going to work on her favor and would be too risky for someone like her who desires to play as safe as possible, allowing for her allies to help in the battle too also allows for her to avoid a situation where she's given orders to hold their ground against the enemy all by themselves which would to took risky for Tanya who likes to play safe.

But in fact, one of the reasons as to why Tanya gave orders for her soldiers to attack the right flank was to misdirect the enemy headquarters into thinking that her goal was to hit the right flank's batteries, which would result on the enemy being indirectly manipulated by Tanya into sending one battalion and an entire regiment of soldiers to protect the right flank batteries. When her plan was a success and the enemy fell on the trap as already anticipated by Tanya, she immediately changed the entire battalion's course and decided to charge directly at the central Republican forces attacking Tanya's allies, by doing this, she saves her allies from the enemy attacks and make them help in the battle too against the enemy. In this strategy, striking the enemy's right flank was just one of Tanya's objectives, if the Republican Army had left them to their own devices, her battalion would have broken through the right flank and left, but if the enemy decided to reinforce the right flank, she would strike the place where the units have been removed, that place being the center of the enemy's forces. In other words, Tanya's strategy would work independently of which one of these two scenarios occurred which demonstrates her insane strategic skills.

She already anticipated that the enemy would take units from the center and call them to the right flank due to the fact that it was simply faster to call units from the center to the right than to the left to the right, the fact that the enemies in the right flank were in a struggle would also contribute with this decision since the one in command would be pressured into sending help to the soldiers in the right flank as fast as possible. Not only this, but supposing that the enemy mages charged in a straight line, the noise and jamming would be such that their ability to detect the imperial soldiers would be temporarily paralyzed. Which would allow for Tanya's battalion to pass through them without being noticed at all, with this, when the units finally reached the right flank to protect it, in the exact instant they finished fanning out, they became completely useless. They couldn't contribute a single thing at the decisive moment where the center was being attacked. Which demonstrates Tanya's impressive trap evasion skills. In fact, Tanya indirectly manipulated the one managing these mages into turning them completely useless. After managing to hit the enemy's central Republican forces that were attacking her allies, Tanya lets her imperial allies fight against the enemy soldiers and distract them while Tanya carries out the remaining aspects of her strategy.

Allowing for her soldiers to make the hard job of actually fighting against the enemy soldiers also guarantees that Tanya's battalion won't be in a life-threatening situation and they won't receive orders from the army to hold their ground against the enemy. Now that the Republican soldiers are too distracted to stop Tanya's battalion from carrying out the rest of her strategy, Tanya aims to take out the enemy's headquarters with a surgical strike, the same tactic used to control the Republic's headquarters during the Rhine Front battles was now going to be used once again to defeat the Republican enemies. The reason for this being because the headquarters act like the “head” that leads the soldiers, without the “head”, the enemy soldiers would be completely aimless and would enter in a severe state of confusion without knowing what to do, which would make things easier for Tanya and her battalion to beat everyone that appears on their way. This entire situation wasn't expected at all by Tanya and she formulated this entire strategy in a matter of literal seconds, which demonstrates her insanely high FRI and ability to adapt to unpredicted situations. This entire strategy also demonstrates Tanya's Impressive Leadership and Social skills due to the fact that all her actions here were made while she was giving orders and leading her battalion.

(FRI=[Problem Solving], PSI, Fluid intelligence, Social skills, Leadership, Cunning, Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Problem Solving, Adaptability, Decision Making, indirect manipulation, mass manipulation, Logical manipulation, Misdirection, Non-Verbal Deception, mass deception, Strategic Deception, Anticipation, Trap Setting, Trap Evasion)

After indirectly manipulating the enemy's headquarters into sending soldiers to protect the right flank, it's shown to us that Tanya's main aim in this strategy was to completely destroy the enemy headquarters command directly in order to cause confusion on the Republican soldiers and give an advantage for her imperial allies fighting against the enemy, such a thing would drastically increase the chances of Tanya's battalion winning this battle. Thanks to Tanya’s tactic of shaking off the enemy reinforcements and ripping through their headquarters, the enemy soldiers were now in absolute panic due to the fact that they don't know what to do anymore, as already said before, the headquarters act like the head who give orders for the body, without it, the enemy soldiers are completely confused without knowing what to do, which gives an opportunity for the imperial soldiers helping Tanya in this battle to eliminate each one of them one by one without having to worry about suffering big losses since the enemy is completely confused now since they can't receive orders from the Headquarters anymore.

Not only this, but the Imperial army that was previously surrounded by the remnants of the Republican Army were now capable of maintaining an organized combat unit due to Tanya's actions, and the situation was now such that the Imperial soldiers could make a breakthrough. Now in this situation, the Imperial Army was free to push forward or retreat without fearing the idea of being punished, in other words, Tanya can freely retreat now if the situation becomes way too dangerous and way too many soldiers end up dying. In fact, now that the enemy flanks can't do much because of the confusion, the Imperial soldiers can resurrect their initial plan of taking them all out which was literally the whole reason as to why they went to the Turus Naval Base. Now Tanya could have the help of the remaining Imperial soldiers that were previously surrounded by the enemy, which would make things way easier for her. Now that the Imperial soldiers appeared to have everything under their control, Tanya's battalion retreated a little from the battlefield, they separated into multiple different groups of companies and started to quickly pulling back around the enemy, Tanya's battalion was practically playing tag with the enemy, they were moving so fast that combat against them was difficult for the enemy. Her goal here is to apply even more pressure on the already confused Republican soldiers in order to give assistance to the Imperial soldiers.

When the Republicans send Reinforcements to surround the enemy, Tanya alongside her battalion ripped right through the surrounding mages by overwhelming them with better mobility and firepower. After this, Tanya's battalion charged directly to the enemy's second headquarters and went to cause a direct hit to the enemy's headquarters once again in order to make the Republican soldiers be even more confused about their current situation. After completely destroying the second enemy headquarters with grenades and fifty-kilo bombs, Tanya's battalion immediately returned to battle in order to continue giving support to the Imperial soldiers, the reason for this being because she guessed that it would be bad to only avoid the enemy while only destroying their headquarters, that's the exact motive as to why she decided to make an anti-surface strike right in the end of the battle to keep her reputation safe, demonstrating impressive decision making. Without any other choice, the enemy soldiers were forced to retreat due to being completely surrounded and due to not having almost any sort of communication with the Republican commanders commanding the battle.

But for some unknown reason, a group of units suddenly appeared and were going to attack Tanya's battalion, facing this situation, she gave orders for two different companies to act like the baits for the enemy, she tells them to position themselves in the rear and pretend to collapse into disarray and flee when the enemy tries to attack. The first thing she does is make two companies pretend to be the rear guard in order to get the enemy's attention and indirectly manipulate them into attacking. Tanya recognizes that soldiers are like enraged bulls that attack with all their might in the moment they see a chance to destroy the enemy, that's exactly why she specified for those two companies to pretend to collapse into disarray when the enemies decide to attack in order to indirectly manipulate them into thinking that the Imperial soldiers are like easy preys. While these two companies pretend to have collapsed into disarray, the other units visible would pretend to flee before the enemy and get some distance in order to indirectly persuade the enemy into continuing to attack.

The two companies would have the special aim of attracting the enemy to the D-3 airspace area. When the enemy finally reached the D-3 airspace area, the other companies that were pretending to flee from the enemy would attack from all sides while forming a cone shape in order to cross the lines of fire. Here she demonstrates good trap setting skills by preparing a situation where the enemy soldiers wouldn't have any way to escape. With this strategy, Tanya managed to free her allies in battle, grant a way for her battalion and allies to retreat if necessary without having to worry about being punished, lower the number of enemy soldiers massively and force them to retreat, doing all this while avoiding direct combat with the enemy in order to preserve her own life and simply destroying all the enemy headquarters to throw the enemy soldiers in confusion and give an advantage for her allies, demonstrating impressive strategy effectiveness. The fact that this entire strategy was made in less than a minute makes everything even more impressive and demonstrates Tanya's impressive Problem Solving skills. The fact that this situation also wasn't predicted by her at all turns this entire strategy into an adaptability feat. Also, this entire strategy to save her imperial allies that were surrounded by Republican enemies count as a trap Evasion feat since this entire situation in the Turus Naval Base was a trap made by the Republican enemies and Tanya managed to avoid it.
