Fourth Volume

Close to the beginning of the volume, Tanya opens a special document that only her and Weiss, who is the vice leader of the battalion, could read. In the document, they were requested to cross the Russy Federation’s borders due to suspicions that they will also enter in the war, Tanya then analysis the General staff’s motives for such a decision:

P1: Currently, the war is in a stalemate between the empire and the Commonwealth with the help of the remaining Republican soldiers.

P2: It's strange for the Russy Federation to only decide to enter in the war now considering its current state.

P3: The General staff isn't composed of a bunch of idiots, the Major Generals like Zettour who work there are considerably smart.

C1: So they probably also thought about the reason as to why the Russy Federation would do such a thing, it's not logical to think that they would overlook the Federation's motives when this is something that even Tanya and Weiss that only received this information recently already considered.

P4: Imperial forces can't simply violate the border for show or on a mere whim, that would be tantamount to handing the other side a free card.

P5: If such a thing becomes a diplomatic issue, the harm that would befall the empire during peacetime would be extraordinary.

P6: Despite Knowing about such a thing, the Higher-ups of the General staff decided to take the risk and have Tanya's battalion sneak into the Federation's borders.

C2: As already said before, the Higher-ups of the General staff are considerably smart, so there must be a reason for that.

P7: The General staff wouldn't take a Decisive measure based on half-baked information from Intelligence.

C3: The reason that they decided to do such a risky action as request for Tanya's battalion to sneak on the Federation's borders implies that they consider it as being the final check and not hesitant reconnaissance.

C4: They are assuming that hostilities will commence and are preparing for the worst-case scenario by moving a few units into place.

C5: In other words, already predicting that the Russy Federation would attack, the Higher-ups wanted Tanya's battalion to attack them first and initiate war against the Federation with an initial advantage due to attacking first while the enemy isn't completely prepared yet.

P8: The mission is obviously to punch into enemy territory, what the Higher-ups want for Tanya's battalion to do is confirm the start of the war and attack at the same time, the empire's literal intention is reconnaissance in enemy territory.

C6: But given Tanya's battalion's current positioning, the true nature of their orders is to prepare for an attack.

FINAL CONCLUSION: The Higher-ups' actual goal with sending Tanya's battalion to sneak into the Federation's territory is to make them confirm an action from the Federation that suggested that they planned on starting war, wait until they actually do it and attack the Federation soldiers with all their strength before they manage to prepare themselves.

After having hypothesized all this, Tanya asks for Weiss to gather everyone from the battalion, the reason as to why she asks for him to do this and not Tanya herself doing this is because they are currently located near a noisy airplane, due to being a child, she isn't capable of speaking as loudly as an adult so she considers to be better to ask for Weiss to gather everyone instead of doing it herself. By perceiving Weiss's guilty expression and hearing him say that he hopes she's not angry at him for questioning the reason as to why he should gather the others, Tanya considers that she unconsciously put on an annoyed face and she understands that his guilty face makes her feel like she owes him some consideration. By perceiving how tense everyone was after receiving the news regarding the possible war declaration coming from the Russy Federation, Tanya makes a few jokes about the situation and makes them remember about the nostalgic events in the Rhine Front in order to calm down everyone.

(EP, Inter EE, Self Awareness, ER, Leadership, EF, Connecting The Dots, Decision Making, Observation, Abductive reasoning, deductive reasoning, critical thinking, Holistic thinking, Linear thinking, convergent thinking)

Suddenly during the operation to spy on the Russy Federation, Weiss ask for Tanya to allow for him to lead the approach toward the enemy staging area, at first, she doesn't understand why he decided to ask such a thing, but after hearing him saying that he considers himself to be better to handle with physical stressed of a no-magic match than Tanya, she understands that when she left the explanation of the operation to Weiss previously due to her being incapable of speaking as loud as him, he finally recalled the disparity between their physiques and he must have realized that in a contest of pure strength that doesn't involve magic, Tanya would be as fragile as a little kid. After confirming that the Russy Federation was really going to attack the empire, Tanya decides to wait until they attack first, the reason for this being because they would have diplomatic problems if they attacked the Federation before a battle between the Federation and the empire even began, it would count as attacking a neutral country, so she waits until the Federation attacks the empire and attacks the enemy soldiers while they weren't expecting an attack at that moment.

After some time, the Federation attacks the Empire and sends a formal declaration of war which is responded to by a warning coming from the empire to all soldiers telling them to attack the Federation. Noticing this, Tanya gives orders for her battalion to abandon their original plan to withdraw and transition into attacking maneuvers. She tells her battalion to deal with the enemy reinforcements in order to lower the amount of military force of the enemy soldiers attacking the Eastern Army Group, she says that the first thing they should do is blow up the stockpiles while assuming strike formation. She also tells the company commanders to seize control of their attack routes and give orders for all units to follow the instructions of their company commanders on how to engage after the raid. In the battle against the Federation, Tanya decides to give orders for her battalion to prepare explosion formulas and shoot them at the enemy's railway guns to cause large explosions.

(FRI=[Problem Solving], EU, EP, Psychological Insight, Social skills, Leadership, Observation, Decision Making, problem solving)

After the events of Tanya's attack in Moskva, she received a request from an officer of the Eastern Army group regarding a bunch of soldiers surrounded at the isolated city of Tiegenhoff, but there's a problem, before this Tanya's battalion received an urgent request coming from the General staff to prepare themselves for an operation, despite knowing about how important orders from the General staff are, Tanya decided to help the soldiers surrounded at Tiegenhoff because she recognized that it was an strategic city in potential, it's an easy city to defend because it's on a river and it's also near the sea, it has some distance from the border between the empire and the Federation where the war is occurring, not only this, but it's extremely close to a transportation hub so it would be easy to receive maritime support from the city. Noticing all this, Tanya makes the difficult choice of ignoring the General staff request to prepare for an operation and decides to go save the soldiers in Tiegenhoff. Demonstrating impressive Decision Making.

While thinking for some time about the idea of helping the soldiers in Tiegenhoff, Tanya inducts that the enemy heavy artillery won't be a significant threat for them. Indirect fire becomes possible only with incomparable teamwork, on the Rhine Front both the empire and the Republic had artillery scouts stationed on the forward-most lines or had aerial mages flying around as observers to collect data in peril of being shot down, only then was effective fire possible, cannons fired at random won't find their mark and observer scouts weren't spotted anywhere in Tiegenhoff based on the reports, in other words, there are currently zero observer scouts for artillery so artillery won't be a problem at all for imperial mages like Tanya and her battalion. Tanya understands that the reason as to why both Weiss and Visha didn't think about this was because they learned too much from their experiences from the Rhine Front to the point where they unconsciously thought that the Federation would have observer scouts.

After having saved the surrounded soldiers at Tiegenhoff, Tanya meets their commander and notices that every nonchalant action he makes is filled with a veteran’s tact and wit, she recognizes that he's a priceless partner and even she tries to form some kind of friendship with him.

(EU, Psychological insight, Observation, Connecting The Dots, Decision Making, deductive reasoning, critical thinking, Holistic thinking, convergent thinking, Linear thinking)

Basically, after Tanya and her battalion came to Tiegenhoff, the Russy Federation decided to make a Large-scale invasion on the city with an amount of enemy units equal to eight entire infantry divisions at minimum, the problem is that Tanya's side can only count with the help of two undermanned divisions mid-retreat excluding her battalion, these same two divisions recently fought a battle against the Russy Federation that forced them into requesting help from Tanya's battalion and she can't expect for them to have the concentration of force to prevent approaches from all directions. Not only this, but they have to weather the Federation army's wave attacks while protecting the remnant civilians in that place, in fact, this restriction only applies to them, the Federation isn't a signatory on several international treaties so the law of war doesn't apply to them, which means that they won't hesitate to attack full-on because of the civilians. Tiegenhoff is a strategic city in potential that could possibly be extremely useful for the empire, giving it away for the Federation would be a bad move for Tanya and she definitely wants to avoid such a scenario.

Before entering into actual combat against the enemy, Tanya explains to Weiss and Visha that their plan is to advance and defend the city, they'll lure the enemy and engage in delaying combat on the outskirts of Tiegenhoff, they will advance and buy time as they get a handle of things. Upon hearing Lieutenant Grantz’s protest about her plan being too intense, Tanya makes fun of him and asks for him to be a pain in the ass somewhere else, the reason as to why she does this is to use Grantz as a way to inflict humor on the rest of the battalion in order to lighten the mood despite them being attacked by an insane amount of soldiers from the Federation, she did this because she understands that panic will always causes problems independently of the situation. After this, Tanya continues to make jokes about the situation by saying that they would aggressively do things that the enemies hate and that as a good girl she naturally takes the initiative to do what others hate, the reason as to why she does this is to further lighten her battalion's mood in order to completely erase the panic off their minds.

After having success to raise her battalion's enthusiasm, Tanya goes to the battlefield in order to attack the enemy soldiers, the original plan was to make a recon-in-force while gaining time for the evacuation of civilians, in other words, what they planned on doing was gaining time for the civilians while gaining information about the exact armaments and enemy forces, and after all this, they would withdraw in order to prepare themselves better to fight against the enemy. But after getting considerably close to the enemies, Tanya's battalion doesn't see a single enemy image coming to attack them, Tanya then anticipates a number of ways to deal with potential enemies and decides to give orders for her battalion to prepare to respond whether enemy mages are present or not. She tells them to prepare a company-sized formation for anti-surface attacks, she also tells them to watch their flanks and support one another while keeping a sharp eye on the air around them. After having given orders for her battalion to prepare themselves for enemy mage's attacks, Tanya gives additional orders for them to assume strike formation and tells the soldiers composing the company that she just made to follow her. Now you might be asking me, why would she ask for these soldiers to follow her? The reason for this being because she plans on doing the same thing she did in the Norden Sea strategy, she's using these soldiers as a wall of meat.

After this, Tanya's altitude plummets to the point where she reaches point-blank range, to the point where she can see the enemy soldiers and they can see her. After having located the enemy commander, Tanya gives orders for her soldiers to shoot the explosive formulas they have been holding at the direction of the explosive formula she's about to shoot, after this, she computes the formula type magic with her computation Orb and manifests it at the appropriate coordinates in the middle of the enemy infantry, blowing them away completely, and after this, her soldier's explosive formulas is fired at the exact same place where Tanya shoot her explosive formula. After eliminating the enemy commander and having gained information about the enemy forces, Tanya give orders for her soldiers to withdraw, but there are some soldiers who think that they should continue eliminating the enemy since they don't have any mages or proper anti-air weapons with them, but this is denied by Tanya who reaffirms that their mission isn't to increase their gains but making a recon-in-force attack, insisting for them to withdraw. But her soldiers still continue to insist that they should continue attacking, Tanya then gives final orders for all units to prepare to withdraw and pull out with their companies, she says that once they regroup, they'll swing by the enemy lines, she also give additional orders for them to make searching for and destroying mage forces their top priority. After they regroup in the air, Tanya notices that Weiss is keenly focused on the ground and reads his mind by perceiving that he also thinks that they should continue attacking.

One of the reasons as to why she gave orders for her battalion to retreat now is because she considered the fact that the idea of attacking the enemies now that they are without air cover, which is an extremely seductive option for any soldier, could be a trap made by the enemy to distract them with a bunch of weaklings while the real fighters attack from the other side, so she simply decided to let them go due to considering the existence of a trap. She understands that as long as the opponents are communists, the possibility that they would use random units as a decoy and attack with their real forces can't be ruled out. After having regrouped her battalion mid-air, Tanya gave orders for her troops to follow and sneak in repeated anti-surface attacks on the Federation army attack route. With this, Tanya manages to solve the problem of the enemy forces attacking Tiegenhoff with eight infantry divisions.

(FRI=[Problem Solving], Fluid intelligence, Strategy, Methods, Tactics, EP, EU, Inter EE, EF, psychological warfare, charisma, Leadership, Social skills, Anticipation, Trap evasion, Observation, Cold reading, indirect manipulation, emotional manipulation, mass manipulation, Problem Solving)

During the events of the fourth Volume of the Light Novel, Tanya managed to deduce the reason as to why the Federation joined the war against the empire only after the Republic's fall:

P1: As far as the empire is aware, there's no logical reason for the Russy Federation to start a war against the empire.

C1: But even if they are not aware of it, there must have been a reason behind the Federation's actions, attacking the empire who was the Federation's ally like this was strange no matter how someone thinks about it.

P2: But as long as they don't know the motive behind the Federation's actions, negotiating with them using the existing paradigm should be avoided, until we grasp the paradigm, it's unclear if it's even possible to negotiate with that country.

P3: Some people think that if the empire gets through the first attack, they'll find a way to get an early peace through negotiations with the Federation.

P4: The government of the Federation is completely authoritarian and worries a lot about propaganda in order to make themselves look way stronger than they actually are.

C2: This means that a cease-fire would mean death to the Federation since the government won't accept the failure of being defeated in the war due to the Federation's political system of wanting to make themselves look strong thanks to propaganda, losing the war would show to the world how weak they are and the government won't accept such a thing.

P5: This means that the Russy Federation government won't accept losing the war or doing anything that implies that they were being defeated by the empire.

C3: Therefore, negotiating a cease-fire with the Federation is a total fantasy, in fact, even small-scale negotiations on the front lines would be difficult.

P5: But Zettour thinks that one of the reasons as to why the idea of having negotiations with the Federation became impossible was because of Tanya's attack on Moskva.

P6: But such a thing isn't true at all, the Federation sees the world in a different way than the Empire does, it is a nation with a tendency to be exclusionary and paranoid about the other countries.

P7: Which means that the values governing its conduct probably emphasize survival above everything.

P8: The reason as to why the François Republic and Commonwealth declared war against the empire was because they feared that it could grow stronger and dominate the entire continent.

P9: For the Federation this feeling would be even stronger due to their paranoia about being attacked by bordering nations close to their territory like the empire.

C4: In other words, the Federation is scared of the empire, or scared of being attacked, if one hypothesizes that it took the initiative to attack for that reason, the choice begins to make sense.

C5: In other words, emotions, the main cause of the Federation's actions and why they initiated this attack against the empire is fear. Military actions obviously are no exception.

P10: The existence of the empire is cause for a fear that the Russy Federation can no longer hope to tolerate.

P11: This also means that the Federation's fear towards the empire is a paranoia that won't disappear until the existence of the empire is eradicated.

C6: If that's the case, then the Russy Federation will only lay down its swords and abandon the war when the empire is completely destroyed.

C7: Which means that any kind of negotiation with the Russy Federation will be completely meaningless since they won't give up until they destroy the empire.

P12: But that still doesn't explain why the Federation didn't stab us in the back while we were fighting against the Republic. If the empire is the object of their fear, what were they waiting for?.

P13: Before the declaration of war of the Federation against the empire, the empire had already finished to take the François Republic out of the war by invading the country.

C8: In fact, attacking the empire at this moment is a completely rational decision in the military realm, the reason for this probably being because the Federation was frightened of the immensely powerful Empire's intention of going so far as to destroy the Republic. The Russy Federation is paranoid and scared of the empire's power to invade their country, after seeing the Empire destroying the Republic in the war and proving its overwhelming power, this fear would grow even stronger and they would notice that if things continue like that, a future where they would be forced to fight against a giant empire without any ally would be possible.

C9: To be more direct, the reason as to why the Federation didn't attack until the Republic was destroyed is because they were so scared to fight against the Empire that they stood by while they destroyed the Republic and then couldn't bear the idea of facing a giant empire on their own.

P14: The Federation is desperate to survive, it's possible to think that they are determined to continue fighting until either the Federation or the empire falls.

C10: Which means that there's no peaceful way to come to terms with the Russy Federation and put an end to the war, either the empire collapses or they do.

C11: In other words, what they plan on doing is a war of annihilation until one of the two countries collapses completely.

Final Conclusion: The mere idea of having negotiations with the Federation in order to reach an early peace is a complete fantasy that isn't even worthy considering since they joined the war due to their paranoia of an imperial attack and won't stop until the empire collapses or they do, the reason as to why they only joined the war after the Republic's fall being because they were fearing the idea of fighting against the empire but suddenly after the Republic's fall, they started to fear the idea of having to fight against a giant empire on their own. This feat is highly impressive due to the fact that even the entire imperial army(which includes their intelligence) didn't have any idea as to why the Federation decided to join the way only now, but Tanya was capable of figuring this out a few days after the Federation joined the war.

This entire reasoning also counts as a good emotional understanding feat since Tanya manages to understand the emotional reasons that induced the Federation into attacking the empire now. What's also worth mentioning is that Tanya deduced the Federation's motive to join the war only after the Republic was defeated with extremely few premises, the only premises for her to deduce the Russy Federation's motivation to join the war was the fact that they are paranoid and the fact that they only joined the war after the François Republic lost against the empire.

(EU, Connecting The Dots, Observation, Deductive Reasoning, Abductive Reasoning, critical thinking, Holistic thinking, Linear thinking, convergent thinking, associative thinking)

During the events of the fourth Volume, the Mosquito 01 unit appeared in the place where Tanya's battalion was in the middle of an attack on Londinium, when this occurred, It's stated that due to the fact that the Rhine was where Tanya has spent the most time, she remembers the name of nearly every single units on the battlefield at the time, which was probably more than one hundred units since the Rhine Front was the place where the majority of soldiers were sent to, demonstrating impressive memory. After this, Tanya gives orders for both Weiss and Visha to pay a nice visit to the 103rd Fighter Wing in order to persuade them into giving information regarding the front battlefield since Tanya wants to avoid dangerous situations as much as possible. During the meeting between majors regarding the group of Unified States aerial mages fighting under the Commonwealth's orders. During the meeting, it's also said that the imperial army has been fighting against the Commonwealth in the west and south so Tanya already figured out, albeit belatedly, that the army and navy have very different usage patterns, in the Commonwealth's army, the mages are assumed to work jointly with other branches of the forces as essentially flying infantry, while in the navy, they're worked hard as an independent branch, which is how things like attack squads dispatched from submarines, boarding parties between ships, close air support for marines, and so on are formed.

Due to already knowing that the Unified States will hit on the empire one day, Tanya proposes in the majors meeting for the empire to show in no uncertain terms what would be the results of an escalation, in other words, what she wants to do is show the empire's military power at its peak by eliminating all the Unified States mages fighting for the Commonwealth in order to incentive the Unified States into not declaring war against the empire since the outcome would result in absolute destruction for both sides, she also suggests for them to act like such a thing was an unforeseen result of engaging since Tanya recognizes the possibility of the Unified States Citizens being galvanized. With this, they would be capable of avoiding an outcome where the Unified States enters the war and causes destruction in the Empire. Demonstrating impressive Trap Evasion skills for Tanya.

Tanya's Strategy to attack Moskva

After causing enough destruction and confusion to the Federation soldiers in the Northern army group's borders, Tanya decides to go attack the Federation's Capital directly with her battalion, she makes this decision due to already anticipating that they wouldn't have to worry about facing strong air defense from the Federation due to the fact that the air defense of the USSR in the real world was considerably weak, so Tanya judged that it would be the same in this word. Before going to the Federation's Capital, Tanya asks for one of her soldiers to ask permission for the General staff to allow their attack, she does this because she wants to confirm that there won't be any political issues with attacking the Federation's Capital, even if the General staff decides to not allow it, there will be a record of their request that shows that they proposed it, and if Tanya's Battalion get the go-ahead, it’ll be an excuse for them to stay away from the main lines for a while. After finally reaching the Federation's Capital, Tanya gives orders for her battalion to form a perfect anti-surface strike formation. Tanya knows that her mages aren't terribly exhausted and they have energy to spare, she estimates that they should still have enough energy to withdraw even if they really go to town instead of just doing a hit-and-run attack like Tanya plans on doing. Noticing this, Tanya decides to improvise her hit-and-run plan completely and decides to knock down the Federation's pride completely.

After having revised and improvised her plan in a matter of seconds, Tanya gives orders for the first company to go with her and attack the red stars on a people's palace, while she does this, she tells the other members of the first company to attack whatever government facilities they can find. The second company must take out the big bronze statues in the middle of the Capital, their main priority is to attack the main bronze statue representing Josef Stalin, but Tanya also gave orders for them to destroy all the other smaller bronze statues too if possible. The third company is going to subdue and destroy the tallest building in Moskva that has a view to Sildberia and eradicate the secret police. And the last company, The fourth company, is going to attack the Kremlin and cause as much damage to it as possible without holding back. After all this, Tanya gives orders for her troops to move out and start to carry out her orders in order to destroy the Federation's pride. After finally starting to receive enemy anti-air fire, Tanya gives orders for all the units composing her battalion to wreak havoc as their company commanders see Fit. She makes it clear for her battalion that their current tactical objective is to make a moderate amount of damage and make fun of the enemies in order to destroy their pride completely, that's why she gave total freedom for the Company commanders to cause destruction the way they see fit, they are free to express their own creative destruction on the enemy Capital. She knows that the Federation will try to cover up the fact that their air space was invaded by Imperial mages(those imperial mages being Tanya and her battalion), and that's why she specifically wants for her battalion to destroy the Capital’s most important monuments since covering such a thing won't be an easy job.

She recognizes due to knowledge about History that the Russy Federation similar to the URSS must be a country supported by propaganda, in other words, they try to make themselves look stronger and more powerful than they actually are, her goal with this plan is to uncover the real Russy Federation and disturb the notion of them being all-powerful by essentially ruining the country's entire reputation while also being capable of destroying the army's morale. After already having wait for enough time to organize her battalion, Tanya give orders for them to attack, and while her subordinates attack the Federation's Capital, she records a video using her computation Orb for PR while keeping both her subordinates and the city in the frame in order to make it clear for anyone that even glanced at the video that they were attacking Moskva. While her company continues to attack the enemy, Tanya has an idea and improvises her plan once again, this time, what she wants to do is record the video of her subordinates attacking the Federation while they sing the Empire's Anthem in order to destroy even more the Federation's reputation.

(Leadership, Social skills, psychological Warfare, Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Anticipation, Improvisation)

During the execution of Tanya's Plan to destroy the Federation's pride, the third company successfully destroyed the tallest building in Moskva down to smithereens. After this, Tanya's battalion gather together and sing the Empire's Anthem while using sound-amplifying formula type magic in order to instantly translate the anthem for the Russian language. After finishing singing the empire's anthem, Tanya gives orders for her battalion to prepare their formula type magic and shoot at the People's palace; with this, her battalion destroys another one of the Federation's most important monuments. But unexpectedly for Tanya, the fourth company demonstrates to have some difficulty to deal with the Kremlin forces, noticing this, she quickly improvises her plan once again and gives orders for the fourth company to change objectives in order to make the most of their limited time, and despite having failed into destroying the Kremlin, they still caused enough destruction on it to be a bother for the Federation. After this, the second company reports to Tanya, revealing that they had success in destroying the bronze statues.

After all this, Tanya decides to embarrass the Federation even more by putting an imperial flag in the middle of the square, doing such a thing wouldn't be a big risk due to the fact that they already have taken down the square of the Federation's Capital so she decides to do it, in the end, the imperial flag will wave on the Federation Capital which will destroy even more their morale, Tanya even predicts that the Federation will turn Moskva into a fortress in order to not allow for such a thing to occur again due to the embarrassment, even if they have to yank a ton of materiel and personnel off the front lines to do it. Not only this, but this entire plan not only has the purpose to destroy the Federation's morale, but it will also serve as a distraction for the Federation, currently the Federation's soldiers are fighting against the empire in the border, so Tanya causing as much destruction as possible in the Federation's Capital won't allow for the Federation to simply ignore then and focus solemnly on the empire, they'll have to mobilize their forces back to the Capital in order to get rid of them, Demonstrating impressive trap setting and mass manipulation. After having decided to put a flag in Moskva’s square, Tanya hears from her subordinates that they didn't have any flag with them to put on the square, but such a thing was already anticipated by Tanya and she already formulated a backup idea in order to get an imperial flag. In other words, she improvised her plans once again here.

Tanya understands that communists are the kind of people that love to make propaganda in order to show how good their side are, they also love movies since it shows a fictional side of the world that sometimes fits their delusions and that they love to use censorship on things that go against what they believe, Tanya understands that the same must apply to communist movies that are affected by censorship in order to be politically correct, and there's also the fact that the Empire and the Russy Federation developed a bad relationship recently, so she deduces that the Russy Federation already made Anti-Empire propaganda movies and that they must have an imperial flag on a film studios since they can't make propaganda about the evil empire without its evil flag. Understanding all this, Tanya gives orders for her battalion to go search for an imperial flag on the Federation's film studios. This is also an impressive improvisation feat and Tanya even anticipated that Visha would have an idea of where they could find a film studio due to her living in the Federation during her childhood. After some time, it's said that the Federation army officers even took their focus off the front lines in order to focus on what was happening in the Capital, in other words, Tanya already had fulfilled one of her goals with this attack that was to be a distraction for the enemy. After this, Tanya's battalion successfully takes the imperial flag from a film studio, they burn the Federation's red flags and place the imperial flag in the square while filming everything with the Federation's film studio camera.

During the execution of Tanya's plan, one of the Federation's officers was seeing everything happening in front of his eyes and instead of feeling anger for the disrespect for his country that Tanya was causing, he became completely obsessed with Tanya herself, it was like love at first sight, he was so obsessed with the idea of making her surrender and be his property that his self-control was reaching its limits, his entire personality was completely transformed from a man with a strange and insincere smile that one might call a communist grim, to a complete pervert and obsessive man, all of this by merely seeing the destruction caused by Tanya and her smile filled with carefree conviction, it's even said that in order to get her as being his perfect doll, he would make a deal with any devil, he would compromise with any political rival, he would make use of any dissidents and would even spare the mages he'd sent to Sildberia for execution simply to have them fight against the empire in order to capture Tanya. Demonstrating insane charisma and Aura. After all this, Tanya's battalion retreated and returned back to the empire before the Federation could arm themselves properly to attack them.

It's also said in the Light Novel that due to the destruction caused by Tanya's battalion in Moskva, the entire city was in a state of panic including the civilians, the people from the empire who normally criticized its military for being inefficient were completely speechless, the morale of the imperial soldiers was boosted while the morale of the soldiers of the Federation was ruined, the entire Federation including the civilians decided to approach the war as if their honor depended of it, and the chances of the Federation agreeing with having negotiations with the Empire to reach peace became completely nonexistent, in other words, Tanya's actions in the Federation's Capital had an effect on two different countries: the Empire(Germany) and the Russy Federation (obviously Russia), which is an impressive mass manipulation and emotional manipulation feat. She did all this while avoiding hitting civilian facilities in order to respect international law that prohibited attacks against civilians. With this, the first tab of this document about Tanya's feats comes to an end.

Fifth Volume

In the very beginning of the fifth Volume, Tanya receive orders for her Kampfgruppe to be reassigned to another place, the news was being given to Tanya by a staff from the Eastern Army Group and she perceived that the comments given to her with a pat on her back were heartfelt words of consolation about their transfer, not only this, despite Tanya's Kampfgruppe being extremely useful for the Eastern Army Group, they weren't showing any signs of being on a fuss with the orders of the General staff, something that was also perceived by Tanya, noticing these things, Tanya comes to the conclusion that they probably knew about this for quite some time and she was the one who was informed at the last possible minute.

After this, Tanya went to a train in order to go to the new place where her Kampfgruppe was reassigned, while being on this train, Tanya was reading newspaper that showed the words of imperial soldiers describing their life in the front lines, Tanya immediately notices that it's filled with propaganda and it's extremely censored, while reading it, Tanya perceives that the words of the soldiers written in the newspaper had the exact same phrasing, and that the openings have long since been written according to a template. Tanya's RMS Queen Of Anjou Ship Strategy

For some context, Tanya was tasked with the mission of sinking the RMS Queen of Anjou ship from the Commonwealth, ship capable of moving over thirty-knots while carrying things like ammunition and multiple experienced enemy soldiers, Tanya has to destroy the RMS Queen of Anjou without counting with the help of ships, or planes, she has to sink it only with the help of her battalion composed of 48 members. And Tanya doesn't even know for sure if her battalion’s firepower will be capable of penetrating the ship due to how insanely large it is. Not only this, but Tanya's battalion has been flying for a long amount of time searching for the ship, which resulted in them being considerably fatigued and while Tanya's battalion is fatigued, the enemy is brimming with energy to come up and intercept them since they are in a passenger ship. The entire ship is also filled with two entire regiments of enemy soldiers with a single regiment being equal to 8 or 10 companies compared to Tanya's four companies. There's also the fact that the enemy commander is an extremely experienced soldier that has already fought against Tanya's battalion before. Another thing that makes everything even more difficult is that Tanya didn't even know about the presence of a regiment worth of soldiers in the ship due to the incompetence of the group responsible for gathering intelligence.

While thinking about the best approach to deal with this situation, Tanya has a strange feeling and gives orders for her battalion to turn around and climb to the sky, after this, it's shown that the enemies shoot a disciplined fire calculated to hit the entire area from below and Tanya's battalion would be hit if they had stayed where they were for one additional second. After this, the enemy starts to prepare a second shot directly aimed at Tanya's battalion, she notices this and gives orders for everyone to abandon the original plan and ascend to get some distance from the ship. The reason as to why she does this is to put herself and her battalion in a safe situation while overcoming the numerical disadvantage. Doing such a thing would make things less difficult for Tanya's battalion since the ship doesn't have any anti-air fire and evading the enemy's disciplined fire while they are in the ship at a distance of eight thousand feet would be easier than dodging their shots when they are chasing Tanya's battalion in a short distance. The reason as to why she decides to do this is thanks to their altitude advantage, the enemy is coming up at their direction and the only thing Tanya has to do to get rid of them is obliterating them with impulsive force. Adding the speed of her battalion's fall to their charge, they'll have the energy advantage over their climbing enemies. That's why she gives immediate orders for her subordinates to dive on the enemies after splitting into companies in order to beat them down. Another reason for this decision made by Tanya is to not allow for the battalion to stay too close to each other since eliminating her soldiers would be easier to the enemy soldiers if they were close to each other than if they were separated, splitting the 203rd Aerial mage battalion into multiple different companies would also force the enemy into having to focus on multiple different enemies at the same time which would weaken their overall fire power.

With Tanya's decision of diving on the enemy, she also managed to spread confusion on them since no one in that ship could possibly anticipate such a thing, the enemy soldiers were extremely confused to the point where leading them became difficult for the commander. After this, it's shown the results of the dive made by Tanya's battalion from the sky, they made through the enemy where they used magic blades at close quarters to cut off the heads of the enemy mages’ commanders, causing confusion on them since there would be lesser people to command the confused enemy mages in the sky. After having success on their first attack on the enemy, Tanya and her battalion accelerated towards the RMS Queen of Anjou ship in order to hit it with multiple shots and withdraw afterwards.

After breaking through the enemy vanguard, Tanya gives orders for her company to break, penetrate the RMS Queen of Anjou in squads and withdraw after each shot. Tanya's Northeast Front Plan/Strategy

Ok, Now it's time for the context, in this specific situation, Tanya lost 10 members from her battalion, in other words, an entire company of soldiers that were soon substituted by a bunch of newbie mages, not only this, but Tanya has a completely new Kampfgruppe filled with non-elite soldiers that she considers to be a bunch of incompetents that should be shot dead. The enemy Tanya is facing are two entire brigades of Federation infantry and the commander of the infantry group of her Kampfgruppe went to a scout without asking for Tanya's permission, which means that the infantry group doesn't have any commander anymore. Another thing that makes everything even more complicated is that Tanya wants to keep the losses of her Kampfgruppe in this battle as low as possible since they are already suffering from a low number of elite soldiers. Not only this, but they are literally in war where the enemy won't hesitate to kill them if they receive the chance to do so.

Already anticipating that the enemies would launch a large-scale attack against the headquarters based on the quantity of soldiers in order to outnumber Tanya's soldiers, Tanya gave orders beforehand for Major Weiss to make a patrol alongside a group of other soldiers in the middle of the night in order to gather info about the overall size of the enemy forces and from which direction exactly they are coming. She already knew that the enemy was going to launch a large-scale attack on the headquarters, but she didn't know exactly when such a thing would occur and how many soldiers they would send exactly, that's why she sent Weiss to make a patrol every single day during the night which is the time where the Kampfgruppe normally would be resting on low-guard, with this, she would be capable of avoiding an sneaky attack from the enemy during the night while the Kampfgruppe is on low-guard, if the enemy attacked during the day, then Tanya's Kampfgruppe wouldn't have any problem to eliminate them, but she recognized that she would have problems if the enemy attacked during the night and that's why she did this, she also sent Weiss specifically to this patrol mission due to recognizing him as being her most competent soldier military-wise so she knew that he would be capable of making a good job due to his high experience in military combat.

After some time, Tanya receives the report made by Weiss that there were two brigades of Federation infantry coming to attack the headquarters which confirmed her prediction. After hearing this, Tanya asks for Visha, who gave these reports to her, to ask for Weiss to have the forward patrol guards retreat from the enemy since they'll be easily swallowed by the large number of enemies. She recognizes that in their current situation keeping losses at a minimum is the best decision for her, that's why she avoids leading her own troops to an unnecessary dangerous situation. But such a thing wasn't needed since Weiss already made the decision of retreating with the patrol group by himself. Tanya then gives orders for Visha to warn the commanders at every rank to know about the presence of enemies in order to make sure that the newbies of the Kampfgruppe won't accidentally shoot the patrol units in a rush to hit the enemy due to being in an unexpected situation. Not only this, but she also gives orders for Visha to reunite all the officers of the Kampfgruppe in order to make sure they'll understand the situation and what they are supposed to do while also being capable of clearing out any questions or confusion they might have.

After gathering all the officers of the Kampfgruppe in the headquarters, Tanya gives orders for Visha to explain their entire situation to everyone by using the map of the battlefield since she's really good at explaining things efficiently and because she has a great understanding of Tanya's intentions, something that Tanya herself comprehends. After finishing explaining the situation to the officers and making sure that they comprehend the situation, Tanya receives a report from Visha that was made by Weiss asking to take a battalion of aerial mages and conduct a delaying attack on the enemy infantry, something that Tanya allows, but give orders for Visha to tell him to slow the enemy down and thin them out in order to give time for everyone in the Kampfgruppe to prepare themselves efficiently, she also give orders for Visha to make sure that he will limit the losses of the battalion the best he can since Tanya's Kampfgruppe is already suffering with a lack of good trained soldiers, if Weiss's attack on the enemy ends up resulting on some soldiers of this battalion dying, then things will become even more complicated for Tanya since they'll have to train another bunch of newbies to implement on the Kampfgruppe. She wants to guarantee that the amount of losses her Kampfgruppe will suffer in this battle is as low as possible since she doesn't want to lose any more of her efficient elite soldiers that she trained with a lot of effort during the events of the first Volume.

With this, Weiss is going to attack the enemy and slow them down in order to give time for Tanya's Kampfgruppe to prepare themselves while playing safe since limiting losses is a top priority given to him by Tanya herself. Tanya understands that in war one must hit the enemy in their weak points first, and although Anti-surface attacks by mage units are usually markedly less effective at night which is their current situation, veterans like Weiss knows how to be capable of hitting the enemy weak points even from the sky, his skills and achievements are worth believing in and that's why Tanya allowed for him to attack the enemy. What Tanya plans to do here is use Weiss as a way to slow down the enemy while lowering their numbers, use the infantry if the enemy manages to continue advancing despite the continuous attacks and then use armored units like Tanks to finish everyone off and guarantee that her Kampfgruppe will triumph in this battle.

But before the beginning of the battle against the two enemy brigades, Tanya noticed that Captain Thon was missing in the meeting, so she asked for Lieutenant Tospan, his subordinate, to use the phone lines in order to call up Captain Thon’s command and ask a report on their status in order to confirm that everything is under control. After continuing to apply pressure on Lieutenant Tospan regarding the whereabouts of Captain Thon, she finally received an explanation as to why he wasn't in the meeting, he literally left his post and went out scouting without asking permission for Tanya beforehand. Now Tanya is in a tough situation since her plan requires the infantry unit to be ready to respond against the enemy approaching.

(Leadership, Cunning, Social skills, Short-term Planning, Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Anticipation, Information Gathering, Direct manipulation, Mass manipulation)

Facing this situation, Tanya doesn't have any other choice other than making damage control and put Lieutenant Grantz, who has relatively extensive experience in defensive lines, to take control of Captain Thon's infantry company and take Captain Thon's subordinate with him, she also tells him to do the infantry fight, defend, not fall back and repel the enemy, despite not having envisioned that this day would come, she still drummed Grantz full of trench-war and defensive-combat experience on the Rhine Front which turned out to be useful now. Here she demonstrates impressive adaptability, problem solving, improvisation and resource management in such a completely unexpected situation. After this, Tanya explains the full situation they are facing and what's going to occur, basically, they are going to be surrounded for sure by the enemy, but the thing is that the enemy is only capable of surrounding Tanya's Kampfgruppe by spreading themselves very thin because of the defensive perimeter, this means that the enemy's first attack won't last for an extended amount of time. With this, Tanya anticipates that the first attack for the enemy won't last for too long. Tanya is going to watch the enemies start running out of breath and then send in the armored units to eliminate everyone since they are literally made to do a single decisive strike on the enemy. The fact that they are on a defensive perimeter makes things easier for Tanya since it forces the enemy into spreading themselves, limiting their overall destructive potency and making it easier to eliminate them one by one, there's also the fact that officers and noncommissioned officers can keep tabs on their subordinates in close coordination while being in a defensive perimeter, in other words, when keeping the newbies of the Kampfgruppe disciplined, the person leading them will be right nearby to guarantee that everything is working normally while correcting any mistakes that might cause problems for the Kampfgruppe in the battle against the enemy infantry.

Not only this, but it's night at the moment which is extremely bad for the visibility of an enemy infantry, this will make things even harder for the enemy infantry since they'll have difficulty shooting Tanya's Kampfgruppe in this battle. In other words, as long as there isn't any attack with artillery or aerial support, Tanya's battalion will successfully take care of it without any problem thanks to the preparations she already made beforehand and their current environment, this was all things she already prepared beforehand due to already foreseeing days before these events that the enemy would launch an attack based on quantity and not quality, so they would launch an attack with a bunch of infantry soldiers but zero aerial forces.

In other words, Tanya's plan to defeat the two brigades of enemy infantry is completely perfect and foolproof, something that Tanya herself acknowledged. This is literally the standard method of defense that has been established in the east where you can only defend a single point if you hole up at one point, but if you can't build a series of defense lines, all you can do is protect yourself. Tanya is going to break down the enemy attack by having the Kampfgruppe infantry unit launch into the holes of the defense lines until the enemy can't take much more and decides to retreat in order to prepare for the second attack against the Kampfgruppe Headquarters. The main use of the defensive lines is literally to guarantee that Tanya's Kampfgruppe won't crumble first, she also says that the Kampfgruppe should keep a tight hold on the reins so the newbies defend their positions. Everything Tanya's Kampfgruppe has to do to complete their positional defense is make it through the enemy's first attack and succeed in countering them in order to force the enemy into abandoning their original plan of attacking a second time.

After making everything clear for her subordinates, Tanya gives orders for Captain Ahrens to keep his unit inside the defense lines and save their punch for later since his unit is filled with reserves and Tanya's Kampfgruppe won't need defense support in this battle. Due to them not being needed in this battle due to the Kampfgruppe not defense needing support, they will be the ones controlling the Armored Forces, Tanya doesn't have to worry about them causing any problem since the enemy infantry won't have any efficient way to destroy the tanks even if Captain Ahrens unit somehow ends up ruining everything and doing something stupid, which has a really low probability of occurring since Captain Ahrens unit are the most experienced in using tanks of the entire Kampfgruppe. Not only this, but having them controlling the Armored Forces is the best decision Tanya can make since they are the weakest unit in the Kampfgruppe due to being mere reserves, they aren't as skilled as the other soldiers, that's why Tanya decided that they should control the tanks in order to allow for the best units in the Kampfgruppe to attack the enemy in the defensive lines while Captain Ahrens armored forces wait until their time to be used as Tanya's trump card in this battle comes, the fact that Captain Ahrens unit is the most skilled one in terms of using Tanks in battlefield also contributed for this decision made by Tanya.

Tanya is certain that her plan will work perfectly without any problems since her Kampfgruppe is literally composed of artillery, infantry and mages, the golden combo that protected the Rhine Front lines from the Republican Army, and they are right now fighting against mere two brigades of enemy infantry, so she can easily anticipate that the defensive lines won't break from the enemy attack. As if the golden combo wasn't already enough to ensure victory on Tanya's side, she also has her trump card to make a counterattack on the enemy, that being the armored forces controlled by Captain Ahrens that will be used to eliminate all the enemy soldiers while they retreat to prepare themselves for the second attack. The aerial mage battalion will cause as much damage and destruction as possible on the enemy, the infantry will eliminate the remaining enemy soldiers from the defensive lines while using the veil of the night on their advantage and they'll have the armored forces as the appropriate troops to counterattack and fortify the foothold.

If the situation looks bad in the battle against the enemy which has a really low probability of occuring due to the clear advantage that Tanya's Kampfgruppe has in this battle due to her preparing her Kampfgruppe for this battle, Tanya will also send Captain Ahrens unit of infantry reserve soldiers who will be controlling the Armored Forces like Tanks that will be waiting on the defensive lines and wouldn't have suffered from the fatigue caused by the battle on both sides, in other words, they would be in perfect shape to attack in case of an unexpected situation and lower the enemy numbers in order to finally use the armored units as the ace up on her shelves, the fact that the enemy infantry would be tired and fatigued after this battle would make things easier for Captain Ahrens to destroy everyone since the enemy normally wouldn't have a chance to destroy the tanks and while being fatigued, their chances of destroying Captain Ahrens tanks would be way lower. This means that although Tanya plans to use the armored forces unit as a way to make a big counterattack on the enemy to push them back to their territory and lower the overall amount of soldiers that the Federation has with them, she doesn't have any problem with sending them earlier in case things don't go according to her plan, which means that the armored forces are her trump card in order to ensure victory in both of these two different outcomes.

(FRI=[Problem Solving], Fluid intelligence, Strategy, Short-term Planning, Methods, Tactics, Leadership, Cunning, Social skills, Resource Management, Anticipation, Prediction, Deductive reasoning, critical thinking, Holistic thinking, Connecting the dots, adaptability, improvisation, problem solving)

After finishing clearing all the officer's doubts about their orders in the battle against the two enemy brigades of Infantry. Tanya gives orders for all units to get to their positions on the double and get going with their defensive battle orders. While all this occurs, Visha keeps direct contact with Major Weiss by using the wireless in order to gain information regarding the situation outside of the Headquarters constantly so she can quickly adapt her plan in case something out of her predictions occur. All this so that Tanya can adapt her plans in case of unforeseen new circumstances in the battlefield.

Tanya then receives information from Major Weiss that he judged that observed artillery by an aerial mage is possible now that they are far enough from the enemy and Tanya immediately allows for Captain Meybert, who controls the artillery group, to receive precise instructions from Weiss regarding the enemy positions in order to shoot at the enemy with the artillery fire, basically, Weiss will tell them the position of the enemy and details about their location, the artillery will use this information to shoot at the expected position of the enemy and Weiss alongside his patrol team will confirm the results of the artillery attack on the enemy, the fact that there aren't any Federation mages means that they don't have to worry about being obstructed by the enemy, all of this will lower even more the number of soldiers from the enemy side. Of course, the veil of the night will make things difficult since it obstructs the view of the artillery and spotters, but this isn't a problem at all since Captain Meybert's artillery unit was trained for night artillery battles on the Rhine and Major Weiss is an extremely experienced soldier so such a thing wouldn't be a big problem for him, which means that the veil of the night won't be as disadvantageous for the enemy as it is for both Weiss and Captain Meybert's artillery unit.

She allows for Captain Meybert to attack the enemy soldiers with artillery attacks while receiving visual support from Weiss in the middle of the battlefield, but also gives direct orders for him to make sure that he sticks to the artillery ammunition allotment since they need to be frugal with their shells, another one of the reasons for this being because it's dark and visibility is terrible, so using all the artillery shells without being certain that they would hit everything wouldn't be worth it considering how low they are in artillery shells. Now, Tanya has Major Weiss and the patrol unit giving information about the location of the enemies to infantry and giving detailed reports about the results, this means that from the time the two enemy brigades of infantry manages to reach the Kampfgruppe headquarters to fight against Tanya's infantry, their number of soldiers will be way lower than it was before coming to the battlefield thanks to the observed fire of the artillery and Tanya will easily surpass the problem of being outnumbered by the enemy infantry. When Captain Meybert starts to give orders for the artillery to shoot, Tanya perceives that the sound produced by the shots was in an extremely regular interval, she hears that the sound of the artillery shots were being produced in an sporadic way which demonstrated that Meybert was following her orders of being careful with shells.

After some time, the enemy reaches the headquarters and exactly according to Tanya's plan, they are being shot by the Kampfgruppe infantry that is in an advantageous position due to being in defensive lines and due to having support of the artillery while shooting on the enemy infantry, not only this, but the enemy soldiers were attacked previously by Weiss battalion which should have caused some fatigue and tiredness on them, which would make their performance in the battlefield less effective than usual and would increase the number of enemy losses caused by Captain Meybert's artillery unit attacks. But in the middle of the night, it's extremely difficult to find the enemy's location, so how did the enemy soldiers find the Kampfgruppe headquarters? Simple, the artillery fire, Tanya purposely used the artillery fire to expose their position in the middle of the night in order to attract the enemy towards Tanya's trap, she did this while already anticipating that the enemy would find them using the artillery fire. Of course, no one would logically think that the enemy would use artillery fire to purposely expose their location, so the enemy soldiers would simply think that Tanya's Kampfgruppe revealed their position by firing blind without knowing that this is exactly what Tanya wanted for them to think.

Suddenly, during the defensive combat of the Kampfgruppe infantry unit commanded by Grantz, Tanya receives a phone call from Grantz himself who reveals that the enemy attacks are sporadic and suspects that it even looks like their aim is to keep them pinned down on the defensive line as a distraction. Having heard this, Tanya hypothesized the presence of more enemy soldiers coming to attack them so she asked Weiss to confirm if there are any new enemy soldiers coming to attack the Kampfgruppe headquarters. After this, Tanya asks for Visha to prepare a strong coffee for Tanya so she can clear her mind, after Visha finished preparing the coffee, Tanya purposely talked loud that the fact that the commander and adjutant of the Kampfgruppe were chatting about coffee was proof that the battle was going on their favor, the reason as to why she does this is to indirectly increase the motivation of the soldiers since it's really important for a commander to appear composed during the middle of a crisis, but of course this is merely Tanya's acting skills speaking, she is completely stressed by this situation as a whole. Demonstrating to be perfectly composed would also improve the performance of the soldiers in the Kampfgruppe since they wouldn't feel like they are in a crisis due to how composed Tanya is, in other words, the stress caused by nervousness wouldn't affect them and they would efficiently follow Tanya's orders without any problem due to believing that everything is under control.

After this, Tanya starts to think about all the different pieces of information regarding the enemy's true aim. From Grantz Report, the infantry unit isn't feeling much pressure from the enemy who appear to attack sporadically, Grantz is a veteran of war so he wouldn't be confused about the force of the enemy, which means that he's become numb to fear and simply can't sense the pressure? Of course not, he's not so valiant as that. Grantz is essentially a person that doesn't approve of war exactly like Tanya, so she can't possibly imagine him being so valiant to the point he became numb to fear. This means that his observation about the enemy attacks is correct. The fact that the infantry unit isn't feeling the pressure implies that they are fighting against an enemy with less than two brigades? No, Weiss wouldn't miscount the number of enemies considering that he's the most experienced soldier on the Kampfgruppe excluding Tanya herself.

Maybe the reason for all this is because the enemy is gathering to take advantage of a weak point in our defensive line? Maybe they are going to tweak and launch an all-out attack once they get an idea of what our lines are like? Tanya continues to think about the enemy's goal, but it doesn't matter how much she tries to think about the answer to the enemy's sporadic attacks, she couldn't find a logical reason, she continued to think but the more she tries to think about it, the more stressed she becomes and she still manages her emotions to not allow her subordinates to notice that she was tense about that whole situation. After some time, it's revealed by Grantz that Tanya's plan was successful and the enemy soldiers' attack was petering out after suffering too many losses in battle.

(Musical intelligence, Psychological Warfare, Psychological profiling, Cunning, Inter EE, Intra EM, Social skills, Leadership, Strategy, Short-term Planning, Methods, Tactics, Analysis, Critical thinking, Holistic thinking, Divergent thinking, Linear thinking, Trap Setting, Anticipation, Indirect manipulation, mass manipulation, emotional manipulation, Gaslighting, Misdirection, acting skills, Non-Verbal deception)

Despite having survived the enemy first attack without any casualties, Tanya still wanted to know about the enemy's true aim so she asked for Visha to gather information about the enemy and she managed to figure out some things about them, the enemies weren't brigades, they were the shells of a brigade, the Federation soldiers attacking the Headquarters are new recruits and it seems like they are concentrating their forces within reach of the commanders’ voices. After hearing this, Tanya abducts that the enemy is operating in tight ranks and that the reason as to why Grantz wasn't having difficulty fighting against them was because Meybert's artillery unit eliminated the majority of the enemy forces. After confirming with Visha that the enemy was simply a bunch of new recruits, Tanya asks for Visha to change her previous observation mission with the mission to take control of the zone while Weiss will continue his patrol on the forward patrol line in order to eliminate any remaining enemy soldier he might find during patrol. Tanya recognizes that doing such a thing will be a good experience for Visha which will allow for her to improve in battle. Not only this, but the enemy doesn't have any anti-air weapons or aerial mages to use to take Visha down, so Tanya doesn't even have to worry about the possibility of her subordinate being eliminated by the enemy forces. For mages, dodging the bullets of infantry units is extremely easy and Visha can also count with the help of her defense shell to protect herself from the enemy shots.

After this, Tanya finally takes the phone in order to talk with Captain Ahrens who is the one commanding the armored forces, the unit supposed to give the final decisive strike on the enemy to finish them off, Tanya makes a phone call to Captain Ahrens and give orders for him to obliterate the remaining enemy soldiers that were blown away in Captain Meybert’s barrage of artillery. Tanya recognizes that now is the best moment to completely unleash the power of the armored forces for three specific reasons: the enemies are still confused, they are a bunch of newbies and are low on number of soldiers at the moment, she can't allow for them to gather with another group of enemy soldiers to attack again or escape back to the Federation borders, so she give orders for the armored forces to completely eliminate the enemy. Tanya also gives orders for Captain Ahrens to count with Visha's observation support in order to guide the armored forces to the position of the enemy.

As already said before, Visha is flying in the sky and the enemy can't do anything about it since they don't have any anti-air weapons or aerial mages, so Visha's life won't be at risk since the enemy won't have any way to shoot her down. In fact, the enemies shooting at Visha would reveal their position to Captain Ahrens armored forces unit which will give a chance for Visha to take some distance temporarily to allow for Captain Ahrens to eliminate the enemy infantry. With this, Tanya will have Captain Ahrens armored forces make the counterattack against the extremely weakened enemy soldiers who suffered multiple losses thanks to Meybert’s artillery unit attacks, after this, the infantry will come to eliminate the remaining soldiers that were left behind and the artillery will also give support in the complete extermination of the enemy soldiers. After some time, Tanya notices that Captain Thon still didn't appeared so she asked for Grantz to check Thon’s location with Lieutenant Tospan, who is his subordinate, Grantz asks if he should put together a search party but Tanya almost immediately refuses this idea since they are still in combat, so taking part of her precious fighting force out of the game at a time like this only to search for a single inept commander wasn't worth it.

After this, Tanya give orders for Grantz to prepare for the enemy's counter or an attack from another unit they scrape together, the reason for this being because she doesn't want to be taken by a surprise attack coming from the enemy and because she already anticipated that the enemy wouldn't give up on attacking the Kampfgruppe headquarters after a single attack, they'll return to attack once again. In fact, the whole reason as to why she wants to eliminate all the enemy infantry soldiers as fast as possible is to indirectly induce fear in the enemy and make them abandon the idea of returning to attack again. After giving orders for Grantz to prepare himself for an enemy counter, Tanya gives orders for Grantz to also participate in the counterattack of the armoured forces by leading the Infantry units, she does this in order to guarantee that they will be capable of eliminating the enemy forces completely and destroy their will to continue fighting against the Kampfgruppe with their second attack.

Of course, she didn't send him to the battlefield alongside the infantry unit, instead he will be managing and leading the members of the infantry unit from the headquarters of the Kampfgruppe in a safe position where he doesn't need to worry about being targeted by the enemy, the reason for this being because Tanya recognizes the importance of being commanded by a competent commander, following orders of the superior and do one's job is really simple, there's nothing complicated about it, but having to fight against the enemy without receiving orders from a commander or support will just result on the infantry unit of the Kampfgruppe falling in confusion, that's why Tanya keeps Grantz commanding the infantry unit at a safe position in order to guarantee that he will be capable of commanding them without his life being at risk.

After this, Captain Ahrens Armored Forces Unit manages to break through the enemy, something that Tanya already had anticipated that would occur but ended up occurring way sooner than she expected. During all the events of Tanya's plan, she observes a map that is updated every single second based on the information given by those observing what's occurring in the battlefield, Tanya uses this map to visualize the position of the enemies, the position of her soldiers and the entire Northeast side of the Eastern Front(which is the place where the battle is taking place), even if she uses a map to do such a thing which kind of debuffs this feat a lot, it is still quite impressive. The utility for this map being extremely obvious, it is necessary for Tanya to manage and adapt her plans in case something unexpected occurs, by using the map, she might even use the environment at her favor while having a clear advantage against the enemy due to knowing the entire battlefield thanks to the map. After noticing how much of an efficient soldier Captain Ahrens was, Tanya asks for one of her subordinates to send a message to him saying for Captain Ahrens to not do anything unreasonable and say that she expects great things coming from him. She recognized that he was a really capable commander and sent this message to avoid the possibility of him doing something dangerous and end up dying since Tanya doesn't want to lose such an efficient commander for an stupid reason.

After this, Tanya tells her subordinates to tell Lieutenant Grantz to send his infantry troops to the battlefield, a company of infantry troops that will be under his command, she also sends Lieutenant Wustemann’s mage unit to go with the infantry company in order to eliminate any soldier that dares to approach them in a sneak attack, with this, she will be capable of keeping the Kampfgruppe’s infantry unit losses at a minimum. The reason as to why she only sent the infantry company now is due to the fact that Captain Ahrens armored forces unit managed to open a gap in the enemy infantry, now that there's a gap in the enemy infantry, Tanya will send her soldiers to flood the gap completely and eliminate everyone without giving any chance for them to resist, not only this, but the infantry units are the ones that are most propitious to suffer losses since they essentially have almost zero ways to defend themselves from an enemy attack, they can't or have defense shells like mages, they don't have a strong armored vehicle like the armored forces, they have to worry about aerial attacks from mages or Fighter planes differently from the Navy whose ships are extremely durable and capable of enduring aerial attacks, and they can't fly to have an aerial advantage against the enemy like the fighter planes, compared to the other units, the Infantry unit is extremely fragile and has higher chances of suffering losses caused by the enemies, that's why she only sent them to the battlefield after she was sure that the situation was on her favor and even sent an entire company of aerial mages with them in order to guarantee with absolute certainty that no one from the infantry unit would die.

After some time, the fighter planes Fleet command dispatched to support Tanya's Kampfgruppe in the battlefield in order to ensure victory, normally they would have a really hard time fighting against the enemies if they were in the front lines due to the enemy actually using aerial mages and fighter planes in the front lines, but in the northeast, where the enemy only focuses on quantity and zero quality, imperial air superiority is completely unwavering with all enemy soldiers being completely eradicated. With this plan, Tanya also managed to prove to the General staff the flexibility of the Kampfgruppe formation and operation, with this, she fulfills her original goal of proving to the General staff the strength of a Kampfgruppe once again and guarantee that their evaluation of her won't go down, which means that she won't be overworked from now on and the General staff will still have her in high regard compared to other imperial soldiers. This entire Plan occurred in a single day which means that this is also a really good demonstration of a Short-term Planning feat shown by Tanya and all the events written here, except those written in the white color, were planned by Tanya beforehand and she simply had to execute the plan that she already had prepared beforehand.

One thing that I want to make clear about this plan is that the execution of the plan IS a strategy, but all the elements of preparation process made by Tanya and the actual progress of the plan are planning feats. In other words, Tanya actually leading her troops and executing the plan is the strategy part of this plan, while the preparation process, the plan as a whole and the progress of the plan is the Planning part.

Here she demonstrates impressive Bodily-Kinesthetic Intelligence by understanding the effects of sleepiness on her childish body.

Tanya comes to the conclusion that winter came sooner than usual, she understands all these strange things that she perceived could only occur if it was already winter. Demonstrating for the first time a Naturalistic intelligence feat.
