Sixth Volume

In the beginning of the sixth Volume, Tanya asks for Visha to prepare a coffee for her using the government rations and surprisingly Visha manages to make a really well-made coffee, after this, it's clearly shown in the Light Novel that Tanya is capable of differentiating good and drinkable coffee from miserable coffee substitute or coffee made with poor-quality beans by merely smelling the fragrance of the coffee.

(Perception)

After some time, Grantz come to Tanya's office in order to talk with her about something important, before Grantz entered inside the office, Tanya heard the sound of footsteps and the rustling of someone brushing off their clothes coming from the door, when Grantz enter inside the office, Tanya starts to think about the sounds she heard. Right now, it's winter on the outside of the office where the soldiers are doing their business, before Grantz entered on her room, she heard the sound of him brushing off his clothes, so she hypothesizes that he was brushing the snow off the overcoat since it is little more than camouflage-esque sheets, if he came in without doing that, the coat would have gotten wet and heavy. About the sound of footsteps she heard, Tanya notices in Grantz’s complexion that he was nervously stamping his feet earlier before entering the office, the reason for this being because what he wants to talk with her involves a heavy report that he needs to give her.

(Observation, Cold reading, abductive reasoning, critical thinking, convergent thinking, Linear thinking)

While talking with her two subordinates, it's revealed in the LN by Tanya herself that she studied the rules of war on land so much in the empire war college that she practically memorized every single one of the rules of war. She's so confident in her expertise in this field that she gives orders to her subordinates based on the rules she memorized despite knowing that she can be sent to be judged by the National Court if she ends up breaking the war rules. This demonstrates Tanya's knowledge about international war laws

(WMI, Crystallized intelligence, Memory, Knowledge)

Suddenly after some time, the Federation makes a large-scale attack on the village where the council for self-government is located and Tanya must protect it in order to ensure that her strategy will work. But she can't avoid thinking about how strange all this is and starts to think about the Federation's actions.

P1: The enemy is making a large-scale attack on the Northeast side of the Eastern Front with a large amount of aerial mages.

P2: Right now, it's already winter and the cold is nature's fury that should affect both the Federation and the empire equally.

C1: To simplify everything, right now is not the moment to have troops on the move since the winter should have caused casualties on both sides.

P3: Independently of such a thing, the Federation still decided to send multiple aerial mages to attack the empire on a winter offensive.

P4: Such a thing is completely illogical and there's no apparent logical explanation for such a decision made by the Federation.

P5: In the current state of the war where both countries are weakened thanks to Winter, gathering information about how bad the situation is on the enemy side is a good tactic.

C2: Maybe this offensive is actually a Recon-in-force mission to gather information about how much the winter affected the empire.

P6: But I heard that the Federation didn't even have enough forces to mount a counterattack against the empire.

C3: So there's no way that they would make a Recon-in-force mission with such a large amount of soldiers despite suffering so many casualties thanks to winter.

P7: According to strategic objectives, Recon-in-force missions would automatically imply that the enemy is making preparations to a forthcoming operation.

C4: This means that it's possible that the Federation is preparing themselves to make a large operation against the empire.

C5: Naturally, that implies that the Federation has the forces necessary to launch a large-scale attack with what they learn from the recon-in-force mission.

P8: But a major offensive doesn't make sense unless you have a lot of forces to commit to the battlefield.

C6: This means that the Federation is claiming to the empire that they already have many soldiers ready for such an operation.

C7: This means that the Federation has recovered themselves from the casualties caused by winter faster than expected.

P9: It's been only a month or two since the Eastern front against the Federation stagnated thanks to winter.

P10: Even the Federation can't escape from the laws of physics, they still have a minimum of training and gear that wouldn't allow for them to recover so fast.

P11: Thinking logically, if they really had enough strategic reserves to mount a general counterattack they would have committed them to battle before their lines initially collapsed, otherwise, the Federation army General staff would have been letting a ton of soldiers just sit around twiddling their thumbs.

C8: Which means that the Federation army doesn't have enough strategic reserves to prepare for a large operation.

P12: First of all, is this intended to be a recon-in-force mission made by the Federation army or not?

P13: If such a thing was really supposed to be the prelude to a major offensive, everything, including the wireless would be bristling with energy due to how intense such recon-in-force missions tend to be.

P14: But as far as Tanya can perceive, the wireless and the situation close to the area the enemy is attacking is almost peaceful and quiet without any signs of energy that recon-in-force missions normally show.

C9: This means that this attack made by the enemy isn't a recon-in-force mission made with the purpose to prepare for a major operation.

P15: If this attack made by the enemy isn't a recon-in-force mission, then this means that they are attacking with the intention of actually advancing on the empire's territory.

C10: This means that there is the possibility that this is actually a Full-Scale attack made by the Federation army against the empire.

P16: But if this was really a full-scale offensive against the empire, the headquarters and Frontline control would have been screaming for a while now, but there wasn't any information about such a thing coming from them.

C11: This means that whatever the Russy Federation is doing, definitely isn't a full-scale attack against the empire.

P17: This means that what the enemy is doing here is neither a full-scale attack nor a Recon-in-force mission in order to make preparations for a major offensive.

C12: This means that this offensive made by the Federation is probably a limited offensive with their intentions being Recon-in-force.

P18: If this is a Russy Federation limited offensive with Recon-in-force intention, then what's the enemy's goal with this?

P19: Right now, both the empire and the Federation are extremely weakened, but they don't know for sure how much each side was affected by winter.

C13: So it's possible that the Federation launched this attack to prove the imperial defensive lines in the Eastern Front and figure out how much they were affected by winter.

P20: But what made the Russy Federation think that doing such a risky thing was worth the risk?

P21: It's the same as my original question about if the enemy has enough troops to perform a recon-in-force mission.

P22: If not, they're throwing away human lives in vain and even if it's the Federation it's hard to imagine such utter wastefulness is permitted.

P23: Of course, if they decide not to attack as a result of the recon-in-force mission, they can keep sacrifices to a minimum, if by chance a few of the many can be protected, every army would celebrate any kind of tragedy.

P24: But such a thing is still a waste for the Federation army and in their current situation, wasting resources like this is not the kind of thing that the army would allow.

C14: This means that the most likely alternative for the Federation's intentions is a harassment attack, they are simply screwing with us.

P25: Harassment attack is a classic tactic used in war that will tire the enemy out which is really useful in the current situation of the war where the empire is weakened thanks to winter.

P26: The problem is that the empire already confirmed that the Federation mobilized a force that's at least brigade strength.

C15: That's too large of a troop commitment for a mere harassment attack, which means that the enemy's true aim is something else.

Final Conclusion: Currently the enemy's aim with this plan is completely unknown for Tanya due to lack of information, but she's completely certain that it isn't a harassment attack, a recon-in-force mission serving as preparation for a major offensive, or a full-scale attack made against the empire.

(Connecting The Dots, Observation, Abductive reasoning, Deductive reasoning, critical thinking, Holistic thinking, convergent thinking, Linear thinking)

After some time have passed, multiple enemy mages make an air raid on the Norden area of the previous territory of the Entente Alliance that was annexed by the empire, Tanya and her Kampfgruppe receive the information about the enemy attack while being in the Eastern Front and she starts to think about this invasion from the enemy:

P1: Right after receiving the information from the Norden that it was going to be attacked by enemy aerial mages, Tanya notices that the enemy is making an infiltration via submarine on the Norden.

P2: The enemy is right now making an air raid against the Norden where the Entente alliance territory is located.

P3: The method that the empire used to attack the Entente Alliance was to use submarines in order to infiltrate the land in an amphibious operation.

C1: There's a chance that the Federation soldiers are mimicking what the empire did with the Entente Alliance in the past.

P4: Right now the imperial army has essentially no anti submarine capabilities, the main forces of the High Seas Fleet have a unit of destroyers as escorts, and they do about as good a job as can be expected.

P5: But there is no reason for the imperial Navy to surrender their precious and extremely useful babies to a fight on the Norden.

P6: They had their hands full securing the waters between the mainland of the Empire and Norden.

C2: This means that the empire or the people in the Norden area can't expect help coming from the High Seas Fleet.

P7: The air fleet and submarines deployed in the northern waters were on a mission to destroy the enemy's sea lane.

P8: This means that at the moment they don't have many resources with them to use against the aerial mages in Norden.

C3: This also means that expecting them to patrol against the enemy submarines was too much to hope for from them.

P9: Right now, the reports are extremely confusing to the point where understanding what was happening was difficult.

P10: Such a thing would only occur if the people in Norden were desperately trying to pass this information to all soldiers due to a crisis.

C4: There's a good chance that this report made by the people in Norden is not a mistake but the truth.

P11: After this, Tanya asks for records of partisans activities and one of her subordinates perceives that they don't have the partisans by the roots.

P12: He then questions if the fact that they don't have the partisans by the roots was a sign of a general uprising.

P13: Right now the enemy is spread out, but the empire has quite a few troops over the Norden guarding the territory.

C5: There's no way that the partisans could expect to rapidly capture the urban area of the Norden.

C6: This means that it's illogical to think that what is occurring in Norden is a general uprising of the partisans.

P14: The enemy mage units that dropped in Norden are mere commandos who have come to cause a disturbance.

C7: In other words, as long as they don't get reinforcements, holding the city center should be no problem for the empire's forces.

P15: As far as Tanya knows, commandos are guerrillas, in other words, they are the guys who are the best at the things people hate.

P16: The city is the area with the largest amount of people and resources for the enemy soldiers going to Norden.

C8: So it's obvious that they would target the city of the Entente Alliance in the Norden Area.

P17: Right now, the Northern army group division is prepared to protect against a landing on the coast.

P18: But the Garrisons are slow and it's difficult to know for sure if they can chase down units as highly mobile as mage units.

C9: Due to their slowness, the best thing the Garrisons can do is defend a point from the enemy attack.

P19: It's questionable whether a second-rate division will even be able to hold the line against the enemy.

P20: In other words, what the empire must do to counter the enemy aerial attack on the Norden is less about holding a position than searching and destroying.

C10: Which is exactly why the empire will call Tanya's Kampfgruppe to defend the Imperial army territory on Norden since they are the empire's strongest military group and are the best in searching and destroying.

FINAL CONCLUSION: Right now the Federation is making a large-scale infiltration on the annexed territory of the Entente Alliance on Norden and Tanya's battalion will be called to help the local Garrison since the General staff probably already figured out that they wouldn't be capable of stopping the enemy attack.

It's implied that she figured all this out in a single minute after reading the information about the enemy attack on Norden. With this, we come to an end of Tanya's feats in the sixth Volume.

(Observation, Deductive reasoning, abductive reasoning, Inductive reasoning, critical thinking, Holistic thinking, Linear thinking, Fast thinking, convergent thinking, Connecting the dots, pattern recognition)

Seventh Volume

In the very beginning of the Light Novel, Tanya's battalion flies above a group of multiple Federation soldiers and Tanya notices that her battalion is clearly outnumbered, she then remembers the heavy losses her battalion suffered in the Northern Sea during the events of the RMS Queen of Anjou strategy, in order to avoid any heavy losses similar to the one that occurred in the Northern Sea, Tanya decides to once again use the type 95 computation Orb despite already having suffered its effects multiple times already through the story, with this, Tanya already suffered the effects of the Type 95 Cursed Orb 8 times throughout the entire Light Novel so far. The curse of the Type 95 Computation Orb drains all energy from the user's body which causes an insane amount of pain equal to having one's own vital energy drained, this pain is converted in religious ecstasy and the amount of agony caused by the combination between the religious ecstasy and the pain Tanya feels was stated to be an horror beyond any description, which means that the amount of mental pain Tanya suffered can't be compared with any other pain ever felt by a human being, which means that things like death by burning alive and torture doesn't even come close to the agony caused by the Type 95 computation Orb's Curse. Anyway, after using the Type 95 computation Orb for the eighth time already in the story, Tanya still managed to keep her sanity without showing any apparent negative effect.

(Intra EM, Intra EE)

During the events of the Seventh Volume, it's revealed that all of Tanya's achievements and feats in battle would be attributed to Colonel Von Lergen in order to create the false idea that he's an extremely good commander. Unexpectedly, Tanya accepted such a thing, the reason for this being because she now has someone she can nominally shove off all her accountability onto, and on top of that, the guys making decisions in Personnel will owe her one. She doesn't really mind about being promoted or receiving medals as recognition for her achievements so this is perfect for Tanya. Anyone not morbidly obsessed with being in the spotlight like Tanya would be thrilled.

(Decision Making)

During the events of the Seventh Volume, Colonel Von Lergen informs Tanya that due to a decision made by the Higher-ups, he will now become the commander of a military unit superior to the Salamander Kampfgruppe named “Lergen Kampfgruppe”, but in fact, this Lergen Kampfgruppe won’t even exist at all, it's going to be an inexistent military group that is actually the Salamander Kampfgruppe. The Lergen Kampfgruppe is actually the Salamander Kampfgruppe but with a different name, military reports and some information about the Salamander Kampfgruppe will be twisted in order to misdirect the enemy into thinking that the Lergen Kampfgruppe actually exists despite it merely being a fabrication by the empire and Tanya. Multiple false info will be spread to the enemy countries in the war and everyone will think that the Lergen Kampfgruppe is an actual military group that is even above the Salamander Kampfgruppe when it is simply the Salamander Kampfgruppe.

Explaining it in a better way, the Salamander Kampfgruppe will continue to exist, but many of its achievements will be associated with the inexistent Lergen Kampfgruppe, with this, the empire will misdirect the entire world into thinking that Colonel Von Lergen(who is the supposed commander of the inexistent Lergen Kampfgruppe) is an extremely competent commander and a threat to the members of the Alliance against the empire. Tanya decided to collaborate with this farce and after the events of the sixth Volume, Tanya starts to make direct requests to the General staff with Lergen's name in order to make them think that the one making the request is really Colonel Von Lergen, all of Tanya's achievements and feats will be twisted in order to make it look like it was Lergen's feats and achievements. All of this to make the enemy countries fear Colonel Von Lergen and think that he is a threat to them in the war despite him literally not being even close to being a threat to them.

This is an impressive demonstration of Fabrication and mass deception feat from Tanya since this Fabrication extended for more than 40 years in the future of the events in the story and multiple countries like the Russy Federation, the Commonwealth, the François Republic, the Unified States and many more were deceived by this false information of the Lergen Kampfgruppe actually existing. What's more impressive here is that Tanya and the General staff managed to misdirect even the Russy Federation that was stated to have almost perfect information gathering skills. This means that Tanya and the General staff managed to deceive hundreds of millions of people(literally more than half a billion people only counting with the Russy Federation's population and the Unified States’ population) into thinking that Lergen was an extremely competent commander that led the empire's greatest military group. Tanya’s strategy to counter the Federation attack

(NGL, thinking about names for Tanya's strategies is extremely difficult since she's literally a strategy spammer…)

For some context about what's the situation here, the Russy Federation launched an offensive against the empire while Tanya's Kampfgruppe was distracted solving the problems at Norden, the empire suffered multiple losses and Tanya received orders to perform a Recon-in-force mission. But something unexpected will occur, the enemy soldiers would be preparing a Large-scale attack on that place with Infantry, War tanks and artillery, she will have to fight against these enemy forces without having any time to prepare herself. Tanya's battalion can't expect to receive help from the Kampfgruppe since they went to a solo mission in the Eastern Front without the rest of the Kampfgruppe and a few members of her battalion, including Tanya herself, are suffering from hunger, which means that her battalion and Tanya herself will have to fight against the enemy forces while suffering with fatigue. Not only this, she doesn't have any information about the artillery division location and doesn't have any information about the enemy current status, which makes everything even more difficult, now we'll see how she solves this problem.

After receiving this mission, Tanya immediately regroups her battalion and goes to the location of the recon-in-force mission. While flying towards the location of the mission, Tanya gives orders for Weiss to split the battalion in three and get a handle of their situation on patrol duty. She wants for Weiss to locate the enemy artillery division since she understands that Artillery are like Gods in the battlefield, it is extremely useful in the war and causes massive destruction on the enemy, she recognizes how dangerous Artillery can be so she give orders for Weiss, her best soldier military-wise, to split the entire battalion in three and locate the artillery in order to destroy it as quickly as possible. Before going to this mission, Tanya's battalion was suffering with hunger including Tanya herself, luckily for her, the Colonel who gave the recon-in-force mission to her gave food to some of her soldiers, but there were still a few soldiers including Tanya herself that didn't even knew about such a thing and we're suffering with hunger. Noticing this, Tanya gives orders for the soldiers that already ate something to go to the patrol mission with Weiss while those who didn't eat anything would be there alongside Tanya waiting for Weiss to return with good or bad news.

During the events of this strategy, a harsh whoosh sound of a flying object pierced her ears and after this, she heard a loud rumble. By merely hearing these things, Tanya identified that this sound came from an enemy artillery shell next to them. Unfortunately for Tanya, they were found by the enemy artillery division and the enemy was shooting artillery shells at them. Noticing this, Tanya immediately warns her soldiers about the artillery shells coming in their direction and gives orders for her soldiers to find shelter on the trenches instead of climbing since the enemy got them pinned. She adapted to the situation in a matter of seconds and gave effective orders to keep her battalion's losses at a minimum. After some time, Tanya gives orders for her officers to rally their troops in order to get ready to counterattack and strike on the double. She understands that what the enemy is doing right now is a preparatory bombardment, she understands that the artillery is shooting at them in order to give time for the infantry to prepare themselves, so she anticipates that the enemy infantry is going to show up and give orders for her soldiers to advance in order to destroy the Artillery before enemy infantry appears since they would be in trouble if they had to fight against the enemy infantry and artillery at the same time. Demonstrating an impressive trap evasion and anticipation feat.

After noticing that the battalion was hesitant to go and attack the artillery, Tanya gives orders for Major Weiss to rally the battalion since he's the one in command of the battalion officially while Tanya is simply the one in command of the Kampfgruppe. She also asks if they have a line to the Colonel that gave food to the battalion and tells him to send a liaison if the line is dead. But unexpectedly for her, the headquarters they just left moments ago was now completely destroyed. She then connects the dots by remembering how the enemy attack was way too soon like they were already prepared, the fact that she considered strange how the lone house being used as a headquarters wasn't destroyed yet by the enemy and how the headquarters was now completely destroyed, she abducts that the enemy artillery already had their target zeroed in from the very beginning and the loan house had been left intact on purpose by the enemy to serve as a target for the artillery. After noticing this, Tanya gave orders for her battalion to assume that the Colonel is now dead and the command chain is unclear, so they should consider an specific spot in the battlefield pointed as being the headquarters temporarily, the reason for this being because Tanya can simply give orders for her battalion to “gather at the headquarters” and they would all go to the specific location specified by Tanya, such a thing would make things easier for them instead of going to what was previously the headquarters since it's way too far from the trenches that they are using to take cover from the artillery. This is also a good improvisation feat since Tanya simply improvised a new place as being their headquarters in the middle of the battlefield.

When Visha proposes for them to search the second in command of what was previously the headquarters, Tanya immediately refuses this idea since she understands that a handoff command with no proper meeting would throw everything into disarray and they can't lose time at the moment since every second counts in their current situation. While continuing to talk with Visha about how her idea wasn't good at their current situation, Tanya immediately shuts her mouth after noticing that the artillery fire has halted. She immediately understands what this means due to her experience in the Rhine Front and anticipates that her battalion was now going to be attacked by the enemy infantry. Luckily for Tanya, a few soldiers from the regiment of that place appeared and started to counterattack the enemy, but due to the fact that their headquarters were destroyed, they were doing a really poor job fighting against the enemy and the possibility of the entire regiment collapsing was now possible. Noticing this situation, Tanya takes command of the regiment of soldiers in order to lead them efficiently since she understands that being leaded by an inexperienced commander is better than not being leaded at all in the war. She never had to take command of such a large group of soldiers before, even her Kampfgruppe doesn't have an amount of soldiers equal to a regiment, not only this, but when she took control of her Kampfgruppe, she didn't had to lead them while being literally on the middle of the battlefield and while being under enemy fire. Despite such a thing, she immediately adapted herself socially to the position of regiment's commander despite literally receiving enemy fire and despite never having led such a large group of soldiers before.

(Musical intelligence, Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Social adaptability, Leadership, Social skills, adaptability, Connecting The Dots, improvisation, Anticipation, Trap Evasion, Observation, abductive reasoning, critical thinking)

After taking command of the Fifty-fourth Regiment, Tanya gives orders for them to go to their positions and prepare to intercept since the enemy is coming. After this, the soldiers of the regiment manage to overpower their confusion now that they are being led by Tanya, they take a defensive position in order to avoid unnecessary losses and prepare to shoot at the enemy. She also asks for the Fifty-fourth Regiment to bring the second-in-command so she can manage the regiment more efficiently, as already said before, Tanya has literally zero experience leading a regiment of soldiers so she wanted to receive help and advice from the second-in-command who must have at least the bare minimum of experience in doing such a thing. But unexpectedly for Tanya, one of the regiment soldiers didn't even know who was the second-in-command, which makes things even more difficult and stressful for Tanya since she'll have to lead the entire regiment without receiving help from anyone with experience in doing such a thing. Noticing how hopeless the situation was, Tanya give orders for the regiment to take cover back where the provisional headquarters used to be and explain what's occurring when a high-ranking appears, but after thinking for some time, she gave orders for the regiment to give the message to the high-ranking officer that Tanya's battalion assumed control of the regiment temporarily and that they should contact Tanya immediately after hearing this message.

Tanya understands that her 203rd aerial mage battalion is the strongest mage unit in the empire at the moment, she understands that any high-ranking officer that heard that they were in a tough situation would immediately try to help in order to avoid losses from the battalion. That's why she gave specific orders for the regiment to mention that the 203rd battalion needs help. But after thinking for some time, Tanya recognizes that coordinating anything with the regiment at their current state, so she decides to use the advantage of an aerial mage battalion's mobility in her favor and pile up a heap of individual victories. Now that the enemy infantry was on the battlefield too, the artillery would refrain from shooting at Tanya's battalion and end up hitting one of their own soldiers, in other words, they would only need to worry about the infantry soldiers in the battlefield. Tanya then calls her battalion and gives orders for them to support the soldiers of the Fifty-fourth Regiment since commanding them is completely impossible at the moment. While the battalion does such a thing, the other units of the regiment of soldiers will defend their positions from the enemy attack.

One of the things that made Tanya make such a decision is because giving support the soldiers and attacking the enemy with the battalion's mobility is the same job as always, the 203rd Battalion already made this exact same kind of mission multiple times before, they already have a lot of experience in doing such a thing, so she can expect that they wouldn't have much trouble giving support to the Fifty-fourth Regiment. After some time, Tanya's battalion starts to attack the enemy and give support to the Fifty-fourth Regiment of soldiers, but unexpectedly for Tanya, the soldiers of the Fifty-fourth Regiment started to run away from the enemy without any apparent reason, but she understands due to experience that the fact that the soldiers were retreating in a panic meant that they had a hole in their defensive lines. Now, the enemy forces were advancing and the Imperial soldiers were retreating, even if Tanya herself didn't want to accept that such a thing was occurring, it was obvious for her that they were being overwhelmed by the enemy. After noticing such a thing, Weiss asks if they should retreat to the second defensive line. But Tanya immediately refuses to do such a thing since a retreat at their current situation would be difficult. If they could move in an organized way, a withdrawal would
at least be a tactical option. It would be more logical than a futile attempt to maintain the crumbling defensive line. But she understands that doing an orderly retreat with the headquarters destroyed wasn't possible.

After thinking for some time, Tanya decides to give orders for her troops to prepare for an assault battle and give orders for Major Weiss to command the 203rd Battalion. While Weiss commands the 103rd Aerial mage battalion, Tanya will command the infantry soldiers of the Fifty-fourth Regiment, she also gives orders for Visha and her company to come with Tanya in order to guarantee that her plan will work. Tanya understands that winning that battle with her battalion alone wasn't going to be possible, that's why she tries her hardest to make the Fifty-fourth Regiment of infantry soldiers return back to battle. But despite screaming with them and asking for the infantry to calm down, they don't listen to anything she says. Noticing this, Tanya decides to appeal to the infantry’s fear by claiming that what they were doing was fleeing before the enemy, in war, fleeing before the enemy is practically the same as betraying one's own country, such a thing results in execution by death by shooting or other methods of execution. Tanya purposely says that what they are doing is fleeing before the enemy in order to force the soldiers into returning to fight against the enemy or be executed due to betraying their own country. She literally forced them into choosing a certain death situation(fleeing before the enemy) or an extremely dangerous situation with high chances of death, but still with chances of victory.

But despite such a thing, the Fifty-fourth Regiment of infantry soldiers still didn't return to battle. Noticing this, Tanya give orders for Visha's company to prepare to fire against the members of the imperial regiment of infantry, what she's trying to do here is bluffing by making the infantry soldiers think that she's going to eliminate them right now due to fleeing before the enemy while using her acting skills to misdirect the infantry soldiers into thinking that she actually plans to shoot at them, with this, she aims to induce fear on the Regiment of infantry soldiers by making them think that she's going to kill them in order to force them into returning to battle against their own will. After this, Tanya asks for one of the Lieutenants of the infantry soldiers to round up his men; she recognizes that he was a Lieutenant by analyzing his Rank Insignia, Age and Ribbon bars. Tanya then starts to say this exact thing to the Lieutenant: “Didn’t you rise through the ranks? Unless you’re a fool who’s just been letting the years go by with your thumb up your ass, do what you need to do. If you can’t, I’ll lay you to eternal rest right here!” The thing is that Tanya says such a thing not in an intimidating tone but in an encouraging one and a smile that almost seems like she's saying: “C’mon, you can do it”.

She understands that higher ranking soldiers are more composed and humorous than normal ones, that's why she tries to make the Lieutenant named Barchet become her ally and help in the battle since she understands that initiating a friendly talk with someone with a good sense of humor was considerably easy. After this, it's shown that Tanya's words of encouragement had an immediate effect and she managed to not only make him calm down, but she also managed to initiate a friendly talk with the Lieutenant Barchet despite having threatened to kill him a few seconds ago. After this, Tanya managed to persuade Lieutenant Barchet into calling his soldiers to help the 203rd Aerial mage battalion of soldiers and she could now count with their help in this battle against the Federation forces. Demonstrating impressive problem solving skills due to managing to solve the problem of the infantry soldiers refusing to fight against the enemy.

Now that Tanya has the infantry of the Fifty-fourth Regiment back at her side, even if she detach some mages to support defense after they take back the western position, it won't need to be that many, if she does it right and get the people of the infantry organized, she might be able to cover most of the flank with the infantry. But it didn't have any apparent effect on the tank, but the 203rd aerial mage battalion still managed to conceal the location of a few imperial soldiers from the war tank due to the smoke screen that weakened its visibility.

Noticing Lieutenant Barchet’s decision to use anti-aircraft guns to pierce the war tank's armor, Tanya gives orders for the mages of the company she took from Visha to come with her and pry the enemy tank's lid off and heat it like canned rations. Tanya decides to use the advantage that aerial mages have of being capable of fighting in three dimensions, aerial mages are more flexible than helicopters and way more difficult to hit. For aerial mages capable of flying with such flexibility, blasting through the canopy of a war tank is considerably easy since war tanks have a lot of difficulty to hit them. After this, Tanya gives orders for Weiss to leave some of the war tanks intact so the infantry soldiers on the ground can use it as cover to attack the enemy while being protected by the sturdy armor of the war tank.

After some time, the enemy starts to run scared and Tanya understands that their resources have come to an end. Noticing this, Tanya gives orders for her soldiers to start a counterattack and charge on the enemy in order to eliminate everyone. After this, it's revealed that the counterattack initiated by Tanya against the enemy soldiers eliminated pretty much all the enemies and even if the enemy decides to strike with artillery once again, Tanya would still be capable of getting rid of the artillery pretty easily since aerial mages have a big advantage due to being difficult to hit and due to being capable of flying with insane mobility and flexibility. With this, she manages to solve the problem of the Federation's unexpected attack completely without suffering any losses from her battalion. After this, Tanya left things for Lieutenant Barchet and the rest of the Fifty-fourth regiment of infantry soldiers since the situation was now under control and because the enemy was extremely confused to the point they couldn't even be considered a threat anymore. After some time, Tanya asks for her battalion's status and receives information that no one died and the battalion only suffered three light wounds. After hearing this, Tanya decides to take the enemy out of the fight once and for all by attacking the Federation soldiers in the flank, she understands that the enemy artillery division must be either out of ammo or building a position and thus left out of the equation due to the fact that she didn't saw any incoming shells since the initial bombardment. After some time, it's revealed that Tanya's battalion managed to repel the enemy attack completely and eliminate the infiltrating enemy unit.

After the battle had come to an end, Tanya decided to keep the 203rd aerial mage battalion guarding the rear for a few hours and then head to the marshaling point at their own pace. The motive for this decision is because differently from slow-moving infantry, aerial mages are made for mobility. In other words, their ability to shoot and scoot is their chief feature, so if there's no need for them to hold a specific position, they can simply fly away without any problem. Not only this, but considering how the 203rd aerial mage battalion can easily outrun the Federation aerial mages, if all they want to do on the Eastern Front is escape, It's extremely easy since the enemy won't have any way to stop them. Not only this, but allowing for a large group of foot soldiers such as the Fifty-fourth Regiment of infantry soldiers to go to the marshaling point by themselves would allow for Tanya to use them as decoys to attract the enemy soldiers. Which demonstrates an impressive trap setting feat performed by Tanya. In other words, when needed, Tanya's retreat will go pretty smoothly.

In other words, Tanya's battalion is in a completely safe situation without any life-threatening risk. The only thing they need to do is stay there to guard the rear and join with the rest of the Salamander Kampfgruppe when they have enough free time. In the end, the Federation soldiers made a pursuit against Tanya's battalion which was already anticipated by her, but due to the fact that it was kinda sluggish, they managed to withdraw to the designated line of retreat in order to regroup with the rest of the Kampfgruppe without any problem. With this, Tanya's strategy to counter the unexpected attack of the Federation soldiers comes to an end. This entire strategy is also a impressão problem solving feat, an Adaptability feat and a Trap Evasion feat since this entire situation was a trap settled by the enemy to eliminate everyone located at that place, and Tanya still manages to solve this problem by adapting to the unexpected situation completely while being capable of avoiding the enemy's trap.

Continuing with Volume 7

During the events of the seventh Volume, Tanya's Kampfgruppe headquarters receive an unexpected visit from Colonel Von Lergen. After talking for some time, Lergen reveals to Tanya that he wants for her Kampfgruppe to look after a Colonel from Ildoa. After some time, Tanya manages to see through Lergen and figure out his aim with such a thing.

P1: Colonel Von Lergen appeared in the Kampfgruppe's headquarters and made a personal request for Tanya to look after a Colonel from Ildoa.

P2: At the moment, the kingdom of Ildoa is making negotiations in order to put an end to the war between the empire and the Commonwealth alongside the Federation.

P3: Lergen wants for Tanya to cooperate so bad with him that he bow to Tanya despite being a member of the General staff, such a thing indicated that this “mission” of looking after the Ildoan Colonel isn't a official mission from the General staff since Lergen could simply give written orders for Tanya and she would be obligated to do it.

P4: After this, Lergen reveals that he was appointed as the commander of a mobile task force above the Salamander Kampfgruppe, the fact that Tanya didn't know about such a thing implies that the General staff placed a lot of effort into omitting this information. The fact that the General staff placed so much effort into concealing such a thing implies that there must be a reason behind it.

FINAL CONCLUSION: Colonel Von Lergen is planning to take the Colonel from Ildoa and have diplomatic negotiations in order to put an end to the war.

(Abductive reasoning, critical thinking, Holistic thinking, Non-Linear thinking, Observation, Connecting The Dots)

While accompanying the Colonel from Ildoa on his military observation at the Salamander Kampfgruppe headquarters, Tanya attacks a church from the Federation and the Colonel from Ildoa appears to dislike this action, asking if she was sure that there wasn't any religious personnel on the church, something that Tanya already confirmed before and winced at him, trying to confirm that there was no one there. After Tanya winced at him, the Colonel said: “Are you suggesting there might be?”. Tanya immediately recognized this as being a leading question, if she says no, the Colonel will ask for proof and if she says there might be, he'll accuse her of going against the law of war. Here she demonstrates impressive verbal reasoning skills by seeing through the Colonel's words in a matter of seconds. Tanya perceived that this was a leading question in a matter of seconds and skillfully avoided it by saying: “I have no idea what you're talking about, you're saying there are emblems there even though there aren't?”, by saying this, she doesn't confirm nor deny the possibility of there being religious personnel in the church while at the same time, not refusing to testify.

(VCI, PSI, Trap Evasion)

Tanya's Strategy to attack the Federation's Headquarters

During the events of the Federation offensive on the empire, the Higher-ups formulate a plan for an operation to gain an advantage against the Federation, in order for this operation to be successful, Tanya received the task to attack the enemy headquarters directly with her battalion, of course, due to being lower in the military hierarchy, she is forced to follow these orders. The enemy headquarters is extremely well defended to the point where even the 203rd aerial mage battalion would have difficulty to pass through its defenses, not only this, but it would also be extremely fortified by enemy mages coming to attack the 203rd aerial mage battalion.

Tanya sent the 203rd aerial mage battalion to this mission instead of the entire Kampfgruppe, this decision was made due to the fact that what they plan on doing is a hit-and-run attack on the enemy headquarters and not a strong offensive, if she brought the entire Kampfgruppe with them, they would be under unnecessary risk and would have high chances of suffering losses, something that Tanya wants to avoid due to what occurred in the events of the strategy to attack the RMS Queen of Anjou. Not only this, but the 203rf aerial mage battalion is literally made to deal with this kind of mission, they are an aerial mage group specialized in quick response and strong maneuvering attacks on the enemy forces. After some time, Tanya's battalion reaches the enemy main position and initiates an attack on their headquarters with the aim to destroy it, after all, as already said before, the headquarters act as the head of soldiers in militarism, when the headquarters are cut off, the soldiers enter in a state of confusion where they barely have any idea of what they should be doing.

By destroying the enemy headquarters, Tanya would be capable of placing the enemy in this type of confusion, making it easier for the empire to advance since the enemy soldiers would have difficulty to deal with the Imperial attacks. During this attack, the enemy headquarters sent multiple aerial mages on the way to Tanya's battalion in order to fortify the headquarters defenses against the Imperial attack. When the Federation aerial mages are coming to attack Tanya's battalion, she immediately decides to take advantage of the altitude difference of two thousand feet in order to smash the enemy with a magic formula attack, thanks to the difference in altitude difference between her mages and the Federation mages, Tanya's battalion is in a safe position since the enemy would hardly be capable of hitting them while being capable of worrying about shots coming from other directions, something that Tanya and her battalion don't have to worry about, due to being above their enemies and having dominance over the sky, Tanya's battalion doesn't have to worry about enemy sneak attacks since all the enemy mages would be below them, but the same doesn't apply to the enemy mages.

When Tanya cast her magic formula at the enemy mages, it's shown to us that her attack didn't had any effect on them since they were using the new models of Computation Orbs, the same new models that are capable of resisting to an attack from Tanya made with her Type 97 Computation Orb(it's not the same as the Type 95). In other words, Tanya's battalion can't hope to pierce through the enemy defensive shells from a distance. While Tanya is thinking about what to do, Lieutenant Grantz decides to initiate a close-quarters combat with the enemy, Tanya considers this as being a bold decision, but her Battalion is currently at an advantage in the battle against the enemy forces and she wants to encourage her troops into making self-starting decisions, not a bold decision similar to the one made by Grantz, but an efficient one that would actually help the battalion, she understands that reprimanding him into not making such a decision anymore could result on him avoiding to make decisions without asking to the commander everytime, she absolutely hates people who only know how to follow orders and she plans to honor Grantz decision here in order to teach him how to make efficient decisions some moment in the future while avoiding discouraging him into making such a thing again.

Due to already making her decision to support Lieutenant Grantz's decision, Tanya decides to give orders for  Major Weiss and his company to support Grantz, she purposely send her strongest and most competent aerial mage to support Grantz in order to guarantee that Grantz will triumph in the battle against the enemy while avoiding suffering as many losses as possible. Not only this, but Lieutenant Grantz's idea is correct and Tanya understands this, the enemy new model of computation Orbs is only efficient to defend themselves against long-distance attacks, but the same doesn't apply with close-quarters combat, in fact, the new models used by the Federation are even weaker than the ones used by Tanya and her battalion, in other words, they would have a clear advantage in close-quarters combat since they would actually have a chance to harm the enemy. Another reason for this decision made by Tanya is because she understands that insisting on attacking at long-distance would guarantee comfort to them since the enemy wouldn't be a threat, but they would also end up wasting too much energy and time needlessly, while attacking at close-quarters, despite being risky, will give to her battalion actual chances to win this battle without suffering many losses. Another thing is that this entire situation wasn't expected by Tanya at all, she didn't expect that the enemy headquarters would be so fortified and still managed to adapt to the situation despite it being unexpected.

(Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Adaptability, Leadership, Social skills, Strategic thinking)

Tanya gives orders for Major Weiss and his company to support Grantz's company attack from the sky, while Major Weiss would be giving support for Grantz in a safe position in the sky, Tanya and her company would go after Frantz and charge to the enemy's echelon. With this, Tanya would go to support Grantz's company while having Major Weiss handle an escape route and support them in case things go wrong, with Major Weiss at her back, she can advance and mop up. In other words, if her plan goes wrong and the enemy manages to put up a fight against them, they could easily return back to eight thousand feet and keep their altitude advantage against the enemy mages. But when she was going to charge on the enemy echelon, Federation anti-air positions began to open up, the Federation headquarters was going to send a hail of flak up into an area where their own mages are flying without not even worrying about the possibility of hitting them. When she noticed this, Tanya immediately began to give new instructions for her soldiers, she told them to not rely on optical deception to create illusions to deceive the enemy, she said that they should put energy into their defensive shells and pull back for now. The reason for this is because she understands that as long as it's zone fire, which isn't likely to score any critical hits, all the enemy can do is up their defenses against the Imperial attack.

When she gave orders for her soldiers to pull back, they did as told by aborting the strike and turning around, with this, she managed to avoid any losses coming from her soldiers, if they had fallen into disarray and gotten separated, they would have ended up flaming dragonflies. Tanya also decides to avoid shooting formulas at the enemies from a long distance since accuracy and power would be terribly low compared to a close range shot at the enemy, not only this, but merely pumping up more mana on their attacks to make up for that discrepancy would result on their fatigue to get even worse, she prefers to keep thinking about what they should do since they can't hope to waste time and energy into a meaningless effort to attack the enemy, her battalion has to be careful with every decision they make. In order to be capable of destroying the enemy headquarters, Tanya must now surpass an enemy that has hard defensive shells that don't connect and that respond with tactics of dodging like crazy and devoting themselves to defense when her battalion attacks in close quarters, in other words, the enemy she has to face is like war tanks. After thinking for some time, Tanya decides to simply ignore the enemy mages for now.

After realizing how difficult it was going to be to surpass the enemy mages, Tanya decided to simply ignore them by breaking through the enemy mages and attacking the enemy surface position directly. Tanya knows that the Federation aerial mages have a terrible aim due to lack of proper training, that's why she gives orders for her soldiers to break through the enemy aerial mages and attack their surface position directly. The Federation aerial mages will have a really hard time trying to hit Tanya's soldiers, if the enemy aerial mages actually try to shoot at her battalion, their missed shots will rain down below and make less work for her battalion, in other words, while advancing on the enemy surface position, Tanya will also try to make the enemy crossfire lines to overlap so the enemy winds up taking themselves out of the battlefield, which would lower the amount of enemy aerial mages trying to take her battalion down. Tanya then give orders for her soldiers to follow her in order to make them be behind her, with this, if the enemy actually managed to shoot at Tanya's direction from behind, the one receiving the shot would be one of the soldiers behind her and not Tanya herself, in other words, she would be in a completely safe position where her life wouldn't be at risk, she wasn't inept to the point where she would be hit by a shot coming in front of her and with this tactic, even shots coming from behind wouldn't be capable of harming her.

Tanya's battalion gets into three different strike formations and charges at once in order to break through the enemy aerial mages. When the enemy aerial mages try to bunch together to intercept the battalion's charge, Tanya’s battalion manifest enough explosion formulas to count as a harassment and launch them to act as a smoke screen, with this, Tanya would be capable of concealing from the enemy her battalion's current location which would made it impossible for the Federation aerial mage soldiers to make efficient shots at them. By the time the enemy mages realize it, it's already too late, Tanya's battalion successfully breaks through and charges. While Tanya's battalion is charging on the enemy surface positions, it's shown that as already anticipated by Tanya, the enemy soldiers sticker to their zone fire and started to shoot at Tanya's battalion direction, these shots doesn't harm Tanya and her battalion but end up killing some of the Federation aerial mages flying in the sky, with this, she manages to indirectly manipulate the Federation soldiers into lowering their own firepower which makes things even easier for Tanya and her battalion. Not only this, but due to being exposed to dense anti-air fire, the Federation aerial mages can't shift from defense to attack in order to attack Tanya's battalion from behind even if they want to. With this, Tanya solves the problem involving the enemy Aerial mages and manages to lower the enemy firepower without any problem.

(FRI=[Problem Solving], Fluid intelligence, Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Anticipation, Trap setting, Leadership, Cunning, Social skills, Problem Solving, Indirect manipulation, mass manipulation, concealment)

While Tanya's battalion is charging on the enemy surface positions, Tanya gives orders for her battalion to manifest explosion formulas in order to destroy the enemy anti-air cannon positions. Tanya purposely made her soldiers shoot magic formulas at close quarters since she knows that when an aerial mage doesn't need to seal an explosion formula in a magic bullet for deliver, the casting speed, area covered and power are highly buffed to the point where the destruction is said to be on a whole nother level. She purposely made her soldiers shoot like this in order to cause as much destruction as possible on the enemy quickly without giving any chance for them to compose themselves and start to act efficiently in battle. With this, Tanya managed to get rid of the enemy soldiers in the surface positions and now Tanya only had to worry about the aerial mage soldiers and the headquarters defenses. After receiving information from Visha that the enemy positions have gone quiet, Tanya raises her voice and tells her soldiers that they would be withdrawing since they would have enemies coming after them.

She understands that the Federation enemies only have tough defense, but their firepower and destructive power are extremely weak compared to other aerial mages. In other words, without interference from other units, eliminating them is extremely easy for Tanya's battalion, that's why she prioritized her troops to destroy the enemy surface positions first while ignoring the aerial mages. If other enemy aerial mages leave their base to give chase to Tanya's battalion, they will be done for. Tanya wants to lure the enemy out of their base in order to make them chase her battalion. If her soldiers pretend to be withdrawing from the enemy headquarters, the enemies would think that they are low on resources and would think that now is the best time to eliminate Tanya's battalion once and for all. The moment her battalion takes the only roughly aimed fire, they pretend to scatter in order to indirectly manipulate the enemy into thinking that they are retreating, but they immediately round about for an assault against the enemy while they are on low-guard due to thinking that the 203rd aerial mage battalion was going to retreat. As anticipated by Tanya, the enemy really thought that the imperial battalion was retreating and started to chase them while being on low-guard. After this, Tanya's battalion takes the opportunity that the enemy aerial mage soldiers were confused due to the imperial battalion suddenly coming to attack them after pretending to be retreating in order to get close to the enemy and attack them quickly in a matter of seconds before the Federation soldiers can even comprehend what's occurring.

After a few minutes of mixing it up in the sky, the 203rd aerial mage battalion prevailed in this battle and eliminated all enemy aerial mages in the sky. With this, Tanya manages to solve the problem about the Federation attack and defeat all the enemy forces. Noticing this, Tanya immediately asks Weiss to give reports about the battalion's losses in this battle in order to get a big picture of the situation. When she receives information from Major Weiss that they found the location of the enemy Main headquarters, Tanya intuitively figured out that it was a trap, she recognizes that in this battle, the Federation enemy soldiers and headquarters demonstrated to be extremely competent in this battle, she understands that the enemy is probably waiting for them to appear and perceives that the enemy main headquarters knows what they're doing due to how much the Federation coordination improved. Now that Tanya recognized the enemy trap, she now has to think of a way to avoid it and she decides to abandon the idea of attacking the enemy main headquarters, what her battalion is doing right now is merely an additional task on top of joining up with the paratroopers’ rangers at the river crossing, there's no reason for her to work up her soldiers so much simply because it's possible, not only this, but a quarter of her soldiers got hurt from their battle against the enemy soldiers, going directly to the enemy's trap and raising the battalion's fatigue could be dangerous for them and cause unnecessary losses. Here she demonstrates impressive Decision Making.

Tanya tries to persuade Weiss into abandoning the idea of attacking the enemy headquarters directly since it could be a trap, but there's a reason as to why she doesn't worry about the possibility of him being punished by the General staff due to failing in the mission, the reason for this being because a commander in an assault wouldn't be in problems if they fail as long as they make a plausible excuse and they have one right in front of them, their soldiers could be fatigued, a quarter of the members of the battalion were hurt and attacking the enemy main headquarters could be a trap, with this, Weiss wouldn't have to worry about suffering big punishments due to failing on the mission. Not only this, but they already made their original job to join up with the imperial paratroopers' rangers at the river crossing, so even if they failed on this mission, they wouldn't be in problems with the General staff at all. After persuading Weiss that they shouldn't attack the enemy main headquarters carelessly since it could be a trap, she gives orders for her soldiers to make a careful examination of the area in order to gain an understanding of their current environment, she also tells them to stay in assault formation and circle around in an effort to search and destroy.

Due to them having concluded the minimum work necessary, Tanya can expect that her battalion wouldn't be in problems with the General staff, not only this, but even if her battalion haven't completely trampled the enemy, since they conducted the assault, they already carried out the General staff’s order to “attack the enemy HQ directly”. Even in the case of the Moskva raid, the true purpose was to achieve strategic results by demonstrating that it was possible to arrive in Moskva and attack, what Tanya's battalion did here is the exact same thing, they showed to the General staff of the empire that attacking the enemy headquarters directly is possible which should be enough to raise the mood of the Major Generals in the General staff and avoid heavy punishments. She even gave orders for her soldiers to collect Geographical data in order to gain a complete understanding of the environment around the enemy main headquarters so that they can attack it efficiently in their next attack. In this strategy, Tanya located the enemy, collected Geographical data for the environment around the enemy headquarters and even held the enemy back. Such things would guarantee that they would meet the General staff's expectations even if it's only a little.

Eighth Volume

During the events of the eighth Volume, Tanya receives orders from the General staff to report to the Eastern army's forward Base. After this, she demonstrates impressive analysis by figuring out that either Colonel Von Lergen or Lieutenant Colonel Uger came with a message merely based on the fact that she received orders from the General staff to report to the Eastern army's forward Base. She understands that the fact that the General staff gave direct orders for her to come alone to the Eastern army's forward Base indicated that whatever they wanted with her involved a message, she also understands that anything too dangerous to transmit in writing, or too important, normally gets delivered by an officer, given the market factors of secrecy and convenience, getting called in is surely the going rate for important information. Since Tanya is a Lieutenant Colonel, the messenger should be no one higher than Colonel, in other words, it's one of the officers who Tanya is acquainted with, Colonel Von Lergen or Colonel Uger. But differently from what she expected, the person she met in the Eastern Army group headquarters was Lieutenant General Von Zettour.

During Tanya's talk with Zettour, the narrator of the story states that if Tanya had felt any hint of Zettour manifesting a Being X-like energy from his body, she may have responded differently. This statement made by the Narrator of the story implies that Tanya is capable of sensing an harmful energy coming from the body of other people, this means that Tanya is capable of perceiving when someone has malicious intentions that could be harmful for her, this demonstrates an insane emotional perception and Perception Statement since this means that Tanya is capable of sensing when someone has a harmful and malicious energy by merely observing them manifesting what the narrator claimed to be a Being X-like harmful energy from their body.

While still talking with Zettour, Tanya talks about the new operation that the empire is currently working on, operation Andromeda, she says that even its name rubs her the wrong way and explains why: basically, she understands that humans are caught in the thrall of words and that in her understanding, names are words, yet the meanings they possess can at times bring about misunderstandings. She makes an example by associating a division from Dacian and a division from the Federation, both are divisions, but the threat they pose is quite different, if a soldier become too used to fight against the Federation, they will end up overestimating Dacia, but if a soldier become too used to fight against Dacia, they will end up underestimating the Federation instead(No feats on this paragraph, this is merely context for Tanya's feat in the next paragraph).

After this, Zettour asks what the name of the operation, “Andromeda”, suggests. Tanya replies by associating it with the Andromeda galaxy, she makes an association with the fact that the galaxy of Andromeda is extremely far and that the operation Andromeda is a huge gamble that is practically the same as reaching a bit too far. She says that for her, at least, it's self-evident that the operation is aiming too far despite their current lack of resources. She says that by the time they're endeavoring to ignore the tyranny of distance, it’s already clear what they should be worrying about first. This is a highly impressive demonstration of VCI=[Similarities], abstract reasoning and abstract thinking coming from Tanya. After saying all this, she also thinks about how military companies hate hearing words like growth strategy and long-term plan coming from their superior since such a thing is signs of an organization that is failing to hide how it's already hit the limit. Another good VCI feat from Tanya here.

During the events of the Soldim 528 Plan/strategy, Tanya perceives that the Federation aerial mages improved a lot in quality in an absurdly low span of time, she then starts to think about it and reason how such a thing is possible:

P1: The Federation aerial mages were extremely weak before to the point where Tanya's battalion was easily capable of eliminating them, but now they have improved so much that even her battalion has difficulty fighting against them.

C1: Normally, this kind of boost would indicate mid-career hires from outside the company or maybe a service that provides training or instructors.

C2: In this case about the Federation forces in specific, the latter must be correct.

P2: When major corporations, worried about being understaffed, do massive hiring rounds, it's not uncommon for them to outsource the training.

P3: And here, too, the commonwealth and Unified States probably have a vested interest in training Federation troops.

C3: If that's the case, then it's also possible that the Federation is using former Republic or Entente Alliance personnel as instructors.

P4: There's demand and there's supply, that's everything required for a perfect overlapping of desires.

P5: But that logic only works with capitalism, the communist party of the Russy Federation will never abandon communism.

P6: The Federation is suffering from lack of support for communism since people are now fighting the empire out of Nationalism.

P7: If the people from the Federation abandoned Communism, the communist party commanding the country would completely cease to exist.

C4: The probability of the communist party allowing their soldiers to be trained by officers from capitalist countries is extremely low since such a thing could harm the faith that the people in the Federation have in Communism.

P8: Even if the communists overcame that conflict of interest, each country has its own doctrines, can something new be applied so rapidly in the middle of a war? Even if they resolve all those problems, is it possible to get results this fast?

C5: Definitely No, human beings have limits that make such an insane progress in quality in such a low amount of time impossible.

P9: But unlike a corporation, the Federation is a country. The state has a monopoly on violence, that is to say, it's the worst type of monopolistic enterprise.

P10: If that's the case, where could the Russy Federation hire such highly capable aerial mages from?

C6: Maybe, it's possible that the communists have developed the concept of competition on the civilian level.

C7: If that was the case, then it wouldn't make sense that the quality of Soviet consumer products is so consistently abysmal. There's no way people who had to recruit specialists from lageri have the redundancy for that.

P11: The Federation army existed before it became the party’s army, in other words, there's a pool of mid-career human resources that could potentially be recruited.

P12: The Lageri is a place where the Federation keeps political prisoners and soldiers from before the army became communist in order to execute them in camps.

P13: In propaganda, the Federation denounced political prisoners as enemies of their state, as class enemies, as reactionaries, as relics of the abominable past, and yet, they preserved a ton of them in the lageri despite them being such a risk for the Federation communist party.

C8: After some time, a theory comes to Tanya's mind. It's possible that the Federation army is taking mid-career soldiers from the lageri and recruiting them to the army so that they can fight against the empire in the war instead of killing them all in the camps. The Federation is using army mage units from the previous era taken directly from the lageri.

FINAL CONCLUSION: It's highly probable that the Federation army is using highly experienced aerial mages from the previous era taken directly from the Lageri in order to make them fight against the empire in the war, which explains the insane amount of quality of coordination shown by the Federation aerial mages in Soldim 528.

Tanya noticed that her current situation was extremely bad since she didn't have any information about how the Federation soldiers from the previous era fought, she didn't have any information about her enemy. After noticing this, she gave orders for Visha to send a request on Colonel Von Lergen’s name requesting for the General staff to search for information about the Federation army soldiers from the previous era. By making this request on Lergen's name, who is a member of operations, she can guarantee that her request will be processed as an internal request and all officers from the General staff would start to search this information requested by Tanya.

(Leadership, Information gathering, connecting the dots, Deductive reasoning, Abductive reasoning, critical thinking, Holistic thinking, Linear thinking, convergent thinking)

During the events of Tanya's Strategy/Plan to survive at Soldim 528, she receives an urgent message from high command: “From General Von Zettour to the Lergen Kampfgruppe. Colonel Von Lergen promptly began the designated actions. At first sight, this message doesn't make any sense for Tanya. But after analyzing it, she figures out the hidden message contained on this message from high command. Tanya observes that the words “Lergen Kampfgruppe” and “Colonel Von Lergen” followed a specific pattern, they were all outward-facing labels, Tanya then guesses that the same is true for the “designated actions”. She then guesses that the actual message should be read with these words removed: “promptly take action”. After thinking for some time, Tanya notices that action, in the end of the message, is assertive spontaneity. In imperial army staff officer education, it's told for the soldiers that their role is to carry out the mission they're given and to obey not the letter but its intent.

Tanya then starts to think about the intention of this message from Zettour's point of view. Based on all the previous major operations initiated by him like the one used to destroy the Republic, he's an expert with an exhaustive knowledge of logistics, his personality certainly rules out passively waiting with no plan, it's even possible that he could seize the initiative independently, he would even favor an aggressive breakthrough action. After thinking about this, Tanya reaches the conclusion that the word “promptly” in the message should indicate “assertiveness”. Zettour has a very radical side and apparently the Higher-ups want for Tanya to take prompt action. If a staff officer like Tanya is being asked to act, then it can only be an order to begin an operation and choose the optimal action. In other words, the message from high command asks for Tanya to come up with a way to break out of her current situation, that situation being the fact that she's surrounded, this means that General Zettour is going to do something that involves Tanya breaking out of her current situation. After this, she connects the dots and figures out that Zettour is planning to make an operation to break the siege by making an aggressive action.

With this, Tanya managed to figure out the concealed message hidden on the text sent to her by high command, the secret message was that Zettour was going to initiate a maneuver battle and her Kampfgruppe should act in concert with their operation. Here, Tanya managed to grasp General Zettour's intentions and read his thoughts with such a complex and weird message. In this entire Reasoning process, Tanya relied on her understanding of Zettour’s personality and his thinking pattern in order to figure out the message in what appears to have taken less than a minute, perceiving how his thinking pattern could possibly change the hidden meaning of the secret message, which demonstrates her impressive inductive reasoning, pattern recognition, abstract reasoning/thinking and VCI feat.
