Tanya's Soldim 528 Strategy/Plan

In order to guarantee that the Andromeda operation was going to be a success, Tanya received orders from Zettour to purposely allow for her Kampfgruppe to be surrounded by enemy forces in order to allow for the soldiers of Group B to initiate an encirclement on the enemy, Tanya's Kampfgruppe will be completely surrounded by four or five entire divisions of enemy soldiers while her Kampfgruppe is merely an augmented regiment and they don't have permission to retreat even in a scenario where they are suffering with lack of food, water or ammunition. Going against this and retreating anyway would be the same as going against the orders of the General staff and would make Tanya be executed or punished heavily. In the hideout they are using as a Base they receive supplies in an extremely slow way and barely have any resources to fight against the enemy. Another thing is that Tanya's Kampfgruppe is by design, fundamentally a breakthrough force, tying it down with orders to defend a static position literally negates all its strengths. Not only this, but Tanya's Kampfgruppe doesn't even have a proper headquarters to use in order to manage things efficiently and she will have to survive in Soldim 528 for multiple days while facing all this adversity. Now let's see how she solves this problem:

Tanya and other officers of the Kampfgruppe hide behind an abandoned rubble in Soldim 528 while observing the enemy soldiers’ movements through the use of binoculars. At this point of the events, Tanya's Kampfgruppe is already completely surrounded by the enemy as planned by Zettour. While she's observing the enemy movements, Major Weiss is analyzing the status of their current situation in order to guarantee that they are completely surrounded as planned. Another thing is that due to already knowing that they would be surrounded by enemy forces, Tanya and the Kampfgruppe prepared trenches beforehand just in case they needed to initiate actual combat against the enemy, with trenches, they would have lower chances of losing soldiers in a fight against the enemy compared to the chances they would have of losing soldiers by fighting without any trenches. At the moment Tanya's Kampfgruppe was successfully surrounded by the enemy, the Imperial air forces, who are also cooperating on this Andromeda operation, immediately sent an observation unit to make a photo analysis in order to figure out the enemy number of enemy soldiers and their overall firepower. It's then revealed to Tanya that the results of this photo analysis revealed that the enemy was formed by four or five divisions of soldiers.

Due to the Imperial air forces also being involved in this operation, Tanya can count with their help in this battle in order to receive air support from her allies. Another thing is that the Imperial army intelligence is constantly observing the Federation army's guard divisions since Tanya's Kampfgruppe would be in a lot of trouble if they had to face a Federation guard division, so in order to avoid such a thing, the Imperial army intelligence is constantly observing their movements to warn the Kampfgruppe in case something unexpected occurs. Also, while Tanya's Kampfgruppe is surrounded by the enemy forces in Soldim 528, the A Group of the Eastern Army Group is currently fighting against the Federation forces in order to perform the Andromeda operation, they are currently conducting a major offensive against the Federation and due to such a thing, the enemy guard divisions were currently located at a battlefield far away from Tanya's current location, with this, Tanya has a guarantee that as long as the A Group continues to fight against the enemy forces to perform their major offensive, the Guard divisions of the Federation won't appear at Soldim 528 to cause trouble.

While still being in Soldim 528, Tanya decides to avoid recon-in-force missions with aerial mages and prefer to observe the enemy's actions and gather information passively, the reason for this being because they are heavily outnumbered and she can't allow for her Kampfgruppe to suffer unnecessary losses, that's why she decides to simply observe the enemy with binoculars alongside the other officers of the Kampfgruppe, Tanya even keeps some of the officers observing the enemy's actions while she is busy talking with her troops in order to constantly gather information about the enemy. She also tells her troops that that day and tomorrow would be dedicated to fending off enemy offensives against the stronghold they were requested to defend by Zettour, after fending off the enemy offensives, her Kampfgruppe would strike at the enemy once they have loosened up a bit since they will be on low-guard and would be an easy prey for her Kampfgruppe. Doing such a thing would also motivate her soldiers' will to fight since assertive action tends to become imperative in order to maintain the defenders’ will to fight. But she understands that motivating her soldiers' will to fight too much would tempt them into being careless, that's why she gives strict orders for them to act passively by doing a force defensive battle with Lieutenant Tospan’s infantry unit in the leading role at the trenches. While this occurs her Kampfgruppe can expect some backup from their allies in this operation, but she gives orders for her soldiers to fight while assuming that they won't receive any reinforcements since it's dangerous to assume that reinforcements will come without having any guarantee of such a thing.

Since she isn't certain if they will receive reinforcements from their allies, Tanya decides that the best thing to do right now is saving some energy just in case something unexpected occurs and somehow cope with whatever the enemy throws at them in the meantime, noticing this, Tanya makes Captain Meybert, who commands the artillery unit, and Captain Ahrens, who commands the armored forces unit, as supporting roles for the Kampfgruppe and she decides to keep them in reserve for the time being. She also decides to send them in as additional forces depending on how the situation plays out and have them prepare with the worst-case scenario in their mind. When Captain Meybert appears on the little Base that the Kampfgruppe is using to hide from the enemy, Tanya tells him to keep the artillery quiet for now since the enemy didn't noticed them there yet, other reasons for this decision made by Tanya is because she doesn't want to throw the entire Kampfgruppe in a battle against the enemy forces yet in order to keep the amount of losses as minimal as possible and due to the fact that they are low on resources. Another thing is that while being in their current hideout, Tanya's Kampfgruppe is receiving supplies from their allies in order to keep the Kampfgruppe’s energy high while having the resources to fight against the enemy if needed. Even if the supplies they are receiving are coming in an extremely low way, any help from their allies is still useful for her plan to protect the stronghold they were requested to protect, Tanya's Kampfgruppe even used their ally, the Council for Self-government, to receive supplies in order to have resources to fight against the enemy forces if needed. The council for Self-government is an independent Federation separatist territory that is managed by its people without interference neither from the empire or the Federation, this independent separatist territory was annexed by the empire in the war against the Federation, but was soon transformed into an independent land by Zettour in a plan to turn the separatists in their allies to fight against the Russy Federation.

After thinking for some time, Tanya decides to give orders for her officers to prepare their troops for nap time on the double since they weren't noticed by the Federation soldiers yet. The reason for this being because she comprehends that if her subordinates don't manage time properly and fail to get enough sleep, that's their own fault, but if they fail to get enough sleep due to the rotation schedule, it's Tanya's own fault, such a thing would cause harm to her reputation as a commander and possibly even make people see her as being inept. Tanya then continues insisting for her officers to tuck their soldiers in so they can establish a rotation schedule and give everyone a chance to sleep properly. She insists on this because she understands that exhaustion in war represents a lot of trouble since it does a real number on one's brain, she understands that the drop in mental acuity in the middle of war is unacceptable. Tanya then gives orders for her vice-commander, Major Weiss, to prepare meals for everyone and make sure that one of every three meals is hot, she even tells him to use aerial mages magic as heat sources to make sure that the meals are hot. Such a thing is against the regulations but Tanya still decided to do it anyway since they are at war, in order to guarantee that their soldiers receive proper rest and food, she doesn't mind going against the regulations since her soldiers not receiving proper sleep and food could possibly result in them dying in a fight against the enemy due to being fatigued, and in Tanya's current situation, if her troops die, she's going to die too since she's either going to be executed by the empire via retreating and going against the General staff orders or staying in Soldim 528 and be killed by the enemy forces. This demonstrates Tanya's impressive decision making skills. Another thing that I forgot to mention here is that Tanya gave one of the companies forming her 203rd aerial mage battalion to General Von Zettour, in other words, now she has only two companies(she lost an entire company worth of aerial mages during the events of the RMX Queen of Anjou strategy). In order to solve this problem, Tanya took the new reserve aerial mage company given to her by the imperial army and added this new reserve company to the 203rd aerial mage battalion temporarily while Grantz's company is alongside Zettour. With this, she managed to solve the problem of being without one of the companies forming her battalion.

(FRI=[Problem Solving], Fluid intelligence, Leadership, Social skills, resource management, Strategy, Short-term Planning, Methods, Tactics, Information gathering, problem solving, Decision Making)

After some time, the Headquarters of Group B from the Eastern Front receives the information that Soldim 528 was surrounded by extremely powerful Federation units as planned. They already expected an enemy attack coming from Soldim 528, but unexpectedly for them, it didn't come from the melting line as expected by the Soldiers of B Group, not only this, but they also figured out that the Lergen Kampfgruppe was surrounded by the enemy. When such a thing occurred, Zettour immediately calmed everyone down as planned and persuaded everyone into going to save Tanya's Kampfgruppe. How did he do this? Simple, Tanya's Kampfgruppe was surrounded by the enemy forces on purpose in order to indirectly manipulate the allied forces into going to rescue them, after all, if they simply abandoned the strongest unit of the empire merely because they were surrounded by enemy forces, the trust that the Imperial soldiers have on command chain would be completely ruined, after all, who would be crazy enough to trust on the command chain of the empire who doesn't even bother to protect and save its soldiers when they are in danger? The trust that the imperial soldiers had on the command chain was already weakened, if they abandoned even Tanya's Kampfgruppe, the most seasoned and decorated military unit of the empire simply because they are surrounded, then all the faith and trust from the imperial soldiers on the command chain would disappear.

Lieutenant General Von Zettour knows about this and uses this fact to threaten the members of the B Group on the Eastern Front. He has so much faith in this plan that he even tries to make even all the reserves of the B Group come to rescue Tanya's Kampfgruppe. The soldiers of B Group send all their matériel to the melting line under the assumption that the enemy's were going to come from that side, such a thing was wrong and it was part of the plan for it to be wrong. Anyway, the superiors of the B Group on the Eastern Front committed a mistake and inside their minds, it was their fault that Tanya's Kampfgruppe was surrounded by the enemy, despite such a thing not being true.

The empire would perceive this incident as being the fault of the superiors in the B Group of the Eastern Front and they would be considered responsible for such a thing unfairly. Now that they are considered as being responsible for such a thing due to the mistake they committed of thinking that the enemy was going to come from the melting line of Soldim 528, the Lergen Kampfgruppe(that's how Tanya's Kampfgruppe is called now for some reasons that aren't important now) was surrounded by enemy forces due to lack of support from their superiors. This is an insane demonstration of Logical manipulation and Trap Setting. Despite them being responsible for such a thing, they still want to give orders for Tanya's Kampfgruppe to return on their own despite being literally surrounded, they were quite literally abandoning the troops due to their own negligence, such a thing was going to prove to the imperial soldiers the negligence of commanders and will cause the trust in command chain to rot away. Of course, Zettour wants to avoid such a thing, what he's doing here is merely a bluff to make the soldiers of B Group cooperate with his plans.

After this, Zettour decides to use the Council for Self-government in his favor, ever since its creation, the Higher-ups of the General staff have been doing their best to keep the Council for Self-government on their side. If the empire did something as negligent as abandoning their own troops in Soldim 528, the trust between the Council for Self-government and the Empire would be completely ruined. This means that the Federation separatists from the Council for Self-government would abandon the idea of fighting on the empire's side since they could be discarded as tools if they stopped being useful, at least, that's what the Empire's decision of giving up on the idea of saving the soldiers in Soldim 528 would imply to the Council for Self-government.

Anyway, as already said, the General staff has been doing their best to create an illusion inside the mind of the people in the council for self-government that the empire is completely good and that they deserve their trust. Abandoning their own troops in Soldim 528 would completely ruin this, that's why Zettour, as a member of the General staff, gives orders for the people in B Group to go and rescue Tanya's Kampfgruppe in order to maintain the false illusion they create inside the minds of the people in the council for self-government since as a member of the General staff, Zettour has the obligation to maintain the Council for Self-government at the empire's side for as much time as possible in order to have them as their allies in the war against the Russy Federation forces.

Now that Zettour gave direct orders for the members of B Group in the Eastern Front to go and rescue Tanya's Kampfgruppe that was surrounded by enemy forces. The soldiers in B Group now have no other choice but to obey to Zettour's orders and go to rescue the Kampfgruppe since not doing such a thing would be the same as going against the orders of the General staff, in other words, the soldiers of B Group would be committing insubordination towards Zettour and the empire would have a valid motive to execute them by death by shooting squad. In other words, now the soldiers of B Group have only two alternatives that they can chose, the first being not go to rescue Lergen Kampfgruppe, which would result on them disobeying orders from the General staff and being executed by death by shooting, in other words, their deaths would be guaranteed, while the second choice would be going to rescue the Lergen Kampfgruppe and have a chance of dying but also having a chance of returning back alive, which means that there's a chance that they could die, but there was also a chance that they could survive, their deaths weren't certain in this alternative, well… I think the best option is clear here.

Obviously, the soldiers of B Group doesn't want to die and that's exactly why, after much resistance against Zettour's endless tries to persuade and manipulate the soldiers of B Group into going to save Tanya's Kampfgruppe, they finally decide to do as told and start to formulate a plan to advance in the surrounded territory of Soldim 528 in order to save the Largen Kampfgruppe who allowed themselves to be surrounded by the enemy in order to give Zettour an opportunity to manipulate them into coming to help so that they can perform the Andromeda operation and push the enemy more further back to their own territory. Now as planned, Tanya's Kampfgruppe can expect help coming from the B Group in order to save them in Soldim 528 so that they can finally perform the Andromeda operation in order to gain an advantage against the Russy Federation in the war. And even if Zettour didn't manage to convince the soldiers of Group B to rescue the Salamander Kampfgruppe, he would give back to Tanya the aerial mage company that she gave to him due to the Andromeda operation. With this, even the most reclusive staffers will come out for the rescue, after all, if Zettour charge in leading the aerial mage company given to him by Tanya, everyone from Group B will leap into action, worried about what might happen if something befalls a member of the General staff like him.

Four days have passed since Zettour managed to persuade the soldiers of B Group into cooperating to save Tanya's Kampfgruppe. Ever since that day, Tanya managed to create a rotation schedule for her troops so that they can get a fairly good amount of rest everyday in order to avoid for anyone to commit any grave mistakes due to lack of proper rest. After some time, Tanya is woken up by Visha since it was already her time to observe while Visha goes to rest. Tanya goes to take command post and allow for Major Weiss to rest too, they have been doing this exact same thing for these four days, changing shifts on who takes command of the Kampfgruppe in order to guarantee that no one of the important members of the Kampfgruppe were going to be affected by tiredness while literally being in the middle of the battlefield surrounded by enemy forces. Also, every time someone is going to take command of the Kampfgruppe, the person who was previously commanding it reveals to the person that was going to take command from now on all the things that happened while the other person was sleeping. With this, they can efficiently manage things and guarantee that they won't have problems with the enemy forces while receiving useful information about the enemy's actions constantly. Tanya's strategy to stay quiet and conceal their presence from the enemy soldiers was also highly useful since the enemy forces didn't made any major attack on Soldim 528 during these four days due to not being aware of the presence of Tanya's Kampfgruppe in that stronghold, as already said previously, the fact that her Kampfgruppe is forced to defend a stronghold nullified almost all its forces since they are originally a breakthrough military group. While talking with Weiss, Tanya hears an extremely loud sound that she immediately recognized as being the sound of enemy heavy artillery. Noticing this, she decided to send the 203rd aerial mage battalion to fight in order to make them gain aerial superiority against the enemy forces and intercept any enemy mages before they even manage to get close to the Kampfgruppe’s Base, due to the lack of resources from the enemy and lack of enemy artillery, the 203rd aerial mage battalion, known for rapid response, would easily be capable of attacking the enemy before they could even react and gain aerial superiority against them. But there's a problem with this, Tanya is also going with the 203rd aerial mage battalion to intercept the enemy, such a thing would result in the Kampfgruppe being without anyone to command. Tanya obviously noticed this and she decided to put Captain Meybert, who commands the artillery unit, in command of the Kampfgruppe since the Kampfgruppe's artillery unit doesn't have anything to do since they don't have any shells with them, not only this, but Captain Meybert also demonstrated to be extremely competent multiple times throughout the Light Novel and Tanya herself recognized how competent he was during the events of the fifth Volume. With this, she solves the problem of not having anyone to command the Kampfgruppe while she's commanding the 203rd aerial mage Battalion.

After putting Meybert in command, it's revealed to us that Tanya has allowed for Captain Meynert and his artillery unit to strike an enemy position earlier before these events, doing this in order to weaken the enemy's ability to attack the Kampfgruppe while spreading some confusion on them. After this, Tanya takes Major Weiss and Visha to round up the battalion in order to intercept the enemy while Captain Meybert will stay on Base to command the Kampfgruppe. In other words, Tanya's battalion will have to intercept the enemy while having some fatigued soldiers. Tanya then decides to keep Major Weiss, Visha and Lieutenant Wustemann on the Kampfgruppe's Base for some time in order to allow for them to prepare themselves while making a formal confirmation about their current situation. She is confident that even with many of her officers suffering with sleepiness, they will be capable of succeeding on fighting against the enemy forces since what they are going to do is basically business as usual, they already did quick response attacks against enemy forces multiple times in the past and they are highly experienced on doing such a thing, in other words, they shouldn't have any difficulty doing the same thing as usual once again.

Not only this, but Tanya also understands that despite Lieutenant Wustemann being a green company commander, his combat experience is far from the point where he can be trusted to do things on his own, that's why Tanya purposely give direct orders for Lieutenant Wustemann to follow her in order to restrict his actions and guarantee that he won't cause problem due to acting on his own. After Wustemann demonstrated to have understood Tanya's orders, she gave attention to him by saying that she liked his attitude, the reason for this being because she understands that young personnel lack experience, but make up for it with enthusiasm, in other words, they only need appropriate education to reach the level of an elite soldier and become useful, she understands that all Wustemann needs to become a highly competent and trustful soldier is a little bit of time and attention to reason, that's why she stated to have liked his attitude in order to motivate him into improving.

Due to recognizing how dangerous it would be to be hit by her own allies, Tanya decided that she should guide her battalion out of the front in order to guarantee that no one will die unnecessarily, after all, one of the only reasons as to why Tanya wanted to have subordinates at her side is to use them as meat shields that she can use to preserve her own life. Tanya also give orders under Colonel Von Lergen's name for the members of the infantry unit to fight until death without having permission to retreat; she also sent Lieutenant Wustemann's aerial mage company to give these orders to fight until death formally to Lieutenant Tospan and gave orders for Tospan to take Lieutenant Wustemann's aerial mage company sent by Tanya as reinforcements to protect Soldim 528 against the enemy infantry. The reason for this being to guarantee that her Kampfgruppe will be capable of resisting the enemy attacks, they are completely surrounded so retreating clearly isn't an option, not only this, but Lieutenant Tospan himself made the suggestion for Tanya to give orders for his unit to fight until death, something that made Tanya perceive that he was the type of soldier that was stubborn and someone who will doggedly carry out whatever they are told to do.

After this, Tanya left command and gave orders for Captain Meybert to take command of the Kampfgruppe while she's gone since he has a handle on the overall situation. Tanya decided to go to the front lines and support her troops while they hold their ground in exchange for their lives, after all, her Kampfgruppe is already suffering with a lack of resources, she can't allow for them to suffer many losses or they won't be capable of resisting for too long. Tanya takes Visha with her so that they both can join the firefight on the periphery in order to make things easier for Lieutenant Tospan and Lieutenant Wustemann. After coming to the battlefield, Tanya gave orders for Lieutenant Tospan's infantry unit to draw the enemy in and hold back their fire since the ammo of the Kampfgruppe is extremely scarce. Suddenly, the enemy artillery starts to shoot shells at Soldim 528 which results in many of the infantry soldiers being hit and killed. Tanya then questions Visha about any information about enemy aerial mages nearby and she receives information that there were magic signals visible but they were maintaining their distance. Noticing how bad their current situation is, Tanya decides to call Major Weiss and make the 203rd aerial mage battalion prevent the possibility of the headquarters being completely pinned down. Tanya decides to make the 203rd aerial mage battalion currently under Weiss command abandon their initial goal of acting as distraction for the enemy forces and make them knock the artillery shells out of the sky by placing mages in defense against artillery. With this, Tanya can at least guarantee that the Kampfgruppe Base won't collapse thanks to the enemy attacks since Major Weiss will take care of them for now.

After some time, the enemy infantry starts to advance as planned and she's now going to eliminate them, but suddenly she has a little idea, she decides to give orders for the aerial mages to hold up and make only the infantry return fire. The reason for this being because Tanya wants to mimic the enemy tactics, she's going to pretend that they don't have any aerial mages with them in order to make the enemy lower their guard and then make the Kampfgruppe’s aerial mages strike the enemy with all their forces. With this, she will essentially be misdirecting the enemy forces into thinking that the Kampfgruppe was weakened and that it was going to be an easy prey for them. The enemy soldiers tend to look at the sky nervously in order to see if there are any enemy aerial mage nearby and they tend to split into squads to run for cover when trying to escape machine gun fire in order to lower their losses in case an enemy aerial mage appears and starts to use magic formulas on them, if the enemy wrongly thought that there wasn't any aerial mages, they would rarely look at the sky and would lower their guard, giving a chance for Tanya's aerial mages to make a sneak attack. The enemy forces are now focused completely on the ground while charging in order to get rid of Lieutenant Tospan's infantry unit, as planned by Tanya, this is a really good demonstration of a Trap setting feat. After perceiving that the enemy fell on the trap, Tanya leaps into flight and starts to open fire on the enemy below with her submachine gun, with this, Tanya manages to eliminate multiple enemy infantry before they are even capable of reacting or even processing what's occurring to them, while the ones that survived lost the will to fight completely and started to run away. This is a highly impressive demonstration of a problem solving feat from Tanya, since by doing this, she managed to eliminate the enemy soldiers quickly before they could even react while not having to waste many resources.

After Tanya finished eliminating the enemy infantry unit, suddenly the enemy aerial mages started to move and head straight to the Kampfgruppe headquarters. Tanya then warns Major Weiss about this and gives orders for him to take care of the enemy aerial mages while receiving support from Visha. With this, Tanya can observe and analyze how Lieutenants Wustemann's aerial mage company operates so that she can have a better idea of what she needs to do to improve his overall skills as an aerial mage commander. The enemy foot soldiers already lost the will to fight completely so Tanya doesn't have to worry about the enemy counter-attacking Lieutenant Wustemann's aerial mage soldiers, not only this, but Lieutenant Wustemann would also be receiving support from Lieutenant Tospan's infantry unit while eliminating all the enemy soldiers, which lowers even more the chances of them suffering losses, so Tanya can simply observe Lieutenant Wustemann's company actions without having to worry about nothing. After all enemy infantry soldiers had been eliminated, Tanya gave orders for Lieutenant Wustemann and Lieutenant Tospan to help collect the dead enemy resources to use for themselves. With this, Tanya will be capable of solving the problem of her Kampfgruppe lacking resources by borrowing useful items from the enemy.

After many days have passed ever since Tanya's Kampfgruppe was sent to Soldim 528, Zettour decided to go to Soldim 528 on his own without having anyone except an armored forces division and Lieutenant Grantz's aerial mage company to protect him, such a thing would force the staff officers of the Eastern Front B Group to send their troops to help Zettour too since they couldn't simply abandon a General from the Imperial army and pretend like nothing happened, if something occurred to Zettour, they would be in serious trouble and he knows about this, that's why he purposely went to Soldim 528 in order to make the soldiers of B Group come to help him against their own will. The great aim of this operation is to push the enemy forces back to their territory in order to secure a resource area to support the imperial war economy. With this mission to eliminate the enemy forces heading to B Group's headquarters before they actually manage to attack the headquarters, Tanya aims to be capable of saving her allies from the Eastern Army group, saving herself from any danger and showing off to Zettour in the process.

(Strategy, Long-term Planning, Methods, Tactics, Leadership, Social skills, Resource management, Cunning, Misdirection, Concealment, Non-Verbal Deception, mass manipulation, Logical manipulation, Indirect manipulation, Gaslighting, Anticipation, Information gathering)

Unexpectedly for Tanya, the enemy forces figured out that the magic signals of the Aerial mages of the Kampfgruppe disappeared way too soon, now Tanya has to fight against an aerial mage company and one entire battalion of aerial mages, including Mary Sioux, who has insane magic aptitude and magical prowess way beyond Tanya even with the Type 95 Computation Orb, Mary’s raw magic aptitude is so great that Tanya herself acknowledged her as being a “damn monster”, she even stated that her ridiculous amount of magic defied all logic during the events of the Tenth Volume, Tanya even said that Mary was using this insane amount of magic to use a magic formula stated to “literally alter the world” and even said that she was like Being X due to how insanely powerful she was. The enemies surprisingly came to attack Tanya's aerial mages directly instead of attacking the stronghold of Soldim 528. While heading towards the Headquarters of B Group, Tanya anticipates that the Federation aerial mages were going to come after her battalion since she knows that they are like hunting dogs, not only this, but turning back while their prey are right in front of them is something that troops from the Federation would never do, she also anticipates that the Federation aerial mages will come after them without not even taking some time to consider their tactics since they wouldn't be capable of changing their plans on the fly, not only this, but capable commanders would try to improve the situation by forcing through the plan that was set in advance rather than wasting time bouncing ideas around. She even suppresses the mana signals of her troops in order to guarantee that her plan will work perfectly. With this, Tanya aims to take the enemy aerial mages out of her way so that she can focus uniquely on attacking the Federation foot troops going to attack the Headquarters. Tanya advances in the direction of an enemy infantry soldier, but unexpectedly for her, the past battles she faced made her be on a lack of ammo, she didn't have any ammunition anymore. She glances down on the ground and perceives an imperial position on the verge of being stormed by enemy infantry, she recognizes that if the imperial troops get overrun, things won't end well for them. That's why she brings her shovel unhesitatingly on an enemy soldier and gives a direct strike boosted by gravitational acceleration since she literally hit this soldier while descending at an extremely high speed.

Of course, this attack was only going to eliminate a single enemy infantry soldier, but there would still be many others around that position. Tanya is aware of this, and that's why she uses her defensive shell and protective film to send the other soldiers in the area nearby flying before finally making landfall. She grabs the gun of one of the fallen infantry soldiers for her in order to solve the problem of being with a lack of ammunition. With this, Tanya manages to complete the mission provided to her indirectly by General Von Zettour and eliminates the enemy soldiers attacking B Group's Headquarters, this allows for the Imperial forces to perform the breakthrough now that the Federation forces were weakened. With this entire strategy, Tanya also managed to solve the problem of being attacked by the enemy multinational unit, the Federation aerial mages, Mary Sioux, the problem of having been sent to the region of Soldim 528, a region that they never went before, the problem of being with a huge lack of resources and the problem of having to reach the Imperial Headquarters in order to defeat the Federation enemy soldiers attacking the Imperial Headquarters of B Group in a really short period of time, doing all of this in a highly impressive and effective Strategy with length of one entire week.

This is also a really good adaptability feat for Tanya since the situation of Mary, the multinational unit and the Federation aerial mages appearing was completely unexpected for Tanya and she managed to adapt in a few seconds to this totally unexpected situation in order to efficiently lead her battalion efficiently. The Strategy/Plan effectiveness was also considerably high due to the fact that the empire managed to break through the Federation forces and advance to the city of Josefgrad in the Russy Federation's territory. Not only this, but now that the empire managed to break through their defenses, the Federation didn't have any other choice but retreat, which means that the Federation army's headquarters would immediately cease any possible attack that they could be making on Tanya's Kampfgruppe that is still in Soldim 528 and completely focus all their efforts on reuniting their soldiers and regrouping to strengthen their defenses against the Imperial breakthrough in the Eastern Front. With this, Tanya would be capable of ensuring the safety of her subordinates in the Kampfgruppe that were previously surrounded by the Federation forces and solve the problem of being surrounded by enemy forces, now they don't need to continue defending the stronghold of Soldim 528 since the Andromeda operation is now under progress in the Federation territory. Not only this, but the planning part of this strategy would make Tanya and Zettour solve the problem of the Federation’s strong defense that didn't allow the empire to advance.

This entire strategy was an insane demonstration of a trap setting feat since this entire situation was a trap made by Zettour and Tanya. A good summarization of the trap would be something like this: Tanya's Kampfgruppe would purposely be surrounded by the enemy to force the Eastern B Group to save them, after this, General Zettour would go to fight against the Federation army all by himself, which would once again force Eastern B Group to go and fight against the Federation, actually guaranteeing that they would do something this time, after these events, Zettour would purposely leak the location of their headquarters to the enemy, in order to indirectly manipulate the Federation to attack them, with this, Tanya's Kampfgruppe that was surrounded would join the battle and kill the soldiers sent to destroy the headquarters from behind, eliminating many of the Federation soldiers in that region and allowing the empire to make a full-scale offensive against the Federation with way less enemy resistance than normal. All of this was a single plan executed by Zettour and Tanya that allowed the empire to enter even more inside the Federation's territory and lowered the military power they had significantly.

Ninth Volume

During the events of the Ninth Volume, Tanya and Uger have a talk at a Cafe that they visited frequently in the past, while they were talking, Uger said that he had bad news to provide to Tanya and said: “I think I mentioned it before, the let's-be-friends-again party I was planning at my buddy's house”. Tanya analyzes the words buddy and party said by Uger and comes to the conclusion that he was talking about making up with someone, given the context of the message he was trying to pass, Tanya reached the conclusion that he was talking about the peace talk negotiations via Ildoa. After figuring this out, Tanya questioned him about it and Uger revealed that the negotiations were not coming together, he says: “Sadly, the distance has grown too large. I didn't feel like talking anymore, so I simply got up and left”. Tanya instantly recognized that Uger was referring to himself as being the Imperial leadership here, she quickly came to the conclusion that the Imperial leadership wasn't capable of tolerating the discomfort of the relationship between Ildoa and the Empire becoming more distant and decided to abandon negotiations. Demonstrating an impressive Verbal reasoning feat from Tanya.

(VCI, Abstract reasoning, Abstract thinking)

After some time have passed ever since her talk with Uger, Tanya was invited to go alongside General Rudersdorf to a funeral in the rear in order to see how destructive and harmful the war was being for the empire, after leaving the funeral, Tanya makes an extremely apathetic comment that made Rudersdorf perceive her as being extremely ignorant of people's feelings. Rudersdorf verbally calls Tanya an idiot for not understanding the human heart and says that he is disappointed in her, this makes Tanya freeze and she feels extremely offended by Rudersdorf comments, this entire situation was extremely humiliating for her. After this, Rudersdorf lowers his head in apology and Tanya thinks that Rudersdorf is actually mocking her, despite all these things occurring during her walk alongside General Rudersdorf, Tanya managed to keep her composure and keep under control the urge to make any counter-argument about his words.

(Intra EM)

After having parted ways with Rudersdorf, Tanya went to sleep, but she quickly woke up due to having been summoned by the General staff, she immediately went to the General staff office only to receive the news from General Rudersdorf that Ildoa has made the disarmament of the imperial troops a fundamental condition for the peace talks. After hearing this, Tanya came to the conclusion that Rudersdorf summoned her in the middle of the night too request an attack on Ildoa, due to having expected such a thing, Tanya foresaw multiple possible futures about how she would make an assault on Ildoa, the worst one of these possible futures being to assault Ildoa while being crammed into a V-1, another one of the possible futures she foresaw was to Schugel pop out from behind the door to reveal that he will cooperate with her on this assault on Ildoa. But unexpectedly for Tanya, foreseeing all these possible futures was useless since Rudersdorf didn't summon her to attack Ildoa.

(General Foresight)

During the events of the Ninth Volume while explaining the details of the Operation Barbaroi for her subordinates, Tanya reveals that General Romel is an extremely famous person who is a victim of constant stalking from the enemy intelligence. She anticipates that bringing General Romel back to the home country, which is quite literally the main focus of the operation, will make the armed forces of the Commonwealth finally do something in order to hold him back and keep him from leaving.

(Anticipation)

After Tanya finished explaining the details of Operation Barbaroi for her subordinates, Colonel Von Lergen appeared and explained to Tanya that he was going to ask for her to do the impossible. After this, Colonel Von Lergen says: “The technical Arsenal has prepared a key that will unlock a few doors for us, I need you to insert it into the keyhole”. Tanya sees the hidden meaning in this message in a few seconds since these were the same words she heard when she performed the Operation Lock Pick in the Rhine Front, an operation where she had to flown into enemy territory with the use of a V-1, a torpedo that flies extremely fast in a straight line and can't be redirected, she quickly perceives that Colonel Von Lergen will request for her to do such a thing again simply based on his vague words.

After this, Dr. Schugel appears in the room and tells Tanya that he prepared the “key” for this operation. Dr. Schugel then guides everyone towards something that has a big cover thrown over it. Tanya once again remember the events of the Operation Lock pick, she states to be capable of seeing the future in this situation and know exactly how things will play out, she's so certain about her anticipation that she compares it with literal clairvoyance claiming that Clairvoyance is hers for this one moment. Of course Tanya doesn't have clairvoyance, this merely shows that Tanya is absolutely certain about her anticipation about how this entire situation with the Torpedo made by Dr. Schugel will play out.

The Operation Lock Pick was an extremely terrible experience for Tanya that made her hate V-1s, something that she demonstrated multiple times throughout the entire Light Novel, when Colonel Von Lergen told Tanya that she was going to perform another mission with the V-1, Tanya completely lost her composure which is quite impressive considering how good her composure and emotional management is(she didn't had any reason to keep her emotions under control here so this is not an anti feat for her composure/Intra EM). After this, Dr. Schugel revealed to Tanya that she wasn't going to fly in a V-1, but she was going to fly in a V-2, an improved version of the V-1 stated by Dr. Schugel to be the most perfect holy-guided torpedo, after hearing such a thing, Tanya used her acting skills to pretend to be happy about Dr. Schugel’s words about how impressive the V-2 was, but in truth, she was extremely saddened by the fact that she was going to fly to enemy territory on a V-1-like torpedo once again. Even after Dr. Schugel revealed that she would be flying alongside literal explosives that will also be located inside of the V-2 and after hearing Schugel literally claiming that the guarantee that she won't be blown away by the explosives depended on God's feelings(i don't know if you remember about it, but Tanya is quite literally God's number 1 enemy in the world of Youjo Senki. So for Tanya, the fact that Dr. Schugel stated that the only guarantee that she wasn't going to be blown away by the explosives depending solely on God's feelings meant for her that her death was practically certain here) Tanya managed to keep her composure and continue using her acting skills perfectly without demonstrating any signs of being nervous.

(VCI, Intra EM, Acting skills, Anticipation, Pattern Recognition, Abstract thinking, Abstract reasoning)

After some time, the submarine where Tanya is located enters the Ildoan sea, they are quickly surrounded by the Ildoan Navy and scouted to the port. In Ildoa, Tanya meets Colonel Calandro once again and starts to verbally talk with him, which demonstrates that Tanya also must have knowledge about the Ildoan language, in other words, Tanya knows how to speak Italian. The fact that she was reading a newspaper from Ildoa also implies the idea that she knows Italian.

(VCI)

Tenth Volume

While Tanya was talking with General Zettour about the new operation similar to the revolving door that he's working on in the Eastern Front, Zettour asks Tanya about what the enemy could do next, Tanya then replies by saying: “Despite realizing it’s what we want them to do, they will knowingly play into our hands to force us to show that very hand. It’s not exactly the same as the revolving door operation on the Rhine front, but I believe this is a good opportunity to encircle them”. After this, Zettour questions Tanya about how she would manage to encircle the enemy forces, she replies by saying: “A pincer movement targeting the salient’s base would be the textbook approach. Once we cut the fools off from their country, we can close ranks and achieve local superiority…”. She formulated this response to Zettour's question in an instant. This is a highly impressive demonstration of a strategic thinking feat from Tanya.

(Strategic thinking, Fast thinking)

While Tanya is still talking with General Von Zettour, he says: “Just like on the Rhine front, we’re only allowed to retreat a distance that will enable us to conduct a counterattack. These are the orders from the homeland. Assuming this is the case, then we can’t simply encircle the enemy while we retreat. There has to be more to it than that”. After hearing Zettour saying this, she abducts that he is planning to make a frontal assault on the enemy forces, but she was wrong. Zettour says that they don't have the time nor the obligation to mount a frontal assault, saying that they would take a more deceptive approach, after saying this, Zettour pointed his finger to an specific location on his map and after seeing it, Tanya abducted his entire plan to deal with the enemy forces and even figured out what he wanted for her to do in an instant, which also demonstrates impressive PSI and Fast thinking. By observing the area where he was pointing at and his previous words, she managed to completely discern his true intentions which is highly impressive since Zettour's intentions are so difficult to read that even Tanya failed to evade a little trap made by him on this volume. This is a highly impressive mind reading feat considering how cunning and difficult to read Zettour is. Basically, what Zettour wanted was to attack an enemy base far deep at the Federation’s territory, he wanted for Tanya to act as a bait to attract the enemy's attention while his subordinates would be attacking the enemy forces.

(PSI, EP, Hot reading, Abductive reasoning, Critical thinking, Fast Thinking, Holistic Thinking, Connecting The Dots, Mind reading)

During the events of the tenth Volume, Tanya and Visha went to attack the enemy multinational voluntary unit forces, while they both were going to attack Lieutenant Colonel Drake who is the leader of the multinational voluntary unit, Drake shoot at them with an explosion magic formula trying to scare both Tanya and Visha since fire is a natural fear that all human beings possess, something that Drake himself states: “Fire should strike fear into the hearts of all!”. Despite such a thing, Tanya and Visha fearlessly flew through the explosion formulas made by Drake, demonstrating an impressive Intra EE feat for Tanya since her determination to conclude the mission is such that she surpassed human beings’ natural fear for fire.

(Intra EE)

After having defeated Lieutenant Colonel Drake, Tanya thinks about how reckless his decision of quite literally acting as a suicidal bomb simply to kill her. While thinking about this, she recalls the moment where she was studying for her college entrance exams in Japan, which demonstrates impressive academics for Tanya.

(Academics)

Throughout the entire Light Novel, Tanya managed to conceal her identity from the other countries in the world for more than 40 years, this is highly impressive considering how good the information gathering of the enemy countries are. For example, during the events of the tenth volume of the Light Novel, it's shown to us that the Russy Federation’s information gathering is completely insane, the Federation’s intelligence is capable of gaining information about something as irrelevant as General Zettour's preference for Black Coffee instead of tea and even gained information about Tanya's preference for Black coffee over tea too.

The Federation's intelligence is so insane that they literally managed to gain information about the Empire's entire plan in the tenth Volume to lure them to their territory and encircle the Federation army, even being capable of predicting, to a certain extent, many of the decisions made by empire in the tenth and eleventh Volume, also being capable of predicting some of Zettour's actions like sending Tanya to attack one of their bases, which is highly impressive considering that he is stated to be a military genius and considering the fact that he is so unpredictable that even Tanya was deceived by him once(not only Tanya, but there's no one in the General staff that can predict Zettour's actions, which also includes Colonel Uger and Colonel Lergen).

The Federation intelligence was so insanely absurd that even the high-ranking officer, Drake, stated that their analysis of the future and information gathering was almost at the level of absolute perfection. Their information gathering is so good that after the events of the Fifth Volume, the Russy Federation always knew the whereabouts of Tanya's battalion at every moment due to the fact that Loria(the leader of the secret police in the Russy Federation, which is practically the same thing as the KGB in the real world) created a group to gain information about the Imperial battalion's whereabouts 24/7. Not only this, but during the events of the twelfth Volume, they managed to gain information about the empire's entire plan to make the capitalist side the greatest contributors in the war, which would minimize the participation of the Federation in the war completely and lower the influence of communism in the world.

As if the Federation’s insane information gathering wasn't insane enough, the Commonwealth also has a really good information gathering via being capable of decoding the empire's code for messages in the army. With this, they were capable of figuring out the exact instant and the exact place in the sky where General Rudersdorf would be flying in an airplane, they even knew that the one inside the airplane was General Rudersdorf and this was an information that was carefully concealed by the empire and was only known by the Higher-ups of the empire and Tanya's battalion that was scouting the airplane. Despite their intelligence gathering being so insane, both these countries and all the other ones involved in the war(Unified States, Ildoa, François Republic, Entente Alliance) failed to gain any information about Tanya's real identity. The reason for this being because since the very beginning of the Light Novel, Tanya always made it extremely difficult for anyone from the enemy side to gain any information about her.

How does she do this? Simple, Tanya always eliminated all the enemy aerial mages she faced to guarantee that there wouldn't be any witnesses about her existence, such a thing was so efficient that she completely concealed her literal existence from the Commonwealth, who considered the “Devil Of The Rhine” as being a myth created by the traumatized soldiers of the François Republic. Not only this, but she managed to conceal her real identity from the Federation and all other countries in the world that were involved in the war despite being the commander of the empire's most deadly aerial mage unit. The fact she successfully concealed her real identity under these circumstances and for 40+ years demonstrates impressive concealment skills. Here are the scans for the Concealment feat mentioned.

(Concealment)

Eastern Front Strategy

During the events of the tenth Volume, Tanya received orders from General Von Zettour to act as a decoy for the enemy forces while he groups his subordinates to attack the Federation, in this strategy, Tanya will face the multinational voluntary unit and Mary Sioux, who was already mentioned many times in this doc to be extremely powerful, for you to have an idea, she managed to survive a direct hit from an optical sniping formula made with Tanya's Type 95 computation Orb, she's capable of using magic formulas stated to literally alter the world, she also has insane magic aptitude and magical prowess way beyond Tanya even with the Type 95 Computation Orb, Mary’s raw magic aptitude is so great that Tanya herself acknowledged her as being a “damn monster”, she even stated that her ridiculous amount of magic defied all logic during the events of the Tenth Volume, Tanya even said that she was like Being X due to how insanely powerful she was. Tanya will have to face the entire multinational unit and Mary Sioux with only one soldier at her side, that being Visha, in other words, she is quite literally fighting a 2v36 against the multinational voluntary unit. Now let's see how this strategy progress:

After receiving her orders from General Von Zettour, Tanya went alongside Visha to the enemy base in order to wait until they send their aerial mages to try and intercept them. She foresaw that the fact that she came with only a single person at her side would make the enemy think that she was simply there for a recon-in-force mission, she also wanted them to think that she is really there for a recon-in-force mission when in fact, her actual goal is to cause damage to the enemy Base and eliminate the commanders of the military voluntary unit. Tanya recognizes that even nowadays, there aren't many soldiers who are capable of flying at an altitude above six thousand feet and reach a height of something like eight thousand feet, due to knowing about this, she decided to give orders for Visha to climb to an altitude of eight thousand feet alongside her, the reason for such an action being because Tanya wants to use the advantage of having aerial superiority over her enemies and she wants to use it on her favor by taking full advantage of such a thing. Now that she clearly has aerial superiority compared to her enemies, it will be extremely difficult or possibly even impossible to hit Tanya and Visha thanks to Inertia, while it will be extremely easy for both of them to shoot at the enemies since they wouldn't have to worry too much about being killed by them since altitude difference would make it extremely difficult for the enemy shots to hit Tanya and Visha thanks to Inertia.

But there was a problem with this tactic of flying at an altitude of eight thousand feet, the enemy soldiers from the multinational voluntary unit also managed to increase their flight altitude to eight thousand feet, but as impressive as this might sound, such a thing was already anticipated by Tanya and was considerably necessary for how she plans to eliminate the enemy aerial mages. Due to already having anticipated that the enemy forces would try to find a way to increase their flight altitude to eight thousand feet, Tanya and Visha raised their flight altitude to ten thousand feet after the enemy aerial mages went to intercept them, in other words, it's highly probable that Tanya and Visha were flying at eight thousand feet simply to indirectly manipulate the enemy soldiers into coming to intercept them due to thinking that they could have a chance against Tanya now that they could fly at the same altitude as her, a false idea that Tanya wanted for the enemy to have, which would make them confidently leave their base and intercept Tanya and Visha. This is also an impressive trap setting feat from Tanya.

Now that she managed to attract the enemy aerial mages and still maintain aerial superiority, Tanya and Visha were clearly at an advantageous position here. Tanya also purposely increased her altitude to ten thousand feet in order to indirectly manipulate the enemy soldiers into climbing to reach both Tanya and Visha in the sky, what she wants to do here is force the enemy aerial mages into climbing until they reach their limits and breathing becomes difficult, when such a thing occurs, Tanya and Visha will strike while the enemy aerial mages are having difficulty to grasp the situation due to lack of oxygen coming to the brain. Which demonstrates an impressive trap setting feat too. But suddenly, Mary starts to advance quickly towards Tanya and Visha aiming to eliminate both of them. Tanya analyzed the mana signal produced by Mary and quickly recognized who she was, she recognized that Mary was the same aerial mage that caused trouble to Visha during the events of Soldim 528 Strategy/Plan. The enemy aerial mages try to shoot some formulas at Tanya and Visha in order to cover Mary on her reckless advance, but due to altitude difference, they both manage to dodge the enemy attacks easily without any trouble.

After a few seconds, the enemy aerial mages stop covering Mary Sioux in order to prepare magic formulas to shoot at Tanya and Visha, in order words, they were all preparing magic formulas to attack them and there was no one shooting formulas at the moment, which means that the enemy aerial mages were now completely defenseless since everyone is preparing their magic formulas instead of having a few aerial mages shooting magic formulas while the rest of them prepares magic formulas to shoot at Tanya and Visha. When Visha noticed such a thing and warned Tanya about it, they immediately started to dive on the enemy aerial mages in order to utilize their height advantage to drop down and close in on the enemy fast. With this, Tanya uses the laws of physics to her advantage by converting altitude into pure speed that will be so quick that wouldn't even allow for the enemy forces to react efficiently. Tanya recognized that the fact that the aerial mages were shooting formulas in unison means that their movement is restricted and they are being commanded by two different people. Tanya already had anticipated that the enemy aerial mages were being commanded by two different commanders and anticipated that there would be a moment where they would be preparing their magic formulas in unison, which would make them completely defenseless.

(Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Leadership, Cunning, Psychological Warfare, Trap Setting, Anticipation, connecting the dots, abductive reasoning, critical thinking, Strategic deception, Misdirection, Non-Verbal deception, mass deception, Logical manipulation, indirect manipulation, mass manipulation, Gaslighting)

Due to the fact that the enemy aerial mages weren't used to flying at such a high altitude, they were focusing mainly on maintaining their flight altitude instead of focusing on the movements made by Tanya and Visha, which gave an opportunity for them to attack the enemy aerial mages and even this situation was already anticipated by Tanya. While flying and evading the enemy magic formulas, Both Tanya and Visha knew each where other was at all times, visualizing the location of their partners while evading the enemy magic formulas, showing a level of coordination that Lieutenant Colonel Drake, who is commanding the enemy forces, considered to be virtually superhuman. After this, Tanya and Visha started to advance on the enemy aerial mages and they managed to misdirect the Lieutenant Colonel Drake into thinking that they were going to advance in order to clash with Mary who was flying towards them quickly. But in fact, Tanya recognized that Mary was kind of the enemy aerial mages' tanker, she was capable of enduring extremely powerful attacks from the imperial soldiers without any difficulty, that's why both Visha and Tanya completely ignored Mary in order to advance on the enemy aerial mages behind her, Tanya and Visha purposely were flying towards Mary in order to misdirect the enemy into thinking that they planned on clashing with her, but when they were going to collide, Tanya and Visha flew past Mary and started to rush towards the enemy aerial mages.

Tanya and Visha were flying at an insanely high speed towards the enemy aerial mages in a way that it was extremely difficult for any of them to react to what was occurring. When they were close enough to the enemy, Tanya and Visha took advantage of their proximity to the enemy aerial mages and unleashed three explosion magic formulas each before making contact with the members of the multinational voluntary unit. The results of this impressive attack made by Tanya and Visha were that only the battle-hardened Federation aerial mages managed to survive while all the rest was completely eliminated. The fact that they were doing a classic formation of concentrated and disciplined fire also made things easy for Tanya and Visha to eliminate almost everyone since the enemy aerial mages were extremely close to each other. The reason as to why Tanya and Visha chose explosion formulas was because it had a large area of effect.

Normally, an aerial mage would easily be capable of protecting himself from these explosion magic formulas, but only in normal circumstances, but the enemy aerial mages current circumstances were anything except normal, they were struggling a lot to keep their altitude at eight thousand feet and they weren't well adapted to the new environment of flying at such a high altitude. Something that made it difficult for the enemy aerial mages to defend themselves against the explosion magic formulas and was already anticipated by Tanya. This attack made by Tanya and Visha also resulted on many of the enemy aerial mages deciding to drop their altitudes in order to be capable of breathing properly and some other aerial mages focused on using air-purification magic formulas in order to be capable of breath and still maintain themselves at the aptitude of eight thousand feet, with this, some enemy aerial mages decided to drop their current altitude and a few other aerial mages decided to keep their current flight altitude, such a thing resulted on the enemy aerial mages formation being completely ruined and making them easy targets for Tanya and Visha. This demonstrates a highly impressive trap setting feat.

Tanya and Visha used three magic formulas, each one of them with three different purposes: the first one had the purpose to cause panic on the enemy aerial mages to make it difficult for them to defend themselves, the second explosion magic formula had the purpose to break their formation line in order to make the enemy completely defenseless, and the third one had the purpose to completely eliminate the routing enemy aerial mages. With this, all the least experienced enemy aerial mages were completely eliminated while only the most experienced ones survived. All of this occurred before Tanya and Visha even managed to enter in close-quarters combat with the enemy. Now that they were already close enough to the enemy aerial mages, Tanya and Visha started to approach the enemy aerial mages at an insane speed in order to initiate close-quarters combat with magically enhanced blades while they were still confused while not giving a chance for them to calm down and focus. After this, Tanya and Visha decided to split up, their aim here is to eliminate the commanders of the enemy aerial mages quickly while the enemy aerial mages are still confused and without knowing what to do.

Tanya's strategy to make the enemy aerial mages enter in a state of panic was extremely effective, which is proven by the fact that Federation aerial mages who were literally stated to be highly experienced were so confused about what was going on that they weren't even doing anything, they were simply waiting for their superior to give orders for them while their superior is literally being attacked by Tanya, despite seeing their superior being under enemy attack, they don't do anything other than wait for orders coming from Lieutenant Colonel Drake. With this, it was going to be really easy for both Tanya and Visha to eliminate the commanders of the multinational voluntary unit since their troops are so in panic that they are simply waiting for orders without doing anything helpful. Such a thing would make Lieutenant Colonel Drake, who is one of the commanders of the multinational voluntary unit here, be completely defenseless against Tanya's attacks since he wouldn't receive support fire from his allies. Something that was also already anticipated by Tanya. When Lieutenant Colonel Drake managed to make his subordinates start to give supporting fire against Tanya, she used an optical camouflage formula in order to conceal her current location from the enemy aerial mages by creating a highly convincing illusion that was extremely similar to Tanya and made this illusion act as decoy. Due to the fact that they are literally in the middle of a war, distinguishing what is real from what isn't is extremely difficult and the fact that Lieutenant Colonel Drake subordinates were panicking made it even more difficult for them to notice that they were shooting at a decoy.

(Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Anticipation, Trap Setting, Inter EM, Cunning, Psychological Warfare, indirect manipulation, mass manipulation, Logical manipulation, emotional manipulation, Gaslighting, insanity inducement, fear inducement, concealment, misdirection, Non-Verbal deception, mass deception, Strategic deception)

Tanya then continued to advance towards Lieutenant Colonel Drake while his subordinates were still confused and shooting at the decoy created by her. After a few seconds, Tanya managed to initiate a close-quarters combat with Lieutenant Colonel Drake and push him back by completely overpowering him, she had the advantage against him due to their size difference and positioning, Lieutenant Colonel Drake was already accepting the fact that he was going to be killed. After some time, Tanya managed to pierce Lieutenant Colonel Drake’s shoulder with her magic blade, but such a thing was part of his plan, he went so far as to cast an optical sniping formula at a distance of a few centimeters between him and Tanya, normally such a thing was considered to be complete insanity, Lieutenant Colonel Drake planned to explode both of them like a suicidal bomb simply to kill Tanya. After noticing what Lieutenant Colonel Drake was about to do, Tanya abandoned the weapon she used to pierce his shoulder and threw a gunstock at his gut, he was already low on oxygen and running out of blood, the pain caused by the gunstock hitting him caused an indescribable agony that nullified the magic formula he was preparing. This is an impressive adaptability feat from Tanya since she managed to think quick and throw a gunstock at Lieutenant Colonel Drake in order to use the fact that he is already injured at her advantage to nullify his magic formula milliseconds before it was about to go off, she had to think about doing such a thing and adapt to this situation in a matter of seconds, possibly she had to think about such a thing even in a matter of milliseconds, but that's a Mid-Ball to High-Ball.

After managing to stop Lieutenant Colonel Drake's attempt to explode both of them, Tanya kicked him away from the sky since he was extremely weakened due to low oxygen, low amount of blood inside him and having been hit by a gunstock stated to be rock-hard. After kicking him away, Tanya took the submachine gun that she stole from Anson Sioux, Mary's father, during the events of the Norden Sea strategy, and used it to eliminate Lieutenant Colonel Drake since her other gun was still stuck on his shoulder, not only this, but submachine guns are capable of shooting bullets at an impressively high speed. But Lieutenant Colonel Drake still attempts to take her down with his final magic formula, an explosion magic formula aimed directly at Tanya, but she easily managed to dodge it with emergency evasive maneuvers but she failed on her goal to eliminate him. Now that Lieutenant Colonel Drake was completely out of the battle since he was now unconscious, the enemy aerial mages were completely in panic and now they couldn't even rely on their superiors’ orders, they were now easy targets for Tanya and Visha. With this, Tanya and Visha didn't had anything to do there anymore, Tanya failed on her goal to kill the enemy commander and Visha failed too, now the only thing they can do is simply withdraw since continuing to fight against the enemy aerial mages was simply going to be a waste of energy, Tanya finished her withdraw and managed to leave the enemy lines, she continues to fly until she completely reaches a position out of the enemy's range.

But even if both of them failed in their mission and were going to return empty-handed, Tanya judged that what they managed to do against the enemy forces would be enough to satisfy General Von Zettour. But suddenly, something unexpected occurs, Tanya starts to feel a slight burning sensation and recognizes that the area where they were located was filled with pre-radiation from a magic formula. It was Mary Sioux who shot a magic formula at a distance of 4-5 kilometers since Tanya stated that she was barely capable of seeing Mary at their current distance, the fact that Tanya was surprised by the range of the attack indicates that not even Tanya or her subordinates are capable of shooting a magic formula at such a distance. After noticing this, both Tanya and Visha immediately begin evasive maneuvers in order to dodge the enemy attacks by pushing their dual cores type 97 Computation Orb to its limits, they both fly in a serpentine path to throw off the enemy's aim. But even with the Type 97 Computation Orb capable of flying at speeds way above 400 kilometers per hour, Tanya and Visha barely managed to escape Mary's magic formula. After noticing this, Tanya uses a magic observation formula to identify the source of the attack, that being Mary, despite her being at a distance of 4-5 kilometers. In other words, if you use Hax in Smart Character Debate, this is an insane perception feat for Tanya.

After having finished identifying Mary, Tanya perceived that she was now preparing two long-range magic formulas, one for Tanya and the other one for Visha, something that Tanya herself considered to be way too much magic for any single person to use, even calling Mary a “damn monster”. But such a thing wasn't a problem for Tanya or Visha since magic formulas at such an insane distance was merely for show, it's extremely easy to dodge them since it takes a considerable amount of time for the magic formula to travel through the distance between Mary and Tanya. But suddenly, Mary starts to prepare a ridiculously large magic formula of predesignated fire in order to blow everything to pieces; it's even stated that the magic formula was capable of warping the sky and producing shock waves so strong that Tanya managed to feel them at a distance of 4-5 kilometers. After noticing this, Tanya starts to make evasive maneuvers and descend at a high speed in order to avoid Mary's attack that would only affect those in the sky. With this, Tanya managed to avoid her attack. After some time, Tanya realizes that Mary is… stupid, really stupid and has a considerable lack of experience. She figured this out merely based on the fact that she was exactly like Being X, which turns this into an impressive intuition feat.

After realizing that Mary Sioux is stupid, Tanya has an idea about how to deal with this situation, she quickly calculates the coordinates and changes her position slightly, she makes this in order to bring the enemy aerial mage allies in the ground into Mary's line of fire. Mary actually falls on this trap made by Tanya and prepares a magic formula without thinking twice. When Tanya noticed that Mary shot a magic formula without worrying about what was behind her, something that any decent aerial mage would do, she was certain that Mary was an idiot. After this, Tanya and Visha continue to indirectly manipulate Mary into causing a friendly-fire against her own allies in order to use her insane magic prowess against her and take down almost all enemy forces. With this, they both manage to cause an accident of friendly-fire on the enemy forces while causing massive losses for the enemy. During the events of this Strategy, Tanya tried to deceive her opponents into thinking that she was really there for a recon-in-force mission, successfully deceived them into thinking that she was going to stay in the altitude of ten thousand feet and attack them with magic formulas form that altitude without advancing towards them, deceived them into thinking that her strategy involved advancing towards Mary Sioux in order to clash with her when she actually had the intention to attack the enemy forces, and also managed to deceive the enemy into thinking that her strategy involved eliminating all the enemy forces when her actual main goal was to eliminate the commander of the enemy forces. With this, she manages to easily conceal the true purpose of her strategy here. This demonstrates a highly impressive level of deception.
