Continuing with Volume 10

During the events of the tenth Volume of the Light Novel, Tanya and Visha were in the middle of an attack in the Federation's Base, but suddenly Tanya is attacked by a long-range magic formula. After this, she used a magic observation formula to identify the source of the attack, that being Mary, despite her being at a distance of 4-5 kilometers. In other words, if you use Hax in Smart Character Debate, this is an insane perception feat for Tanya. It's possible to say that the distance between Mary and Tanya was 4-5 kilometers due to Tanya's statement about barely being capable of seeing Mary without using a magic observation formula, the fact that the human sight can see at a distance of 5 kilometers strongly supports this idea.

(Perception)

During the events of the tenth Volume, Tanya remembers about Oda Nobunaga and the revolution he brought about, she is aware that his revolutionary military tactics were extremely impressive and his formidable defense literally went down in Japanese history. She even knows about a significant event for him that was the battle of Okehazama at Dengaku Hazama. Tanya also knows about the events that occurred before Imagawa Yoshimoto invaded Owari Province, also knowing that Oda Nobunaga decided to adopt defensive tactics by sending their samurai to behead Imagawa Yoshimoto and intercept their invaders. She also knows that this is how the Oda clan successfully defended their castle.

Not only this, but she also knows that if the Shogun Nobunaga had just sat idle in his castle waiting for the enemy to make the first move, his life would have ended with a valiant defense and his legacy would have been relegated to a footnote in history, only noticed by the odd history buff. Not only this, but she also demonstrated a few pages later to have knowledge about Oda Nobunaga’s actions when he was surrounded, when such a thing occurred, the Oda Clan chose to target the weakest links, the Asai and Asakura Clans, in the chain that bound him to win his freedom. This demonstrates Tanya's impressive knowledge about Japanese Culture and History, which is also an impressive VCI=[Information] and Crystallized intelligence feat for Tanya. She even has knowledge about Shimazu, one of Japan's most infamously insane warlords who had the habit of limiting his defensive actions to his sphere of influence.

(VCI, Crystallized intelligence)

During the events of the tenth Volume, Tanya was in a meeting at the foreign affairs office alongside Colonel Von Lergen and the counselor of the foreign office named Conrad, during this meeting, Colonel Von Lergen started to talk about military secrets in front of Tanya and she perceived that whatever he was going to say, was extremely bad, the reason for this being because she perceived the hesitation and inner turmoil lurking just behind his words, she completely perceived that whatever he planned to say wasn't good news. This is highly impressive since Colonel Von Lergen's acting skills are capable of almost deceiving even Tanya, it's said that despite having spent a lot of time with him in the military, Colonel Von Lergen seemed to be his usual self on the surface for Tanya, even going so far as to claim that if she didn't knew how much the General staff officers stressed keeping things concise, she probably would never had managed to recognize Colonel Von Lergen's ironclad poker face.

(EP, Seeing through people)

After some time, Tanya receives a mission from General Romel to attack the Commonwealth in a nighttime attack, the plan will be composed of two phases. After Tanya finished informing her troops about this new mission, Major Weiss came to talk with her and despite not having anything that suggested what he planned to talk about with her, Tanya managed to anticipate what he wanted to discuss thanks to the considerably high emotional understanding that she has on her subordinates. This demonstrates an impressive EU and mind reading feat for Tanya.

(EU, Anticipation, Mind Reading)

During the events of the Tenth Volume, Tanya and her battalion were taken by surprise by a trap prepared by the Commonwealth who managed to decipher the Empire's encoded messages and figure out that Tanya's battalion was going to attack the mainland. But despite having been taken by surprise in this situation, Tanya's battalion still managed to easily eliminate all the enemy forces without any problem, the aerial mages of the Commonwealth were so terrified by this situation that some of them were literally on the verge of having a mental breakdown due to how terrifyingly bad their situation against Tanya's aerial mage battalion was.

(Inter EM, Psychological Warfare, emotional manipulation, Direct manipulation, Individual manipulation, Mass manipulation, Insanity inducement, Fear inducement)

During the events of Tanya's strategy in the Commonwealth Mainland Battle, she used the Type 95 Computation Orb in order to eliminate Lieutenant Colonel Drake, which means that once again she had to suffer the effects of its curse, this means that Tanya had to suffer the effects of the Type 95 computation Orb's Curse eleven times so far in the story. And after such a thing, she still managed to maintain her sanity and she was still behaving normally.

(Intra EM, Intra EE)

Multiple times throughout the Light Novel, Tanya demonstrates to have knowledge about the laws of physics and Inertia, multiple times using it at her advantage in the middle of her battles, one example of this being when she charged on a bunch of enemy aerial mages with an altitude difference of two thousand feet in order to gather mass since the faster you are, the stronger your mass will be, in other words, the faster you are, the stronger your attacks and destructive capacity will be when you hit the target directly since you will accumulate mass thanks to the high movement speed. I think this is more than enough to prove that Tanya has knowledge about Physics.

(Crystallized intelligence, Knowledge, Knowledge Application)

Tanya's strategy in the Commonwealth Mainland Battle

Tanya and her battalion were going to make a surprise attack in the Commonwealth Mainland, but they were the ones who were taken by surprise, the Commonwealth managed to decipher the Empire's encoded messages and they were already aware beforehand that her battalion was going to attack the mainland. In this situation, Tanya only has a few battlecruisers to help her and she's aware that they would be defeated if they fought the Commonwealth directly, she also can't expect any help coming from Imperial submarines in this battle. Tanya will literally have to fight against an entire brigade of enemy aerial mages and their Naval Fleet stated to be the strongest in the entire world, she has to fight against all of them while having only an aerial mage battalion and a few battle cruisers at her side. Not only this, but retreating isn't an option for Tanya since she can’t abandon the imperial ships her battalion is supposed to protect. Now let's see how she solves this problem.

After thinking for some time about what she should do in this situation, Tanya gives orders for Visha to alert the ships that they can continue with evasive maneuvers without her battalion since they will draw the enemy mages away. Tanya recognized that it was impossible for her to defeat everyone in this situation with the current resources she has with her, that's why she decides to allow for the imperial ships to retreat with evasive maneuvers while her battalion will take down the enemy aerial mages since aerial mages move faster than a ship, in other words, the enemy aerial mages would be the first ones to reach the imperial ships and Tanya wants to avoid this. She doesn't need to worry too much about the enemy warships since they aren't fast enough to catch up with the imperial ships. After this, Tanya gave orders for her subordinates to get ready and fly circles around the extremely slow Commonwealth Fleet. Tanya then gives orders for Visha to prepare a radio broadcast for all signals since Tanya wants to send a message to the enemy: “This is the imperial army. Calling all pathetic amateurs. We're here to teach you a lesson. Enjoy the maneuvers we've prepared over in the Eastern Front”. This message has the purpose to provoke the enemy and induce anger in them in order to raise the chances of them committing mistakes due to being enraged.

After the provocation made by Tanya on the Commonwealth aerial mages, the brigade's movements weren't Uniform anymore and there were even some enemy aerial mages who were ignoring orders and moving Independently. It was clear that Tanya's provocation had successfully made the enemy aerial mages commit stupid mistakes induced by anger. With this tactic, Tanya managed to transform the enemy's aerial mage Brigade into an aerial mage Brigade of unorganized soldiers, now eliminating every single one of them was possible. The enemy aerial mages then start to scatter before charging on Tanya's battalion, such a thing made it possible for the enemy aerial mages to be taken in Friendly-fire, which would make things easier for Tanya and her aerial mage battalion. After this, Tanya give orders for her battalion to prepare themselves and they immediately focus on the battlefield, Tanya's aerial mage battalion then start to charge on the enemy forces, the reason for this being because an aerial mage battalion will inevitably be surrounded against an Brigade of aerial mages, in order to avoid such a thing, the aerial mage battalion must charge first and break the enemy's formation.

Having an altitude difference of two thousand feet compared to the enemy forces, all the four companies of Tanya's aerial mage battalion split up into four cone formations so that they can easily support each other on their charge. Her battalion will fall like four drills and penetrate the Commonwealth's unorganized line. While Tanya's battalion was charging towards the enemy, the enemy aerial mages stopped flying in order to aim and start firing, but such a thing makes them easy targets for Tanya's battalion and it easily allows them to eliminate the enemy forces, even the enemy aerial mages who used defensive shells to protect themselves were easily eliminated by the magic blades of Tanya's subordinates, they also use the help of gravity created by their charge on a difference in altitude of two thousand feet to pierce the enemy defensive shells without any problem. After getting close enough to the enemy forces, Tanya's battalion eliminates many of the enemy aerial mages with the use of magic blades and magic explosion formulas, being capable of eliminating a big portion of the enemy forces.

After a few seconds, Tanya and her first officer decided to lead some enemy aerial mages as preys to Lieutenant Wustemann's aerial mage company so that they can eliminate them, the reason for this being because Lieutenant Wustemann's company is the least experienced one in her battalion which is shown multiple times throughout the Light Novel. That's why she decides to lead some enemy aerial mages to them so that her subordinate's company can eliminate the enemy forces and gain some experience in battles. After this, Major Weiss' company starts moving fast and driving the enemy forward while Tanya brings her company with her to begin the hunt. The reserve company of Lieutenant Wustemann only needed to rush the group of confused enemy aerial mages and eliminate them one by one alongside Tanya's company. Unexpectedly for her, Lieutenant Wustemann's aerial mage company was having difficulty to fight against the enemy aerial mages despite Tanya's elite subordinates practically one-shotting them. But they still managed to beat all the enemy aerial mages anyway. With this, Tanya aims to efficiently make Lieutenant Wustemann's aerial mage company gain some experience in battle in order to turn them into professionals some moment in the future.

(Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Leadership, Social skills, Psychological Warfare, Intra EM, EF, direct manipulation, indirect manipulation, mass manipulation, emotional manipulation)

Still in the battlefield, Tanya gives orders for her subordinates to shoot explosion formulas at the enemy forces since explosion formulas are extremely useful to eliminate groups of enemy aerial mages since the range of the explosion is considerably high. Currently, Tanya's aerial mage battalion is clearly dominating the battlefield against the unorganized Commonwealth aerial mage Brigade. Tanya tries to use explosion formulas alongside her subordinates in order to increase the amount of casualties that the enemy aerial mage Brigade would face and try to rout the enemy forces, but such a thing wasn't effective since the enemies are maintaining unit cohesion on the whole. After this, Tanya give orders for First Lieutenant Grantz to protect First Lieutenant Wustemann's company and go take care of some enemy aerial mages in order to apply more pressure on the enemy forces since they are so weakened that their command chain is barely working and they probably wouldn't last another attack or two from Tanya's battalion. She also gives orders for him to push the enemy aerial mages away from their formation line in order to divide and conquer, in other words, force the enemy forces into staying away from each other in order to turn them into easy prey for Tanya's aerial mage battalion. She also gives orders for Lieutenant Wustemann to follow Lieutenant Grantz and observe his actions in order to guarantee that he will learn something on the battlefield and gain experience.

While giving orders for her subordinates, Tanya continues to attack any enemy aerial mage that dares to charge at her with a magic blade. After a long session of yapping in order to make Lieutenant Wustemann calm down, Lieutenant Grantz and Lieutenant Wustemann alongside their aerial mage companies go to attack the enemy forces, they skillfully toy with their catch and maneuver in a way to keep their attention away from first Lieutenant Wustemann’s company that is filled with newbies. The enemy forces are way too worried about any danger they see directly ahead of them, but such a thing was their doom, they weren't capable of maintaining situational awareness in all directions and were hit by Lieutenant Wustemann and Lieutenant Grantz from an angle they never saw coming. After this, Tanya's subordinates easily outmaneuver the enemies, isolating them from the Brigade in order to perform divide and conquer tactics, with this, Lieutenant Wustemann and Lieutenant Grabtz manage to take down an entire battalion of enemy aerial mages without suffering any casualties. After some time, Lieutenant Wustemann's aerial mage company starts to make proper charges on the enemy and it's clear that they don't need the more experienced members of the battalion to surround the enemies for the newbies. Noticing this, Tanya give orders for Major Weiss to allow for the newbies of Lieutenant Wustemann company to handle things from now on and perform the same job as usual, Tanya's battalion is known for their decapitation tactics of focusing on eliminating the enemy commander and that's what they plan on doing here.

Tanya reorganizes the first company that she has under her command in order to prepare to charge on the formation line and locate their commander to eliminate him. Tanya then gives orders for her subordinates to follow her towards the center of the enemy formation, eliminating the enemy commander is the only way for her battalion to win without having to waste many resources since they are vastly outnumbered. On Tanya's orders, Major Weiss and his troops begin to fly in alongside her and conduct their attack runs, they produce an inferno of explosion formulas that absolutely blow the Commonwealth mages who pepper the skyline out of the air. Tanya and her troops cut straight through the Commonwealth aerial mage formation and halfway through their attack, the Imperial Naval ships below do their best to send up some supporting anti-air fire against the enemy aerial mages in order to give support for Tanya's aerial mage battalion in their charge against the enemy formation line. Tanya's aerial mage battalion pushes their way past the enemies and her subordinates move toward their floating target, the commander of the Commonwealth aerial mage Brigade.

After flying for some time on the enemy's aerial mage formation line, Tanya finds the commander of the aerial mage Brigade and gives orders for her subordinates to advance towards him and kill him before he can even process what's happening. With this, Tanya's aerial mage battalion manages to completely eliminate the enemy commander and it was now a matter of time before the enemy forces collapsed completely, without having someone to give orders for them, the enemy aerial mages would be completely panicked and in despair at their current situation, they were being completely eliminated by the imperial forces and now they were all by themselves without not even having someone to give orders for them and tell them what to do, such a thing obviously threw the enemy aerial mages at an state of panic and confusion, which made things extremely easy for Tanya and her battalion to eliminate a big portion of the enemy forces. With this, Tanya managed to turn the enemy aerial mage Brigade into a bunch of completely useless soldiers who weren't capable of doing anything other than shooting desperately or dying at the hands of her battalion, now the enemy Brigade represented practically no harm to her aerial mage battalion. After taking down the enemy aerial mage Brigade commander and almost completely rendering useless the enemy forces, Tanya gave orders for her aerial mage battalion to quickly sink the Commonwealth warships in order to guarantee that they wouldn't be capable of taking down any of the imperial warships.

(Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Inter EM, EF, Psychological Warfare, Leadership, Social skills, resource management, indirect manipulation, mass manipulation, emotional manipulation)

Unexpectedly for Tanya, Lieutenant Colonel Drake was still alive and he decided to take command of what remained of the enemy aerial mage Brigade, he also took an entire company of Commonwealth elite aerial mages with him. Now Tanya will have to fight against a company of aerial mages and what remained of the enemy aerial mage Brigade, but this is not the problem here, the problem is that Lieutenant Colonel Drake is by far the best commander that the Commonwealth had with them, he was so insane in tactical and strategic prowess that he was even capable of matching Tanya and counter many of her traps, their tactical prowess and tactical decisions was extremely similar, so this is kind of a Lelouch Vs Xing Ke situation but Tanya can't relax for a single second here since she's in the middle of an intense battlefield where losing focus will guarantee her death, not only this, but Lieutenant Colonel Drake managed to motivate the brigade of aerial mages into returning to battle, but not for the sake of protecting their homeland, but to simply eliminate the enemy through what Tanya considered to be a demonstration of pure barbarism, the enemy forces weren't fighting for their homeland anymore or for any kind of pride, they were now fighting solely with the purpose of murdering Tanya's battalion, with this, Lieutenant Colonel Drake turned the battlefield into what the LN stated to be a version of hell on earth.

Tanya warns her subordinates about the incoming elite enemy aerial mages, she says that the duck hunt they were performing on the enemy aerial brigade was over and her battalion immediately forms back up in a new formation after hearing this. Tanya does the same as Lieutenant Colonel Drake and urges her troops into fighting against the enemy with the pure purpose of murdering them. After some time, both Drake and Tanya give orders at the same time for their troops to fire against the enemy forces, Tanya giving orders for her subordinates to shoot at Drake's troops and Drake giving orders for his troops to shoot at Tanya's battalion. With this, the battle begins with both sides laying into their enemies everything they had at the exact same time. A battle of raw firepower unfolded where the elite mages on either side prioritized a quick victory over preserving their strength, with this, the battle became an endurance test for both Tanya and Drake. The aerial mages on each side gambled their victory on massive magic formulas that created chains of explosions that lit up the night sky in order to take down the enemy.

While Tanya's subordinates gave their best to keep their defensive shells active in order to stay alive in that chaotic battlefield, Tanya gave orders for her subordinates to stay in formation in order to effectively attack the enemy forces and keep the outgoing violence tightly coordinated, Drake gave the exact same orders as Tanya for his troops at the exact same time. Both sides were fighting at lung-collapsing altitudes where expert aerial mages on either side relied on the uppermost limits of their knowledge and experience as the battle raged on, both sides constantly used explosion magic formulas for suppression, though they constantly looked for the opportunity to land the occasional hit. Both sides also used Lure and deception formulas to try to conceal their presence and take the enemy forces in a trap in order to kill each other. After some time, both sides closed the distance to use magic blades to attack the enemy while fully preparing for hand-to-hand combat in the air, but such a thing was merely a bluff, despite their willingness to use explosion magic formulas at point-blank range, an uneasy distance emerged. Both sides ebbed and flowed, going back and forth as they read each other's movement at the highest level. The reason for this being because they wanted to induce the enemy into charging towards them in order to take them on a trap. Both Tanya and Drake had the same idea and planned on using the exact same trap, which resulted in their tactics countering each other.

After this, Tanya flew at the center of her battalion's formation in order to make an organized attack. After grouping with the 203rd aerial mage battalion, Tanya gave orders for her subordinates to direct magic formulas on the enemy forces, both sides tried to create holes in the formation of their enemies in order to suppress each other's strength with their firepower. After some time, Tanya's battalion manages to take control of the skies for a second time in this battle under Tanya's orders, after this, she also gave orders for each company forming her battalion to charge and not get boxed in by the enemy's suppression fire by keeping the enemy pinned down with their speed. Tanya planned on using Inertia once again in order to quickly charge towards the enemy forces before they can react and perform decapitation tactics by eliminating the enemy commander, Lieutenant Colonel Drake. But in the Commonwealth, there was no one who was more familiar with the empire's decapitation tactics than Lieutenant Colonel Drake, that's why he managed to analyze and react to the 203rd aerial mage battalion's movements with a speed that was unlike anything Tanya had ever seen before.

(Leadership, Cunning, Social skills, Psychological Warfare, Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Trap Setting, direct manipulation, mass manipulation, bluffing, Non-Verbal deception)

After easily dodging the tentative decapitation tactic from Tanya's battalion, Lieutenant Colonel Drake gave orders for his troops to fill the entire sky with fire in order to force Tanya's battalion into abandoning control of the sky and give a chance for him to take control of the sky. After this, Lieutenant Colonel Drake gave orders for his troops to shoot concentrated fire on the enemy forces, something that was already anticipated by Tanya and her battalion so they didn't have any difficulty avoiding this trap by performing some evasive maneuvers. Tanya's aerial mage battalion was already prepared to avoid any magic formula that the enemy try to shoot at them since decapitation tactics quite literally depended entirely on getting dangerously close to the enemy, due to knowing exactly how to deal with such a situation, they could easily dodge an enemy attack without any difficulty if they had to face this situation. After this, Lieutenant Colonel Drake made his troops disperse randomly while advancing to fire back while they're strafing. The fact that they were pulling off such a counter meant that the enemy was capable of seeing through Tanya's battalion maneuvers. Tanya's aerial mage battalion still managed to dodge the enemy magic formulas with ease since they were highly experienced in fighting in air battles.

After realizing that the elite enemy aerial mage company with Drake as their leader was far too dangerous, Tanya gave orders for Visha to reorganize the troops of the 203rd aerial mage battalion in order to force their way through the enemy forces. After this, Tanya's battalion abandoned maneuvering around the enemy for a better position and began climbing to the sky as fast as possible, now the entire imperial unit was surging upward at once. Tanya's aerial mage battalion climbs to an altitude of 14 thousand feet, an altitude that was completely unreachable for the enemy forces and Tanya wanted for it to be this way, she didn't wanted for the enemy forces to follow her battalion and it was an altitude far too high for the enemy forces to attack them. In this insane altitude, Tanya's aerial mage battalion began to reorganize and create a new penetration formation to make their way through the enemy formation in order to attempt another decapitation tactic against Lieutenant Colonel Drake. Climbing to this altitude also made the newer troops of the enemy side lose their nerves since they were way too impatient to wait until Tanya's battalion returns to battle. Such a thing would raise the possibility of them committing any mistake and would make them less useful on the battlefield.

After some time, Tanya's aerial mage battalion plunges towards the enemy forces at a speed that not even the Commonwealth mages could react to due to the insane altitude difference and the laws of physics. They were going to plunge directly to eliminate Lieutenant Colonel Drake and there was nothing he could do to stop them with his current lack of good resources. Lieutenant Colonel Drake was taken in an unavoidable trap that would guarantee his fall in this battle. Despite Knowing that there was no way he was going to survive this battle, Lieutenant Colonel Drake decided to cast a magic formula in order to take down Tanya once and for all. Tanya and Lieutenant Colonel Drake were about to clash against each other with both of them going to attack each other with the only purpose to kill their opponent in this battle. Both of them chose the most reliable way to kill each other, the only way to kill an enemy aerial mage that is a guarantee that they will be killed, it being the use of explosion formulas at ultra-close range. They were both planning to use multiple magic formulas against each other. They both were aware that using magic formulas so close was something that could endanger the caster as much as it threatened the enemy, it was essentially a tactic that was just one step below suicide bombing.

Due to being aware of such a thing, they both managed to calculate/anticipate an extremely tiny window of opportunity where they could pull off something that seemed to be impossible, by doing such a thing, they would be capable of time their attack attack perfectly while transfer all their magic into their protective film and defensive shell immediately after casting the magic formula. They would have to make such a thing in an interval of literal milliseconds before they are affected by the explosion and they were successful in doing such a thing. They both planned on bringing the enemy within range, then threw up a strong enough shield to survive the ensuing catastrophe, even if they were caught up in the explosion, the fact that they tried to shield themselves would allow them to have a chance to survive. The chances of surviving were extremely small, but as long as they could take down the enemy in front of them while having a chance to come out of the battle alive, the decision was already made. But unexpectedly for them, they both made the exact same decision to shoot a formula at ultra-close range, such a thing resulting in the explosions merging and being stronger than expected, they both were taken on the large explosion, but Lieutenant Colonel Drake managed to survive by pouring all his mana into his defenses.

But Tanya made an even more drastic decision: she decided to use the Type 95 computation Orb and push all its four cores to their absolute limits,  Tanya also continued to use magic to create her own oxygen since the explosion deprived the entire area around them of oxygen. While doing this, she was also preparing a powerful magic formula to shoot at Drake in order to guarantee his death and even opted to cut off a handful of other formulas. Here, Tanya uses four different magic formulas alongside putting some mana to raise a proper defensive shell. Both Tanya and Lieutenant Colonel Drake executed the same move and also used the same tactics throughout their battle, the difference of quality in their equipment was the only thing that led to Tanya's victory which demonstrates how much of a difficult opponent Lieutenant Colonel Drake was. Tanya's victory over the enemy commander raised her troops morale while it lowered the morale of the enemy forces to the point where the enemy Brigade of newbies were retreating due to fear of dying.

After the fall of Lieutenant Colonel Drake in the battlefield, Tanya gave orders for her subordinates to make a volley fire of three explosion formulas on the elite commonwealth aerial mages that didn't had a commander anymore, thanks to this, Tanya's battalion explosion formulas was insanely effective and the enemy forces were eliminated completely. With the enemy aerial mages completely out of the way of Tanya's aerial mage battalion, the Commonwealth warships following the imperial ships were now completely vulnerable for the attacks of her battalion. With this, Tanya and her battalion managed to create a path for their imperial allies in the ships to escape the Commonwealth warships. After this, Tanya gave orders for her subordinates to retreat since it was highly probable that reinforcements from the Commonwealth would come to give support against the imperial forces; her battalion already did enough to cover the imperial Navy's retreat and there was no problem in retreating anymore. With this strategy, Tanya managed to take down the enemy Brigade of aerial mages that had been the core of the Commonwealth's air defense, doing this while keeping the imperial losses to a minimum despite having been ambushed. This entire strategy made by Tanya is also an impressive demonstration of a Trap Evasion and problem solving feat since Tanya was completely taken by surprise in this situation due to being ambushed by the enemy forces who had time to prepare themselves for her battalion's attack. She also managed to completely adapt to the situation despite not having expected it and even adapted to an opponent who had a level of tactical prowess at her level. What's even more impressive is the fact that this entire situation counts as an improvisation feat, Tanya's original plan here was to make a sneak attack on the enemy, but this plan was completely ruined since the enemy was aware of their attack, so Tanya had no other choice but to completely improvise a whole new strategy to survive in this situation and adapt to any adversity that appears. With this, the third tab of this document comes to an end.

Eleventh Volume

During the events of the Eleventh Volume of the Light Novel, General Von Zettour told Tanya that he wanted her to kill General Rudersdorf. He explained that he wasn't capable of accepting defeat and that in the empire's current situation, he was like a Cancer that must be eliminated. But hearing her superior’s words, Tanya says that Zettour's words are absurd and claims that she doesn't have any other choice but to shoot him to defend her dignity. After this, Tanya explains how killing General Rudersdorf wasn't going to be a good idea for Zettour. She says that eliminating Rudersdorf was a waste of good human resource, the empire is not in a position where they can afford to lose their Higher-ups so easily, nevermind throwing them away. She says that if Zettour wants to kill Rudersdorf, the empire needs to ensure a return to all the investment that went to General Rudersdorf.

Not only this, but she also says that If they kill General Rudersdorf, all that will be left for them is a single murder. But if they were to stop the mastermind behind a coup d’état, it would serve a purpose in increasing their influence. After this, Tanya suggests that they should focus on devising a plan for what happens after they foil General Rudersdorf’s Plan B. She says that his death would trigger a selective purge of soldiers within the army. They could use this confusion to bring the Supreme High Command under the control of the General Staff Office and effectively create a short-term central command for the war. With this, Tanya makes General Zettour forget about the idea of eliminating General Rudersdorf and even manages to recover their relationship temporarily since General Zettour and Rudersdorf will have a fight in the future that will drain General Von Zettour's patience almost completely, which will result on Zettour asking Tanya to kill Rudersdorf. Anyway, this is an impressive demonstration of Social skills, direct manipulation, relationship management and persuasion.

(Social skills, Relationship Management, direct manipulation, individual manipulation, persuasion)

During the events of the eleventh Volume, Tanya receives a request from General Von Zettour to kill Rudersdorf, the reason for this being because Rudersdorf was being unreasonable about what the empire should do to avoid losing the war. During an scout of an imperial airplane, with Rudersdorf inside it, by the 203rd aerial mage battalion, a Commonwealth bomber airplane appears and an entire battalion of enemy aerial mages start to attack the imperial airplane in order to assassinate Rudersdorf, in the end, the airplane is hit by a magic formula and Rudersdorf is now dead. After this, Tanya went to talk with General Zettour about what occurred and revealed to him that she's certain that the Commonwealth cracked the empire's codes for communication. Her observations for this were simple:

P1: During the events of Tanya's strategy in the Commonwealth Mainland Battle, the enemies unexpectedly managed to predict that her battalion was coming and prepared themselves for their arrival.

P2: Now during Tanya's mission to kill General Rudersdorf, the Commonwealth once again manages to predict that General Rudersdorf would be flying at an specific airplane, at a specific location and at a specific moment, such a thing obviously couldn't be a coincidence.

P3: The fact that Rudersdorf would be flying at that specific airplane was something that was only mentioned in an encoded message by the empire.

FINAL CONCLUSION: The Commonwealth Intelligence managed to crack the imperial army codes for communication and managed to figure out that General Rudersdorf would be flying on an airplane at that specific moment through the encoded messages.

(Abductive reasoning, critical thinking, Holistic thinking, Non-Linear thinking, Observation, Connecting The Dots)

After finishing her mission to attack Ildoa and the royal Ildoan military army, Tanya receives a call from General Von Zettour and while talking with him, she hears him grinning through the phone and figures out that whatever he plans to say isn’t going to be good for her. This demonstrates an impressive level of Emotional perception since a person's grin doesn't produce any kind of sound, instead, the act of grinning or smiling while saying something can result in subtle changes on a person's voice, something that Tanya managed to perceive here. What makes this even more impressive is that she managed to perceive this through an audio call which would make it difficult for Tanya to recognize any changes in Zettour's emotions or voice. With this, the eleventh Volume comes to an end and sadly Tanya didn't receive many buffs here.

Twelfth Volume

During the events of the twelfth Volume, General Zettour and Colonel Calandro had a meeting in order to discuss a cease-fire between Ildoa and the empire(Tanya is also in this meeting in order to guarantee that Calandro won't try to harm Zettour). During the events of this meeting, General Zettour reveals to Colonel Calandro that the invasion of Ildoa was made solely with the purpose of forcing the Unified States into joining the war against the empire. After hearing this, Colonel Calandro’s mind was motivated to kill Zettour by an urge of sheer panic about how absurd Zettour's words were, despite Calandro not showing any signs of wanting to kill Zettour in the surface, Tanya managed to read his mind and figure out that he wanted to harm the imperial General, she then chimed in to make her presence known for Calandro.

(Mind reading)

During the events of the tenth Volume, while Tanya was talking with Colonel Von Lergen, they received orders from command and Tanya anticipated exactly which orders they received. Tanya, Colonel Von Lergen, the 203rd aerial mage battalion and the imperial 8th panzer division will perform a full-scale attack on the enemy field troops and they will surround the enemy forces from the flank. This is a highly impressive anticipation feat since there were tens even hundreds of different possibilities of orders that Tanya and Colonel Von Lergen could have received, but Tanya easily managed to figure out the exact orders given to them by the army's command, Tanya's anticipation was also confirmed to be correct by Colonel Von Lergen.

She anticipated this based on the fact that according to the orders General Zettour gave, the field soldiers sent in to defend the Ildoan capital were unable to retreat for political reasons. Therefore, the Empire had successfully managed to use the capital as bait to trap the enemies in their current position. With this in mind, there was only one plausible course of action the Imperial Army had left to take…and that was a full-scale attack. The Empire would likely allocate half of their armored forces to create an echelon to penetrate the enemy line south of the capital, taking out any field troops who would try to establish their base while posing a counterattack with any surviving troops. It was simple. Their enemy had spent the last week accumulating as much firepower as they could for one goal: to protect the capital. The Empire would, in turn, crush the culmination of all their effort in a single blow.

(Anticipation, deductive reasoning, critical thinking, Holistic thinking, Observation)

One thing that I didn't mention so far but was mentioned multiple times during the story of the Light Novel is that Tanya can hear alarm sirens inside her mind when she feels that she is in danger. In other words, everytime Tanya is placed in a dangerous situation, she hears alarm sirens inside her mind that tell her that something is wrong. This demonstrates an impressive intuition and perception feat from Tanya.

(Perception, Intuition)

During Tanya's talk with Zettour at Ildoa, it's revealed to us that she has knowledge about law which was already kind of implied multiple times throughout the LN but only now it was clearly implied in a way that doesn't sound like biased glazing.

(Crystallized intelligence, Knowledge)

During the events of the twelfth Volume, Tanya did an exercise training with her troops from the Kampfgruppe, the result was… disappointing to say the least. After putting an end to this exercise, Tanya recalls all her subordinates movements during the exercise training despite suffering with a headache due to how frustratingly bad the exercise was. She recalls that her subordinates are mobile and can maintain their mobility for long periods of time. Even if it's only an exercise, their movements alone are quite nimble. They aren’t afraid to advance when they need to and can maintain unit cohesion even while withdrawing. But a few seconds after recalling her subordinates’ movements and actions during the exercise training, Tanya recognized that what they were doing was basically the same thing as what they did on the Eastern Front. Demonstrating an impressive pattern recognition feat.

(Memory, Pattern recognition)

After some time, Tanya received sealed orders from General Von Zettour, the contents of the orders were for Tanya's Kampfgruppe to secure the area around the Ildoan Capital. After reading this, Tanya starts to think about these orders and abducts that her superior plans to do a strategic reorganization of the war fronts, also reaching the conclusion that these orders to secure the area around the Capital have the purpose to give time for the empire to perform this strategic reorganization.

P1: Tanya received orders from General Von Zettour to secure the area around the Ildoan Capital.

P2: The fact that General Von Zettour gave orders for Tanya to secure the area around the capital and not the capital is strange, such a thing implies that he has no intention of focusing on the capital at all.

INITIAL CONCLUSION: Maybe it's meant to be some sort of diversion or a ruse to distract international observers. Anyone who sees the empire secure the area around the capital and set up defensive positions would assume that the imperial army is adopting a defensive position.

COUNTER-CONCLUSION: But what if such a thing is merely a deception planned by Zettour? Considering his personality and strategic thinking, such a thing makes sense.

P3: Then this means that the imperial army's true goal is something else.

P4: Tanya is fairly confident about her understanding of Zettour's intentions. She can't really imagine him pushing for the empire to occupy the Ildoan peninsula in its entirety.

P5: After some time, Tanya recalls that the empire already did something like this before during the events of the revolving door operation in the Rhine Front.

SECOND CONCLUSION: Maybe General Von Zettour is planning on abandoning the Ildoan capital to perform a second revolving door operation.

COUNTER-CONCLUSION: But after a few seconds, Tanya recognizes that this idea is ridiculous. The empire has only just taken the city, normally, securing it is the obvious next step. And when it comes to the Capital, the political significance is tremendous. Securing the surrounding area would usually imply securing the capital itself. This much should be a given. Would the Empire ever voluntarily abandon such a strategic point? The empire obviously wouldn't do such a thing.

P6: The orders given to Tanya and her Kampfgruppe are to secure the area around the Capital. So it makes sense that the rest of the world would make the assumption that the empire is fortifying its defenses in Ildoa.

THIRD CONCLUSION: What such a thing would mean for the war? The answer is simple, it's precisely the same thing the empire did on the Rhine Front. In the empire's current situation, retreating is easy, General Von Zettour is obscuring his real intentions of making a strategic reorganization of the war fronts. The orders given for Tanya's Kampfgruppe to secure the area around the Ildoan Capital have the purpose to give time for the empire to perform this strategic reorganization.

P7: But Tanya still don’t understand the point of occupying the capital for a few days, only to
retreat right after. Is it political? Or is there a military goal?

P8: Perhaps it’s an appeal to the Empire’s strength, as if saying that we’re still powerful enough to capture the capital.

FOURTH CONCLUSION: If that’s the case, then the answer is simple. It’s important to behave in a way that is easy for others to understand. We have to demonstrate that we are still powerful enough to capture the Capital. Whatever we do, it needs to be overt and forceful enough that there’s no room for misinterpretation. It’s all an act. A performance.

FINAL CONCLUSION: General Von Zettour is obscuring his real intentions of making a strategic reorganization of the war fronts. The orders given for Tanya's Kampfgruppe to secure the area around the Ildoan Capital have the purpose to give time for the empire to perform this strategic reorganization. Not only this, but occupying the Capital for a few days has the purpose to demonstrate to the world that the empire is still powerful enough to capture the Capital. It’s all an act. A performance.

This also demonstrates an impressive Analysis feat from Tanya since she abducted General Von Zettour's plan merely based on the orders given to her.

(Abductive reasoning, critical thinking, Holistic thinking, Linear thinking, convergent thinking, divergent thinking, Observation, connecting the dots, Analysis)

We will go to our campsite, start a nice fire here, cook some meat there, splatter some bloody meat everywhere, then help ourselves to whatever canned goods we can find. Everything we need is already at the campsite, so it’ll be a great time”. Here are the subtle messages hidden inside Tanya's words:

We will go to our campsite(we will go to the enemy base next to the Ildoan Capital)

Start a nice fire here(set the entire enemy base on fire)

Cook some meat there(explode our enemies so much that their bodies will be completely carbonized)

Splatter some bloody meat everywhere(eliminate all the enemy soldiers around the base)

Then help ourselves to whatever canned goods we can find(And after doing all this, we shall take any resources from the enemy that we can find)

Everything we need is already at the campsite(all the resources we need to collect are already at the enemy Base)

Thirteenth Volume

During the events of the thirteenth Volume, while talking with Colonel Lergen and Colonel Uger, Tanya demonstrates a completely unrivaled Social Cognition feat. This will be extremely long…

(Tanya’s Thoughts)

“The Empire’s approach to operational command is based around transmitting targets to commanders. To put it in extreme terms, the Empire’s approach to orders is akin to saying, We are going to provide our guests with dinner, so acquire enough steaks for four people. In the extreme example, what kind of steaks per se, and how to acquire them, is up to the commander to decide. They could buy their own preferred cut and cook it to their liking. Or if they were worried the flavor would be underwhelming, they could telephone a steakhouse and have steaks delivered. They could even place it all into the hands of a talented chef who just happens to live next door. Everything is permitted. On the other hand, if the only option is game meat, then the commander’s only choice would be to take the classic approach and go hunting.

Even an outsider can likely understand that much. But that is just the tip of the iceberg when it comes to how the Empire does things. The byword of necessity means that commanders of all ranks are expected to go above and beyond as a matter of course. Using the example of steaks, a commander might interpret those orders to mean, The fundamental requirement is to provide dinner, but a different menu would serve perfectly well for vegetarian guests, and so, rather than steaks, they’d provide a vegetarian meal more to the guests’ liking. This would qualify as a clear-cut example of observing the intention of the order, even while not taking any of the directions literally for the particulars. Within the Empire’s organizational culture, this is the fundamental role expected of commanders at all levels.

But what about by-the-book orders, with oversight of everything down to the smallest details? The basic framework of the order, Provide steaks for four people, might be the same. However, the finer points would be clarified in the manual, with a scheme to ensure that the same results could be achieved regardless of who’s carrying the orders out. For instance— Provide steaks for four people from Steakhouse A. All steaks must be cooked medium-rare. If catering from Steakhouse A is not available, instead procure 150 grams of red meat per person from Butcher B, at a budget of up to 3,000 yen per person; cook steaks to medium in accordance with the standard manual for steak preparation; arrange and serve in a simplified fashion as appropriate to wartime conditions.

If 600 grams of said meat cannot be obtained solely from Butcher B, Hamburg steaks may be permitted (but only if command is informed and prior consent is obtained). If preparing Hamburg steaks, the ratio of ground meat must meet or exceed military standards. If obtaining the appropriate meat from a butcher proves difficult, report back immediately. Under this approach, the issue would be whether this litany of eventualities could account for all circumstances. For instance, if the existence of vegetarians has been accounted for, then a clause such as If vegetarians are present among the guests, providing suitable vegetarian meals can be included in advance, allowing the commander to make such arrangements without delay. But if not? The order is to provide steaks. And if, unfortunately, Steakhouse A is able to provide catering for four? Well then, the order can be fulfilled.

The commander can arrange steaks for four people. Naturally, upon realizing that one of the guests is a vegetarian, an obvious issue will arise. But the order given leaves no room for interpretation. It calls for steaks from Steakhouse A. At that moment, the commander on the ground will be left in a dilemma. Follow orders, or commit insubordination? These are the obvious differences between the Empire’s approach and a by-the-book approach to giving orders. At a glance, the Empire’s way of doing things seems superior. An organization like the Empire, composed of officers who can think for themselves, obviously seems preferable at first glance.

A place where people can do what needs to be done, appropriately and without constant interference from higher-ups over every little thing. It would seem to be the ideal workplace. The reality, however, is that the latter type of organization is far more robust. And why is that? The answer is simple. Because the latter system allows anyone to fulfill orders to a reasonable degree. The Empire’s style relies on an unspoken agreement among commanders, with results and on-site adjustment evidently relying on every person understanding the role they need to play for the greater goal. Imagine another unit has been tasked with providing a wine that pairs well with the steak.

If the person responsible for providing the steaks has suddenly, in their judgment, decided to switch to a vegetarian menu instead, then ideally, under the Empire’s approach, once the person has informed others of the change, the one in charge of providing the wine would, of their own accord, pay special attention to the fining technique used and choose wines that don’t use any animal products. For instance, in response to requests for “an X-year bottle of A,” they might decide, Naturally, I will secure Wine A, but even without being told to do so, I will also secure a wine suitable for vegans. The servers and valets would then take similar care. All without anyone needing to give directions. As long as information is shared, each person will be able to make an appropriate judgment.

This is what allows the Imperial Army to turn quickly on a dime… They are highly adaptable to changes in circumstance and, when necessary, can even do the unthinkable and ignore finalized plans. The Empire’s strategic planning takes such flexibility for granted. However, without mutual understanding and confidence of one another and of how each unit will behave under the circumstances, such flexibility is impossible. In other words, what would such an arrangement look like when they are placed outside their own backyard? Considered in that light…I tremble. With close acquaintances such as Lergen and Uger, it would be one thing, but if told to cooperate with a colonel I know almost nothing about? How am I supposed to figure out how this unknown colonel might act during large-scale maneuvers in enemy territory?”

What's more surprising is that this is merely one of Tanya's inner monologues throughout the story, with almost each inner monologue buffing her Social Cognition. If we consider every Inner monologue shown by her in the story, we can easily perceive how Tanya is capable of demonstrating an extremely in-depth understanding of all the Social variables of the Imperial army and the society as a whole(both the society of the world before she was reincarnated and the society of the other world after Tanya was Isekaied)

(Social Cognition)

Through the entire Light Novel, Tanya pretended to be a patriotic soldier in the empire, the reason for this being because she wanted to receive gain some benefits by pretending to be loyal to the Empire, what's impressive here is that she kept this Facade for 10+ Volumes of the Light Novel and no one suspected of the fact that she isn't a patriotic Nationalist, she managed to conceal this fact from everyone for literal years. She deceived General Zettour, General Rudersdorf, Colonel Lergen, all the people involved with the army she met during her military career and all her subordinates of the Salamander Kampfgruppe which also includes Visha, the same Visha that is capable of perceiving subtle emotions on people's words, the same Visha who is capable of comprehending Tanya's intentions in an instant and understand her intentions/thoughts merely based on vague words or facial expressions. Tanya managed to deceive even Visha verbally and Non-verbally which demonstrates an insane Deception feat from Tanya. In fact, Tanya even states that almost all, if not all, her subordinates from the 203rd aerial mage battalion have the ability to immediately understand her intentions due to how much time they spent together. Which essentially means that Tanya hid the fact that she wasn't a patriot and managed to deceive 48+ soldiers that had the ability to understand her intentions in an instant. The fact that this deception prolonged for literal years is also completely insane.

(Verbal Deception, mass Deception, Non-Verbal Deception, Acting skills, Concealment)

Zettour developed such a interest on Tanya that he literally gave an entire augmented battalion to her despite she merely being a Lieutenant Colonel(for you to be the commander of a battalion, you have to at least be a Major), the fact that Tanya's social integration on Zettour was so extreme that he gave to her the role of major despite her literally not having any experience in commanding a single company is highly impressive.

General Zettour had such a good impression of Tanya that he literally gave to her a whole augmented battalion that serves directly under the General Staff's command, such a thing is really rare for any imperial military group, in the own story, it's shown to us that the 203rd aerial mage battalion(the one commanded by Tanya) is the only imperial military group that responds directly to the General staff. This is an insane demonstration of a Social Integration, influence building and social skills feat from Tanya.

But these “aerial mages” aren't exactly “aerial” since they can't fly at all, such a thing doesn't really bother Tanya since she likes to fight against weaklings to preserve her own life. Noticing this, Tanya sarcastically says that First Lieutenant Grantz has a commendable spirit and that she will make the preparations for him to pick on strong enemies instead. How will she do this? Simple, Tanya says that she will send First Lieutenant Grantz into Federation territory so that he can take on an enemy garrisoned aerial mage battalion all by himself. Hearing this, First Lieutenant Grantz gets terrified, since what Tanya is proposing is sending him to a certain-death situation.

During the events of the Thirteenth Volume, Tanya and First Lieutenant Grantz go to the Federation territory for reconnaissance about what's occurring. During their reconnaissance, they spot a road dyed in white to be camouflaged with the snow, a large convoy alongside a hunt infantry unit with warhounds. Tanya then creates the hypothesis that what they saw in this reconnaissance: the large convoy that made clear that the Federation was planning an offensive and the war hounds patrolling the area, all these things are merely part of the whole thing that the Federation is planning. If that's true, then the enemy is gathering all throughout the back lines.

The enemy forces patrolling around the area that the large convoy passed didn't appear to look like normal infantry. They were an extremely well-equipped infantry, with no magic signature. If Tanya's previous hypothesis about these enemy forces making patrol being merely part of the whole thing the Federation is planning, then this means that differently from what the empire was expecting, the Russy Federation doesn't plan to make a Large-scale offensive in spring, their offensive will probably occur way sooner than that. And Tanya's hypothesis was proven to be correct later on in the Light Novel.

(Abductive Reasoning, Critical Thinking, Holistic Thinking, Observation, Connecting The Dots)

After the Federation full-scale offensive was initiated, the Eastern Army group decided to put their defensive plan in action to stop the enemy offensive, but the problem is that this defensive plan was made with the incorrect idea that the Federation was going to make an offensive at spring, the Eastern Army group didn't had enough resources to repel the enemy offensive at this current situation. Tanya noticed this, that's why she made the important decision of sending false orders under General Zettour’s name to the Eastern Army Group to force them into making a strategic withdrawal. What's important is that what Tanya is making here is basically something that no logical person in the military would try to do, falsifying orders from a General would definitely result in death by firing squad and Tanya is aware of this.

Despite being aware of the consequences of such a thing, Tanya still decided to put her plan in action and send false orders to the Eastern Army, thanks to these false orders, Tanya made the highly impressive feat of avoiding the empire's collapse, it was certain that the empire was going to be annihilated with this enemy offensive, but she managed to avoid such a thing and thanks to the fact that the empire didn't collapse with the enemy offensive, the Imperial army was capable of repelling the enemy offensive in the future and the empire not only survived this full-scale offensive, but they managed to counter the enemy offensive in the future and inflict heavy losses on the Russy Federation. With this, the Thirteenth Volume and the fourth tab of this document comes to an end.

Fourteenth Volume

While flying with the division of aerial mages that she took in order to counter the Rising Dawn Operation(this will be explained better in the “Operation Rising Dawn Counter Strategy”), Tanya figured out the position of her troops by merely using charted flight time and astronomical observations. (Crystallized Intelligence, Knowledge, Academics, Knowledge Application)

While leading a division of aerial mages with her battalion, Tanya finds a big convoy of Federation's supply trucks that apparently isn't being protected by aerial mages since Tanya doesn't detect magic signals, but she considers the possibility of hidden aerial mages and quickly calculates in a few seconds the possibility of a group of aerial mages hidden. “Considering my unit’s level of training, the absence of detected mages can only mean that none are present. A reasonable assumption. But can you be certain? What if there is something unique about their computation orbs that allows them to camouflage themselves? You’ve seen quite recently that such things are possible, right? During training exercises at the beginning of the year, perhaps? The voice of caution whispers, still insistent. The voice is right, of course.

Such tactical miracles don't exist in the world. But I choose to jettison those unnecessary concerns. If the Federation Army wanted to deploy mages to guard such a massive logistics train, they would be area denial troops. If they intended to intercept mages they weren’t even sure would come, it would make far more sense to send a traditional-force escort rather than to have mages lying in ambush. Besides, if that were the case—if some monomaniacal lunatic managed to prepare the kind of ambush I imagine—wouldn’t it have made more sense to have those troops slip through the imperial defensive line at the start of the offensive and strike the Empire’s aerial mages while they slept? In other words, there is no way enemy mages are present right now. What we need to do is obvious: hit the convoy with overwhelming force.”

This is a great demonstration of Analytical Thinking, Risk Calculation and Critical Thinking, something that Tanya spams on every single strategy made by her.
