(Intra EE)

Operation Rising Dawn Counter Strategy

In the very beginning of this Volume, we already see how chaotic things are on the battlefield due to the Federation's Rising Dawn Operation. Chaos could be seen anywhere and even the commanders were extremely stressed, while all of this occurred, Colonel Kramer received orders addressed to the Eastern Chief Inspector of the Eastern Command. This message obviously was the fabricated one made by Tanya, she used Colonel Lergen’s name and General Zettour's encryption to create an extremely convincing fabricated message that made it impossible for anyone to come to the conclusion that it could possibly be fake. It had Zettour's one-time encryption and the explicit mention of instructions “apparently” coming from him. Noticing all these things, the Eastern Command was seriously considering retreat merely based on an improvised message made by Tanya. She even mobilized her 203rd aerial mage battalion and gathered aerial mages to fight against the enemy alongside them to logically manipulate the ones in command, making them think that these fabricated orders were actually real.

Even if someone wanted to order her battalion to stop with this, they couldn't, Tanya's battalion responds directly to the General staff, so they have the authority to mobilize independently, without having to follow orders coming from other branches of the army. Tanya’s misdirection was fortified with many layers that made it look convincing, asking through the radio whether the orders were true or not wasn't smart at all since the message would definitely be intercepted by the Federation(and there's also the fact that it's completely impossible to know whether these orders are true or false since the current state of the front made it impossible for them to confirm whether the orders were true or false, and even if they somehow managed to contact Zettour to ask him directly, Tanya already sent some of her subordinates to explain everything to him and make him lie by saying that these orders were indeed true) and thanks to this message, the empire avoided many losses and also avoided the possibility of ending up losing the war against the Russy Federation.

(Strategy, Tactics, Methods, Fabrication, Misdirection, Mass Deception, Non-Verbal Deception, Logical manipulation, Decision Making)

When Tanya noticed that the only way to save the empire was to fabricate orders, she formulated a short strategy to do such a thing smoothly. The situation is as bad as it could possibly be, Tanya's battalion can't hope for the possibility of having reinforcements, replacements, or support units coming to join them, and the situation they're facing is way more terrible than anything that Tanya already faced in the past(including Soldim 528). The Russy Federation quite literally understood the disposition of the Eastern Imperial Army better than even the Empire itself. Of course, the chances of having success weren't guaranteed, but making such a strategy was the only thing she could do with such a short amount of time. L This strategy is a gamble with a 50% chance of failure and 50% chance of success. What I mentioned above about her fabrication of the orders was the initial aspect of her strategy. The orders told the Lieutenant General of the Eastern Command to put the “defensive plan number 4” in action, but such a thing didn't exist before. This isn't explained completely in the Light Novel since it's not one of the main focus of this Volume, but the best explanation here is that before the Lieutenant General even saw the fabricated orders, Tanya's subordinates fabricated this “defensive plan number 4” (alongside 3 other defensive plans in order to not make it look suspicious) and placed it inside his office, the fact that the orders written in this defensive plan were extremely vague also implies the idea that it was fabricated(Zettour wouldn't normally make such a vague plan without any motive and if we look at things from Tanya's POV, then it being so vague makes sense since she is in a hurry to save the empire).

Tanya made it look like the folder containing the defensive plan number 4 has always been there and the Lieutenant General simply hasn't noticed until now. With all these things, Tanya indirectly manipulated him into applying this defensive plan and following the orders mentioned on it. While all of this occurs, Tanya and the 203rd aerial mage battalion gathered a large number of aerial mages in order to persuade them to help in the counterattack against the Federation, Tanya gave orders for Major Weiss to talk directly with these aerial mages and spam sophistry to make his arguments about the reason as to why the 203rd aerial battalion suddenly got the authority to command an entire division of aerial mages. In the end, Major Weiss explains everything perfectly without showing the stress and anxiety on his face, having success in persuading everyone to help. While he's explaining everything, Tanya is making full use of Colonel Lergen's name by fabricating a mountain of orders to manage things and gather enough resources to resist against the Federation's offensive, she also made the necessary orders for the Salamander Kampfgruppe using her own name at its full extent, using all authority and influence that she could use at that moment.

Tanya's goal here is to deploy three entire regiments despite not having the authority to do such a thing and hammer the enemy's logistics, weakening their offensive so that the empire has more time to defend, she also aims to hit the enemy's second echelon. Even if her battalion fails in this mission, they would already have helped a lot and they could let General Zettour take care of their unfinished work. Through the entire execution of this strategy, Tanya spams critical thinking, considering the best actions that she should take to raise her chances of success, always making the best decisions that the situation allows her to make, even deciding to leave some friendly bases be eliminated without helping in order to not waste their resources meaninglessly.

But she perceives that Major Weiss was feeling bad due to this decision made by Tanya, since she can't afford to lose time discussing with him, she allows a certain level of support to the friendly bases. Her suggestion is to use friendly positions, isolated in the enemy territory, as stepping stones during their deployment. The troops would not be prohibited from providing temporary assistance with base defense when the situation calls for it. This way, while the Battalion wouldn't be going out of their way to save their fellow allies, they wouldn't abandon them either, she made this decision aiming to calm the burden on Weiss's mind. But this wasn't successful since he asked if they couldn't do extractions, Tanya understands that such a thing isn't possible, the enemy's rear and logistics train, which are their main targets, would require them to throw everything they can at them. The Battalion may be functioning as a substitute strategic air force, but she understands that saving as many people as possible isn't realistic and she already eliminated this idea from her mind. That's why she's mainly focusing on weakening the enemy's capacity to continue their offensive.

Tanya then finally decides to prohibit any support for ground units outside of their designated objectives for the simple reason that they can't afford to do so. This easily eliminates Weiss's suggestion of going to save allied forces, Tanya is mainly focusing on guaranteeing that the field army won't be completed wiped out by the Federation, if the Federation manages to wipe them out completely, then all their hopes of winning would be eradicated, Tanya even persuades Major Weiss to accept such an idea, saying that saving the field army units would save more lives than saving the ground units, making it clear to him which one would save more lives and be a better approach to this situation. After a few pages, it's shown to us that the Lieutenant General was deceived by Tanya's false orders and was indirectly manipulated into giving orders for all the soldiers to retreat as planned by her. With this, the entire Eastern Army Groups were indirectly manipulated by Tanya into retreating.

(Fluid intelligence=[Problem Solving], Strategy, Tactics, Methods, Fabrication, Misdirection, Non-Verbal Deception, Mass Deception, Indirect Manipulation, Indirect Manipulation, Mass manipulation, Strategic Thinking, Critical Thinking, Problem Solving, Decision Making)

With the imperial soldiers deciding to retreat, Tanya was finally on the process of achieving one of her goals with this strategy, lowering the empire's losses by avoiding as many meaningless deaths as possible so that they can have enough time to prepare themselves and defend themselves against the Federation's offensive, and even if she had success in doing that, she would still have to rely on luck and if the empire had a chance to beat the Federation with her help. Now, Tanya took the entire division of aerial mages with her battalion and under Tanya's orders, were deployed in three regiments to conduct air strikes on multiple different locations deep into enemy territory at once. Resisting against the Russy Federation's offensive in that area as much as possible. The division of aerial mages was sent to attack under Tanya's orders having a single goal in mind: hit the key supply points that were previously abandoned by the imperial soldiers, not giving a chance for the Federation to steal their supplies and use the supplies against them. Despite not having any experience in doing this, Tanya adapts herself to control a large group of soldiers using merely her direct speech without using radio and hand signs, she also adapted herself on the spot to be capable of skillfully commanding an entire division of aerial mages, despite not having experience in doing such a thing.

Tanya also gave orders for the aerial mages to make a contour flight since the Russy Federation would definitely be capable of picking up their radio signal if they climbed higher. Also having aerial mages on the rear to protect the division from sneak attacks coming from behind. But such a thing ends up making some of the aerial mages feel exhausted and crash on the ground, making Tanya's situation even more difficult. Noticing this, Tanya gives orders for Visha to tell them to make complete radio silence in order to conceal their presence from the enemy, the ones who aren't capable of coming back and joining the division should wait until the scheduled time of attack before unsealing their magic and returning to base. Another fact that is really important for Tanya's plan is that the disparate units that weren't capable of retreating in time would be mounting helpless defenses that will help the attacks of Tanya's division of aerial mages made against the enemy logistics by draining their ammunition and fuel stores. The Russy Federation will take some time to overwhelm these remaining strongpoints, even if it's not much.

Tanya is using these units as decoys to buy more time. In the sky Tanya's battalion and the regiment of aerial mages find a big convoy of trucks, Tanya understands that due to its size that truck must be a lifeline for the Federation army's Frontline troops. After calculating the possibility of aerial mages protecting the convoy, coming to the conclusion that it was impossible, Tanya gave orders for her troops to fire at the enemy convoy. Tanya gives orders for them to punch through and wipe the enemy convoys, with this, the Regiment that was being commanded by Tanya(the division of aerial mages divided itself in three different regiments which I already mentioned before, each one being commanded by a different person and attacking different areas) breaks Rank, reforming a moment later into an assault formation. After this, Tanya decides to break through with Visha and use the Type 97 Computation Orb’s speed at its full potential, quickly attempting to break through, Visha accompanies Tanya in order to cover her and keep her eyes focused solely on what lies ahead while she guards Tanya's flank.

When she is close enough, Tanya uses an explosive formula and destroys the supply trucks. Having success in messing with the enemy's logistics for now. Suddenly, the enemy air defense units were deployed to protect the cargo vehicles with a flak barrage. Tanya quickly adapted to this situation in a few seconds, cooperating with Visha to fire off magic formulas at the air defense units in order to decrease their movement speed. While all of this is happening, the Division of aerial mages striking in formations are attacking the enemy forces like an iron hammer. While Tanya's battalion is attacking the enemy air defense units, the cargo vehicles are retreating, using a smart strategy to buy time that impresses even Tanya, but this distracts her for less than a second for what came right after this. The Federation army unleashed a steady and carefully composed curtain of defensive fire into the air. The barrage of defensive fire was so insane that the LN itself says that it almost looked like the space belonged to Federation fire, with the sky coming in a distant second.

Tanya quickly adapted to this, using her defensive shell to protect herself from the enemy fire. After this, Tanya decided to adjust her trajectory, climb a little higher in the air and begin accelerating, ready to dive on the enemy cargo vehicles without hesitation. Tanya and Visha maintain exquisite coordination as they dive into the sea of the enemy's waiting anti-air fire, using explosion formulas to destroy as many cargo logistics vehicles as possible, they focus on the blast radius, spraying the area with magic rounds while unleashing occasional optical sniping formulas at the tanks whenever an opportunity arises. After all this, Tanya notices that the division of aerial mages as a whole was becoming sluggish. Noticing this, she decides to fly headfirst into where the AA fire is thickest and show the others that they’ll be fine if they’re careful. Attempting to motivate them to continue to attack the enemy's logistics efficiently.

She believes that doing such a thing is possible due to the fact that the anti-aircraft fire is dense enough to give American imperialism a run for its money. But blitzkrieg pilots would dive into fire from blitzkrieg altitudes without defensive shells, even if their survival chances were almost nil. By comparison, mages are relatively blessed. After all, the Type 97’s defensive shell can repel a direct hit from even a 40 mm round, a veritable mobile assault computation orb. As long as they have the Type 97, the likelihood of pulling out safely after trading somewhat explosive unpleasantries is actually quite good. Of course, the risk is still not zero. Safety and peace of mind are not identical, but the risk is within acceptable margins, I'll take these odds. As for cumulative risk from repeated passes, that all depends on where you set your time variable. Tanya calculates the risk by using impressive critical thinking and analytical thinking, making a great decision for the sake of the division as a whole.

(Strategy, Methods, EF, Inter EE, Leadership, Social Skills, Social Adaptability, Cunning, Critical Thinking, Analytical Thinking, Adaptability, Risk Taking, Decision Making)

Tanya gives direct orders for the aerial mages to follow her and dive straight in. Tanya and Visha plunge in headfirst, the one commanding the entire division of aerial mages and her adjutant, with the commander and her wingman leading the charge, the others have no choice but to persist and follow them. After six passes, the number of enemy anti-aircraft vehicles gradually became lower with gaps on their flanks beginning to show. Their attacks have also resulted in another tactical side effect, this is the fact that they can see better now. The strikes have left several ground targets in flames, improving visibility and improving their capacity to continue their attacks. After this, Tanya decides to lead from the front once again, attempting to motivate the bravery of these aerial mages. Cutting through the enemy flank and using magic formulas to cause great damage on the enemy.

Tanya's magic bullets went flying forward with unerring accuracy. As they reach a point above the enemy column, a massive magic explosion formula manifests in a vortex of destruction, far beyond what a truck’s thin skin could possibly defend against. Meanwhile, a magic bullet from Visha rips through the same area and the rest of the regiment follow close behind with their own attack runs. After all of this, Tanya uses words of provocation to motivate the aerial mages and raise their bravery, she understands that humans are creatures of pride and planned to use this in order to manipulate these mages. With merely a few words of provocation, the Imperial mages now fear potential ridicule more than the curtain of lead rising from below. After eliminating the logistics convoy in the area, Tanya decides to go quickly to her next target as fast as possible. They will search the roads and railways that the Imperial Eastern Command has identified as probable main supply routes and attack any supply trains we discover.

Additionally, Tanya plans to pick off any other logistic vehicles they spot if the opportunity presents itself. Since each regiment that is composing the division of aerial mages is attacking different places, Tanya would be capable of eliminating 4 entire logistics convoy if each regiment managed to destroy a single convoy in a single day(it's 4 due to the convoy that Tanya's regiment attacked just now + another convoy that she plans to attack on this exact same day). Each Regiment is keeping contact with each other, which allows Tanya to give orders through the radio when needed and gather information about what the other regiments see or do. Suddenly, the second regiment found two entire regiments of aerial mages from the Russy Federation, after receiving this information, Tanya sent a message to the Second Regiment, giving orders to prioritize fighting the enemy mage unit instead. Saying that for now, they can ignore logistic targets. After this, they have to return to base so she can keep their noses to the grindstone next time and she gives additional orders for them to clear the enemy quickly and then come back for food and rest. The way she quickly gives orders to the second regiment despite this situation being completely out of her expectations due to how quickly such a thing occurred, she thought extremely quickly and gave methodical orders to deal with this situation.

After these events, Tanya receives a report from the third regiment, saying that they didn't find any supply convoys and deciding to act on their own, they advance to the shunting yard, burning it down. Nearby, they found a depot and we're currently attacking it, showing that Tanya's decision to split the division of aerial mages in three different regiments is proving to be the best possible decision she could make. While looking for the enemy logistics, Tanya gives orders for her troops to use radar to scan the area, which could attract enemy mages coming to intercept them. Tanya is aware of this, but right now, not finding the location of the enemy logistics would be worse than having the regiment's location discovered by the enemy. But unexpectedly, her regiment of aerial mages is intercepted by enemy aircraft. This situation was completely out of Tanya's expectations, but she quickly adapted herself to this situation, giving orders for the regiment of aerial mages to regroup into combat formation. Tanya once again flies on the front, aiming to lead and inspire the imperial regiment of aerial mages. After a short fight, the enemy fighters decided to retreat.

Tanya originally had deduced the fact that the enemy air base was nearby, that's the only explanation as to how mere aircrafts with a single engine were capable of intercepting them so quickly. Having this idea in mind, Tanya decides to follow these aircrafts in order to find the enemy air bases nearby and destroy all their aircrafts. When they find the enemy air base, Tanya decides to give orders to prepare explosion formulas, saying that as soon as the flammable substances in the air base are blown away, they would move in and clear it out. She's going to make an airborne drop. With this, Tanya was capable of making a powerful attack on the enemy air base, while stealing maps from the Russy Federation and destroying all the resources on the airfield.

(EF, Inter EE, Social Skills, Leadership, Strategy, Methods, Information Gathering, Adaptability, Decision Making, Risk Taking)

After all these things, we see First Lieutenant Grantz’s perspective about the events, Tanya gave to him the order of sending a note to Zettour explaining what she did, she made this decision due to knowing that according to General Von Zettour's personality, he would probably ratify the orders and act like they are genuine since it was favorable for the imperial army in its current situation. When Grantz gave the note to General Zettour, he truly ratified the orders, confirming Tanya's prediction of his decision and great understanding of his personality.

After ratifying the orders, General Von Zettour lied to his own adjutant, shielding Tanya's violation of the rules in the military and the fabrication she made of orders coming from Zettour, while lying by saying that these orders were really made by him. Grantz also helped concealing everything by saying to people that he wanted to deliver a message to General Zettour (the note that was given to him by Tanya), but he never actually said what the note contained, so the information about Tanya's fabricated orders were only known by five people: Weiss, Visha, Zettour, Grantz and Tanya herself. And all of these people were either way too rational to do anything against Tanya or were completely loyal to her.

With this, Tanya completely concealed the fact that the orders were fabricated for +40 years in the future. Since it's mentioned in the events of the future that these orders were a decision made by Zettour instead of being something made by Tanya. With this, Tanya quite literally saved the Imperial Army from a massive loss and gave them a chance to survive against the Federation's Rising Dawn operation. After this event, Tanya continues to attack the enemy logistics with her battalion in order to weaken the enemy's power and give a chance for the empire to survive. Unexpectedly, during another one of Tanya's attacks on the enemy logistics, she receives the information on radio that the Federation Army was making an Airborne drop against the Eastern Army Command.

When Tanya heard this information on radio, she gave orders for Captain Meybert to guarantee that the Eastern Army Command doesn't get wiped out by the enemy, since the entire Eastern army would enter in a state of confusion due to not receiving any orders or instructions. She is so decisive about the idea that the Eastern Army Command can't be wiped out, that Tanya literally tells Captain Meybert to give full support to the Eastern Command, even if this means sacrificing the entire Kampfgruppe in the process. She made such a hard decision in a matter of seconds, which demonstrates Tanya's absurd capabilities of Leadership and decision making that literally allowed the empire to resist against the Federation's Rising Dawn Operation.

In the end, thanks to the support of the Salamander Kampfgruppe against the Federation army and Tanya's aerial mage division, which eliminated the reinforcements that were supposed to help destroy the Eastern Army Command, the Eastern Army Command managed to survive in a situation where it seemed they would be eliminated in a few hours. Easily solving this problem that appeared to be almost hopeless and having success in countering the Federation Army's initial plans. Not only this, but it's also shown in chapter 4 of this Volume that Tanya's decision of gathering an entire division of aerial mages to target the enemy logistics was the response to their full-scale offensive that was sure to hurt the Federation the most. Her decision was so perfectly aligned to the situation that even the Federation Army, which anticipated almost all the things that the empire would do during this operation, hadn't predicted an attack directly on their logistics with ALL the aerial mages that the empire had in the Eastern Front. Normally doing such a thing without hesitation and with such absurd decisiveness was completely unrealistic, which only demonstrates how absurd Tanya's decision making skills, Risk Taking, critical thinking and unpredictability are.

This is an absurd demonstration of a trap evasion feat performed by Tanya that allowed the empire to survive in an almost completely hopeless situation, with the Light Novel itself making a statement about how this was the best decision Tanya could make against the Federation army command. Here's a full Document explaining all the adversity that Tanya was facing here.

(Fluid intelligence=[Problem Solving], Strategy, Methods, Tactics, Leadership, EU, Concealment, Unpredictability, Fabrication, Decision Making, Risk Taking, Problem Solving, Prediction, Trap Evasion)

Processed 954k letters of a sheaf of multiple military personnel request forms in a few seconds during the events of the first Volume of the Youjo Senki Light Novel.

WMI

VSI
During all the events of Tanya's Northeast Front strategy/Plan in the second tab of this doc, she observes a map that is updated every single second based on the information given by those observing what's occurring in the battlefield, Tanya uses this map to visualize the position of the enemies, the position of her soldiers and the entire Northeast side of the Eastern Front(which is the place where the battle is taking place) that extends through 37 square kilometers, even if she uses a map to do such a thing which kind of debuffs this feat a lot, it is still quite impressive. In fact, the fact that she used the map to visualize the battlefield is completely irrelevant now since in the sixth Volume, she managed to visualize the entire Northeast side of the Eastern Front again but this time without having to look at the map since she memorized it completely, but this feat is inferior to the original one in some aspects since she's only visualizing the battlefield here while on the other feat shown in the fifth Volume, she was visualizing the battlefield, her soldiers and the enemy soldiers with the help of the map.

Short doc explaining this VSI feat

VCI

[Information]
Tanya knows how to speak seven different languages: German, Japanese, English(was stated to have perfect command over the English language btw), Ildoan(Italian in real life), Russian, French and Dacian(which is Romanian in real life)

Another good feat is that, during the events of the tenth Volume, Tanya remembered about Oda Nobunaga and the revolution he brought about, she is aware that his revolutionary military tactics were extremely impressive and his formidable defense literally went down in Japanese history. She even knows about a significant event for him that was the battle of Okehazama at Dengaku Hazama. Tanya also knows about the events that occurred before Imagawa Yoshimoto invaded Owari Province, also knowing that Oda Nobunaga decided to adopt defensive tactics by sending their samurai to behead Imagawa Yoshimoto and intercept their invaders. She also knows that this is how the Oda clan successfully defended their castle.

Not only this, but she also demonstrated a few pages later to have knowledge about Oda Nobunaga’s actions when he was surrounded, when such a thing occurred, the Oda Clan chose to target the weakest links, the Asai and Asakura Clans, in the chain that bound him to win his freedom. This demonstrates Tanya's impressive knowledge about Japanese Culture and History, which is also an impressive VCI=[Information] and Crystallized intelligence feat for Tanya. She even has knowledge about Shimazu, one of Japan's most infamously insane warlords who had the habit of limiting his defensive actions to his sphere of influence. In fact, throughout the entire story of the Light Novel, Tanya demonstrated in-depth cultural and historical knowledge involving the countries of: Mongolia, Japan, Germany, France, England (she literally appears to have read many of Shakespeare's works), Russia and a few other countries.

[Vocabulary]
She studied at the empire war college which is the best military-related academy of the empire that also teaches to its students things like linguistic and other languages, there was a moment in the first Volume of the Light Novel where she made a military report that was stated to be the kind of report that would only be made by a professional or someone with high experience due to the impressive analysis and linguistic proficiency, she's also a bookworm and read many books when she still studied in the empire War college. Not only this, but during the events of the fifth Volume, it's implied that Tanya already read some poetry in her past life which is implied once again in the Fourteenth Volume. Another thing is that during her past life, Tanya studied for college entrance exams in Japan which are insanely difficult. She also appears to have read many of Shakespeare's works.

[Similarities]
During the events of Tanya's Strategy/Plan to survive at Soldim 528, she receives an urgent message from high command: “From General Von Zettour to the Lergen Kampfgruppe. Colonel Von Lergen promptly began the designated actions. At first sight, this message doesn't make any sense for Tanya. But after analyzing it, she figures out the hidden message contained on this message from high command. Tanya observes that the words “Lergen Kampfgruppe” and “Colonel Von Lergen” followed a specific pattern, they were all outward-facing labels, Tanya then guesses that the same is true for the “designated actions”.

She then guesses that the actual message should be read with these words removed: “promptly take action”. After thinking for some time, Tanya notices that action, in the end of the message, is assertive spontaneity. In imperial army staff officer education, it's told for the soldiers that their role is to carry out the mission they're given and to obey not the letter but its intent.

Tanya then starts to think about the intention of this message from Zettour's point of view. Based on all the previous major operations initiated by him like the one used to destroy the Republic, he's an expert with an exhaustive knowledge of logistics, his personality certainly rules out passively waiting with no plan, it's even possible that he could seize the initiative independently, he would even favor an aggressive breakthrough action. After thinking about this, Tanya reaches the conclusion that the word “promptly” in the message should indicate “assertiveness”.

Zettour has a very radical side and apparently the Higher-ups want for Tanya to take prompt action. If a staff officer like Tanya is being asked to act, then it can only be an order to begin an operation and choose the optimal action. In other words, the message from high command asks for Tanya to come up with a way to break out of her current situation, that situation being the fact that she's surrounded, this means that General Zettour is going to do something that involves Tanya breaking out of her current situation. After this, she connects the dots and figures out that Zettour is planning to make an operation to break the siege by making an aggressive action.

With this, Tanya managed to figure out the concealed message hidden on the text sent to her by high command, the secret message was that Zettour was going to initiate a maneuver battle and her Kampfgruppe should act in concert with their operation. Here, Tanya managed to grasp General Zettour's intentions and read his thoughts with such a complex and weird message. In this entire Reasoning process, Tanya relied on her understanding of Zettour’s personality and his thinking pattern in order to figure out the message in what appears to have taken less than a minute, perceiving how his thinking pattern could possibly change the hidden meaning of the secret message, which demonstrates her impressive inductive reasoning, pattern recognition, abstract reasoning/thinking and VCI feat.

[Comprehension]
In the second volume, after hearing Visha saying that Tanya's perception of divisions was peculiar after she referred to a group of fifty-thousand men without any useful anti-air weapon trying to fight mages as being a division. Tanya notices that by saying that the enemies are “divisions” she ended up accidentally making her soldiers feel nervous despite the enemy not even being a threat to begin with due to lack of useful anti-air weapons, she notices that this was a lax use of specialized vocabulary and corrected herself by calling the Dacian enemies a “fifty-thousand-man mob on a reckless rampage”.

Another good VCI=[Comprehension] feat occurred during the events of the eighth Volume, after talking with Zettour about the empire's new operation called Operation Andromeda, Tanya thinks about how military companies hate hearing words like growth strategy and long-term plan coming from their superior’s mouth since such a thing are signs of an organization that is failing to hide how it's already hit the limit.

Not only this, but during the events of the First Volume of the Light Novel Zettou questions Tanya about how she would achieve victory in the war if she was the one in control of the empire's army, and she responds saying: “We carry out a thorough bloodletting and crush the enemy's ability to continue fighting”, here, Tanya purposely uses words that soldiers liked to hear like “Carry out”, “thorough” and “Crush”.

FRI
Tanya's best feats in problem solving would be her Soldim 528 Strategy and the Rising Dawn Counter Strategy. During the events of the Soldim 528 Plan/Strategy, Tanya had to face an entire battalion of multinational aerial mages, multiple mages from the Federation army and Mary Sioux, who was capable of surviving magic formulas from two aerial mage companies, an charge from these same two aerial mage companies, a direct hit from a sniping formula made with Tanya's Type 95 Computation Orb capable of creating Nuke-like explosions and even fight against Visha to the point she was completely tired, even Major Weiss wasn't certain if he could beat Mary.

Not only this, but Mary Sioux had such an insane amount of magic aptitude that Tanya herself recognized Mary as being a “damn monster”, she even stated that her ridiculous amount of magic defied all logic during the events of the Tenth Volume, not only this, but Tanya even said that Mary was using this insane amount of magic to use a magic formula stated to “literally alter the world” and even said that she was exactly like Being X due to how insanely powerful and bothering she was. Tanya had to face all these enemies while having only two elite aerial mage companies at her side and one replacement company filled with newbies, not only this, but she also had to beat them quick enough in order to reach the headquarters of B Group since an attack from the Federation was occurring there.

Another thing that makes this situation be even more difficult is that Tanya received orders from Zettour to purposely allow for her Kampfgruppe to be surrounded by enemy forces in order to allow for the soldiers of Group B to initiate an encirclement on the enemy, Tanya's Kampfgruppe will be completely surrounded by four or five entire divisions of enemy soldiers while her Kampfgruppe is merely an augmented regiment and they don't have permission to retreat even in a scenario where they are suffering with lack of food, water or ammunition. Going against this and retreating anyway would be the same as going against the orders of the General staff and would make Tanya be executed or punished heavily.

In the hideout they are using as a Base they receive supplies in an extremely slow way and barely have any resources to fight against the enemy. Another thing is that Tanya's Kampfgruppe is by design, fundamentally a breakthrough force, tying it down with orders to defend a static position literally negates all its strengths. Not only this, but Tanya's Kampfgruppe doesn't even have a proper headquarters to use in order to manage things efficiently and she will have to survive in Soldim 528 for one entire week while facing all this adversity and while constantly having to repel enemy attacks. She created a strategy to solve all these problems while having to manage the emotions of every single one of her soldiers in order to make sure that their morale doesn't fall to rock bottom.

Memory
During the events of the fourth Volume, the Mosquito 01 unit appeared in the place where Tanya's battalion was in the middle of an attack on Londinium, when this occurred, It's stated that due to the fact that the Rhine was where Tanya has spent the most time, she remembers the name of nearly every single units on the battlefield at the time, which was probably more than one or two hundred units since the Rhine Front was the place where the majority of soldiers were sent to, and due to the fact that Tanya's battalion is the 203rd Aerial mage battalion, which means that there are 202 other aerial mage units in the empire, not only this, but this is only about units composed of aerial mages, but there are also infantry units, artillery units and Fighter plane units, the number of units in the Rhine Front could easily reach 300+ units considering that it was the hardest and most deadly front in the war, demonstrating an impressive memory  feat.

Fluid Intelligence

[Problem Solving(or simply NCPS)]
Tanya's best feats in problem solving would be her Soldim 528 Strategy and the Rising Dawn Counter Strategy. During the events of Tanya's Soldim 528 strategy, Tanya managed to solve the problem of being attacked by the enemy multinational unit, the Federation aerial mages, Mary Sioux, the problem of having been sent to the region of Soldim 528, a region that they never went before, solve the problem of being surrounded by enemy forces and constantly having to counter enemy attempts to invade the stronghold of Soldim 528, the problem of being with a huge lack of resources and solve the problem of having to reach the Imperial Headquarters in order to defeat the Federation enemy soldiers attacking the Imperial Headquarters of B Group in a really short period of time. Not only this, but the planning part of this strategy also made Tanya and Zettour solve the problem of the Federation’s strong defense that didn't allow the empire to advance.

[Learning ability]
During the events of the first Volume of the Light Novel, Tanya received the cursed item from a deity similar to Being X, this cursed item was the type 95 Computation Orb, a computation Orbs that provided its user with high levels of mana and amplified their magic aptitude to levels beyond human comprehension. This item quite literally gives the highest level of magic to its user, something that would be impossible for anyone to control, a person that received such power wouldn't even be capable of understanding how to use it at first, let alone using the Type 95 at its maximum potential. But on her first time interacting with this item, Tanya adapted herself cognitively to its power and managed to use it to its full potential in the later volumes of the story. Demonstrating a cognitive adaptability and cognitive discipline feat that allows her to adapt to the highest levels of mana, despite being in a situation that would make a normal person faint. This would also be a good demonstration of Learning Ability since Tanya managed to fully control this power after using it once.

[Abstract thinking]
During the events of Tanya's Strategy/Plan to survive at Soldim 528, she receives an urgent message from high command: “From General Von Zettour to the Lergen Kampfgruppe. Colonel Von Lergen promptly began the designated actions. At first sight, this message doesn't make any sense for Tanya. Zettour has a very radical side and apparently the Higher-ups want for Tanya to take prompt action.

If a staff officer like Tanya is being asked to act, then it can only be an order to begin an operation and choose the optimal action. In other words, the message from high command asks for Tanya to come up with a way to break out of her current situation, that situation being the fact that she's surrounded, this means that General Zettour is going to do something that involves breaking out of her current situation. After this, Tanya connects the dots and figures out that Zettour is planning to make an operation to break the siege by making an aggressive action.

With this, Tanya managed to figure out the concealed message hidden on the text sent to her by high command, the secret message was that Zettour was going to initiate a maneuver battle and her Kampfgruppe should act in concert with their operation. Here, Tanya managed to grasp General Zettour's intentions and read his thoughts with such a complex and weird message, which demonstrates impressive mind reading, abstract reasoning/thinking and VCI feat.

[Logical reasoning]
I have Logical reasoning as being deductive reasoning + inductive reasoning + abductive reasoning. So if you want to know how impressive Tanya's Logical reasoning is, then simply read the first three feats listed in the Reasoning/Thinking Section of this tab. But I'm also aware of people that have Logical reasoning as being a reasoning process that contains all the three reasoning cats mentioned above, if you scale Logical reasoning like this, then I recommend for you to read Tanya's feat in “Inductive reasoning” since it is the best reasoning feat that combines abductive, inductive and deductive reasoning.

Crystallized intelligence
Currently, Tanya has knowledge about History, Psychology, Mythology, Science: [Physics and Chemistry], Law, Politics, Economy, International and National laws, Militarism(alongside all the areas of knowledge that militarism covers such as: anthropology, Sociology and Cultural studies) and all areas of knowledge that are linked to Human resources since Tanya worked with human resources in her past life.

Linguistic intelligence
When she decided to attack Dacia’s Capital, In order to respect war rules while also being capable of causing as much destruction and civilian lives lost as possible in the Capital, Tanya decides to send an announcement to the radio of the Dacian Capital civilians and soldiers, warning about the empire's attack while pretending to be a little kid:

(Tanya)
|
“Thish is a warning. We, the imperial army, are now commenshing an attack on a military supply facility! We will begin maneuvers thirty minutes from now. We solemnly pwedge. Errybody swears to fight fair and square, according to international law”

This in an insane demonstration of VCI, Linguistic intelligence, Communication, Deception and Manipulation, what Tanya does here is basically announcing to people that the empire is going to attack while using her childish voice on her favor, she also say some words wrong like “Thish”, “pwedge”, “errybody” and “Commenshing” on purpose to strengthen even more the idea that the person giving the announcement is a child making a prank.

Not only this, but during the events of the twelfth Volume, Tanya calls all the officers of the Salamander Kampfgruppe and reveal to them the details of the operation Harassment that they will perform. Tanya does this by making a camping metaphor: “The plan is simple. We will go to our campsite, start a nice fire here, cook some meat there, splatter some bloody meat everywhere, then help ourselves to whatever canned goods we can find. Everything we need is already at the campsite, so it’ll be a great time”. Here are the subtle messages hidden inside Tanya's words:

We will go to our campsite(we will go to the enemy base next to the Ildoan Capital)

Start a nice fire here(set the entire enemy base on fire)

Cook some meat there(explode our enemies so much that their bodies will be completely carbonized)

Splatter some bloody meat everywhere(eliminate all the enemy soldiers around the base)

Then help ourselves to whatever canned goods we can find(And after doing all this, we shall take any resources from the enemy that we can find)

Everything we need is already at the campsite(all the resources we need to collect are already at the enemy Base)

Visual Spatial Intelligence
During the events of Tanya's Norden Sea strategy, she decided to attack an enemy ship, she thought that the ship she was about to attack was a destroyer by merely seeing a turret firing at random, by merely seeing the ship's turret, she was capable of making out the shape of the ship which looked like a destroyer despite suffering with poor visibility.

Musical Intelligence
During the events of the Seventh Volume of the Light Novel, it's shown that while Tanya was flying with her aerial mage battalion on the battlefield, a harsh whoosh sound of a flying object pierced her ears and after this, she heard a loud rumble. By merely hearing these things, Tanya identified that this sound came from an enemy artillery shell next to them.

Existential intelligence
“Tanya has an unwavering belief that human dignity lies in thinking. The human race, evolved from monkeys, feels that thought is what sets them apart from other species and makes them humans. That's why she can't understand why believers assume they are blessed and abandon rationality. The moment a person loses the capacity to think, to question, Tanya considers them no longer human but a machine. And that is why the individual Tanya Degurechaff reveres thought, loves debate, and sneers at dogmatism from the bottom of her heart. So of course she laughs at the fanatics, the blind believers. She can't stand that those dumbasses are just like the blind followers of communism and other dogmas who built mountains of corpses through social experimentation. The feeling stems from her views on humanity. Thinking is sacred because trial and error is inherent to existence.”

Naturalistic intelligence
After waking up on the next day after the feast to commemorate the Kampfgruppe victory against the two enemy brigades in the end of the fifth Volume, Tanya notices that the color of the sky was strangely white, the temperature was also weirdly cold and she noticed that it's snowing on the outside of her room, noticing all this. Tanya understands that winter came sooner than usual, she understands all these strange things that she perceived could only occur if it was already winter. Demonstrating for the first time a Naturalistic intelligence feat.

Logical Mathematical Intelligence
During the second part of the empire's operation to defeat the Republic in the third Volume, Tanya was tasked with a search and intercept mission. While being in the middle of this search and intercept mission, she estimated that a Republican foot soldier would only be capable of carrying three days worth of supply, the motive as to why she estimated this was because the Imperial troops managed to cut off the Republican Army's supply lines.

Bodily Kinesthetic Intelligence
After the end of the battle against the two enemy infantry brigades in the fifth Volume, Tanya makes a feast to commemorate the victory against the enemies with her subordinates, during the feast, Tanya feels the sleepiness affecting her and understands there's nothing she can do about it, due to being in the body of a child, Tanya can't resist the sleepiness and stay up at night due to the physiological demand, she understands that sleep affects adults and children differently. Here she demonstrates impressive Bodily-Kinesthetic Intelligence by understanding the effects of sleepiness on her childish body.

Through the entire Light Novel, Tanya demonstrates a highly impressive understanding of her subordinates' emotions and psychology. Intra EM
Was capable of maintaining her sanity and continue behaving normally after suffering the effects of the Type 95 computation Orb's Curse, it's said in the Light Novel that when she used it, she felt all her energy leave all the cells of her body which would practically be equal to having one's own vital energy dragged out of one's body, despite it being so terrifying, the pain was soon converted into religious ecstasy after a few seconds and it's said that the sensation of agony and joy blending to rattle her mind was an horror beyond any description. Not only this, but it's even stated that she felt like her brain was being devoured by worms during the events of the twelfth Volume after using the Type 95. In fact, she suffered its effects not only one, not only two, but more than 12 times so far in the story and she still managed to keep her sanity with the only negative effects being that she lost a part of her memory.

As if this wasn't already enough, the Type 95 Computation Orb also corrupts Tanya's mind and slowly forces her into becoming a God believer against her own will. Visha even mentioned during the events of the fifth Volume that the energy placed on Tanya's attacks using the Type 95 were so insane that she considered it to be too much for any other soldier to handle, which means that any other person using the Type 95 Computation Orb wouldn't be capable of resisting the high amount of energy that it drains from the user and the pressure placed on their mind and would end up dying, and we are not even talking about the sensation of joy and agony blending to rattle on Tanya's mind when she uses the Type 95, something that Visha has literally no way of knowing since she can't see the psychological effects that the Type 95 has on Tanya's mind, which means that the overall amount of agony caused by it is way bigger than Visha mentioned, which also means that its negative effects can cause an amount of agony more than enough to kill a soldier, who are literally trained to face stress and have high mental fortitude.

Not only this, but during the events of the fifth Volume, Tanya shoots at the enemy multiple times with the attacks of the Type 95 cursed computation Orb with each attack draining all energy from her cells and creating an amount of agony inside her mind stated to be a horror beyond any description, which means that the amount of agony felt by Tanya every time she uses the Type 95 Computation Orb can't be compared to any other pain ever felt by a human being that can be described, which means that the agony caused by it far surpasses those of death by burning alive or torture. Despite the Type 95 computation Orb being so insanely destructive for Tanya's mental health, she still managed to keep her sanity despite using it 12 times so far in the story and she wasn't demonstrating to have fell in insanity at all, she literally maintained her humorous and sometimes serious personality normally despite having suffered such an insane amount of agony twelve times. After using the type 95 Orb, Tanya behaves normally, in a way that not even her subordinates, having an EP capable of seeing through her intentions immediately, are capable of perceiving all the stress and agony caused by the Type 95 Orb.

it's said in the Light Novel that when she used the Type 95 computation Orb, she felt all energy leave all the cells of her body which would practically be equal to having one's own vital energy dragged out of one's body, despite it being so terrifying, the pain was converted into religious ecstasy and it's said that the sensation of agony and joy blending to rattle her mind was an horror beyond any description, which means that the agony was so big that it couldn't possibly be compared to any other pain ever felt by a human being, which means that things like death by burning alive and torture doesn't even come close to the agony Tanya felt. ER
After being reborn to the new strange world in the very beginning of the first volume, Tanya thinks about her past life, she recalls the moment before her death when she was firing an employee: “I always knew that I wasn't the best, incapable of competing against the geniuses and becoming equal to the ones with talent by merely using dedication and effort, my personality became quite distorted. I've become a mess of jumbled complexes. Truly benevolent people are inspiring. As far as hypocrisy goes, I have what society as a whole considers to be healthy. Despite my awareness of how unpleasant I am, I still maintain the arrogant belief that I am superior to this incompetent idiot crying in front of me. At least in terms of cost-benefit, I have maintained superior results. So, even though restructuring departments designed to consolidate through layoffs are a pain, I take it seriously”.

Empathy

(Tanya's Thoughts)

“The war has been recklessly directed in the first place. The stupid radio stations and newspapers were celebrating the sweeping victory in the northern theater with dreck like: “This is the empire's secret plan to avoid a multifront war, and the roar of artillery heralds the birth of a new order”. And now, thanks to the sudden attack, they're slinging daily propaganda about the Diabolical François Republic. But the people on the front lines don't care about propaganda, that's only good for making jokes about the brass to kill time in the trenches. They want to yell something like “if you can afford to broadcast propaganda all the way to the front, get more men and supplies over here”. The soldiers in the front lines are the ones in trouble if the higher ups keep arguing about causes and ideals instead of facing reality”.

Here she not only shows impressive social Awareness and Social Cognition, but also shows great Empathy and emotional understanding due to her empathizing with the Empire soldiers' emotions to the point where she even understands how they feel about the Empire's actions without them not even having to tell her.

Social Skills

Not only this, but during the events of the Seventh Volume, Tanya managed to persuade a Lieutenant from an infantry regiment into helping her in the battlefield despite having literally threatened to kill him and the other members of the regiment a few seconds ago, Tanya said a few words of encouragement to him that were disguised as a threat and it's said that the effects of her words of encouragement were instantaneous, she managed to start a friendly talk with him and make him give orders for his allies to help her battalion despite having literally threatened to kill him and his allies a few seconds ago. Demonstrating highly impressive social skills.

Charisma
During the execution of Tanya's plan to attack Moskva, one of the Federation's officers was seeing everything happening in front of his eyes and instead of feeling anger for the disrespect for his country that Tanya was causing, he became completely obsessed with Tanya herself, it was like love at first sight, he was so obsessed with the idea of making her surrender and be his property that his self-control was reaching its limits, his entire personality was completely transformed from a man with a strange and insincere smile that one might call a communist grim, to a complete pervert and obsessive man, all of this by merely seeing the destruction caused by Tanya and her smile filled with carefree conviction, it's even said that in order to get her as being his perfect doll, he would make a deal with any devil, he would compromise with any political rival, he would make use of any dissidents and would even spare the mages he'd sent to Sildberia for execution simply to have them fight against the empire in order to capture Tanya.

Not only this, but during the events of the Seventh Volume, Tanya managed to persuade a Lieutenant from an infantry regiment into helping her in the battlefield despite having literally threatened to kill him and the other members of the regiment a few seconds ago, Tanya said a few words of encouragement to him that were disguised as a threat and it's said that the effects of her words of encouragement were instantaneous, she managed to start a friendly talk with him and make him give orders for his allies to help her battalion despite having literally threatened to kill him and his allies a few seconds ago.

Leadership
During the events of the first Volume, Tanya becomes the commander of a military augmented battalion formed by 48 different soldiers, each one of them being a bunch of newbies. What's impressive about this is that throughout the entire Light Novel Tanya transforms this bunch of newbies into the empire's strongest and most dangerous aerial mage unit, through the entire Light Novel, with the use of meticulous leadership, Tanya turns her battalion into an almost invincible force in the battlefield, with her insane leadership, she also made her subordinates learn how to comprehend her intentions in an instant. Something that is extremely useful on the battlefield.

During all the events and volumes of the Light Novel, it's shown that not a single one of her subordinates dares to raise their voices against her. With her impressive leadership, she turned a bunch of newbies into the perfect aerial mage unit, a bunch of elite aerial mages that don't dare to go against Tanya's orders, but at the same time have great respect for her and are capable of understanding her intentions in an instant.

Not only this, but during the events of the 14th Volume, Tanya leads an entire division of aerial mages in an strategic attack against the Federation army's logistics, leading everyone in an operation that took more than 2 or 3 days, while having merely 15 minutes of time to sleep and having to confront a powerful enemy like the Federation army. All of these are insane demonstrations of a Leadership feat.

Resource management
During the events of Tanya's Northeast Front Strategy/Plan, she kept a group of troops from her Kampfgruppe patrolling the entire area in search of enemies. When the enemies appeared, Major Weiss who was leading the patrol took the 203rd aerial mage battalion and attacked the enemy forces under Tanya's orders. After this, Tanya made Major Weiss help with observed artillery shots at the enemy forces where he tells the enemy location to artillery and they shoot at that exact location. The artillery fire attracted the enemy forces to the Kampfgruppe Base as planned by Tanya and the Kampfgruppe's infantry soldiers were already waiting for the enemy troops to come, the Kampfgruppe's infantry were protected by defensive lines while the enemy didn't have anything to defend themselves. After the enemy started to run away, Tanya liberated the War Tanks that she kept hidden as a trump card to eliminate all the remaining enemy soldiers as fast as possible. Tanya made all her troops including the aerial mages, the infantry and War Tanks chase the enemy forces to eliminate all of them. This entire strategy demonstrates an insane level of resource management.

Social integration
During the events of the first three volumes of the Light Novel, it's shown to us how impressive Tanya's Social Integration is, basically, after the events in the first volume that gave to Tanya the Silver Wings Assault Badge, she started to perform well in multiple missions and purposely demonstrate her competency In front of her colleagues and teachers in the War college in order to pass a good impression of her intelligence and competency as a soldier to them. She quickly started to participate in important battles for the empire and was essential for the imperial victories in each one of these battles, even going as far as being considered a demon by the soldiers in the Rhine Front and gaining the silver Wings Assault Badge, an extremely important demonstration of how powerful of an aerial mage Tanya is.

After repeating this process over and over again, Tanya managed to meet with Zettour and in a few minutes she developed a nice talk with him, suggesting multiple events that could occur in the future of the empire and suggesting ways to counter these events. Zettour developed such a interest on Tanya that he literally gave an entire augmented battalion to her despite she merely being a Lieutenant Colonel(for you to be the commander of a battalion, you have to at least be a Major), the fact that Tanya's social integration on Zettour was so extreme that he gave to her the role of major despite her literally not having any experience in commanding a single company is highly impressive.

General Zettour had such a good impression of Tanya that he literally gave to her a whole augmented battalion that serves directly under the General Staff's command, such a thing is really rare for any imperial military group, in the own story, it's shown to us that the 203rd aerial mage battalion(the one commanded by Tanya) is the only imperial military group that responds directly to the General staff. After these events, Tanya and her aerial mage battalion participated on important missions from all four sides of the country where battles were occurring, with this, Tanya became friends with four different branches of the imperial army: the Northern army group, the Eastern Army group, the General staff, and the military group responsible for the creation of the Computation Orbs.

She developed such a good relationship with all of these branches of the imperial army that when she was in trouble due to sinking a Commonwealth ship, all of these four groups decided to put pressure on the empire's National Court and force them to give to Tanya the verdict of innocent despite her being guilty since she actually did such a thing. This is an insane demonstration of a Social Integration, influence building and social skills feat from Tanya.

Social Cognition
During the events of the thirteenth Volume, while talking with Colonel Lergen and Colonel Uger, Tanya demonstrates a completely unrivaled Social Cognition feat. Extremely long text alert…

(Tanya’s Thoughts)

“The Empire’s approach to operational command is based around transmitting targets to commanders. To put it in extreme terms, the Empire’s approach to orders is akin to saying, We are going to provide our guests with dinner, so acquire enough steaks for four people. In the extreme example, what kind of steaks per se, and how to acquire them, is up to the commander to decide. They could buy their own preferred cut and cook it to their liking. Or if they were worried the flavor would be underwhelming, they could telephone a steakhouse and have steaks delivered. They could even place it all into the hands of a talented chef who just happens to live next door. Everything is permitted. On the other hand, if the only option is game meat, then the commander’s only choice would be to take the classic approach and go hunting. Even an outsider can likely understand that much.

But that is just the tip of the iceberg when it comes to how the Empire does things. The byword of necessity means that commanders of all ranks are expected to go above and beyond as a matter of course. Using the example of steaks, a commander might interpret those orders to mean, The fundamental requirement is to provide dinner, but a different menu would serve perfectly well for vegetarian guests, and so, rather than steaks, they’d provide a vegetarian meal more to the guests’ liking. This would qualify as a clear-cut example of observing the intention of the order, even while not taking any of the directions literally for the particulars. Within the Empire’s organizational culture, this is the fundamental role expected of commanders at all levels. But what about by-the-book orders, with oversight of everything down to the smallest details? The basic framework of the order, Provide steaks for four people, might be the same.

However, the finer points would be clarified in the manual, with a scheme to ensure that the same results could be achieved regardless of who’s carrying the orders out. For instance— Provide steaks for four people from Steakhouse A. All steaks must be cooked medium-rare. If catering from Steakhouse A is not available, instead procure 150 grams of red meat per person from Butcher B, at a budget of up to 3,000 yen per person; cook steaks to medium in accordance with the standard manual for steak preparation; arrange and serve in a simplified fashion as appropriate to wartime conditions.

If 600 grams of said meat cannot be obtained solely from Butcher B, Hamburg steaks may be permitted (but only if command is informed and prior consent is obtained). If preparing Hamburg steaks, the ratio of ground meat must meet or exceed military standards. If obtaining the appropriate meat from a butcher proves difficult, report back immediately. Under this approach, the issue would be whether this litany of eventualities could account for all circumstances.

For instance, if the existence of vegetarians has been accounted for, then a clause such as If vegetarians are present among the guests, providing suitable vegetarian meals can be included in advance, allowing the commander to make such arrangements without delay. But if not? The order is to provide steaks. And if, unfortunately, Steakhouse A is able to provide catering for four? Well then, the order can be fulfilled. The commander can arrange steaks for four people.

Naturally, upon realizing that one of the guests is a vegetarian, an obvious issue will arise. But the order given leaves no room for interpretation. It calls for steaks from Steakhouse A. At that moment, the commander on the ground will be left in a dilemma. Follow orders, or commit insubordination? These are the obvious differences between the Empire’s approach and a by-the-book approach to giving orders. At a glance, the Empire’s way of doing things seems superior.

An organization like the Empire, composed of officers who can think for themselves, obviously seems preferable at first glance. A place where people can do what needs to be done, appropriately and without constant interference from higher-ups over every little thing. It would seem to be the ideal workplace. The reality, however, is that the latter type of organization is far more robust. And why is that? The answer is simple. Because the latter system allows anyone to fulfill orders to a reasonable degree.

The Empire’s style relies on an unspoken agreement among commanders, with results and on-site adjustment evidently relying on every person understanding the role they need to play for the greater goal. Imagine another unit has been tasked with providing a wine that pairs well with the steak. If the person responsible for providing the steaks has suddenly, in their judgment, decided to switch to a vegetarian menu instead, then ideally, under the Empire’s approach, once the person has informed others of the change, the one in charge of providing the wine would, of their own accord, pay special attention to the fining technique used and choose wines that don’t use any animal products. For instance, in response to requests for “an X-year bottle of A,” they might decide,

Naturally, I will secure Wine A, but even without being told to do so, I will also secure a wine suitable for vegans. The servers and valets would then take similar care. All without anyone needing to give directions. As long as information is shared, each person will be able to make an appropriate judgment. This is what allows the Imperial Army to turn quickly on a dime… They are highly adaptable to changes in circumstance and, when necessary, can even do the unthinkable and ignore finalized plans. The Empire’s strategic planning takes such flexibility for granted. However, without mutual understanding and confidence of one another and of how each unit will behave under the circumstances, such flexibility is impossible.

In other words, what would such an arrangement look like when they are placed outside their own backyard? Considered in that light…I tremble. With close acquaintances such as Lergen and Uger, it would be one thing, but if told to cooperate with a colonel I know almost nothing about? How am I supposed to figure out how this unknown colonel might act during large-scale maneuvers in enemy territory?”

What's more surprising is that this is merely one of Tanya's inner monologues throughout the story, with almost each inner monologue buffing her Social Cognition. If we consider every Inner monologue shown by her in the story, we can easily perceive how Tanya is capable of demonstrating an extremely in-depth understanding of all the Social variables of the Imperial army and the society as a whole(both the society of the world before she was reincarnated and the society of the other world after Tanya was Isekaied)

Communication
During the events of the twelfth Volume, Tanya calls all the officers of the Salamander Kampfgruppe and reveal to them the details of the operation Harassment that they will perform. Tanya does this by making a camping metaphor: “The plan is simple. We will go to our campsite, start a nice fire here, cook some meat there, splatter some bloody meat everywhere, then help ourselves to whatever canned goods we can find. Everything we need is already at the campsite, so it’ll be a great time”. Here are the subtle messages hidden inside Tanya's words:

We will go to our campsite(we will go to the enemy base next to the Ildoan Capital)

Start a nice fire here(set the entire enemy base on fire)

Cook some meat there(explode our enemies so much that their bodies will be completely carbonized)

Splatter some bloody meat everywhere(eliminate all the enemy soldiers around the base)

Then help ourselves to whatever canned goods we can find(And after doing all this, we shall take any resources from the enemy that we can find)

Everything we need is already at the campsite(all the resources we need to collect are already at the enemy Base)

Influence building
Tanya has the title of “The Devil of the Rhine”, in fact, she is the most feared soldier that the imperial army had with them and one of the top five imperial mages of the present era, she was so feared that the people of the commonwealth’s HQ of voluntary army Frontline command demonstrated extreme surprise by merely hearing that she was coming to battle, she casually flew through the death zone, single-handedly slaughtered an entire company, she's the only soldier in the empire's history to have both the Silver Wings assault badge with Oak Lives and not have died, despite the people that achieved this achievement having received the badge after dying.

She was capable of using interference magic formulas so powerful that it distorted space itself, and she literally defeated the entire Dacian Army composed of 50k+ soldiers alongside her battalion in a single day and caused so much destruction to Dacia that the country's government almost collapsed, all of this in a single day and with only 50 soldiers at max at her side. All these feats and other things were what turned her into the empire's most feared Named soldier, and what's even more impressive is that she did all this while eliminating any witness and destroying any recordings which resulted in people being uncertain about if she even existed, this was so effective that the Higher-ups of the Entente Alliance and Commonwealth considered the Devil of the Rhine as being a myth created by the traumatized soldiers of the François Republic that already lost their sanity.

Influence
Tanya's influence is so big that multiple different departments of the empire’s military came together to protect her when she was being judged by the Empire's national Court, her influence was simply so big that four different military departments applied pressure on the National Court and forced them into judging Tanya as being Innocent of the accusations charged against her, despite these accusations being extremely serious like Tanya attacking a neutral war power country's ship.

Not only this, but Tanya also has access to Colonel Von Lergen’s military stamp and can even send messages to the General staff office under his name, which means that Tanya has more or less an amount of authority and influence equal to a Colonel in the Imperial army, a Colonel is only below a Lieutenant General and a General in authority/influence. She also has a good relationship with two imperial Generals like Zettour and Romel, and is friends with Colonel Uger, who is Zettour's assistant. She has a friendship with Colonel Lergen who was Rudersdorf assistant. She also has connections with the Elinium arms factory that creates computation Orbs and multiple military groups in the imperial army. Not only this, but she has enough authority to interfere directly with the imperial army's command.

Relationship Management
During the events of the Eleventh Volume of the Light Novel, General Von Zettour told Tanya that he wanted her to kill General Rudersdorf. He explained that he wasn't capable of accepting defeat and that in the empire's current situation, he was like a Cancer that must be eliminated. But hearing her superior’s words, Tanya says that Zettour's words are absurd and claims that she doesn't have any other choice but to shoot him to defend her dignity. After this, Tanya explains how killing General Rudersdorf wasn't going to be a good idea for Zettour. She says that eliminating Rudersdorf was a waste of good human resource, the empire is not in a position where they can afford to lose their Higher-ups so easily, nevermind throwing them away. She says that if Zettour wants to kill Rudersdorf, the empire needs to ensure a return to all the investment that went to General Rudersdorf.

Not only this, but she also says that If they kill General Rudersdorf, all that will be left for them is a single murder. But if they were to stop the mastermind behind a coup d’état, it would serve a purpose in increasing their influence. After this, Tanya suggests that they should focus on devising a plan for what happens after they foil General Rudersdorf’s Plan B. She says that his death would trigger a selective purge of soldiers within the army. They could use this confusion to bring the Supreme High Command under the control of the General Staff Office and effectively create a short-term central command for the war. With this, Tanya makes General Zettour forget about the idea of eliminating General Rudersdorf and even manages to recover their relationship temporarily since General Zettour and Rudersdorf will have a fight in the future that will drain General Von Zettour's patience almost completely, which will result on Zettour asking Tanya to kill Rudersdorf.

Self-Awareness

(Tanya's Thoughts)

“I always knew that I wasn't the best, incapable of competing against the geniuses and becoming equal to the ones with talent by merely using dedication and effort, my personality became quite distorted. I've become a mess of jumbled complexes. Truly benevolent people are inspiring. As far as hypocrisy goes, I have what society as a whole considers to be healthy. Despite my awareness of how unpleasant I am, I still maintain the arrogant belief that I am superior to this incompetent idiot crying in front of me. At least in terms of cost-benefit, I have maintained superior results. So, even though restructuring departments designed to consolidate through layoffs are a pain, I take it seriously”

Psychological insight
Through the entire Light Novel, Tanya demonstrates a highly impressive understanding of her subordinates' emotions and psychology. Her understanding of their psychology is so good that sometimes she is capable of anticipating what they're going to say and what they are thinking or how they feel about something merely based on vague words or minimal/subtle changes in their facial expressions. She gained such an impressive insight of their emotions and psychology in merely a few years, which demonstrates a really impressive psychological Insight feat. With such an insane understanding of her subordinates’ emotions and entire psychology, Tanya is capable of comprehending their intentions similarly to how her subordinates are capable of comprehending her intentions immediately. She understands the emotions of every single member of her battalion easily, as if they were literal open books for her. Something that is shown fairly consistently in the final volumes of the Light Novel.

Psychological Warfare
Tanya's entire Battalion training plan is by far her best Psychological warfare feat due to how many traps it has in order to guarantee that her soldiers would either reach insanity or give up on the training made by Tanya.
