Hot reading
During the events of the Tenth Volume, while talking with Zettour, he pointed his finger to a specific location on his map and after seeing it, Tanya abducted his entire plan to deal with the enemy forces and even figured out what he wanted for her to do in an instant. By observing the area where he was pointing at and his previous words, she managed to completely discern his true intentions which is highly impressive since Zettour's intentions are so difficult to read that even Tanya failed to evade a little trap made by him on this volume.

Seeing through people
During the events of the tenth Volume, Tanya was in a meeting at the foreign affairs office alongside Colonel Von Lergen and the counselor of the foreign office named Conrad, during this meeting, Colonel Von Lergen started to talk about military secrets in front of Tanya and she perceived that whatever he was going to say, was extremely bad, the reason for this being because she perceived the hesitation and inner turmoil lurking just behind his words, she completely perceived that whatever he planned to say wasn't good news.

This is highly impressive since Colonel Von Lergen's acting skills/emotional management is capable of almost deceiving even Tanya, it's said that despite having spent a lot of time with him in the military, Colonel Von Lergen seemed to be his usual self on the surface for Tanya, even going so far as to state that if she didn't knew how much the General staff officers stressed keeping things concise, she probably would never had managed to recognize Colonel Von Lergen's ironclad poker face.

Composure
Tanya is capable of maintaining her sanity and continue behaving normally after suffering the effects of the Type 95 computation Orb's Curse, it's said in the Light Novel that when she used it, she felt all her energy leave all the cells of her body which would practically be equal to having one's own vital energy dragged out of one's body, despite it being so terrifying, the pain was soon converted into religious ecstasy after a few seconds and it's said that the sensation of agony and joy blending to rattle her mind was an horror beyond any description. Not only this, but it's even stated that she felt like her brain was being devoured by worms during the events of the twelfth Volume after using the Type 95. In fact, she suffered its effects not only one, not only two, but more than 12 times so far in the story and she still managed to keep her sanity with the only negative effects being that she lost a part of her memory.

As if this wasn't already enough, the Type 95 Computation Orb also corrupts Tanya's mind and slowly forces her into becoming a God believer against her own will. Visha even mentioned during the events of the fifth Volume that the energy placed on Tanya's attacks using the Type 95 were so insane that she considered it to be too much for any other soldier to handle, which means that any other person using the Type 95 Computation Orb wouldn't be capable of resisting the high amount of energy that it drains from the user and the pressure placed on their mind and would end up dying, and we are not even talking about the sensation of joy and agony blending to rattle on Tanya's mind when she uses the Type 95, something that Visha has literally no way of knowing since she can't see the psychological effects that the Type 95 has on Tanya's mind, which means that the overall amount of agony caused by it is way bigger than Visha mentioned, which also means that its negative effects can cause an amount of agony more than enough to kill a soldier, who are literally trained to face stress and have high mental fortitude.

Not only this, but during the events of the fifth Volume, Tanya shoots at the enemy multiple times with the attacks of the Type 95 cursed computation Orb with each attack draining all energy from her cells and creating an amount of agony inside her mind stated to be a horror beyond any description, which means that the amount of agony felt by Tanya every time she uses the Type 95 Computation Orb can't be compared to any other pain ever felt by a human being that can be described, which means that the agony caused by it far surpasses those of death by burning alive or torture. Despite the Type 95 computation Orb being so insanely destructive for Tanya's mental health, she still managed to keep her sanity despite using it 12 times so far in the story and she wasn't demonstrating to have fell in insanity at all, she literally maintained her humorous and sometimes serious personality normally despite having suffered such an insane amount of agony twelve times. After using the type 95 Orb, Tanya behaves normally, in a way that not even her subordinates, having an EP capable of seeing through her intentions immediately, are capable of perceiving all the stress and agony caused by the Type 95 Orb.

Mental Fortitude
Was capable of maintaining her sanity after suffering the effects of the Type 95 computation Orb's Curse, it's said in the Light Novel that when she used it, she felt all her energy leave all the cells of her body which would practically be equal to having one's own vital energy dragged out of one's body, despite it being so terrifying, the pain was soon converted into religious ecstasy after a few seconds and it's said that the sensation of agony and joy blending to rattle her mind was an horror beyond any description. Not only this, but it's even stated that she felt like her brain was being devoured by worms during the events of the twelfth Volume after using the Type 95. In fact, she suffered its effects not only one, not only two, but more than 12 times so far in the story and she still managed to keep her sanity with the only negative effects being that she lost a part of her memory.

Not only this, but during the events of the fifth Volume, Tanya shoots at the enemy multiple times with the attacks of the Type 95 cursed computation Orb with each attack draining all energy from her cells and creating an amount of agony inside her mind stated to be a horror beyond any description, which means that the amount of agony felt by Tanya every time she uses the Type 95 Computation Orb can't be compared to any other pain ever felt by a human being that can be described, which means that the agony caused by it far surpasses those of death by burning alive or torture. Despite the Type 95 computation Orb being so insanely destructive for Tanya's mental health, she still managed to keep her sanity despite using it 12 times so far in the story.

Not only this, but the Type 95 computation Orb has a curse placed on it by a Higher-dimensional Being like Being X, it was created with the purpose of forcing its user to develop some faith every time they use it, but even after using it and suffering the effects of the curse 12 times, Tanya still managed to keep her personality that is completely against religion and the idea of God existing. This means that Tanya's mental fortitude is so strong that not even a Transcendental and Higher-dimensional Being can mess with her mind.

Determination
it's said in the Light Novel that when she used the Type 95 computation Orb, she felt all energy leave all the cells of her body which would practically be equal to having one's own vital energy dragged out of one's body, despite it being so terrifying, the pain was converted into religious ecstasy and it's said that the sensation of agony and joy blending to rattle her mind was an horror beyond any description, which means that the agony was so big that it couldn't possibly be compared to any other pain ever felt by a human being, which means that things like death by burning alive and torture doesn't even come close to the agony Tanya felt.

She literally decided to pass through this insane amount of agony and pain voluntarily simply to gain more influence in the Army and in the fifth Volume of the Light Novel, she used it once again in order to take revenge for the members of her battalion that died during a mission and manage to actually complete the mission, in fact, she used the Type 95 cursed Orb during the events of the fifth Volume five times at least, she shoot multiple shots with the power of the type 95 cursed Orb with each shot draining all energy from her body and creating an amount of agony inside her mind stated to be an horror beyond any description.

Another additional information is that the Type 95 computation Orb also corrupts Tanya's mind the more she uses it, the fact that she still managed to keep her mind free from what she considers to be the foolishness of blind followers of religion thanks to her determination to continue being a rational and liberal human being demonstrates that her determination is so strong that not even a cursed tool created by a Transcendental and Higher-dimensional Being similar to Being X can completely corrupt her mind.

Perseverance
Despite knowing about the effects of the Type 95 computation Orb’s curse which was told to her beforehand by the sector of Being X, Tanya still decided to use it against the Republic soldiers that were chasing her during the events of the first volume, what she knew about the Computation Orb's Curse was that it would destroy her sanity completely and fill her mind with religious thoughts in order to force her into becoming a religious person against her own free will, despite already knowing about all this and after experiencing the effects of the computation Orb's curse, she still decided to use it against a bunch of enemy soldiers with size lower than a company and against a bunch of enemy soldiers in the fifth Volume only to take revenge for her dead subordinates and complete her mission given by the General staff. In fact, she used the power of the Type 95 Cursed Orbs five different times in order to attack the enemy, with each shot draining all energy from her body and converting it into religious ecstasy, and after all this, the agony of having her energy drained and the religious ecstasy merged and the agony Tanya felt was stated to be an horror beyond description, which means that the amount of agony she felt can't be possibly compared to any other agony ever felt by a human being that can be described, which means that the amount of agony Tanya felt far surpasses those of death by burning alive or torture, and she was aware of all the consequences of using it beforehand, but still decided to use it multiple times to take revenge for her fallen soldiers.

Stress tolerance
During the events of the entire Light Novel, Tanya is forced to pass through multiple stressful situations, as a literal child, she has to cope with the fact that she was sent to a world dominated by war when she never had to pass through a war in her previous life. She has to cope with the fact that she was now a soldier in the empire's army, fighting for a country despite knowing that they are doomed to lose the war. She can't even betray the empire and try to escape since she would be executed if someone figured it out and there was no way the government of any country would allow for her to live there despite knowing that she was once an imperial soldier, she can't even formally ask to leave the military since she was way too useful for the General commanding the army. Almost every time she receives orders to accomplish impossible goals and constantly puts her life at risk, something that she consistently demonstrates to hate since she doesn't want to die.

As if all of this wasn't bad enough, Tanya's own ideology and rationalism makes her feel stressful about her environment, she despises wars since it was a severe waste of human resources(she worked with human resources on her past life, which makes everything even worse for her since she understands how badly every loss ruins the country's economy). To make everything even worse, a Transcendental Being literally gave to her a cursed tool that would turn her into what she hates the most, a God-believer, and there was nothing she could do to prevent it, if she wanted to survive in that world, she had no other choice but to use this tool since it gives immense power to her. Another transcendental being also frequently makes Tanya's life harder by forcing her to face impossible odds and ruining her plans as shown in her battalion plan at the first volume, to make things even worse, the identity of this second transcendental being is God himself, a being whose existence Tanya refuses to recognize at any cost, she refers to this transcendental being as being the devil and its existence by itself is reason for a hate and anger that fills Tanya's mind every time something goes wrong in the Light Novel.

Returning back to the cursed tool that was given to Tanya, as I already said before, it turns Tanya into a God-believer every time she uses it, but that's not everything it does, it is also capable of inflicting agony on its user’s mind, in the Light Novel, it's said that Tanya's mind is filled with an horror beyond any description every time she uses it, something even worse than death by fire and torture, she even feels like worms are eating her brain when she uses it due to how much it rots her mind, she felt all of this agony 12 different times btw. Tanya has to cope with the stress caused by all these things despite being only a child(even if she was isekaied in the body of a child but has the mind of an adult, in the Light Novel it's shown that Tanya's age actually caused some negative effects on her mental fortitude since she has to sleep way sooner than usual since her childish mind felt sleepiness extremely quick), the mind of a child being extremely weaker in stress tolerance than an adult's mind.

She has to cope with all this stress while being in the middle of a war and without having any experience with these things in her past life, a high-scale war like the one that Tanya faces is more than enough to make anyone who never experienced a war before lose their mind and sanity. The fact she copes with all this stress and is not only capable of keeping her sanity, but also maintaining her sense of humor, maintaining her composure when new adversity appears and formulating strategies to surpass this adversity is an insane demonstration of stress tolerance. What’s even more impressive is that all of her subordinates, who have enough EP to see through her intentions immediately, don’t have any Idea of how much stress Tanya has to cope with exactly.

Cognitive discipline
During the events of the first Volume of the Light Novel, Tanya received the cursed item from a deity similar to Being X, this cursed item was the type 95 Computation Orb, a computation Orbs that provided its user with high levels of mana and amplified their magic aptitude to levels beyond human comprehension. This item quite literally gives the highest level of magic to its user, something that would be impossible for anyone to control, a person that received such power wouldn't even be capable of understanding how to use it at first, let alone using the Type 95 at its maximum potential. But on her first time interacting with this item, Tanya adapted herself cognitively to its power and managed to use it to its full potential in the later volumes of the story. Demonstrating a cognitive adaptability and cognitive discipline feat that allows her to adapt to the highest levels of mana, despite being in a situation that would make a normal person faint. This would also be a good demonstration of cognitive discipline since Tanya managed to fully control this power after using it once.

Impulse management
In the fifth Volume, Despite receiving a mission from the General staff to perform an experiment on a strategically unimportant piece of land where Tanya should defend that piece of land against the Federation forces for some time in order to understand how powerful they are, Tanya managed to control her emotions and keep her impulses of screaming with Uger, who is informing her about these orders. Tanya was practically requested by the General staff to go and die in order to solve a problem within the General staff regarding divergence of view about how the Empire should deal with the Federation in the war, the fact that it was direct orders from the General staff didn't allowed for Tanya to refuse to carry out the orders. Tanya even recognized that the General staff saw her Kampfgruppe as being something even lower than Guinea Pigs. Not only this, but Uger, a mid-tier General staff officer in charge of handling supplies that is capable of making anything happen if it was necessary for war effort, could only assure to Tanya that her Kampfgruppe was going to have access to the current existing supply lines, but they couldn't expect any kind of extra supplies, that was the only thing he could promise to her and despite perceiving this, Tanya managed to maintain her Composure and hold back the impulse of screaming with him. What makes everything even more impressive about this feat is the fact that she received these orders to fight until death with her allies on the spot, she didn't have any time to calm herself down and relax, she simply managed her emotions and kept her composure after receiving these orders on the spot.

Manipulation/Deception

Direct Manipulation
During the events of Tanya's battalion training plan in the first Volume, literally all of Tanya's actions to make her subordinates abandon the training were highly impressive demonstrations of a direct manipulation feat. During the events of these plans she forced them to pass through multiple life-threatening situations where their life was at risk multiple times. Some of her subordinates were on the verge of getting crazy and were completely paranoid due to how terrifying this training was. In the later volumes, Tanya goes as far as to discipline her Soldiers completely, firstly transforming a bunch of weaklings into the most powerful aerial force of the Empire by pushing them to their absolute limits physically, mentally, psychologically and emotionally. Spamming so much EF on them that all of them became capable of dealing with insane levels of stress far beyond what's considered to be normal for a soldier, formulate strategies to solve unexpected problems quickly and created such a powerful bond with Tanya that they are capable of seeing through her intentions immediately. All of this was the result of Tanya’s impressive EF and direct manipulation over the participants for entire years.

Indirect manipulation
This is a document that explains Tanya's indirect manip in Soldim 528, which is clearly her best indirect manip feat:

Tanya's indirect manip in Soldim 528

Individual manipulation
During the events of the seventh Volume while she was intimidating the Fifty-fourth Regiment of infantry soldiers into returning to battle, Tanya asks for one of the Lieutenants of the Fifty-fourth Regiment of infantry soldiers to round up his men; she recognizes that he was a Lieutenant by analyzing his Rank Insignia, Age and Ribbon bars. Tanya then starts to say this exact thing to the Lieutenant: “Didn’t you rise through the ranks? Unless you’re a fool who’s just been letting the years go by with your thumb up your ass, do what you need to do. If you can’t, I’ll lay you to eternal rest right here!” The thing is that Tanya says such a thing not in an intimidating tone but in an encouraging one and a smile that almost seems like she's saying: “C’mon, you can do it”.

She understands that higher ranking soldiers are more composed and humorous than normal ranked soldiers, that's why she tries to make the Lieutenant named Barchet become her ally and help in the battle since she understands that initiating a friendly talk with someone with a good sense of humor and good composure was considerably easier than initiating a friendly talk with someone in absolute despair, and when you manage to get the higher ranking soldier at your side, the remaining soldiers will slowly decide to join you too. After this, it's shown that Tanya's words of encouragement had an immediate effect and she managed to not only make him calm down, but she also managed to initiate a friendly talk with the Lieutenant Barchet despite having literally threatened to kill him and the infantry regiment a few seconds ago.

Mass manipulation
During the events of Tanya's plan to attack the Federation's Capital, It's said in the fourth Volume of the Light Novel that due to the destruction caused by Tanya's battalion in Moskva, the entire city was in a state of panic including the civilians, the people from the empire who normally criticized its military for being inefficient were completely speechless, the morale of the imperial soldiers was boosted while the morale of the soldiers of the Federation was ruined, the entire Federation including the hundreds of millions of civilians decided to approach the war as if their honor depended of it, and the chances of the Federation agreeing with having negotiations with the Empire to reach peace became completely nonexistent due to how much damage Tanya caused to their honor, in other words, Tanya's actions had long-term effects on two different countries around the world: the Empire(Germany) and the Russy Federation (obviously Russia), which is an impressive mass manipulation feat.

Logical manipulation
During the events of the Seventh Volume, Colonel Von Lergen informs Tanya that due to a decision made by the Higher-ups, he will now become the commander of a military unit superior to the Salamander Kampfgruppe named “Lergen Kampfgruppe”, but in fact, this Lergen Kampfgruppe won’t even exist at all, it's going to be an inexistent military group that is actually the Salamander Kampfgruppe. The Lergen Kampfgruppe is actually the Salamander Kampfgruppe but with a different name, military reports and all information about the Salamander Kampfgruppe will be twisted in order to misdirect the enemy into thinking that the Lergen Kampfgruppe actually exists despite it merely being a fabrication by the empire and Tanya. Multiple false info will be spread to the enemy countries in the war and everyone will think that the Lergen Kampfgruppe is an actual military group that is even above the Salamander Kampfgruppe when it is simply the Salamander Kampfgruppe.

Explaining it in a better way, the Salamander Kampfgruppe will continue to exist, but many of its achievements will be associated with the inexistent Lergen Kampfgruppe, with this, the empire will misdirect the entire world into thinking that Colonel Von Lergen is an extremely competent commander and a threat to the members of the Alliance against the empire. Tanya decided to collaborate with this farce and after the events of the sixth Volume, Tanya starts to make direct requests to the General staff with Lergen's name in order to make them think that the one making the request is really Colonel Von Lergen, all of Tanya's achievements and feats will be twisted in order to make it look like it was Lergen's feats and achievements, not only this, but multiple other false information would be spread about Colonel Von Lergen being an outstanding commander. In the Eleventh Volume, the empire even made Colonel Lergen help in the invasion of Ildoa while commanding a Regiment of soldiers to strengthen this false image of Lergen being an outstanding commander.

All of this was made with the purpose to make the enemy countries fear Colonel Von Lergen and think that he is a threat to them in the war despite him literally not being even close to being a threat to them. This is an impressive demonstration of a Deception and Logical manipulation feat from Tanya since this farce extended for more than 40 years in the future of the events in the story and multiple countries were deceived by this false information of Colonel Von Lergen being the leader of an extremely powerful military unit, which also includes the Russy Federation. The fact that Tanya misdirected even the Federation’s intelligence, that was stated to have almost perfect information gathering skills and could predict General Zettour's actions, something that not even Tanya is capable of doing, implies that the multiple layers of deception and logical manipulation in this feat are so complex, intricate and effective that not even a country with nigh-perfect information gathering can bypass it, this quite literally turns this into one of the best Logical manipulation I ever saw in SCD.

Emotional manipulation
During the events of Tanya's battalion training plan in the first Volume, literally all of Tanya's actions to make her subordinates abandon the training were highly impressive demonstrations of a direct manipulation feat. During the events of these plans she forced them to pass through multiple life-threatening situations where their life was at risk multiple times. Some of her subordinates were on the verge of getting crazy and were completely paranoid due to how terrifying this training was. In the later volumes, Tanya goes as far as to discipline her Soldiers completely, firstly transforming a bunch of weaklings into the most powerful aerial force of the Empire by pushing them to their absolute limits physically, mentally, psychologically and emotionally. Spamming so much EF on them that all of them became capable of dealing with insane levels of stress far beyond what's considered to be normal for a soldier, formulate strategies to solve unexpected problems quickly and created such a powerful bond with Tanya that they are capable of seeing through her intentions immediately. All of this was the result of Tanya’s impressive EF and direct manipulation over the participants for entire years.

Positive manipulation
During the events of Tanya's battalion training plan in the first Volume, literally all of Tanya's actions to make her subordinates abandon the training were highly impressive demonstrations of a direct manipulation feat. During the events of these plans she forced them to pass through multiple life-threatening situations where their life was at risk multiple times. Some of her subordinates were on the verge of getting crazy and were completely paranoid due to how terrifying this training was. In the later volumes, Tanya goes as far as to discipline her Soldiers completely, firstly transforming a bunch of weaklings into the most powerful aerial force of the Empire by pushing them to their absolute limits physically, mentally, psychologically and emotionally. Spamming so much EF on them that all of them became capable of dealing with insane levels of stress far beyond what's considered to be normal for a soldier, formulate strategies to solve unexpected problems quickly and created such a powerful bond with Tanya that they are capable of seeing through her intentions immediately. All of this was the result of Tanya’s impressive EF and direct manipulation over the participants for entire years.

Gaslighting
During the events of Tanya's Battalion Training plan, she concealed her true intentions with this plan by manipulating everyone's perception to make them think that she was really trying to train them, she even used her acting skills to pass this idea to all the soldiers involved and not a single one of them suspected of this. The fact that she was using training methods that actually buffed the soldier's skills would also conceal her true intentions with this. Half water rations, only half of the soldiers carrying a knife to protect each other and not being capable of using the computation Orbs force the soldiers into adapting to difficult situations, SERE reinforces their abilities to resist extensive enemy interrogation and their abilities to survive in unexpected scenarios, and the hell week reinforces their abilities to keep themselves focused on difficult situations where sleeping or get distracted results on instant death, all the training methods in this training were things that buffed the soldier's overall abilities to surpass adversity, which would conceal her true plan.

Persuasion
During the events of the second Volume, Tanya purposely mentions her case while acting confidently to indirectly manipulate the staff into thinking that her case was logical, it would sound more persuasive for him. In case people think that one's giving a constructive suggestion with the aim of accomplishing one's mission, even an excuse(which is what Tanya is trying to do here by indirectly manipulating the officer into allowing retreat in case things don't go well so she can preserve her life in an unexpected situation) transforms into the genuine truth. And even if she fails, what she said isn't rebellious enough to cause trouble to her. She also ask if she has permission to abort the mission in a worst-case scenario, she says that if her unit fails, the fleet would be exposed to needless danger, she also says that if they don't take preserving the fleet seriously enough, they may let the republican or commonwealth fleet slip in, which would render the blockade meaningless and create a very dangerous situations.

Here Tanya purposely uses Logical arguments to persuade the officer into accepting the idea of aborting the mission if such a bad scenario occurs. After this, she states that due to a quirk of the command structure, she can neither command nor be commanded by marine mages, so she asks permission to advise aborting the mission if the marine regiment commanders agree. The reason as to why she said this is because she understands that the imperial Navy staff officer must know how things work on the military, the Navy's original job is only providing escorts to the mouth of the bay and maybe firing their cannons, so he definitely doesn't want to exacerbate mage command structure issues. With this, Tanya managed to persuade the officer into accepting aborting the mission if the marine regiment commanders agreed while also being capable of concealing her true intentions with this.

Fear inducement
 During the events of the thirteenth Volume, Tanya and her aerial mage battalion receives orders to attack a mechanized infantry brigade that has aerial mages mixed between the normal infantry. But these “aerial mages” aren't exactly “aerial” since they can't fly at all, such a thing doesn't really bother Tanya since she likes to fight against weaklings to preserve her own life. But while attacking the enemy, First Lieutenant Grantz questions Tanya about how wrong it is to fight against a bunch of weaklings like the enemy mages that can't even fly like them.

Noticing this, Tanya sarcastically says that First Lieutenant Grantz has a commendable spirit and that she will make the preparations for him to pick on strong enemies instead. How will she do this? Simple, Tanya says that she will send First Lieutenant Grantz into Federation territory so that he can take on an enemy garrisoned aerial mage battalion all by himself. Hearing this, First Lieutenant Grantz gets terrified, since what Tanya is proposing is sending him to a certain-death situation.

Tanya even makes it look like she won't go back on her decision by saying that she agrees completely with First Lieutenant Grantz about how they shouldn't pick on weaklings. She says all this while grinning constantly to make clear to her subordinate that she is serious and you terrify him even more. First Lieutenant Grantz was begging for mercy and his face was extremely tense. But of course, this is all a bluff, Tanya wouldn't risk losing one of her precious meat shields due to simply having one of her subordinates have an opinion that is contrary to what she believes. In the end, First Lieutenant Grantz apologized for questioning the situation and simply went to carry out Tanya's orders.

Insanity inducement
During the events of Tanya's battalion training plan in the first Volume, literally all of Tanya's actions to make her subordinates abandon the training were highly impressive demonstrations of an insanity inducement feat. During the events of these plans she forced them to pass through multiple life-threatening situations where their life was at risk multiple times. Some of her subordinates were on the verge of getting crazy and were completely paranoid due to how terrifying this training was. With this plan, Tanya also managed to transform a bunch of nobodies into elite aerial mages by pushing them to their absolute limits physically and emotionally.

Non-Verbal Deception
During the events of the Seventh Volume, Colonel Von Lergen informs Tanya that due to a decision made by the Higher-ups, he will now become the commander of a military unit superior to the Salamander Kampfgruppe named “Lergen Kampfgruppe”, but in fact, this Lergen Kampfgruppe won’t even exist at all, it's going to be an inexistent military group that is actually the Salamander Kampfgruppe. The Lergen Kampfgruppe is actually the Salamander Kampfgruppe but with a different name, military reports and all information about the Salamander Kampfgruppe will be twisted in order to misdirect the enemy into thinking that the Lergen Kampfgruppe actually exists despite it merely being a fabrication by the empire and Tanya. Multiple false info will be spread to the enemy countries in the war and everyone will think that the Lergen Kampfgruppe is an actual military group that is even above the Salamander Kampfgruppe when it is simply the Salamander Kampfgruppe.

Explaining it in a better way, the Salamander Kampfgruppe will continue to exist, but many of its achievements will be associated with the inexistent Lergen Kampfgruppe, with this, the empire will misdirect the entire world into thinking that Colonel Von Lergen is an extremely competent commander and a threat to the members of the Alliance against the empire. Tanya decided to collaborate with this farce and after the events of the sixth Volume, Tanya starts to make direct requests to the General staff with Lergen's name in order to make them think that the one making the request is really Colonel Von Lergen, all of Tanya's achievements and feats will be twisted in order to make it look like it was Lergen's feats and achievements, not only this, but multiple other false information would be spread about Colonel Von Lergen being an outstanding commander. In the Eleventh Volume, the empire even made Colonel Lergen help in the invasion of Ildoa while commanding a Regiment of soldiers to strengthen this false image of Lergen being an outstanding commander.

All of this was made with the purpose to make the enemy countries fear Colonel Von Lergen and think that he is a threat to them in the war despite him literally not being even close to being a threat to them. This is an impressive demonstration of a Fabrication and Non-verbal deception feat from Tanya since this farce extended for more than 40 years in the future of the events in the story and multiple countries were deceived by this false information of Colonel Von Lergen being the leader of an extremely powerful military unit, which also includes the Russy Federation. The fact that Tanya misdirected even the Federation’s intelligence, that was stated to have almost perfect information gathering skills and could predict General Zettour's actions, something that not even Tanya is capable of doing, implies that the multiple layers of deception in this feat are so complex, intricate and effective that not even a country with nigh-perfect information gathering can  bypass it, this quite literally turns this into one of the best misdirection and Non-verbal Deception feats I ever saw in SCD.

Fabrication
During the events of the Seventh Volume, Colonel Von Lergen informs Tanya that due to a decision made by the Higher-ups, he will now become the commander of a military unit superior to the Salamander Kampfgruppe named “Lergen Kampfgruppe”, but in fact, this Lergen Kampfgruppe won’t even exist at all, it's going to be an inexistent military group that is actually the Salamander Kampfgruppe. Explaining it in a better way, the Salamander Kampfgruppe will continue to exist, but many of its achievements will be associated with the inexistent Lergen Kampfgruppe, with this, the empire will misdirect the entire world into thinking that Colonel Von Lergen is an extremely competent commander and a threat to the members of the Alliance against the empire. The fact that Tanya misdirected even the Federation’s intelligence, that was stated to have almost perfect information gathering skills and could predict General Zettour's actions, something that not even Tanya is capable of doing, implies that the multiple layers of deception in this feat are so complex, intricate and effective that not even a country with nigh-perfect information gathering can  bypass it, this quite literally turns this into one of the best Fabrication feats I ever saw in SCD.

Verbal Deception
Throughout the story of the Light Novel, Tanya lies multiple times to the members of her battalion and lies multiple times to Visha too, the same Visha who has such an insane understanding of Tanya's emotions that there were moments where she could anticipate Tanya's orders, there were even moments where Visha comprehended Tanya's intentions completely based merely on extremely vague words that came out of her mouth, Tanya herself stated that Visha was the only one capable of understanding her completely.

The fact that Tanya can make lies that not even Visha can see through despite having such a high understanding of Tanya's emotions demonstrates how difficult it is to see through Tanya's words and figure out if what she says is the truth or a lie. In fact, the ability to comprehend Tanya's intentions is something that all her subordinates also have which was stated by Tanya herself, so she can make lies that not even 48+ people capable of comprehending her intentions in an instant can see through.

Bluffing
During the events of Tanya's soldier training plan in the first Volume, before the soldiers started marching to the third waypoint of the training, Tanya gave an advice to them, telling her soldiers to move carefully, such a thing made her soldiers feel paranoid about what they were going to face this time, they didn't had any relevant information about what was going to occur and now Tanya gave advice for them to act carefully without any apparent reason.

Such a thing made the soldiers feel paranoid and probably had the aim of making them quit the training due to fear that what was going to occur would be worse than all the things that already occurred previously in this training(for a little quick context, they were placed in life-threatening situations multiple times). While they were marching, they found Military dobermans and an armed bomber company flying a search overhead, but nothing that was unavoidable for the soldiers.

But despite seeing that the things they were facing were easy to evade, Tanya's warning for the soldiers to move carefully, alongside all the previous actions of her plan made them completely paranoid, they were unconsciously thinking that there must be something dangerous ahead, despite this not being true, the idea of something dangerous being ahead was a bluff that Tanya used to apply Psychological pressure on them and made the soldiers quit the training due to their fear.

But surprisingly for these soldiers there was nothing. Tanya technically didn't bluff when she told her soldiers to walk carefully, she warned them about this in order to avoid the possibility of anyone being harmed to the military dobermans or the armed bomber Company, at least that's what the soldiers would wrongly think, in fact her actual plan with this was to apply Psychological pressure on the soldiers in order to force them into quit the training. Tanya's bluffing was so effective that her soldiers were feeling paranoid, thinking that practically everything around them could be a danger to their lives. What's also worth noting is that no one managed to see through Tanya's bluffing, despite her subordinates’ EP being capable of comprehending Tanya's intentions immediately after staying at her side for only 3 years.

Mass Deception
During the events of the Seventh Volume, Colonel Von Lergen informs Tanya that due to a decision made by the Higher-ups, he will now become the commander of a military unit superior to the Salamander Kampfgruppe named “Lergen Kampfgruppe”, but in fact, this Lergen Kampfgruppe won’t even exist at all, it's going to be an inexistent military group that is actually the Salamander Kampfgruppe. The Lergen Kampfgruppe is actually the Salamander Kampfgruppe but with a different name, military reports and all information about the Salamander Kampfgruppe will be twisted in order to misdirect the enemy into thinking that the Lergen Kampfgruppe actually exists despite it merely being a fabrication by the empire and Tanya. Multiple false info will be spread to the enemy countries in the war and everyone will think that the Lergen Kampfgruppe is an actual military group that is even above the Salamander Kampfgruppe when it is simply the Salamander Kampfgruppe.

Explaining it in a better way, the Salamander Kampfgruppe will continue to exist, but many of its achievements will be associated with the inexistent Lergen Kampfgruppe, with this, the empire will misdirect the entire world into thinking that Colonel Von Lergen is an extremely competent commander and a threat to the members of the Alliance against the empire. Tanya decided to collaborate with this farce and after the events of the sixth Volume, Tanya starts to make direct requests to the General staff with Lergen's name in order to make them think that the one making the request is really Colonel Von Lergen, all of Tanya's achievements and feats will be twisted in order to make it look like it was Lergen's feats and achievements, not only this, but multiple other false information would be spread about Colonel Von Lergen being an outstanding commander. In the Eleventh Volume, the empire even made Colonel Lergen help in the invasion of Ildoa while commanding a Regiment of soldiers to strengthen this false image of Lergen being an outstanding commander.

All of this was made with the purpose to make the enemy countries fear Colonel Von Lergen and think that he is a threat to them in the war despite him literally not being even close to being a threat to them. This is an impressive demonstration of Fabrication and mass deception feat from Tanya since this Fabrication extended for more than 40 years in the future of the events in the story and multiple countries like the Russy Federation, the Commonwealth, the François Republic, the Unified States and all other countries in the world were deceived by this false information of a “Lergen Kampfgruppe” actually existing. This means that Tanya and the General staff managed to deceive the entire world since the “existence” of the inexistent Lergen Kampfgruppe was even documented in history(literally more than half a billion people only counting the Russy Federation's population and the Unified States’ population).

Acting skills
Through the entire Light Novel, Tanya pretended to be a patriotic soldier in the empire, the reason for this being because she wanted to receive gain some benefits by pretending to be loyal to the Empire, what's impressive here is that she kept this Facade for 10+ Volumes of the Light Novel and no one suspected of the fact that she isn't a patriotic Nationalist, she managed to conceal this fact from everyone for literal years.

She deceived General Zettour, General Rudersdorf, Colonel Lergen, all the people involved with the army she met during her military career and all her subordinates of the Salamander Kampfgruppe which also includes Visha, the same Visha that is capable of perceiving subtle emotions on people's words, the same Visha who is capable of comprehending Tanya's intentions in an instant and understand her intentions merely based on vague words.

Tanya managed to deceive even Visha with her acting skills, which demonstrates an insane Deception feat from Tanya. In fact, Tanya even states that almost all, if not all, her subordinates from the 203rd aerial mage battalion also have the ability to immediately understand her intentions due to how much time they spent together. This means that her acting skills managed to deceive 48+ people that had the ability to comprehend her intentions immediately for literal years. The fact that this deception prolonged for literal years is also completely insane.

Concealment
Throughout the entire Light Novel, Tanya managed to conceal her identity from the other countries in the world for more than 40 years, this is highly impressive considering how good the information gathering of the enemy countries are. For example, during the events of the tenth volume of the Light Novel, it's shown to us that the Russy Federation’s information gathering is completely insane, the Federation’s intelligence is capable of gaining information about something as irrelevant as General Zettour's preference for Black Coffee instead of tea and even gained information about Tanya's preference for Black coffee over tea too.

The Federation's intelligence is so insane that they literally managed to gain information about the Empire's entire plan in the tenth Volume to lure them to their territory and encircle the Federation army, even being capable of predicting, to a certain extent, many of the decisions made by empire in the tenth and eleventh Volume, also being capable of predicting some of Zettour's actions like sending Tanya to attack one of their bases, which is highly impressive considering that he is stated to be a military genius and considering the fact that he is so unpredictable that even Tanya was deceived by him once(Tanya wasn't the only one incapable of predicting Zettour, there's literally no one in the General staff that can predict Zettour's actions, which also includes Colonel Uger and Colonel Lergen).

The Federation intelligence was so insanely absurd that even the high-ranking officer, Drake, stated that their analysis of the future and information gathering was almost at the level of absolute perfection. Their information gathering is so good that after the events of the Fifth Volume, the Russy Federation knew the whereabouts of Tanya's battalion at every moment due to the fact that Loria(the leader of the secret police in the Russy Federation, which is practically the same thing as the KGB in the real world) created a group to know the battalion's whereabouts 24/7. Not only this, but during the events of the twelfth Volume, they managed to gain information about the empire's entire plan to make the capitalist side the greatest contributors in the war, which would minimize the participation of the Federation in the war completely and lower the influence of communism in the world.

As if the Federation’s information gathering wasn't insane enough, the Commonwealth also has a really good information gathering via being capable of decoding the empire's code for messages in the army. With this, they were capable of figuring out the exact instant and the exact place in the sky where General Rudersdorf would be flying in an airplane, they even knew that the one inside the airplane was General Rudersdorf and this was an information that was carefully concealed by the empire and was only known by the Higher-ups of the empire and Tanya's battalion that was scouting the airplane. Despite their intelligence gathering being so insane, both these countries and all the other ones involved in the war(Unified States, Ildoa, François Republic, Entente Alliance) failed to gain any information about Tanya's real identity. The reason for this being because since the very beginning of the Light Novel, Tanya always made it extremely difficult for anyone from the enemy side to gain any information about her.

How does she do this? Simple, Tanya always eliminated all the enemy aerial mages she faced to guarantee that there wouldn't be any witnesses about her existence, such a thing was so efficient that she completely concealed her literal existence from the Commonwealth for years, The Commonwealth literally considered the “Devil Of The Rhine” as being a myth created by the traumatized soldiers of the François Republic. Not only this, but she managed to conceal her real identity from the Federation and all other countries in the world that were involved in the war despite being the commander of the empire's most deadly aerial mage unit. The fact she successfully concealed her real identity under these circumstances and for 40+ years demonstrates impressive concealment skills.

Sophistry
(She's currently featless in this subcategory, maybe she will receive a sophistry feat in the next volumes, but she's featless in this subcategory in Volumes 1-14)

Strategic Deception
During the events of the Eastern Front Strategy, Tanya tried to deceive her opponents into thinking that she was really there for a recon-in-force mission, successfully deceived them into thinking that she was going to stay in the altitude of ten thousand feet and attack them with magic formulas form that altitude without advancing towards them, deceived them into thinking that her strategy involved advancing towards Mary Sioux in order to clash with her when she actually had the intention to attack the enemy forces, and also managed to deceive the enemy into thinking that her strategy involved eliminating all the enemy forces when her actual main goal was to eliminate the commander of the enemy forces.

Information Control

Unpredictability
During the events of the Fourteenth Volume, it's shown in chapter 4 that Tanya's decision of gathering an entire division of aerial mages to target the enemy logistics was the response to the enemy's full-scale offensive that was sure to hurt the Federation the most. Her decision was so perfectly aligned to the situation that even the Federation Army, which anticipated almost all the things that the empire would do during this operation, and was capable of predicting the empire's entire plan in the twelfth Volume, hadn't predicted an attack directly on their logistics with literally All the aerial mages that the empire had in the Eastern Front, which is absurd considering that the empire was on a difficult situation with a lack of soldiers and resources in the Eastern Front. Normally doing such a thing without hesitation and with such absurd decisiveness was completely unrealistic, which only demonstrates how absurd Tanya's unpredictability is. Not even her subordinates, who can quite literally perceive her true intentions in an instant, anticipated Tanya's plan to falsify orders.

Reasoning/Thinking

Deductive Reasoning
During the events of the fourth Volume of the Light Novel, Tanya managed to deduce the reason as to why the Federation joined the war against the empire only after the Republic's fall:

P1: As far as the empire is aware, there's no logical reason for the Russy Federation to start a war against the empire.

P2: The Empire was the Russy Federation’s ally, attacking one's ally is something stupid that no one would do without a good motive.

C1: Even if the Empire is not aware of it, there must have been a reason behind the Federation's actions.

P3: But as long as we don't know the motive behind the Federation's actions, negotiating with them using the existing paradigm should be avoided, until we grasp the paradigm, it's unclear if it's even possible to negotiate with that country.

P4: Some people think that if the empire gets through the first attack, they'll find a way to get an early peace through negotiations with the Federation.

P5: The government of the Federation is completely authoritarian and worries a lot about propaganda in order to make themselves look way stronger than they actually are.

C2: This means that a cease-fire would mean death to the Federation since the government won't accept the failure of being defeated in the war due to the Federation's political system of wanting to make themselves look strong thanks to propaganda, losing the war would show to the world how weak they are and the government won't accept such a thing.

P6: This means that the Russy Federation government won't accept losing the war or doing anything that implies that they were being defeated by the empire.

C3: Therefore, negotiating a cease-fire with the Federation is a total fantasy, in fact, even small-scale negotiations on the front lines would be difficult.

P7: But Zettour thinks that one of the reasons as to why the idea of having negotiations with the Federation became impossible was because of Tanya's attack on Moskva.

P8: The Russy Federation is a nation with a tendency to be exclusionary and paranoid about the other countries.

C4 Zettour’s hypothesis isn't true at all, the Federation sees the world in a different way than the Empire does, their paranoia would already be enough to make any form of negotiation impossible.

C5: Considering how paranoid they are, it's possible to consider that the values governing its conduct probably emphasize survival above everything.

P9: The Empire has become stronger and stronger, being capable of fighting two powerhouses of Europe at the same time by itself.

P10: The reason as to why the François Republic and Commonwealth declared war against the empire was because they feared that it could grow stronger and dominate the entire continent.

P11: For the Federation this feeling would be even stronger due to their paranoia about being attacked by bordering nations close to their territory, like the empire.

C6: In other words, the Federation is scared of the empire, or scared of being attacked, if one hypothesizes that it took the initiative to attack for that reason, the choice begins to make sense.

C7: In other words, emotions, the main cause of the Federation's actions and why they initiated this attack against the empire is fear. Military actions obviously are no exception for this.

P12: The existence of the empire is cause for a fear that the Russy Federation can no longer hope to tolerate.

P13: This also means that the Federation's fear towards the empire is a paranoia that won't disappear until the existence of the empire is eradicated.

C8: If that's the case, then the Russy Federation will only lay down its swords and abandon the war when the empire is completely destroyed.

C9: This means that any kind of negotiation with the Russy Federation will be completely meaningless, they simply won't give up until they destroy the empire.

P14: But that still doesn't explain why the Federation didn't stab us in the back while we were fighting against the Republic. If the empire is the object of their fear, what were they waiting for?

P15: Before the declaration of war of the Federation against the empire, the empire had already finished to take the François Republic out of the war by invading the country.

P16: In the War, the Empire was demonstrating an immensely powerful intention of going so far as to completely destroy the Republic.

C10: Attacking the empire at the current state of the War is a completely rational decision in the military realm, the reason for this being because the Federation was frightened of the immensely powerful Empire's intention of going so far as to destroy the Republic.

C11: The Russy Federation is paranoid and scared of the empire's power to invade their country, after seeing the Empire destroying the Republic in the war and proving its overwhelming power, this fear would grow even stronger.

P17: In the current state of the war, the Empire will only grow stronger and stronger, until they become a giant nation controlling Europe as a whole, with no one to oppose it.

C12: The Russy Federation probably noticed that if the War continued like that, a future where they would be forced to fight against a giant empire without any ally would be possible.

P18: The Russy Federation didn't attacked the Empire even while the Republic was being completely destroyed and overwhelmed.

C13: The reason for this, as already established earlier, is because they were so scared to fight against the Empire that they stood by while the Francois Republic was destroyed.

P19: A few months after the Republic's loss against the Empire, the Russy Federation initiated an attack against the Imperial Mages.

C14: As already established earlier, the Russy Federation's initial decision was to stand by while the Republic was overwhelmed, but then they couldn't bear the idea of facing a giant empire on their own, choosing to ally with the Commonwealth and the remnant forces of the Francois Republic.

P20: The Federation is desperate to survive, it's possible to think that they are determined to continue fighting as much as they can.

P21: The Federation won't accept any peace negotiations or anything that implies that they were losing to the Empire, they will continue to fight until the end of the War without asking for peace.

C15: With this idea in mind, it's possible to say that there's no peaceful way to come to terms with the Russy Federation and put an end to the war, either the empire finally collapses or they do.

C16: In other words, what they plan on doing is a war of annihilation until one of the two countries collapses completely.

Final Conclusion: The mere idea of having negotiations with the Federation in order to reach an early peace is a complete fantasy that isn't even worthy considering since they joined the war due to their paranoia of an imperial attack and won't stop until the empire collapses or they do, the reason as to why they only joined the war after the Republic's fall being because they were fearing the idea of fighting against the empire but suddenly after the Republic's fall, they started to fear the idea of having to fight against a giant empire on their own.

This feat is highly impressive due to the fact that even the entire imperial army(which includes their intelligence) didn't have any idea as to why the Federation decided to only join the war now, but Tanya was capable of figuring this out a few days after the Federation joined the war.

Inductive reasoning
During the events of Tanya's Strategy/Plan to survive at Soldim 528, she receives an urgent message from high command: “From General Von Zettour to the Lergen Kampfgruppe. Colonel Von Lergen promptly began the designated actions. At first sight, this message doesn't make any sense for Tanya.

But after analyzing it, she figures out the hidden message contained on this message from high command. Tanya observes that the words “Lergen Kampfgruppe” and “Colonel Von Lergen” followed a specific pattern, they were all outward-facing labels, Tanya then guesses that the same is true for the “designated actions”. She then guesses that the actual message should be read with these words removed: “promptly take action”. After thinking for some time, Tanya notices that action, in the end of the message, is assertive spontaneity. In imperial army staff officer education, it's told for the soldiers that their role is to carry out the mission they're given and to obey not the letter but its intent.

Tanya then starts to think about the intention of this message from Zettour's point of view. Based on all the previous major operations initiated by him like the one used to destroy the Republic, he's an expert with an exhaustive knowledge of logistics, his personality certainly rules out passively waiting with no plan, it's even possible that he could seize the initiative independently, he would even favor an aggressive breakthrough action. After thinking about this, Tanya reaches the conclusion that the word “promptly” in the message should indicate “assertiveness”. Zettour has a very radical side and apparently the Higher-ups want for Tanya to take prompt action. If a staff officer like Tanya is being asked to act, then it can only be an order to begin an operation and choose the optimal action. In other words, the message from high command asks for Tanya to come up with a way to break out of her current situation, that situation being the fact that she's surrounded, this means that General Zettour is going to do something that involves Tanya breaking out of her current situation. After this, she connects the dots and figures out that Zettour is planning to make an operation to break the siege by making an aggressive action.

With this, Tanya managed to figure out the concealed message hidden on the text sent to her by high command, the secret message was that Zettour was going to initiate a maneuver battle and her Kampfgruppe should act in concert with their operation. Here, Tanya managed to grasp General Zettour's intentions and read his thoughts with such a complex and weird message. In this entire Reasoning process, Tanya relied on her understanding of Zettour’s personality and his thinking pattern in order to figure out the message in what appears to have taken less than a minute, perceiving how his thinking pattern could possibly change the hidden meaning of the secret message, which demonstrates her impressive inductive reasoning, pattern recognition, abstract reasoning/thinking and VCI feat.

Abductive reasoning
While talking with Colonel Von Lergen in the third volume, Tanya abducts the Empire's entire operation to defeat the François Republic in the war:

P1: According to what was said, the Empire's Shock and Awe operation has the main purpose to cause confusion in the enemy headquarters and give an opportunity for the empire to attack with all their power.

P2: Rails had been laid on the runway to launch the supplemental acceleration devices. And resting on top of these tracks are the constructs themselves, hooked up to a mind-numbing number of boosters while workers fill the fuel tanks with an unbelievable amount of highly volatile liquid propellant.

P3: Doing such a high-scale change in the rails system would cause a great impact on secrecy, concealing such a thing wouldn't be easy at all.

C1: Considering how difficult it would be to keep all of this as a secret, it's highly probable that Higher-ups have a firm intention to go through with the operation by the time the rails were laid out and the rockers began fueling.

P4: But in truth, this is a totally disproportionate amount of effort for hitting the enemy headquarters, something like this could be made with lesser resources.

P5: Wasting Resources like this is strange, even more strange considering that the Empire is the nation doing this, which was low on resources when the Entente Alliance was still fighting in the beginning of the war.

C2: There's probably a concealed second intention behind this operation made by the General Staff which even Tanya isn't aware of.

P6: After this, she thinks that the cost-effectiveness of the operation is suspiciously bad, knowing that the General staff wouldn't really invest so much to achieve such a single objective.

P7: She is aware that this isn't the sort of operation General Von Zettour would propose with his cool-headed thoughts on attrition warfare.

P8: She also considers Rudersdorf’s participation in this operation to be strange, after all, he is a maneuver warfare specialist.

P9: The Main Focus of this operation is an unorthodox operation that totally relies on cunning tactics that don’t involve large battles.

Initial-conclusion: Maybe the secret intention with this operation was to cause chaos in the enemy headquarters and lead to an even larger battle, Rudersdorf involvement supports this idea since he is a specialist in causing chaos on the enemy.

P10: Causing such a large-scale battle on purpose would definitely put the Higher-ups of the General Staff out of commission.

Counter-conclusion: The idea of the General Staff planning to cause chaos in the headquarters with the purpose of leading to an even larger battle must be wrong, the aim of the empire with destroying the enemy headquarters must be another thing that I didn't noticed yet.

P11: Destroying the enemy headquarters would throw them into chaos. In war, the headquarters work as the head that controls everything, destroy it and the enemy will be completely aimless.

P12: At that point, even a modern army devolves into little more than a mob, which will allow for the empire to advance and initiate maneuver warfare with the Republic.

P13: If the Empire capitalizes in the confusion to make a move, it'll be able to break from the current trench Warfare and change the style of Warfare to its advantage.

C5: That's probably the General staff's true aim, If General Von Rudersdorf capitalizes in the confusion to make a move, he'll be able to break from the current trench Warfare back into maneuver warfare.

P14: A Modern Army, even when nestled in trenches, only exists thanks to its brain, the headquarters.

P15: If you look at how weakened the Red Army was after Stalin's purge, it's possible to see that there's no room for debate about what happens to an army that has lost its command structure.

P16: Not only this, but in a normal nation under regular circumstances, probably the only country that could continue fighting after losing its regular army on the front is the 21st century’s American army.

P17: For quite some time, the Republic has been advancing in the empire's territory, but if we think that such a thing was made on purpose to lure the enemy in, then everything starts to make sense.

C6: This operation aims to encircle and annihilate the enemy after throwing them in disarray by destroying their headquarters. In other words, what the Empire is trying to do with this operation is luring the Republican Army inside the empire territory to encircle and annihilate the enemy.

P18: Daring to allow the enemy to take a strategic location, it's impossible for the enemy to ignore something so tempting right before their eyes.

C7: In other words, it's possible to deduce that the Republican Army will take the bait as planned by the Higher-ups of the empire and continue advancing inside an strategic position of the empire territory.

P19: Before all these events, the Higher-ups in the empire decided to make an entire reorganization of the defensive lines.

C8: Possibly even such a thing was done by the Higher-ups with the intention of baiting the enemy into the empire's territory.

P20: It was already established that the General Staff plans to destroy the enemy's headquarters and make the enemy army confused.

C9: The aim of this operation is to lure the enemy inside the empire's territory, encircle and eliminate them, and advance while they are confused due to not having contact with the headquarters.

C10: If that's the case, then this will be a mobile battle, but not just a mere breakthrough, it's going to be like a revolving door where the enemy enters inside the empire's territory and is pushed all the way back.

Final Conclusion: The Empire's actual goal with this entire Operation is to lure the Republican Army into their territory, destroy their headquarters to confuse the enemy soldiers to make them easy preys for the empire, encircle the confused Republican Army and then force a battle to push the enemy all the way back to their territory. Tanya simply abducted the empire's entire operation before it even came to an end and while having little information about it.

This feat is highly impressive due to the fact that the empire's goal here was thoroughly concealed by the army in a way that not even the Francois Republic noticed what they were planning to do before it was too late, but Tanya was capable of abducting everything in seconds(at max a minute if you low-ball this feat massively) with basic information about the empire's plan.

Associative thinking
During the events of the first Volume, she was using the type 95 computation Orb before it was cursed by Being X, but it was highly defective and it exploded multiple times on past experiments. In this feat, Tanya demonstrated her knowledge of Murphy's law and associated it with her current situation. Murphy's law implies the idea that anything that can go wrong, will go wrong. This is also an impressive abstract thinking feat.

(Tanya's Thoughts)
|
“Humans have learned through experience that, as indicated by Murphy’s Law. Anything that can go wrong will. If an office employee has the ability to cause trouble, trouble there shall be. For example, it’s common knowledge in personnel management not to keep a bankrupt employee in a department dealing with finances. In the same vein,  flying with an orb that can explode at any time is like sitting around waiting for the blast to happen”.

Strategic Thinking
Tanya's Amphibious Operation plan is by far her best demonstration of a strategic thinking feat due to how effectively it improves the empire's situation in the war by guaranteeing the defeat of the Entente Alliance and leaving the François Republic all by itself in the war against the empire.

Linear thinking
During the events of the fourth Volume of the Light Novel, Tanya managed to deduce the reason as to why the Federation joined the war against the empire only after the Republic's fall:

P1: As far as the empire is aware, there's no logical reason for the Russy Federation to start a war against the empire.

P2: The Empire was the Russy Federation’s ally, attacking one's ally is something stupid that no one would do without a good motive.

C1: Even if the Empire is not aware of it, there must have been a reason behind the Federation's actions.

P3: But as long as we don't know the motive behind the Federation's actions, negotiating with them using the existing paradigm should be avoided, until we grasp the paradigm, it's unclear if it's even possible to negotiate with that country.

P4: Some people think that if the empire gets through the first attack, they'll find a way to get an early peace through negotiations with the Federation.

P5: The government of the Federation is completely authoritarian and worries a lot about propaganda in order to make themselves look way stronger than they actually are.

C2: This means that a cease-fire would mean death to the Federation since the government won't accept the failure of being defeated in the war due to the Federation's political system of wanting to make themselves look strong thanks to propaganda, losing the war would show to the world how weak they are and the government won't accept such a thing.

P6: This means that the Russy Federation government won't accept losing the war or doing anything that implies that they were being defeated by the empire.

C3: Therefore, negotiating a cease-fire with the Federation is a total fantasy, in fact, even small-scale negotiations on the front lines would be difficult.

P7: But Zettour thinks that one of the reasons as to why the idea of having negotiations with the Federation became impossible was because of Tanya's attack on Moskva.

P8: The Russy Federation is a nation with a tendency to be exclusionary and paranoid about the other countries.

C4 Zettour’s hypothesis isn't true at all, the Federation sees the world in a different way than the Empire does, their paranoia would already be enough to make any form of negotiation impossible.

C5: Considering how paranoid they are, it's possible to consider that the values governing its conduct probably emphasize survival above everything.

P9: The Empire has become stronger and stronger, being capable of fighting two powerhouses of Europe at the same time by itself.

P10: The reason as to why the François Republic and Commonwealth declared war against the empire was because they feared that it could grow stronger and dominate the entire continent.

P11: For the Federation this feeling would be even stronger due to their paranoia about being attacked by bordering nations close to their territory, like the empire.

C6: In other words, the Federation is scared of the empire, or scared of being attacked, if one hypothesizes that it took the initiative to attack for that reason, the choice begins to make sense.

C7: In other words, emotions, the main cause of the Federation's actions and why they initiated this attack against the empire is fear. Military actions obviously are no exception for this.

P12: The existence of the empire is cause for a fear that the Russy Federation can no longer hope to tolerate.

P13: This also means that the Federation's fear towards the empire is a paranoia that won't disappear until the existence of the empire is eradicated.

C8: If that's the case, then the Russy Federation will only lay down its swords and abandon the war when the empire is completely destroyed.

C9: This means that any kind of negotiation with the Russy Federation will be completely meaningless, they simply won't give up until they destroy the empire.

P14: But that still doesn't explain why the Federation didn't stab us in the back while we were fighting against the Republic. If the empire is the object of their fear, what were they waiting for?

P15: Before the declaration of war of the Federation against the empire, the empire had already finished to take the François Republic out of the war by invading the country.

P16: In the War, the Empire was demonstrating an immensely powerful intention of going so far as to completely destroy the Republic.

C10: Attacking the empire at the current state of the War is a completely rational decision in the military realm, the reason for this being because the Federation was frightened of the immensely powerful Empire's intention of going so far as to destroy the Republic.

C11: The Russy Federation is paranoid and scared of the empire's power to invade their country, after seeing the Empire destroying the Republic in the war and proving its overwhelming power, this fear would grow even stronger.

P17: In the current state of the war, the Empire will only grow stronger and stronger, until they become a giant nation controlling Europe as a whole, with no one to oppose it.

C12: The Russy Federation probably noticed that if the War continued like that, a future where they would be forced to fight against a giant empire without any ally would be possible.

P18: The Russy Federation didn't attacked the Empire even while the Republic was being completely destroyed and overwhelmed.

C13: The reason for this, as already established earlier, is because they were so scared to fight against the Empire that they stood by while the Francois Republic was destroyed.

P19: A few months after the Republic's loss against the Empire, the Russy Federation initiated an attack against the Imperial Mages.

C14: As already established earlier, the Russy Federation's initial decision was to stand by while the Republic was overwhelmed, but then they couldn't bear the idea of facing a giant empire on their own, choosing to ally with the Commonwealth and the remnant forces of the Francois Republic.

P20: The Federation is desperate to survive, it's possible to think that they are determined to continue fighting as much as they can.

P21: The Federation won't accept any peace negotiations or anything that implies that they were losing to the Empire, they will continue to fight until the end of the War without asking for peace.

C15: With this idea in mind, it's possible to say that there's no peaceful way to come to terms with the Russy Federation and put an end to the war, either the empire finally collapses or they do.

C16: In other words, what they plan on doing is a war of annihilation until one of the two countries collapses completely.

Final Conclusion: The mere idea of having negotiations with the Federation in order to reach an early peace is a complete fantasy that isn't even worthy considering since they joined the war due to their paranoia of an imperial attack and won't stop until the empire collapses or they do, the reason as to why they only joined the war after the Republic's fall being because they were fearing the idea of fighting against the empire but suddenly after the Republic's fall, they started to fear the idea of having to fight against a giant empire on their own.

This feat is highly impressive due to the fact that even the entire imperial army(which includes their intelligence) didn't have any idea as to why the Federation decided to only join the war now, but Tanya was capable of figuring this out a few days after the Federation joined the war.

Non-Linear thinking
When Tanya was about to die in her past life, time started to be in slow motion, and she noticed an old man facing him, who was similar to those kind of old man's who commonly appear in Isekai novels. By noticing this, he(Tanya was a man in her past life, so this might be a little confusing) formulated three possible explanations for this.

First possibility: Miraculously, I survived and a doctor is examining me, but I can't quite figure it out. In other words, it's possible that my eyes or brain have suffered severe trauma.

Second possibility: I'm dying and this is an illusion caused by the hallucination. Maybe my life is flashing before my eyes.

Third possibility: I woke up in the real world after mistaking a dream for reality. Maybe I'm still half asleep.

Convergent thinking
During the events of the fourth Volume of the Light Novel, Tanya managed to deduce the reason as to why the Federation joined the war against the empire only after the Republic's fall:

P1: As far as the empire is aware, there's no logical reason for the Russy Federation to start a war against the empire.

P2: The Empire was the Russy Federation’s ally, attacking one's ally is something stupid that no one would do without a good motive.
