Divergent thinking
Suddenly, during the events of the sixth Volume, the Federation makes a large-scale attack on the village where the council for self-government is located and Tanya must protect it in order to ensure that her strategy will work. After thinking for some time, Tanya figures out through the use of impressive logical reasoning that the Federation's aim with this plan isn't a harassment attack, a recon-in-force mission serving as preparation for a major offensive, or a full-scale attack made against the empire.

P1: The enemy is making a large-scale attack on the Northeast side of the Eastern Front with a large amount of aerial mages.

P2: Right now, it's already winter and the cold is nature's fury that should affect both the Federation and the empire equally.

C1: To simplify everything, right now is not the moment to have troops on the move since the winter should have caused casualties on both sides.

P3: Independently of such a thing, the Federation still decided to send multiple aerial mages to attack the empire on a winter offensive.

P4: Such a thing is completely illogical and there's no apparent logical explanation for such a decision made by the Federation.

P5: In the current state of the war where both countries are weakened thanks to Winter, gathering information about how bad the situation is on the enemy side is a good tactic.

C2: Maybe this offensive is actually a Recon-in-force mission to gather information about how much the winter affected the empire.

P6: But I heard that the Federation didn't even have enough forces to mount a counterattack against the empire.

C3: So there's no way that they would make a Recon-in-force mission with such a large amount of soldiers despite suffering so many casualties thanks to winter.

P7: According to strategic objectives, Recon-in-force missions would automatically imply that the enemy is making preparations to a forthcoming operation.

C4: This means that it's possible that the Federation is preparing themselves to make a large operation against the empire.

C5: Naturally, that implies that the Federation has the forces necessary to launch a large-scale attack with what they learn from the recon-in-force mission.

P8: But a major offensive doesn't make sense unless you have a lot of forces to commit to the battlefield.

C6: This means that the Federation is claiming to the empire that they already have many soldiers ready for such an operation.

C7: This means that the Federation has recovered themselves from the casualties caused by winter faster than expected.

P9: It's been only a month or two since the Eastern front against the Federation stagnated thanks to winter.

P10: Even the Federation can't escape from the laws of physics, they still have a minimum of training and gear that wouldn't allow for them to recover so fast.

P11: Thinking logically, if they really had enough strategic reserves to mount a general counterattack they would have committed them to battle before their lines initially collapsed, otherwise, the Federation army General staff would have been letting a ton of soldiers just sit around twiddling their thumbs.

C8: Which means that the Federation army doesn't have enough strategic reserves to prepare for a large operation.

P12: First of all, is this intended to be a recon-in-force mission made by the Federation army or not?

P13: If such a thing was really supposed to be the prelude to a major offensive, everything, including the wireless would be bristling with energy due to how intense such recon-in-force missions tend to be.

P14: But as far as Tanya can perceive, the wireless and the situation close to the area the enemy is attacking is almost peaceful and quiet without any signs of energy that recon-in-force missions normally show.

C9: This means that this attack made by the enemy isn't a recon-in-force mission made with the purpose to prepare for a major operation.

P15: If this attack made by the enemy isn't a recon-in-force mission, then this means that they are attacking with the intention of actually advancing on the empire's territory.

C10: This means that there is the possibility that this is actually a Full-Scale attack made by the Federation army against the empire.

P16: But if this was really a full-scale offensive against the empire, the headquarters and Frontline control would have been screaming for a while now, but there wasn't any information about such a thing coming from them.

C11: This means that whatever the Russy Federation is doing, definitely isn't a full-scale attack against the empire.

P17: This means that what the enemy is doing here is neither a full-scale attack nor a Recon-in-force mission in order to make preparations for a major offensive.

C12: This means that this offensive made by the Federation is probably a limited offensive with their intentions being Recon-in-force.

P18: If this is a Russy Federation limited offensive with Recon-in-force intention, then what's the enemy's goal with this?

P19: Right now, both the empire and the Federation are extremely weakened, but they don't know for sure how much each side was affected by winter.

C13: So it's possible that the Federation launched this attack to prove the imperial defensive lines in the Eastern Front and figure out how much they were affected by winter.

P20: But what made the Russy Federation think that doing such a risky thing was worth the risk?

P21: It's the same as my original question about if the enemy has enough troops to perform a recon-in-force mission.

P22: If not, they're throwing away human lives in vain and even if it's the Federation it's hard to imagine such utter wastefulness is permitted.

P23: Of course, if they decide not to attack as a result of the recon-in-force mission, they can keep sacrifices to a minimum, if by chance a few of the many can be protected, every army would celebrate any kind of tragedy.

P24: But such a thing is still a waste for the Federation army and in their current situation, wasting resources like this is not the kind of thing that the army would allow.

C14: This means that the most likely alternative for the Federation's intentions is a harassment attack, they are simply screwing with us.

P25: Harassment attack is a classic tactic used in war that will tire the enemy out which is really useful in the current situation of the war where the empire is weakened thanks to winter.

P25: The problem is that the empire already confirmed that the Federation mobilized a force that's at least brigade strength.

C15: That's too large of a troop commitment for a mere harassment attack, which means that the enemy's true aim is something else.

Final Conclusion: Currently the enemy's aim with this plan is completely unknown for Tanya due to lack of information, but she's completely certain that it isn't a harassment attack, a recon-in-force mission serving as preparation for a major offensive, or a full-scale attack made against the empire. She deduced all of this in a few seconds after receiving information about the Federation's attack.

Analytical thinking
After trying her best to convince the commander to allow for her to attack the enemy at the Brest Naval Base in the third volume, Tanya analysis her current situation in order to judge what could be the results of her actions:

(Tanya's Thoughts)
|
“If I don’t do anything, there won’t be any trouble, but that’s completely missing the point. Think of Rudel—I shouldn’t be reproached for attacking an enemy country. In other words, if I’m not going to end up before a tribunal after the war, then… this is a permissible risk. Let’s assume we’re attacking. Until just a little while ago, I argued the best I could, but I’m no longer in a position to receive official support. Probably the only contact I have at this point is the submarine we worked with when we used the V-1s. They’ve probably established a patrol line. But honestly, it’d be risky to attempt a pickup over the water without arranging things ahead of time. Considering the possibility that we don't find each other, it's probably safer not to count on it to begin with. I don't want to attack alone, but it seems like the only way. For better or worse, if we use the V-1s we have, we can break through to Brest unimpeded. Then at the very least I can have General de Lugo(the one responsible for the Republic's plan that Tanya figured out through the use of abductive reasoning previously) take his leave from the world. In a way, this is like a hostile takeover of a remarkable new company on the rise. We need to nail down our patents and assets and eliminate any future threat to our company, it's only logical. We'll have a much easier time if we take him out now. I can't stand the idea of history mocking us for our irrational hesitation when we should have intervened.”

Fast Thinking
During a trip for the empire's war college in the first Volume, the instructor takes them to a place with a hill far away, after this, he asks one of the students named Viktor to imagine the following situation: “An enemy built a defensive firing position on that hill. You're ordered to make a swift advance on them with a battalion. Propose a strategy”. After hearing the instructor's question, Tanya formulates a strategy almost instantly to the point that she formulated this strategy before Viktor even managed to verbally answer the instructor's question which means that she formulated this strategy in literal milliseconds, her strategy was as follows: “A firing position on that hill? If it was up there, we wouldn't be able to break through or go around, would we? We’d either have to make a dejected retreat or use the heavy artillery to pummel them from a distance. Or maybe have mages charge in”. The fact that she formulated this little short strategy almost instantly is what makes this feat actually impressive.

After this, the instructor questions Tanya about what she would do in such a peculiar situation. She quickly formulates a new strategy to deal with this situation, this strategy being to make a large retreat and then maneuver around along a different ridgeline. It's then shown to us her thoughts regarding this strategy: “Then the best thing to do is avoid unnecessary casualties. Luckily, depending on the ridgeline, it won’t even take any longer than any other tactic. More importantly, there’s no need to launch a reckless attack. Ordering a charge against a position with a good field of fire is not only reckless but cruel. Can guys like that get jobs in the General Staff? All I can say is I hope not. In any case, the only way to beat firepower with flesh is by having more soldiers than bullets…”, she thought about all this in mere milliseconds before the instructor was even capable of saying anything about her answer.

But the instructor questions her about what she would do if she didn't have enough time to successfully make her strategy to maneuver around along a different ridgeline. She then thinks about a new strategy in some few seconds, this strategy involves employing skirmish tactics with mages and infantry, in this strategy, the mages would take out the emplacement and the infantry can back them up. Her thoughts regarding this strategy are also shown to us exactly the way it was shown before: Aerial mages could definitely capture the position. We’d have been ready to take casualties, but it would be much better than trying to break through with infantry alone. I mean, I’m an aerial mage. If you’re asking me what I would do if I were in command, then it’s not unreasonable for me to have mages with my infantry battalion”. She thought about all this in an instant.

Intuitive thinking
During the events of Tanya's strategy to attack the Federation headquarters in the seventh Volume, she receives information from Major Weiss that they found the location of the enemy Main headquarters, Tanya intuitively figured out that it was a trap. She recognizes that in this battle, the Federation enemy soldiers and headquarters demonstrated to be extremely competent, she understands that the enemy is probably waiting for them to appear and perceives that the enemy main headquarters knows what they're doing due to how much the Federation coordination improved.

Strategy/Planning

(I used the Planning/Strategy Subcategories from the original DAX categories document, the AI version of the doc and the all categories for SCD Document, I'll also use a few other additional planning/strategy subcategories whose meaning you can understand by merely reading the name):
Original DAX Categories Doc

AI version of DAX Categories Doc

All categories for SCD Doc

Strategy fortitude
Tanya's strategy in the Norden Sea and her battalion training strategy/Plan are the best demonstrations of strategy fortitude by Tanya.

Strategy efficiency
Tanya's Turus Naval Base strategy in the third volume of the Light Novel or her battalion training plan/strategy in the first Volume are extremely good in strategy efficiency. During the events of the Turus Naval Base strategy, Tanya efficiently used the resources that she had in hand in order to formulate a strategy that would: not put her nor her battalion in direct danger, a strategy that would help her save her allies who were captured by the Republic forces, a strategy that would guarantee to Tanya that the imperial forces could retreat if necessary without worrying about being punished, and a strategy that would guarantee that the imperial forces would manage to beat the Republic forces with losses at a minimum. All of these aims were achieved by Tanya with a single strategy despite not having many resources at hand.

Strategy alignment
Tanya's Eastern Front Strategy in the third tab of this document or her battalion training plan/strategy in the first Volume of the Light Novel at the first tab of this doc are her best strategy alignment feats. During the events of her Eastern Front strategy, literally all Tanya's actions before Mary's intervention aligned to guarantee the smooth progress of the Strategy.

Strategy building
Tanya's Eastern Front Strategy in the Tenth Volume of the Light Novel and her Turus Naval Base strategy in the third volume of the Light Novel are definitely her best strategy building feats considering that she had to adapt to these situations completely and build her strategy while having to worry about lack of resources and enemy attacks.

Strategy forecasting
During the events of Tanya's Eastern Front Strategy in the Tenth Volume of the Light Novel at the third tab of this document, she foresaw that the fact that she came with only a single person at her side would make the enemy think that she was simply there for a recon-in-force mission, predicted that the enemy forces have improved and could now fly at eight thousand feet, anticipated that the enemy aerial mages were being commanded by two different commanders and anticipated that there would be a moment where they would be preparing their magic formulas in unison, which would make them completely defenseless, anticipated that the enemy aerial mages would be focusing mainly on maintaining their flight altitude instead of focusing on the movements made by Tanya and Visha since they weren't used to flying at such a high altitude, which gave an opportunity for Tanya to attack the enemy aerial mages, anticipated that the enemy aerial mages would have difficulty to defend themselves for her unexpected charge since they were altitude at eight thousand feet and weren't well adapted to the new environment of flying at such a high altitude, anticipated that the enemy aerial mages would be completely scared after her unexpected charge, so scared that they would forget to help their commander despite him being under attack and so scared that they would be completely incapable of doing anything without orders. She anticipated that all these things would occur before the battle even began.

Strategy Complexity
Tanya's Eastern Front Strategy in the Tenth Volume of the Light Novel at the third tab of this doc is by far the best demonstration of strategy complexity she showed so far in the story.

Strategy Logistics
Tanya's battalion training plan/strategy in the first tab of this document and her Soldim 528 Strategy/Plan in the third tab of this doc are the best ones for strategy logistics. In her Soldim 528 Strategy, all actions made by Tanya were made with her current situation taken into account, she efficiently built her strategy and repelled the enemy attacks while sacrificing only the minimum of resources for the sake of her troops.

Strategy Flexibility
Tanya's Strategy in the Commonwealth mainland battle at the third tab of this doc and her Soldim 528 Strategy/Plan are right now her best strategy flexibility feats due to how many unexpected adversities she faced during the events of these strategies that forced Tanya to adapt her strategy to the unexpected situations.

Strategy Execution
Tanya's Soldim 528 Strategy/Plan during the events of the eighth Volume and her battalion training plan/strategy in the first volume of the Light Novel are the best demonstrations of strategy execution shown by Tanya so far due to how smooth, well-planned and effective the execution of the strategy was.

Strategy Effectiveness
In Tanya's Turus Naval Base strategy at the third volume of the Light Novel, it's shown how efficient the strategy was when we see its end results. Tanya managed to free her allies in battle, grant a way for her battalion and allies to retreat if necessary without having to worry about being punished, lower the number of enemy soldiers massively and force them to retreat, doing all this while avoiding direct combat with the enemy in order to preserve her own life and simply destroying all the enemy headquarters to throw the enemy soldiers in confusion in order to give an advantage for her allies.

Strategy Control
Literally all the events of Tanya's battalion training plan/strategy were under her control and occurred according to her predictions. She had absolute control over everything her subordinates did during a training with a duration of 1 entire month.

Planning Contingencies
During the events of Tanya's Battalion training plan/strategy, we are shown the various Contingencies that were prepared by Tanya beforehand in order to guarantee that her soldiers would give up some moment of the training. In the end of her plan I listed all of Tanya's contingencies, so I suggest for you to go read it in order to understand this feat better.

Planning Versatility
Tanya's Battalion training strategy/plan in the first tab of this document is by far her best Planning Versatility feat because of the multiple contingencies and the multiple different ways to reach the desired outcome for Tanya that the plan could accomplish, in the end of the first volume I listed all of the eleven contingencies during the events of the Battalion training Plan, so I suggest for you to go read it in order to understand this feat better. Anyway, what matters here is that Tanya's plan could have succeeded in 11 different scenarios, actually twelve different scenarios if we consider her first action of exploding the barracks.

Planning coverage
Tanya's Battalion training strategy/plan in the First Volume or her amphibious operation Plan in the second volume at the first tab of this doc are really impressive demonstrations of planning coverage due to the many things these plans cover.

Planning foresight
Tanya's Battalion training strategy/Plan in the first tab of this doc is her best planning foresight feat since she foresaw multiple different ways her plan could go wrong, created contingencies for them, even going as far as creating a contingency that she considered to be foolproof, Tanya also predicted the actions of her subordinates and how her actions would affect them through the progress of the plan.

Planning Intricacies
Tanya's Battalion Training strategy/plan in the first volume of the Light Novel at the first tab of this document is by far her best planning intricacies feat since that plan had something like 12+ different traps and it was quite difficult to see through it.

Preparation Process
During the events of Tanya's Battalion training strategy/plan in the first Volume, Tanya prepared beforehand the most insane training methods from every special unit known to military history in order to use it in her plan. The first phase of her training would be american-style stuff like water acclimation training, but her soldiers will do an even harder version of it, they would do an extremely hard altitude acclimation training. The second phase will be the infamous hell week, in this phase, the soldiers will be forced to sleep a total of four hours in four days. In case someone manages to somehow resist the hell week, they would have a long SERE training, which will be a packed schedule of resistance to enemy interrogation and intense survival training.

After the SERE and the hell week, Tanya would throw out her soldiers to an extremely long distance and they would have to march without the use of magic through the Alpen Mountains for an entire week. During this training, the soldiers would have only the absolute minimum sleep and rest. The training methods used are based on the worst battle records, like half water rations, the soldiers not being allowed to carry food and the use of computation orbs lead to instant failure, they'll only be allowed to carry a knife with them one for two people, in other words, only half of the soldiers would have a knife with them while the other half wouldn't have any knife with them. She does everything that she can in order to make the soldiers fall into insanity and give up on the training. In this training, even the slightest error made by a soldier results in them failing on the training.

And in case the plan goes wrong, Tanya has foolproof insurance to guarantee that things will go well for her. This foolproof insurance being an early mass-production prototype made by Scientist Schugel, the same Chief engineer that made the type 95 computation Orb that Tanya uses during the entire story of the LN, the same guy who is famous for making prototypes of computation orbs that end up exploding or malfunctioning. What Tanya plans on doing with these early prototypes is making her soldiers use it and wait for it to malfunction, after all, these are early versions of computation orbs that Schugel is working on, they are not even completed yet and he's literally known for making computation Orbs that malfunction and explode. When a single one of these Prototypes of Computation Orbs made by Schugel malfunction and explode, the other soldiers seeing such a thing will notice how dangerous the situation is and will finally decide to quit. All of the things stated above were events that Tanya prepared beforehand to use on her plan.

Short-term planning
Tanya's plan in Soldim 528(the vast majority of this was actually a strategy) and her amphibious operation plan at the second volume of the Light Novel are currently her best Short-term planning feats due to how complex and effective these plans were.

Long-term planning
Tanya's entire Battalion Training strategy/plan on the first volume of the Light Novel at the first tab of this doc had a duration of one entire month and was extremely complex.

Planning Formulation Speed
Tanya formulated the main aim and process for her amphibious operation Plan in the second tab of this doc in a few minutes and formulated her entire battalion training plan in a single day.

Methods
Tanya's Soldim 528 strategy in the third tab of this document or her battalion training plan/strategy in the first tab are insanely good demonstrations of methods.

Tactics
Tanya's entire plan in Soldim 528 and her battalion training strategy/plan are extremely good examples of tactics for Tanya.

Most Difficult Situations
During the events of the Soldim 528 Plan/Strategy, Tanya had to face an entire battalion of multinational aerial mages, multiple mages from the Federation army and Mary Sioux, who was capable of surviving magic formulas from two aerial mage companies, an charge from these same two aerial mage companies, a direct hit from a sniping formula made with Tanya's Type 95 Computation Orb capable of creating Nuke-like explosions and even fight against Visha to the point she was completely tired, even Major Weiss wasn't certain if he could beat Mary.

Not only this, but Mary Sioux had such an insane amount of magic aptitude that Tanya herself recognized Mary as being a “damn monster”, she even stated that her ridiculous amount of magic defied all logic during the events of the Tenth Volume, not only this, but Tanya even said that Mary was using this insane amount of magic to use a magic formula stated to “literally alter the world” and even said that she was exactly like Being X due to how insanely powerful and bothering she was. Tanya had to face all these enemies while having only two elite aerial mage companies at her side and one replacement company filled with newbies, not only this, but she also had to beat them quick enough in order to reach the headquarters of B Group since an attack from the Federation was occurring there.

Another thing that makes this situation be even more difficult is that Tanya received orders from Zettour to purposely allow for her Kampfgruppe to be surrounded by enemy forces in order to allow for the soldiers of Group B to initiate an encirclement on the enemy, Tanya's Kampfgruppe will be completely surrounded by four or five entire divisions of enemy soldiers while her Kampfgruppe is merely an augmented regiment and they don't have permission to retreat even in a scenario where they are suffering with lack of food, water or ammunition. Going against this and retreating anyway would be the same as going against the orders of the General staff and would make Tanya be executed or punished heavily.

In the hideout they are using as a Base they receive supplies in an extremely slow way and barely have any resources to fight against the enemy. Another thing is that Tanya's Kampfgruppe is by design, fundamentally a breakthrough force, tying it down with orders to defend a static position literally negates all its strengths. Not only this, but Tanya's Kampfgruppe doesn't even have a proper headquarters to use in order to manage things efficiently and she will have to survive in Soldim 528 for one entire week while facing all this adversity and while constantly having to repel enemy attacks. She created a strategy to solve all these problems while having to manage the emotions of every single one of her soldiers in order to make sure that their morale doesn't fall to rock bottom.

Adaptability

Social Adaptability
During the events of the seventh Volume, Tanya decided to take command of the Fifty-fourth regiment of soldiers in order to lead them efficiently since their commander was killed by the Federation and they didn't have anyone to lead them. She never had to take command of such a large group of soldiers before, even her Kampfgruppe that is composed by an aerial mage battalion, an entire infantry company, an armored forces unit and an artillery unit doesn't have an amount of soldiers equal to a regiment of infantry soldiers, not only this, but when she took control of her Kampfgruppe, she didn't have to lead them while being literally on the middle of the battlefield and while being under enemy fire. Despite such a thing, she immediately adapted herself socially to the position of regiment's commander despite literally receiving enemy fire and despite never having led such a large group of soldiers before.

Cognitive Adaptability
During the events of the first Volume of the Light Novel, Tanya received the cursed item from a deity similar to Being X, this cursed item was the type 95 Computation Orb, a computation Orbs that provided its user with high levels of mana and amplified their magic aptitude to levels beyond human comprehension. This item quite literally gives the highest level of magic to its user, something that would be impossible for anyone to control, a person that received such power wouldn't even be capable of understanding how to use it at first, let alone using the Type 95 at its maximum potential. But on her first time interacting with this item, Tanya adapted herself cognitively to its power and managed to use it to its full potential in the later volumes of the story. Demonstrating a cognitive adaptability and cognitive discipline feat that allows her to adapt to the highest levels of mana, despite being in a situation that would make a normal person faint. This would also be a good demonstration of cognitive adaptability since Tanya managed to fully control this power after using it once.

Environmental Adaptability
During the events of the first Volume, Tanya managed to fully adapt to the new world where she was sent to against her will by Being X, a world filled with magic and facing an extremely destructive war, despite Tanya originally being from a world that didn't have magic and wasn't facing such a high scale war, she not only managed to completely adapt to the new things about that world despite it being so different from her original world, but even demonstrated to be one of the best magic users in the future during her career as an aerial mage despite being in the body of a child and the fact that there wasn't magic in her original world.

Not only this, but during the events of the eighth Volume of the Light Novel, Tanya had to adapt to the environment in Soldim 528. She was completely surrounded by enemy forces that attacked her Kampfgruppe's headquarters every single day, she was suffering with an extremely low amount of resources and had to survive in this situation for one entire week, but she easily managed to adapt to this situation, while surviving for 7 days and solving the problems involving the lack of resources.

Situational Adaptability
Tanya's entire strategy to counter the Russy Federation attack in the Seventh Volume of the Light Novel at the second tab of this doc is an impressive Situational adaptability feat due to the fact that she didn't expect that situation at all and had to completely adapt to the new situation in a matter of seconds while efficiently leading her troops in order to guarantee victory against the enemy soldiers.

Not only this, but during the events of her Strategy in the Commonwealth mainland battle that can also be found in the Tenth Volume of the Light Novel, Tanya had to adapt herself to fight against an entire brigade of enemy aerial mages in an ambush and even had to adapt to an enemy that had the same level of tactical prowess as her.

Another Good Feat was when Tanya casually adapted herself to control Divine Power after merely using the type 95 Cursed Orb once. Learning how to use it at its full potential after using it once.

Sensory + Cold Reading

Observation
Through the entire Light Novel, Tanya demonstrates a highly impressive understanding of her subordinates' emotions and psychology. Her understanding of their psychology is so good that sometimes she is capable of anticipating what they're going to say and what they are thinking or how they feel about something merely based on vague words or minimal/subtle changes in their facial expressions. She gained such an impressive insight of their emotions and entire psychology in merely a few years, which demonstrates a really impressive Observation and Emotional Perception feat. With such an insane understanding of their emotions, Tanya is capable of comprehending their intentions similarly to how her subordinates are capable of comprehending her intentions immediately. She perceives and understands their emotions perfectly as if they were open books for her, doing such a thing with 48 different people.

Perception
During the events of her Norden Sea strategy, Tanya figures out that she was specifically being attacked by battle cruisers by merely looking at the flash made by the battle cruiser guns. Not only this, but during the events of the tenth Volume of the Light Novel, Tanya and Visha were in the middle of an attack in the Federation's Base, suddenly Tanya was attacked by a long-range magic formula that she managed to dodge with some difficulty. After this, she used a magic observation formula to identify the source of the attack, that being Mary, despite her being at a distance of 4-5 kilometers.

In other words, if you use Hax in Smart Character Debate, this is an insane perception On-screen feat for Tanya. Another thing is that during the events of the eighth Volume, Tanya observed the insignias, race, gear and uniform of the enemy aerial mage soldiers despite them literally moving at 97 meters per second and being in the middle of a close-quarters combat. Another good feat is that During Tanya's talk with Zettour during the events of the eighth Volume, the narrator of the story in the Light Novel states that if Tanya had felt any hint of Zettour manifesting a Being X-like energy from his body, she may have responded differently to his weird actions. This statement made by the Narrator of the story implies that Tanya is capable of seeing harmful energy coming from the body of other people.

This means that Tanya is capable of perceiving when someone has malicious intentions that could be harmful for her, this demonstrates an insane emotional perception and Perception statement since this means that Tanya is capable of sensing when someone has harmful and malicious intentions by merely observing them manifesting what the narrator claimed to be a Being X-like harmful energy from their body. Sadly this ability was never actually shown to us in the Light Novel, so this is completely invalid in scaling methods such as Proven Ability(PA), but I still decided to mention it here for those who use normal scaling.

Cold reading
While being in a train during the events of the Fifth Volume, Colonel Von Uger, a friend of Tanya from the war college appears, Tanya then welcomes him with a silly joke and Uger also makes a little joke. Normally with the joke Tanya made, people with good sense of humor should laugh boldly which wasn't the case with Uger, noticing this, Tanya judged that he didn't develop a good sense of humor yet, but after thinking for some time, Tanya notices something strange, Uger isn't the type to banter or make jokes naturally, the fact that someone like him picked up a sense of humor, even if it's a lousy one, makes Tanya recognize that such a thing wasn't a good sign.

Tanya recognizes that compared to the other soldiers of the War college that are either idiosyncratic or sincere, both her and Uger are the extremely serious soldiers, due to harsh experiences in the front line, soldiers tend to develop some sense of humor like Tanya did, but it was different with Uger, he acted on the rear and didn't had to worry about developing sense of humor since he didn't fought battles directly, Tanya recognizes that him making jokes is extremely out of his character and perceives that despite his eyes seeming to be smiling, they weren't.

After this, Uger gives stuffed kilos of Arabica Coffee to Tanya since he knows that it's difficult to get any from the front lines, Tanya then perceives that Uger is being considerate of condition soldiers face on the front lines, despite such a thing being ideal for a General staff officer like him, Uger isn't the type to be comfortable mixing business and personal errands like this. In other words, he came to that train to talk with Tanya about something so bad that his conscience decided to make an exception. While continuing to talk with Uger, Tanya perceives that he wanted to make their talk look like a private conversation. Noticing this, Tanya figures out that whatever he wants to talk with her must be something that he can't let other people hear, in other words, an unofficial message from the General staff.

Foresight

Prediction
During the events of the second volume, it's stated by Zettour himself that all the events of the Light Novel that implied that the world war was going to occur were progressing exactly like Tanya said during the time he talked with her in the first volume. Not only this, but multiple times in the Light Novel, Tanya predicts strategies and actions from enemy countries despite having literally zero information about it.

Anticipation
Tanya literally spams this on practically every single improvised strategy she makes when she's facing an unexpected situation. A good example of Tanya's anticipation skills would be during the events of Tanya's Eastern Front Strategy in the Tenth Volume of the Light Novel at the third tab of this document, in this volume, she foresaw that the fact that she came with only a single person at her side would make the enemy think that she was simply there for a recon-in-force mission, anticipated that the enemy forces have improved and could now fly at eight thousand feet, anticipated that the enemy aerial mages were being commanded by two different commanders and anticipated that there would be a moment where they would be preparing their magic formulas in unison, which would make them completely defenseless, anticipated that the enemy aerial mages would be focusing mainly on maintaining their flight altitude instead of focusing on the movements made by Tanya and Visha since they weren't used to flying at such a high altitude, which gave an opportunity for Tanya to attack the enemy aerial mages, anticipated that the enemy aerial mages would have difficulty to defend themselves for her unexpected charge since they were altitude at eight thousand feet and weren't well adapted to the new environment of flying at such a high altitude, anticipated that the enemy aerial mages would be completely scared after her unexpected charge, so scared that they would forget to help their commander despite him being under attack and so scared that they would be completely incapable of doing anything without orders. She anticipated that all these things would occur before the battle even began and anticipated all of her enemy's actions(at least before Mary intervened).

Trap Setting
Tanya's entire Battalion Training plan in the first Volume and her trap setting in Soldim 528 are both insane demonstrations of Trap setting feats. Tanya's Battalion training plan literally had a large amount of contingencies and traps that had the purpose of guaranteeing that her soldiers wouldn't be capable of keep going. It quite literally had 12+ complex traps prepared by Tanya, almost all of the traps here would be enough to either make a person lose their sanity and go nuts, or make them develop some kind of extreme trauma.

Trap Evasion
Tanya's entire strategy during the events of the Fourteenth Volume is an insanely absurd demonstration of a trap evasion feat from Tanya since she was completely taken by surprise by the enemy's ambush and even had to face an enemy with the same level of tactical prowess as her. Here's a document explaining the entire Situational Adversity that Tanya had to face.

Miscellaneous

Problem solving
Tanya's best feats in problem solving would be her Soldim 528 Strategy and the Rising Dawn Counter Strategy. During the events of Tanya's Soldim 528 strategy, Tanya managed to solve the problem of being attacked by the enemy multinational unit, the Federation aerial mages, Mary Sioux, the problem of having been sent to the region of Soldim 528, a region that they never went before, solve the problem of being surrounded by enemy forces and constantly having to counter enemy attempts to invade the stronghold of Soldim 528, the problem of being with a huge lack of resources and solve the problem of having to reach the Imperial Headquarters in order to defeat the Federation enemy soldiers attacking the Imperial Headquarters of B Group in a really short period of time. Not only this, but the planning part of this strategy also made Tanya and Zettour solve the problem of the Federation’s strong defense that didn't allow the empire to advance.

Learning ability
During the events of the first Volume of the Light Novel, Tanya received the cursed item from a deity similar to Being X, this cursed item was the type 95 Computation Orb, a computation Orbs that provided its user with high levels of mana and amplified their magic aptitude to levels beyond human comprehension. This item quite literally gives the highest level of magic to its user, something that would be impossible for anyone to control, a person that received such power wouldn't even be capable of understanding how to use it at first, let alone using the Type 95 at its maximum potential. But on her first time interacting with this item, Tanya adapted herself cognitively to its power and managed to use it to its full potential in the later volumes of the story. Demonstrating a cognitive adaptability and cognitive discipline feat that allows her to adapt to the highest levels of mana, despite being in a situation that would make a normal person faint. This would also be a good demonstration of Learning Ability since Tanya managed to fully control this power after using it once.

Creativity
During the events of the fourth Volume, Tanya invents the concept of “Kampfgruppe” and proposes for the General staff to turn it into a real unit to use in battle. Kampfgruppe is a military unit composed of a battalion of aerial mages, an artillery unit, an armored forces unit and a company of infantry soldiers. Its main focus is being highly flexible and its different components are organically bound into a cohesive unit that can be attached or detached as necessary, resulting in a level of adaptability and flexibility that no one would get with a normal formation. Each arm of the Kampfgruppe retains a degree of autonomous combat ability and it is the most efficient military unit for breakthroughs, Tanya invented this concept for the imperial army and even someone like General Von Zettour haven't thought about such a formation of an unit, despite being considered a military genius in the empire and having extremely high experience, while Tanya thought about such a thing while having only three years worth of experience fighting in the war. In fact, not a single one of the staffers in the General staff thought about such a thing despite the story referring to them as being Geniuses.

Academics
Tanya graduated from the Empire War college without any problem and got extremely high scores in every single area while she was studying in the War college, her scores were so high that she was considered to be the second best student in the Imperial War College. Not only this, but during the events of the tenth Volume, Tanya reveals to us that she studied for her college entrance exams some moment in the past before being Isekaied to this strange world of Youjo Senki.

Connecting The Dots
While talking with Colonel Von Lergen in the third volume, Tanya abducts the Empire's entire operation to defeat the François Republic in the war:

P1: According to what was said, the Empire's Shock and Awe operation has the main purpose to cause confusion in the enemy headquarters and give an opportunity for the empire to attack with all their power.

P2: Rails had been laid on the runway to launch the supplemental acceleration devices. And resting on top of these tracks are the constructs themselves, hooked up to a mind-numbing number of boosters while workers fill the fuel tanks with an unbelievable amount of highly volatile liquid propellant.

P3: Doing such a high-scale change in the rails system would cause a great impact on secrecy, concealing such a thing wouldn't be easy at all.

C1: Considering how difficult it would be to keep all of this as a secret, it's highly probable that Higher-ups have a firm intention to go through with the operation by the time the rails were laid out and the rockers began fueling.

P4: But in truth, this is a totally disproportionate amount of effort for hitting the enemy headquarters, something like this could be made with lesser resources.

P5: Wasting Resources like this is strange, even more strange considering that the Empire is the nation doing this, which was low on resources when the Entente Alliance was still fighting in the beginning of the war.

C2: There's probably a concealed second intention behind this operation made by the General Staff which even Tanya isn't aware of.

P6: After this, she thinks that the cost-effectiveness of the operation is suspiciously bad, knowing that the General staff wouldn't really invest so much to achieve such a single objective.

P7: She is aware that this isn't the sort of operation General Von Zettour would propose with his cool-headed thoughts on attrition warfare.

P8: She also considers Rudersdorf’s participation in this operation to be strange, after all, he is a maneuver warfare specialist.

P9: The Main Focus of this operation is an unorthodox operation that totally relies on cunning tactics that don’t involve large battles.

Initial-conclusion: Maybe the secret intention with this operation was to cause chaos in the enemy headquarters and lead to an even larger battle, Rudersdorf involvement supports this idea since he is a specialist in causing chaos on the enemy.

P10: Causing such a large-scale battle on purpose would definitely put the Higher-ups of the General Staff out of commission.

Counter-conclusion: The idea of the General Staff planning to cause chaos in the headquarters with the purpose of leading to an even larger battle must be wrong, the aim of the empire with destroying the enemy headquarters must be another thing that I didn't noticed yet.

P11: Destroying the enemy headquarters would throw them into chaos. In war, the headquarters work as the head that controls everything, destroy it and the enemy will be completely aimless.

P12: At that point, even a modern army devolves into little more than a mob, which will allow for the empire to advance and initiate maneuver warfare with the Republic.

P13: If the Empire capitalizes in the confusion to make a move, it'll be able to break from the current trench Warfare and change the style of Warfare to its advantage.

C5: That's probably the General staff's true aim, If General Von Rudersdorf capitalizes in the confusion to make a move, he'll be able to break from the current trench Warfare back into maneuver warfare.

P14: A Modern Army, even when nestled in trenches, only exists thanks to its brain, the headquarters.

P15: If you look at how weakened the Red Army was after Stalin's purge, it's possible to see that there's no room for debate about what happens to an army that has lost its command structure.

P16: Not only this, but in a normal nation under regular circumstances, probably the only country that could continue fighting after losing its regular army on the front is the 21st century’s American army.

P17: For quite some time, the Republic has been advancing in the empire's territory, but if we think that such a thing was made on purpose to lure the enemy in, then everything starts to make sense.

C6: This operation aims to encircle and annihilate the enemy after throwing them in disarray by destroying their headquarters. In other words, what the Empire is trying to do with this operation is luring the Republican Army inside the empire territory to encircle and annihilate the enemy.

P18: Daring to allow the enemy to take a strategic location, it's impossible for the enemy to ignore something so tempting right before their eyes.

C7: In other words, it's possible to deduce that the Republican Army will take the bait as planned by the Higher-ups of the empire and continue advancing inside an strategic position of the empire territory.

P19: Before all these events, the Higher-ups in the empire decided to make an entire reorganization of the defensive lines.

C8: Possibly even such a thing was done by the Higher-ups with the intention of baiting the enemy into the empire's territory.

P20: It was already established that the General Staff plans to destroy the enemy's headquarters and make the enemy army confused.

C9: The aim of this operation is to lure the enemy inside the empire's territory, encircle and eliminate them, and advance while they are confused due to not having contact with the headquarters.

C10: If that's the case, then this will be a mobile battle, but not just a mere breakthrough, it's going to be like a revolving door where the enemy enters inside the empire's territory and is pushed all the way back.

Final Conclusion: The Empire's actual goal with this entire Operation is to lure the Republican Army into their territory, destroy their headquarters to confuse the enemy soldiers to make them easy preys for the empire, encircle the confused Republican Army and then force a battle to push the enemy all the way back to their territory. Tanya simply abducted the empire's entire operation before it even came to an end and while having little information about it.

This feat is highly impressive due to the fact that the empire's goal here was thoroughly concealed by the army in a way that not even the Francois Republic noticed what they were planning to do before it was too late, but Tanya was capable of abducting everything in seconds(at max a minute if you low-ball this feat massively) with basic information about the empire's plan.

Mind reading
During the events of the Tenth Volume, while Tanya was talking with General Von Zettour, he says: “Just like on the Rhine front, we’re only allowed to retreat a distance that will enable us to conduct a counterattack. These are the orders from the homeland. Assuming this is the case, then we can’t simply encircle the enemy while we retreat. There has to be more to it than that”. After hearing Zettour saying this, she figured out that he was planning to make a frontal assault on the enemy forces, but she was wrong.

Zettour says that they don't have the time nor the obligation to mount a frontal assault, saying that they would take a more deceptive approach, after saying this, Zettour pointed his finger to a specific location on his map and after seeing it, Tanya abducted his entire plan to deal with the enemy forces and even figured out what he wanted for her to do in an instant. By observing the area where he was pointing at and his previous words, she managed to completely discern his true intentions which is highly impressive since Zettour's intentions are so difficult to read that even Tanya failed to evade a little trap made by him on this volume.

Not only this, but throughout the entire Light Novel, he never lost his composure except in situations where there was no need for him to keep his composure. This is a highly impressive mind reading feat considering how cunning and difficult to read Zettour is. Basically, what Zettour wanted was to attack an enemy base far deep at the Federation’s territory, he wanted for Tanya to act as a bait to attract the enemy's attention while his subordinates would be attacking the enemy forces.

Improvisation
During the events of the tenth volume, Tanya's original plan to attack the Commonwealth mainland was to make a sneak attack on the enemy, but this plan was completely ruined since the enemy was aware of their attack, so Tanya had no other choice but to completely improvise a whole new strategy to survive in this situation and adapt to any adversity that appears. This means that Tanya's entire strategy in the Commonwealth Mainland Battle could be considered as an improvisation feat.

Decision Making
After the Federation full-scale offensive was initiated, the Eastern Army group decided to put their defensive plan in action to stop the enemy offensive, but the problem is that this defensive plan was made with the incorrect idea that the Federation was going to make an offensive at spring, the Eastern Army group didn't had enough resources to repel the enemy offensive at this current situation. Tanya noticed this, that's why she made the important decision of sending false orders under General Zettour’s name to the Eastern Army Group to force them into making a strategic withdrawal. What's important is that what Tanya is making here is basically something that no logical person in the military would try to do, falsifying orders from a General would definitely result in death by firing squad and Tanya is aware of this.

Despite being aware of the consequences of such a thing, Tanya still decided to put her plan in action and send false orders to the Eastern Army, thanks to these false orders, Tanya made the highly impressive feat of avoiding the empire's collapse, it was certain that the empire was going to be annihilated with this enemy offensive, but she managed to avoid such a thing and thanks to the fact that the empire didn't collapse with the enemy offensive, the Imperial army was capable of repelling the enemy offensive in the future and the empire not only survived this full-scale offensive, but they managed to counter the enemy offensive in the future and inflict heavy losses on the Russy Federation. All of these things were only possible due to Tanya's decision to send false orders to the Eastern Army group.

Risk taking
While fighting against a group of Republican soldiers, Tanya decides to negotiate with them and ask for them to surrender peacefully, she then thinks about this choice she made:

(Tanya's thoughts)
|
“Perhaps they will open the doors for negotiations, and things will go well for both sides. No one can call themselves an adult if they are bound by preconceptions. Maybe I’ve only been looking at Republican soldiers as stereotypes. People are more than appearances. Surely we need to gain a thorough understanding of people's inner selves to interact with them properly. All individual personalities are one of a kind and thus deserving respect. Even in the middle of a war, if it might be possible to negotiate with someone, you should be sincere with them. Of course, negotiating with the enemy will naturally get you court-martialed. Forsaking combat is treated the same as fleeing before the enemy; you have no way to talk yourself out of the death by firing squad that awaits. However, if I can avoid meaningless combat as an upright individual, I'll accept the personal risk. If I can make myself understood, I'm willing to forget opportunities for promotion and time off. I'll earn them by defending myself from war-crazed maniacs”

Knowledge
Currently, Tanya has knowledge about History, Psychology, Mythology, Science: [Physics and Chemistry], Politics, Economy, Law, International and National laws, Militarism(alongside all the areas of knowledge that militarism covers such as: anthropology, Sociology and Cultural studies) and all areas of knowledge that are linked to Human resources.

Knowledge Application
In the third volume, during an operation in the Rhine front where Tanya and her battalion invaded the Republic's headquarters, she demonstrates to have scientific knowledge when explaining to one of her soldiers that they can use Carbon monoxide or hydrogen to cause an explosion in that place and make the oxygen burn up, which would result on the enemy asphyxiating. She applied this knowledge to her current situation and managed to eliminate all the soldiers hidden inside a secret base underground without not even having to directly fight against them.

Another good feat is that during many of her battles alongside her battalion, Tanya demonstrates to have knowledge about the laws of physics and Inertia, multiple times using it at her advantage in the middle of her battles, one example of this being when she charged on a bunch of enemy aerial mages with an altitude difference of two thousand feet in order to gather mass since the faster you are, the stronger your mass will be, in other words, the faster you are, the stronger your attacks and destructive capacity will be when you hit the target directly since you will accumulate mass thanks to the high movement speed.

Not only this, but during the events of the Light Novel, Tanya quite literally uses her knowledge about training methods to create a completely foolproof plan, she used her knowledge to create a plan that didn't have any chance to fail, with the only reason as to why it failed being because Being X ended up intervening.

Pattern Recognition
During the events of Tanya's Strategy/Plan to survive at Soldim 528, she receives an urgent message from high command: “From General Von Zettour to the Lergen Kampfgruppe. Colonel Von Lergen promptly began the designated actions. At first sight, this message doesn't make any sense for Tanya. But after analyzing it, she figures out the hidden message contained on this message from high command. Tanya observes that the words “Lergen Kampfgruppe” and “Colonel Von Lergen” followed a specific pattern, they were all outward-facing labels, Tanya then guesses that the same is true for the “designated actions”. She then guesses that the actual message should be read with these words removed: “promptly take action”. After thinking for some time, Tanya notices that action, in the end of the message, is assertive spontaneity. In imperial army staff officer education, it's told for the soldiers that their role is to carry out the mission they're given and to obey not the letter but its intent.

Tanya then starts to think about the intention of this message from Zettour's point of view. Based on all the previous major operations initiated by him like the one used to destroy the Republic, he's an expert with an exhaustive knowledge of logistics, his personality certainly rules out passively waiting with no plan, it's even possible that he could seize the initiative independently, he would even favor an aggressive breakthrough action. After thinking about this, Tanya reaches the conclusion that the word “promptly” in the message should indicate “assertiveness”. Zettour has a very radical side and apparently the Higher-ups want for Tanya to take prompt action. If a staff officer like Tanya is being asked to act, then it can only be an order to begin an operation and choose the optimal action. In other words, the message from high command asks for Tanya to come up with a way to break out of her current situation, that situation being the fact that she's surrounded, this means that General Zettour is going to do something that involves Tanya breaking out of her current situation. After this, she connects the dots and figures out that Zettour is planning to make an operation to break the siege by making an aggressive action.

With this, Tanya managed to figure out the concealed message hidden on the text sent to her by high command, the secret message was that Zettour was going to initiate a maneuver battle and her Kampfgruppe should act in concert with their operation. Here, Tanya managed to grasp General Zettour's intentions and read his thoughts with such a complex and weird message. In this entire Reasoning process, Tanya relied on her understanding of Zettour’s personality and his thinking pattern in order to figure out the message in what appears to have taken less than a minute, which demonstrates her impressive inductive reasoning, pattern recognition, abstract reasoning/thinking and VCI feat.

Information Gathering
During the events of the eighth Volume, after figuring out how the Federation aerial mages quality improved so much in such a short amount of time, Tanya gave orders for Visha to send a request on Colonel Von Lergen’s name requesting for the General staff to search for information about the Federation army soldiers from the previous era. By making this request on Lergen's name, who is a member of operations, she can guarantee that her request will be processed as an internal request and all officers from the General staff would start to search this information requested by Tanya. In other words, Tanya could make requests for the General staff to search any kind of information on Colonel Von Lergen's name and efficiently gather information from them.

Analysis
During the events of the Twelfth Volume, Tanya received sealed orders from General Von Zettour, the contents of the orders were for Tanya's Kampfgruppe to secure the area around the Ildoan Capital. After reading this, Tanya starts to think about these orders and abducts that her superior plans to do a strategic reorganization of the war fronts, also reaching the conclusion that these orders to secure the area around the Capital have the purpose to give time for the empire to perform this strategic reorganization.

P1: Tanya received orders from General Von Zettour to secure the area around the Ildoan Capital.

P2: The fact that General Von Zettour gave orders for Tanya to secure the area around the capital and not the capital is strange, such a thing implies that he has no intention of focusing on the capital at all.

Initial Conclusion: Maybe it's meant to be some sort of diversion or a ruse to distract international observers. Anyone who sees the empire secure the area around the capital and set up defensive positions would assume that the imperial army is adopting a defensive position.

Counter-Conclusion: But what if such a thing is merely a deception planned by Zettour? Considering his personality and strategic thinking, such a thing makes sense.

P3: Then this means that the imperial army's true goal is something else.

P4: Tanya is fairly confident about her understanding of Zettour's intentions. She can't really imagine him pushing for the empire to occupy the Ildoan peninsula in its entirety.

P5: After some time, Tanya recalls that the empire already did something like this before during the events of the revolving door operation in the Rhine Front.

Second Conclusion: Maybe General Von Zettour is planning on abandoning the Ildoan capital to perform a second revolving door operation.

Counter-Conclusion: But after a few seconds, Tanya recognizes that this idea is ridiculous. The empire has only just taken the city, normally, securing it is the obvious next step. And when it comes to the Capital, the political significance is tremendous. Securing the surrounding area would usually imply securing the capital itself. This much should be a given. Would the Empire ever voluntarily abandon such a strategic point? The empire obviously wouldn't do such a thing.

P6: The orders given to Tanya and her Kampfgruppe are to secure the area around the Capital. So it makes sense that the rest of the world would make the assumption that the empire is fortifying its defenses in Ildoa.

Third Conclusion: What such a thing would mean for the war? The answer is simple, it's precisely the same thing the empire did on the Rhine Front. In the empire's current situation, retreating is easy, General Von Zettour is obscuring his real intentions of making a strategic reorganization of the war fronts. The orders given for Tanya's Kampfgruppe to secure the area around the Ildoan Capital have the purpose to give time for the empire to perform this strategic reorganization.

P7: But Tanya still don’t understand the point of occupying the capital for a few days, only to
retreat right after. Is it political? Or is there a military goal?

P8: Perhaps it’s an appeal to the Empire’s strength, as if saying that we’re still powerful enough to capture the capital.

Fourth Conclusion: If that’s the case, then the answer is simple. It’s important to behave in a way that is easy for others to understand. We have to demonstrate our intent to defend the capital and be as brazen about it as we can. Whatever we do, it needs to be overt and forceful enough that there’s no room for misinterpretation. It’s all an act. A performance.

Final Conclusion: General Von Zettour is obscuring his real intentions of making a strategic reorganization of the war fronts. The orders given for Tanya's Kampfgruppe to secure the area around the Ildoan Capital have the purpose to give time for the empire to perform this strategic reorganization. Not only this, but occupying the Capital for a few days has the purpose to demonstrate the empire's intent to defend the capital and be as brazen about it as we can. It’s all an act. A performance.

This also demonstrates an impressive analysis feat from Tanya since she abducted General Von Zettour's plan merely based on the orders given to her.

Multitasking
It's stated in the very end of the third volume that Tanya was flying in the lead of a combat mission while commanding combat at the same time which demonstrates impressive multitasking due to the fact that she also has to worry about not being shot down by the enemy since she's doing this in the middle of the battlefield.

Intuition
During the empire's operation to defeat the Republic in the third volume, Tanya feels a strange feeling coming from the direction of the Ocean in the Rhine Front,  from that same direction, an enemy unit passing through the maritime identification zone was coming, and she noticed this through the sheer use of intuition, the fact that she figured this out before the people commanding the operation that are talking in the radio turns this feat even more impressive.

Manipulation Resistance
Throughout the entire Light Novel, Tanya uses the Type 95 computation Orb in order to win a few important battles and to guarantee her victory over impossible odds, but one important detail is that the Type 95 computation Orb has a curse given to it by an entity similar to Being X, who is a Transcendental and Higher-dimensional Being that aims to transform Tanya into a follower of religion. Everytime Tanya uses the Type 95 Computation Orb and suffers the effects of its curse, her mind is slowly corrupted and she slowly becomes a blind follower of religion, but despite having used it 12 times so far in the story of the Light Novel, Tanya still managed to keep her mind sound and rarely demonstrates to be bothered by the manipulation of the Type 95 Computation Orb's Curse, this means that Tanya's manipulation Resistance is so insane that not even a tool created by a transcendental and Higher-dimensional Being similar to Being X is capable of manipulating her mind easily.

Dissonance Resistance
Despite being capable of resisting the manipulation of the entity that created the Type 95 computation Orb, its effects still existed inside Tanya's mind against her own will, its effects were so strong that sometimes Tanya ended up unconsciously saying something that implied that she actually followed religion despite this not being true, Tanya sees such a thing as a violation of her right to believe on whatever she wants to believe and her right to be a rational and thinking human being, she likes thinking so much that she stated during the events of the fifth Volume that she would prefer to kill herself with a gun instead of stop thinking by becoming what she hate the most, a blind follower.

Summarizing everything that I just said in a way that makes sense, Tanya has something similar to two personalities inside her mind, one being her original personality that absolutely hates religion and doesn't believe in God and the other personality created by the Type 95 computation Orb is a follower of Religion and constantly mentions God during battles, multiple times demonstrating admiration for the deity, despite such a thing, it's shown that Tanya rarely demonstrates to be suffering due to the fact that her mind is in a constant dissonance between a version of her that is literally what she hate most and the true version of Tanya, demonstrating impressive dissonance Resistance.
